### [1] [Defining 2-group symmetries on lattices and extracting their anomalies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37745)
*Yitao Feng,Yu-An Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了有限2-group对称性（0-形式G与1-形式Π₂混合）的具体格点定义，通过对称性算符之间的局部关系识别刻画混合的Postnikov类[ρ]∈H³(BG,Π₂)，并发展了一维反常提取方法，还在d=1,2,3维构造了G=Π₂=ℤ₂的量子比特对称作用，实现了H^{d+2}(B𝒢,U(1))分类的全部反常类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高群（higher-group）对称性及其反常通常以连续场论或拓扑语言描述，缺乏微观格点层面的具体实现与判据。作者希望把2-group对称性及其混合结构落实到具体的格点算符与局域关系上，从而为研究反常对称性提供微观框架。

Method: 1）用对称性算符之间的局域关系给出有限2-group对称性的格点定义，这些关系直接识别出刻画0-形式与1-形式混合的Postnikov类[ρ]∈H³(BG,Π₂)；2）提出一维反常提取方法：将对称性操作限制在一个区间上，并比较其在两端点附近的复合结果；3）对G=Π₂=ℤ₂构造d=1,2,3维的显式量子比特殊对称作用，其中部分实现无反常、部分有反常但共享同一非平凡Postnikov类；4）用上同调分类H^{d+2}(B𝒢,U(1))检验构造的完备性。

Result: 构造出的量子比特作用具有相同的非平凡Postnikov类，却涵盖无反常与有反常两类实现；其上同调反常由H^{d+2}(B𝒢,U(1))分类，该群在d=1,2时为ℤ₂，在d=3时为ℤ₂×ℤ₂，并且所构造的作用在相应维度下实现了每一个类。

Conclusion: 本文将高群对称性及其反常与具体格点操作相联系，给出了研究反常对称性的微观框架；表明同一Postnikov类下可存在不同反常性质的实现，且格点构造足以穷尽上同调分类所允许的全部反常类。

Abstract: Ordinary $0$-form symmetries $G$ can mix with $1$-form symmetries $Π_2$ to form a $2$-group $\mathcal{G}$. We give a concrete lattice definition for a class of finite $2$-group symmetries in terms of local relations among symmetry operators. These relations identify the Postnikov class $[ρ]\in H^3(BG,Π_2)$ that characterizes the mixing. We develop a method to extract anomalies of these symmetries in one spatial dimension by restricting symmetry operations to an interval and comparing their composition near its endpoints. We also construct explicit qubit symmetry actions in $d=1,2,3$ spatial dimensions for a $2$-group with $G=Π_2=\mathbb{Z}_2$. These actions share the same nontrivial Postnikov class but include both anomaly-free and anomalous realizations. Their cohomological anomalies are classified by $H^{d+2}(B\mathcal G,U(1))$, which is $\mathbb{Z}_2$ for $d=1,2$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ for $d=3$, and our constructions realize every class in these dimensions. Our results connect higher-group symmetry and its anomalies to concrete lattice operations, providing a microscopic framework for studying anomalous symmetries.

</details>


### [2] [Fundamental limits on photonic state preparation from phase-space majorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37755)
*Twesh Upadhyaya,Oliver Hahn,Ulysse Chabaud,Nikolaos Koukoulekidis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文建立了"弱完全相对优序化"（weak, fully relative majorization）理论，用于比较相空间函数（如Wigner函数）的"无序度"，从而给出概率性量子态转换在精确与近似意义下的基本界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备复杂光量子态通常依赖从简单态经实验可实现的概率性操作转换而来，但此前缺乏对这类概率性转换（尤其近似转换）的基本极限的理论刻画。作者希望为此类态制备建立普适的理论下界工具。

Method: 提出弱完全相对优序化理论，通过比较相空间函数的无序度来刻画态转换的可行性；框架可针对不同系统与不同允许操作集合灵活定制；重点研究多模光子系统中的Wigner函数，并应用于压缩态、猫态、Gottesman–Kitaev–Preskill态与Fock态。

Result: 对涉及上述态的精确与近似转换，给出了严格的不可能性（no-go）结论与权衡关系（tradeoff relations）；既导出了新的界限，也重现了已知的紧致界限。

Conclusion: 相空间无序度对光子态制备构成强限制，表明概率性转换方案在根本上受限于相空间函数的相对优序化结构。

Abstract: Preparing complex quantum states of light for quantum information processing tasks often proceeds by converting from simpler states via experimentally accessible operations. We provide fundamental bounds on such probabilistic conversions, both exact and approximate. To do so, we establish a theory of weak, fully relative majorization that compares the disorder of phase-space functions. Our framework is versatile and can be tailored to different systems and sets of allowed operations. Focusing on Wigner functions in multimode photonic systems, we establish rigorous no-go results and tradeoff relations for exact and approximate conversions involving squeezed states, cat states, Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill states, and Fock states. In doing so, we derive novel bounds and recover known tight ones. Our results demonstrate that phase-space disorder imposes strong limitations on photonic state preparation.

</details>


### [3] [Magic in Grover's Algorithm and the Role of Oracle Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37772)
*Alessio Paviglianiti,Vincenzo Savona*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文刻画Grover算法全过程中的非稳定子性（magic），给出稳定子Rényi熵的解析预测并证明其强度不随系统规模增大而增长；同时分析电路分解带来的magic开销，指出解已知时可实现magic有界的有效分解，而不可知实现（如3-SAT）则产生体积律非稳定子性，从而排除基于稳定子的有效模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover算法是量子优势的教科书范例，但已有研究多关注查询复杂度，缺乏对其相对于可高效模拟的稳定子态的量子复杂度（即非稳定子性/magic）增长的系统刻画，也缺少对电路编译（分解为基本门）所额外引入magic的分析。

Method: 以稳定子Rényi熵为量化指标，计算每次Grover迭代结束时的编译无关magic并导出解析预测；进而研究将电路分解为基本门所产生的magic开销，区分“解已知”与“不可知（agnostic）”两类实现，并以3-SAT问题作为后者的具体算例进行检验。

Result: 得到Grover每次迭代末magic的解析预测，并证明其随系统规模增长保持intensive；解已知时存在有效分解，即使使用大量Toffoli门也能使magic保持有界；而对3-SAT等不可知实现，确认其产生体积律非稳定子性。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了Grover算法中magic的增长规律以及“解可知性”在电路编译中的关键作用：解已知可实现magic有界的编译，而不可知场景下magic呈体积律增长，使基于稳定子的方法无法有效模拟实际相关问题。

Abstract: Grover's algorithm is a textbook example of quantum advantage, providing a quadratic query speedup over classical methods for unstructured search. In this Letter, we characterize the non-stabilizerness, or magic, developed throughout Grover's protocol, quantifying quantum complexity with respect to the efficiently simulable stabilizer states. First, we compute the compilation-independent magic at the end of each Grover iteration, deriving an analytic prediction for the stabilizer Rényi entropy and proving that it remains intensive as the system size grows. We then investigate the magic overhead due to decomposing the circuit into elementary gates. When the solution to the search problem is known, we show that an efficient decomposition exists that keeps magic bounded despite using an extensive number of Toffoli gates. In contrast, agnostic implementations are expected to generate volume-law non-stabilizerness, as we confirm for the 3-SAT problem, which rules out efficient simulation by stabilizer-based methods in practically relevant cases. Our findings clarify the growth of magic in Grover's algorithm and the role of solution-awareness in its circuit compilation.

</details>


### [4] [Entanglement measures and LOCC conversion criteria from Schur-Weyl duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37785)
*Anton van Cleemput,Otfried Gühne,Albert Rico*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于Schur-Weyl对偶与半定规划(SDP)收敛层级的方法，可在任意精度下优化纠缠谱函数在混合态分解上的取值，并据此刻画LOCC态转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态间可能转换的刻画与纠缠量化是理解量子资源的核心，但此类问题需要对转换协议或态分解做复杂优化，显式结果稀缺，缺乏通用可计算的工具。

Method: 利用纠缠谱的自然多项式基与Schur-Weyl对偶，构造对混合态分解下纠缠谱函数优化的收敛半定规划层级；并通过提取最优分解来建立判据。

Result: 方法可任意精度逼近纠缠谱函数的最优值，也能处理纠缠形成(entanglement of formation)等非多项式度量并刻画纠缠维数；给出任意维度下纯态到混合态LOCC不可转换的证书层级，以及LOCC可转换的启发式方法。

Conclusion: 该SDP层级为双分态纠缠谱泛函的优化与LOCC转换判定提供了系统、可收敛且适用于任意维度的计算框架。

Abstract: The characterization of possible transformations between quantum states and the quantification of entanglement are central for understanding quantum resources. Explicit results are, however, scarce since they require complicated optimizations over transformation protocols or state decompositions. We introduce a method to optimize functions of the entanglement spectrum of bipartite states over mixed-state decompositions with arbitrary accuracy. Our results are based on a convergent semidefinite programming hierarchy of optimizations over entanglement spectra using a natural polynomial basis and Schur-Weyl duality. Our approach can also be used to determine non-polynomial measures, such as the entanglement of formation, and to characterize the dimensionality of entanglement. Moreover, extracting the decomposition allows us to formulate criteria to determine state transformations under local operations and classical communication (LOCC). In particular, we provide a hierarchy of certificates of LOCC non-convertibility from pure to mixed states in any dimension and a heuristic approach for LOCC convertibility.

</details>


### [5] [Topological Quantum Error-Correcting Thresholds of a Symmetry-Enriched Toric Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37791)
*Jiasheng He,Ryan Thorngren*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究一种新拓扑量子纠错码在独立比特-相位翻转噪声下的纠错阈值：通过映射到经典自旋玻璃模型，发现X、Z等概率时顶点综合征阈值比环面码提高30%，且阈值随Z分量减少而升高，无Z错误时达0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑量子码的实用性能取决于其在噪声下的阈值，需要评估新型码相对于环面码在非对称噪声下的阈值表现，明确其优势与局限。

Method: 将码的纠错问题映射为经典自旋玻璃模型，用统计力学方法计算顶点综合征与面（plaquette）综合征的阈值；对无Z错误情形给出严格可纠性解析证明；并借助无序版本的Peierls论证导出顶点综合征阈值的下界。

Result: Pauli X与Z等概率时，顶点综合征阈值较环面码提升30%，而面综合征阈值下降；Z分量概率低于X分量时两者阈值均上升，Z错误缺失时阈值达到0.5。

Conclusion: 新码在偏置噪声下具有阈值优势，存在一个可完美纠错的特殊极限情形，且顶点综合征阈值有严格下界，但其面综合征方向存在代价。

Abstract: We study the error thresholds of a new topological quantum error-correcting code under the independent bit-flip and phase-flip noisy channel. By mapping to classical spin-glass models, we find that if Pauli X and Z errors are equally probable, the threshold of vertex syndromes is improved by 30% compared to that of the toric code, while the threshold of plaquette syndromes is lowered. Both thresholds increase when the Z component is less likely than the X component, reaching 0.5 if the Z component is absent. An analytical proof justifies this special case of perfect correctability, and a disordered version of Peierls's argument establishes a lower bound for the vertex-syndrome threshold.

</details>


### [6] [Lower Bound for Region of Non-Distillable Magic States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37807)
*Akul Sethi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展Campbell与Browne关于"bound magic states"的证明，给出了位于稳定子八面体之外、但仍无法实现通用量子计算的状态区域的更紧下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: magic state是容错通用量子计算的主要方案，但其理论尚不完善，甚至"哪些单量子比特态具有magic"这一基本问题仍开放；Campbell与Browne已证明八面体外存在非通用态，本文旨在刻画该区域的具体范围。

Method: 在Campbell与Browne原始证明的基础上进行推广与细化，通过分析稳定子八面体之外的态在magic distillation/通用性判据下的行为，推导非通用区域的边界。

Result: 得到该"bound magic"区域的下界，即明确了稳定子八面体之外至少有一块可被界定为无法用于通用计算的状态集合。

Conclusion: 稳定子多面体之外的状态并非都具备通用计算能力，"magic"的判定需要更精细的几何刻画；本文的结果为该区域的定量界定提供了进展。

Abstract: Magic states are a leading proposal for realizing fault-tolerant and universal quantum computation. At the present moment, much of the theory of magic states is not understood. Even simple questions such as what single qubit states have magic remain open. In Campbell and Browne's seminal paper on bound magic states, they prove the existence of states outside of the stabilizer octahedron which cannot be used to achieve universal computation. In this paper we expand upon their proof and determine a lower-bound on this region.

</details>


### [7] [Stellar Representation of Gottesman Kitaev Preskill States on the Double Discrete Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37831)
*Aidan Zlotak,Nicolas Fabre,Romain Murenzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在环面构型的双离散柱相空间上建立协变积分量子化，给出Wigner分布与星形表示，并用其零点的有无与分布刻画理想GKP逻辑态、其叠加态及（有限压缩）真空态的量子资源特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP态是容错量子计算架构的核心，但其量子资源（非高斯性、可经典模拟性）仍缺乏清晰的相空间刻画工具；双离散柱相空间对平移不变态（如GKP态）特别适配，因此需要在该相空间上建立系统的量子化与表示框架。

Method: 在双离散柱相空间（构型空间为环面）上发展协变积分量子化，导出Wigner分布与星形（stellar）表示；分析理想GKP逻辑态（相当于该相空间的相位本征态）、其叠加态、真空态及有限压缩GKP态在该相空间上的零点结构随峰宽与包络宽度的变化。

Result: 理想GKP逻辑态的星形表示在该相空间上无零点，而其叠加态的星形表示有无穷多零点；真空态虽为高斯态却在该相空间上有零点；有限压缩GKP态的零点随峰宽与包络宽度移动，并在宽度取特定值时落到该相空间上。

Conclusion: 星形表示的零点结构可作为区分GKP逻辑态、叠加态与真空的相空间判据；结果与"真空是使GKP架构难以经典模拟的资源"这一认识相呼应。

Abstract: Covariant integral quantization is developed on the double discrete cylinder phase space, with the torus as configuration space, and yields the Wigner distribution and the stellar representation. This phase space is particularly well suited to translationally invariant states, such as the Gottesman Kitaev Preskill (GKP) states. These states are central to fault-tolerant quantum computing architectures, yet their quantum resources are still under investigation. The stellar representations of ideal GKP logical states, which behave as phase eigenstates of the double discrete cylinder, have no zeros on this phase space, whereas those of their superpositions have infinitely many. The vacuum, although Gaussian, has zeros on this phase space, and the zeros of finitely squeezed GKP states move with the widths of the peaks and of the envelope, and reach this phase space for fine-tuned widths. These features echo the role of the vacuum as the resource that makes GKP-based architectures hard to simulate.

</details>


### [8] [Shor's algorithm compiled on cat qubits: 22 593 qubits for RSA-2048 in 3.14 days](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37839)
*Axel Pappalardo,Dahmun Goudarzi,Victor Hertz,Hugo Jacinto,Larissa Kroell,André Schrottenloher,Peter Shanahan,Élie Gouzien,Rémi de la Vieuville,Vivien Londe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对超导猫量子比特（cat qubits）上运行Shor算法进行了端到端资源估计，从算法数学描述一直到芯片布局上的物理量子比特分配与调度。在重复猫码与LDPC猫码两种架构下编译主导子程序，给出RSA-2048、secp256k1上的ECDLP以及FFDLP-512的物理资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子资源估计多停留在逻辑层或缺少从算法到物理芯片布局的完整链路，尤其缺乏针对猫量子比特（噪声偏置特性、门集与注入流程均不同于表面码）的系统性评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，为基于偏置噪声量子比特的密码破解类应用提供可比较、可复用的端到端资源基准。

Method: （1）综述RSA、ECDLP、FFDLP的现代Shor算法版本，明确其逻辑线路与时空权衡；（2）将主导子程序编译到重复猫码与LDPC猫码两种架构的物理操作层，为此设计奇偶校验Cnot门、将多目标Cnot门扩展到LDPC猫码架构，并把主动注错流程适配到猫量子比特；（3）基于上述原语，把Gidney 2025年RSA-2048估计中的加法器移植到猫量子比特，并结合Kroell等FeMoco估计中的工厂设计与查找表编译（phaseup采用较粗估计），生成芯片布局与调度方案。

Result: 在1 μs纠错周期、10 μs反应时间、电路级相位翻转噪声p_Z=5×10⁻⁴且无比特翻转的假设下：RSA-2048（完整编译配置）约需23 000个猫量子比特、3.14天；复用已编译操作，secp256k1上的ECDLP最多需30 000个猫量子比特、1小时；所提出的中间基准FFDLP-512约需6 700个猫量子比特、21分钟。以空间换时间时，RSA-2048可在约611 000个猫量子比特上用1.25小时完成。若增加距离-3比特翻转码的级联层，可将比特翻转时间要求从约100年放宽到约1小时，此时RSA-2048约需45 000个猫量子比特。

Conclusion: 猫量子比特架构在偏置噪声假设下可给出具有竞争力的RSA-2048破解资源规模（万级量子比特、天级时间），且空间—时间可按需权衡；同时指出"无比特翻转"这一强假设可通过轻量级联显著放宽，代价是量子比特数适度增加。该工作提供了从算法到芯片布局的完整估计框架，可作为后续偏置噪声架构与资源评估的可比基准。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end resource estimation of Shor's algorithm on superconducting cat qubits, from its mathematical description to the allocation and scheduling of physical qubits on a chip layout. We review modern versions of Shor's algorithm for RSA, ECDLP and FFDLP, and make their logical circuits and trade-offs explicit. We compile the dominant subroutines down to physical-qubit operations on two architectures: one based on repetition cat codes and one on LDPC cat codes. We design a parity-Cnot gate for cat qubits, extend a multi-target Cnot gate to the LDPC-cat architecture, and adapt an active injection procedure to cat qubits. Building on these primitives, we adapt to cat qubits the adder from Gidney's 2025 resource estimate for factoring RSA-2048. We combine our adder compilation and a coarser estimate for the phaseup with the factory designs and lookup compilation from the FeMoco resource estimation of Kroell et al. Together, these lead to chip layouts and schedules for factoring RSA-2048.
  Assuming a 1$μ$s error-correction cycle time, a 10$μ$s reaction time, circuit-level phase-flip noise with probability $p_Z = 5 \times 10^{-4}$ and no bit-flips, factoring RSA-2048, the configuration we compile in full, requires about 23 000 cat qubits and 3.14 days. Reusing the compiled operations, solving ECDLP on secp256k1 requires at most 30 000 cat qubits and 1 hour, and FFDLP-512, which we propose as an intermediate benchmark, about 6 700 cat qubits and 21 minutes. Trading space for time, RSA-2048 runs in 1.25 hours on about 611 000 cat qubits. The no-bit-flip requirement (a bit-flip time of about 100 years) can be relaxed to a bit-flip time of about one hour by adding a concatenation layer with a distance-3 bit-flip code, in which case RSA-2048 needs about 45 000 cat qubits.

</details>


### [9] [Ground-state energy estimation compiled on cat qubits: 7 164 qubits for FeMoco-54 in 23.5 hours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37840)
*Larissa Kroell,Hugo Jacinto,Victor Hertz,Axel Pappalardo,Adel Ben Moussa,Manuel Rispler,Peter Shanahan,Élie Gouzien,Christophe Vuillot,Vivien Londe*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对Low等人的态和平方(sum-of-squares)块编码算法在超导cat qubit上的实现进行了详细的资源估计，为FeMoco基准的基态能量估计给出量子比特数与运行时间指标。

Abstract: We provide a detailed resource estimate of the state-of-the-art sum-of-squares block encoding algorithm for electronic structure Hamiltonians of Low et al. on superconducting cat qubits. To minimize the cost of Clifford corrections induced by magic state injections we tailor injection gadgets to each subroutine. Assuming a cycle time of 1$μ$s, a reaction time of 10$\mathrmμ$s, a phase estimation precision of 1 mHa and circuit-level phase-flip noise with probability $p_Z = 5 \times 10^{-4}$ with negligible bit-flip error, FeMoco-54 requires fewer than $7\,500$ cat qubits with an expected runtime of under one day for a single phase estimation run using an LDPC-cat architecture. Abstract: We formulate the standard quantum teleportation protocol within the framework of deformation quantization, employing the Stratonovich--Weyl (SW) correspondence and the associated star product on the coadjoint orbits of $SU(2)$. Each qubit is represented by a spherical phase space $S^2$ endowed with the Kirillov--Kostant--Souriau (KKS) symplectic structure. Within this setting, we explicitly construct the phase space representations of the entangled Bell resource, the controlled-NOT and Hadamard operators, the projective measurements, and the conditional Pauli corrections. We demonstrate how the quantum information initially encoded in the Wigner function for the input qubit is redistributed through bipartite and tripartite phase space correlations. Upon consideration of a conditional unitary transformation, the measurement outcome produces a Wigner function on the receiver's coadjoint orbit that is related to the input by an outcome-dependent rotation, which can subsequently be inverted through the implementation of an appropriate Pauli correction. The covariance of the SW correspondence provides a geometric interpretation of this process, in which quantum information is redistributed and reconstructed without transferring the underlying symplectic geometry. Our formulation can also be extended to study quantum teleportation in more general phase space geometries equipped with suitable star products.

TL;DR: 本文回应Shackel基于Bertrand悖论对2014年"普适平均导出Born规则"的批评，提出以"无差异(indifference)"为核心的普适测量概念，证明任何无差异普适测量都唯一地给出Born概率，2014年的胞腔构造只是无穷多种实现之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2014年作者与Aerts在扩展Bloch表示下把Born规则解释为对所有可选测量相互作用方式的普适平均，但Shackel指出其结果依赖离散化方式、平均序列恒定、且对"所有"密度的平均含义不清。本文把这些批评当作刺激，重新审视该概念，以澄清普适平均的良定义性并给出不依赖具体离散化的唯一性论证。

Conclusion: "无差异"是使普适平均良定义且唯一的关键，Born规则的统计性可理解为测量选择方式不偏袒任何结构的后果；2014年构造并非特设的离散化，而是无穷多等价实现之一。结果把Gleason定理的几何内容浓缩为正交落点，并给出可检验的偏离参数，可推广到N结果与Bertrand悖论框架。

Abstract: With D. Aerts, we showed in 2014, within the extended Bloch representation of quantum mechanics, that the Born rule can be understood as resulting from a universal average over all the possible ways in which the measurement-interactions can be selected. The cellular construction used there was since questioned, in the context of Bertrand's paradox, by N. Shackel, who observed that the result depends on the discretization adopted, that the sequence of averages is constant, and that the sense in which the average is taken over "all" densities remains unclear. Taking these criticisms as a stimulus, we re-examine the notion. The key is to recognize that a universal measurement is a random way of selecting a measurement which does not privilege any portion of the structure describing the available interactions, a property we call indifference. We prove that every indifferent universal measurement, however constructed, produces exactly the Born probabilities: the universal average is unique, and the cellular construction of 2014 is one among infinitely many ways of obtaining it, exact at every finite resolution. Non-trivial indifferent universal measurements exist, but must involve deterministic measurements: Dirichlet processes are indifferent universal measurements reaching every way of selecting an interaction, whereas an indifferent universal measurement free of deterministic components necessarily selects the uniform density, so that the Born rule holds not only on average but with certainty. Deviations from the quantum statistics in sequences of measurements are governed by a single parameter, in principle accessible to experiment. The only geometric inputs are the Euclidean length of the membrane and the orthogonal landing of the point-particle on it; the latter is where the content of Gleason's theorem lies. Generalizations to N outcomes and to Bertrand's paradox are discussed.

Abstract: A unified Fourier framework for quantum weight enumerators of prime-power-dimensional qudits is developed in this paper. We introduce the notions of Weyl power spectrum and Weyl-conjugation autocorrelation for the Weyl-operator quantities nderlying the Shor--Laflamme weight enumerators. Representing these quantities as formal vectors, we show that they form a symplectic Fourier transform pair, from which the quantum MacWilliams identity follows by applying the Hamming-weight functional. This framework extends to a family of twisted Fourier transforms, including the Rains shadow transform. We show that identical-copy Bell sampling corresponds to a twisted autocorrelation related to the shadow enumerator through Weyl translation and Hamming projection. We further prove that all twisted Fourier transforms are Weyl translations of either the ordinary MacWilliams transform or the Rains shadow transform. This two-class reduction implies that the twists yield no additional independent coefficientwise positivity constraints under compatible projections. Nevertheless, the framework leads to refined MacWilliams and shadow identities that retain more Fourier information than ordinary Hamming weight enumerators. We employ these refined identities in linear programming, together with simplex mapping and shadow-coset counting, to exclude surviving candidate weight distributions. These techniques yield tighter bounds for binary and qutrit quantum codes, including nonexistence results for specific code parameters.

Abstract: The Pusey--Barrett--Rudolph (PBR) theorem is commonly taken to establish that a pure quantum state represents a physical property of the system to which it is assigned, rather than merely encoding knowledge. In this work, I distinguish between state-first and dynamics-first physical theories, and argue that this reading presupposes the former. These two types of theories support different approaches to causal explanation, and one of PBR's stated assumptions depends on the state-first approach. Using a recent interpretation of quantum theory as a concrete example, I show how a dynamics-first theory can explain the PBR correlations while maintaining a broadly epistemic reading of quantum states. I conclude that the PBR theorem supports a dilemma: either the quantum state is part of reality, or reality is not fundamentally built out of states.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Rapid preparation of a target quantum state and reset to the many-body ground state are essential for quantum information processing. While the results demonstrate consistent improvements over standard QAOA for MWIS, only marginal gains are observed for MKP, with the advantage diminishing as the problem size increases. Overall, these findings highlight both the potential of variational Grover mixers and the trade-off between their performance gains and the additional resources required for their training.

Abstract: In a tilted Rice--Mele chain, the Wannier--Stark (WS) ladder becomes staggered, \(E_n=n\Fo+(-1)^{n+1}A\). Abstract: This review aims to provide researchers, experimentalists, and quantum engineers with an updated and technically grounded perspective on the implementation of quantum gates in superconducting NISQ devices. The goal is to establish a comprehensive bridge between theoretical quantum circuit synthesis and its practical, experimental implementation, focusing on superconducting quantum technologies. We systematically examine the diverse landscape of superconducting qubits and processor architectures, ranging from conventional transmons to alternative and unconventional designs, evaluating their unique design trade-offs, connectivity constraints, device physics, and materials impact. Moving from theory to actual implementation, we detail how single- and two-qubit operations are synthesized via microwave-driven and flux-activated pulses. We here present our pulse engineering, wave shaping, calibration workflows, demonstrating how to bridge the gap between abstract gate models and real-world hardware limitations in the direction of a hardware-software co-design framework. Conclusion: Goldbach表示可在随机分束器+素数投影的量子光学装置中物理实现，其统计量即探测概率；表示的叠加结构决定了相位估计的精度标度差异，从而把数论统计与量子计量精度极限联系起来，为利用量子光学探测量论问题提供途径。

Abstract: We derive a relation between Goldbach representations and photon-number statistics in a quantum-optical setup. Considering an even-number Fock state injected into a stochastic beam splitter with uniformly distributed transmissivity and projections of the two output modes onto prime subspaces, we demonstrate that each successful outcome corresponds to an Goldbach representation and that the averaged detection probability is exactly proportional to the number of Goldbach representations. Then, employing the Hardy--Littlewood asymptotic formula, we demonstrate that the post-measurement state generated by a beam splitter with fixed transmissivity exhibits standard quantum limit scaling of the ultimate precision limit in terms of phase estimation, whereas an equally weighted superposition of Goldbach representations exhibits Heisenberg scaling. These results provide a quantum-optical realization of Goldbach representations and relate their statistics of with quantum metrology.

Abstract: Quantum and hybrid quantum-classical algorithms have the potential to improve the accuracy and scale of quantum chemistry and impact a broad range of applications such as drug discovery and catalysis. In particular, subspace methods such as sample-based quantum diagonalization (SQD) can already tackle classically challenging systems. However, practical algorithms that go beyond the computation of electronic ground and excited states are still scarce. Here, we introduce the variable-geometry sample-based quantum diagonalization (VGSQD) algorithm, which extends SQD to variable molecular geometries by reusing quantum samples from a reference geometry at displaced ones, thus significantly reducing quantum computational cost. Using circuits of up to 124 qubits on ibm-quebec, an IBM Heron r2 quantum processor, we determine the equilibrium geometry and energy of the weakly bound, strongly correlated beryllium dimer, as well as the water molecule. Finally, we extend VGSQD to chemical reactions and compute the minimum energy path and energy barrier of a hydrogen exchange reaction. The results are comparable with state-of-the-art classical methods such as CCSD(T) and DMRG. This work broadens the scope of quantum algorithms for chemistry, from energies to geometry optimization and reaction pathways, both essential for practical applications.

Abstract: Quenched thermodynamics combines the equilibrium physics of fixed coupling configurations with a prescribed distribution of disorder. Problems related to quenched thermodynamics are generally intractable to approximate. We provide a quantum algorithm to calculate quenched disorder averages for the transverse-field Ising model, whose coupling configurations are organized into gauge classes. We show how to estimate these averages by preparing approximate Gibbs states through ancilla-assisted imaginary-time evolution. At each measurement, the ancilla record identifies the gauge class to which the energy estimate contributes.
  We validate our framework through an open $3\times3$ lattice. A one-step circuit resolves all sixteen flux classes, and a finite-shot calculation estimates the quenched energy for independently distributed bond signs. Thermodynamic integration extends the reconstruction to a quenched free-energy estimate, while comparison with exact diagonalization identifies the finite-step approximation as the dominant source of error. This use of the measurement record provides access to the thermodynamics of frustrated spin systems with a disorder distribution chosen independently of the circuit's sampling frequencies.

</details>


### [49] [Generalized Mean-Field Ising Model as a Benchmark Family with Exact Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38803)

Motivation: 现有可精确求解的伊辛模型要么结构过于平凡，要么难以同时兼顾解析可解性与具有挑战性的优化结构，缺乏能超越精确对角化规模的可控基准。

Abstract: Exactly solvable models provide opportunities to understand complex optimization problems while offering controlled benchmarks for computational methods. However, nontrivial models that combine analytical tractability with challenging optimization structure remain scarce. Here, we introduce the Generalized Mean-Field Ising (GMFI) family, a densely connected and inhomogeneous class of Ising Hamiltonians whose ground states can nevertheless be obtained exactly. This exact solution reveals a hidden low-dimensional structure and enables a complete number-theoretic classification of different system sizes and degenerate ground states. Uniquely, the GMFI family can be used to benchmark both quantum and classical optimization methods over system sizes far beyond exact diagonalization. We investigate the GMFI family using several approaches, including theoretical analysis of quantum annealing, experiments on a D-Wave quantum processor, the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA), imaginary-time evolution (ITE), greedy algorithm, simulated annealing, and a quantum-inspired greedy algorithm. Despite the GMFI model's exact solvability, it exhibits nontrivial algorithmic behavior: although the quantum-annealing gap is expected to decrease with increasing system size, it shows pronounced and unexpected system-size-dependent oscillatory signatures. These results establish GMFI models as a rare bridge between exact many-body theory, number theory, optimization, and quantum hardware, providing a scalable benchmark for identifying and understanding algorithmic limitations in classical and quantum computing.

</details>


### [50] [Generating Multipartite Entanglement via Structured-Bath Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38807)
*Baptiste Debecker,Philipp Westhoff,Visal So,Sebastian Paeckel,Guido Pagano,John Martin,Mattia Moroder,François Damanet*

Method: 在弱耦合理论下研究相互作用Ising链：将强对称性用于把非二次生成元的稳态构造约化为对任意Ising耦合的经典问题；并通过工程化浴谱将时间浴关联转化为经典空间关联，再经强对称性提升为量子关联；辅以精确马尔可夫嵌入与张量网络模拟检验超出解析区间的机制。

Abstract: We present how to dissipatively prepare genuinely multipartite-entangled steady states with mutually independent local reservoirs by harnessing an Abelian strong symmetry and temporal bath correlations. In interacting Ising chains, we demonstrate that, for a broad class of system-environment couplings, flat baths exponentially erase Ising-axis correlations. Structured baths overcome this obstruction by converting temporal bath correlations into classical spatial correlations. Strong symmetry then promotes these correlations to the quantum domain, enabling global multipartite entanglement. Within weak-coupling theory, we obtain exact steady states despite the generator's nonquadratic nature: strong symmetry reduces their construction to a classical problem for arbitrary Ising couplings. This reduction reveals an exact connection between classical distinguishability and entanglement. We then show how to engineer the bath spectrum to increase the correlation length and enable the preparation of GHZ-like states from separable initial conditions. We test the mechanism beyond the analytical regime using exact Markovian embeddings and tensor-network simulations. Our approach is particularly suited to superconducting and trapped-ion platforms, and we propose a concrete implementation for the latter using sympathetically cooled motional modes.

</details>


### [51] [Key Evaluation of a Relativistic Quantum Key Distribution Over a Turbulent Free-space Optical Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38808)
*G. Bebrov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了湍流自由空间光信道下相对论量子密钥分发（RQKD）在单光子与相干态两种实现方案中的性能，建立了湍流致相位涨落影响干涉可见度与密钥率的理论模型，并给出系统设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由空间RQKD链路不可避免受大气湍流影响，但湍流对干涉可见度及安全密钥生成的具体定量影响尚未明确，缺乏设计优化依据。

Method: 将湍流诱导的相位涨落纳入密钥率分析，构建描述大气湍流对干涉可见度与密钥生成影响的理论模型；在真实信道条件下进行数值仿真，评估可达通信距离与密钥率。

Result: 湍流通过降低干涉可见度使协议性能下降，退化严重程度由干涉臂间距r（或信号态与参考态时间间隔ΔT）与Fried参数之比决定；单光子协议在所有考察湍流区间仍可工作，而相干态方案强烈依赖干涉仪几何（尤其ΔT），存在安全成码阈值。

Conclusion: 定量刻画了大气湍流对RQKD的影响，并为自由空间RQKD系统的设计与优化提供了实用指导。

Abstract: We investigate the performance of relativistic quantum key distribution (RQKD) over turbulent free-space optical channels for both single-photon and coherent-state implementations. A theoretical model is developed to describe the effect of atmospheric turbulence on interferometric visibility and secret-key generation by incorporating turbulence-induced phase fluctuations into the key-rate analysis. Numerical simulations are performed under realistic channel conditions to evaluate the achievable communication distance and secret-key rate. The results show that atmospheric turbulence degrades the protocol performance by reducing interference visibility, with the severity of the degradation determined by the ratio between the interferometer-arm separation $r$ (or temporal separation of signal and reference states $ΔT$) and Fried's parameter. The single-photon protocol remains operational over all investigated turbulence regimes, whereas the coherent-state implementation exhibits a critical dependence on the interferometer geometry (especially on $ΔT$), leading to a threshold beyond which secure key generation is no longer possible. We further discuss how energy-induced mass superpositions could be directly probed using external gravitational field sensors. By contrast, observing gravity-induced entanglement involving clocks would require substantially greater experimental advances due to decoherence from spontaneous emission. Abstract: Proposals to probe the quantum nature of gravity through entanglement between spatially separated masses have attracted considerable interest. We show that the density matrix after $n$ splitting stages is related to that of the $(n-1)$-splitting protocol through local parity dephasing. Two of the resulting sectors reproduce the preceding state, while the other two acquire additional gravitational phases. Removing the locally generated parts gives an exact lower bound on the change in negativity, expressed in terms of the preceding state's negative partial-transpose eigenspace. For two splitting stages, this leads to an explicit condition on three gravitational phases under which the additional split cannot reduce negativity. We also show that further splitting cannot shorten the lifetime of the generated entanglement. If the local internal system preserves the full branch label, the positional entanglement is retained upon recombination. We describe an implementation using a reusable two-level system and local memories, and compare it with a readout without a memory register. For the latter, we derive an exact condition for when an additional splitting stage improves the readout, based on a $2\times 2$ Schur complement. Numerical simulations show an advantage in positional negativity over the rates considered, with an improvement surviving the restricted readout at all decoherence rates considered at the final readout time.

Abstract: Repeated-use quantum state transfer (QST) goes beyond the single-use paradigm by accounting for the memory that accumulates inside the transmission channel when it is not reset between uses. Consequently, the map governing the $n^{\mathrm{th}}$ use of the channel differs from use to use and carries a signature of each previous use. We ask whether this use number can be recovered from transmission data alone, and show that it can: a simple feed-forward neural network, given as input the Bloch vectors sent and received over a single use of an XX spin-chain channel, is able to identify the use number with an accuracy exceeding 90%. Exploiting the U(1) symmetry of the channel, we find that a reduced two-component representation of the transmitted states improves the accuracy further, to about 95%. We characterise the dependence of the classifier on the size and composition of the training data, finding that the number of sampled states and of sampled timings are equally important. We also identify the data set sizes required to reach a given accuracy. Finally, we quantify the severity of the residual misclassifications with an earth-mover distance on the confusion matrix.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于sweep rule的Toric码容错方案依赖多尺度解码器状态、尺度相关的通信延迟或expansion性质，缺少几何简单码上固定局部纠错规则的严格阈値与解码证明；同时Kubica与Preskill提出的高维立方复形上sweep修正的局部存在性问题尚未解决。

Result: 得到三项结果：(1) 显式的高维sweep rule，即立方(d,d')-Toric复形上的局部sweep修正选择算法，回答了Kubica–Preskill的局部存在性问题；(2) 容错计算：证明含噪sweep循环与逻辑门交错的电路级阈值定理，特别是4D Toric码可在无多尺度解码器、无expansion编码的情况下采用固定恒定深度的基于syndrome的纠错循环（未主张总深度开销恒定）；(3) 局部随机噪声下的理想解码：初始局部随机Pauli错误率p低于阈值时，边长n的(d,d')-Toric码经n^{d+1}次无噪声sweep循环以至少1-O(poly(n))p^{Ω(n)}的概率恢复编码态，推广了独立错误的既有保证。

Conclusion: 几何简单的立方(d,d')-Toric码（含4D Toric码）配合固定、恒定深度的局部sweep纠错循环即可实现容错量子计算，无需多尺度解码器、expansion或尺度相关延迟，并在电路级与局部随机Pauli噪声下获得严格阈值与解码保证。

Abstract: We study fault-tolerant quantum computation using a fixed, local error-correction rule and a geometrically simple code: the cubical $(d,d')$-Toric code with $d',d-d'\geq 2$, including the 4D Toric code. Under sufficiently weak local-stochastic Pauli noise, our construction uses noiseless, bounded-depth classical processing per physical time step. Its correction cycle acts only on the current syndrome, has constant depth, and is repeated unchanged during idle logical computation; it requires neither multiscale decoder states nor scale-dependent communication delays.
  Our proof treats errors and syndromes as chains in a cubical complex, combining geometric shrinking under the sweep rule with a space--time explanation-tree argument inspired by Toom's theorem. It does not rely on expansion. We establish three results:
  1.Explicit higher-dimensional sweep rule. We give a local algorithm for choosing sweep corrections on cubical $(d,d')$-Toric complexes, resolving the local-existence question of Kubica and Preskill.
  2. Fault-tolerant computation. We prove a circuit-level threshold theorem for noisy sweep cycles interleaved with logical gates. In particular, the 4D Toric code admits a fixed, constant-depth syndrome-based correction cycle without a multiscale decoder or expansion-based encoding. We do not claim constant total-depth overhead.
  3. Ideal decoding under local-stochastic noise. For an initial local-stochastic Pauli error of rate $p$ below threshold, $n^{d+1}$ noiseless sweep cycles recover the encoded state on a side-length-$n$ $(d,d')$-Toric code with probability at least $1-O(\operatorname{poly}(n))p^{Ω(n)}$, extending earlier guarantees for independent errors.

</details>

Abstract: Floquet driving reshapes cavity spectra, but single-photon sideband populations do not reveal how it changes two-photon exchange. Here we show theoretically that delay-resolved Hong--Ou--Mandel (HOM) coincidences can probe this response in a collectively coupled cavity. A frequency-entangled signal photon scatters linearly from the driven cavity while its idler provides a delayed reference. Averaging over an unsynchronized drive phase removes coherence between different net output orders but preserves exchange interference within each order. Fourier transformation of the coincidence trace yields two drive-dependent exchange branches associated with the Floquet-polariton spectrum. As the drive changes, the branches shift and exchange weight; the dominant physical output order also changes, a transfer not reproduced by single-photon sideband populations. We further map the coincidence-trace response over drive frequency and amplitude and connect it to a count-model-dependent Fisher information. Floquet control thus adds a tunable exchange spectrum to HOM interferometry and gives quantitative targets for an experiment.

Abstract: Hybrid discrete- and continuous-variable (DV-CV) encodings have been developed to overcome operational drawbacks of each individual encoding, while fusion-based quantum computation (FBQC) has emerged to address the practical challenges of scalability toward fault-tolerant photonic quantum computation. We develop protocols for directly generating these resource states without requiring local hybrid logic such as the Hadamard gate, together with a tailored decoding strategy that exploits the error information contained in individual hybrid-fusion outcomes. We demonstrate that hybrid FBQC achieves a photon loss threshold of 2.231% while requiring only 12 hybrid fusions per surface-code unit cell, substantially improving upon the 0.89% threshold and 60-fusion requirement of previous hybrid measurement-based scheme. These results establish optical hybrid FBQC as a high-threshold resource-efficient route to scalable fault-tolerant photonic quantum computation.

Abstract: Quantum reference frame (QRF) transformations relate the description of a physical system to different quantum observers, but their connection to concrete experimental phenomena remains largely unclear. Here we show that the trajectory-sharpening effect obtained by correlating a probed system with an effective negative-mass reference admits a natural interpretation as a QRF transformation to the frame of a negative-mass system. Using a minimal toy model of free particles with signed masses, we identify the Einstein-Podolsky-Rosen-type observables responsible for this effect with a pair of commuting relational observables that, under the instantaneous QRF transformation, are mapped to operators local to distinct subsystems. We then show that the corresponding relational trajectory is reproduced by the dynamical QRF transformation, where the transformed relational position acquires an explicit time dependence. We introduce QRF-invariant sharpness measures for relational trajectories and derive monogamy relations showing that a trajectory cannot be made simultaneously arbitrarily sharp with respect to two distinct negative-mass frames. We further show that trajectory sharpness can instead reside in a collective reference mode encoded across two physical systems, even when neither constituent alone provides an arbitrarily sharp reference, so that sharpness can be distributed rather than localized in a single constituent frame. These results provide an operational, experimentally relevant interpretation of QRF transformations for continuous-variable systems.

Abstract: Can measurements chosen adaptively, in response to earlier outcomes, be exponentially more informative than measurements fixed in advance? In many quantum settings, adaptivity helps at most polynomially; known exponential advantages require coherent measurements across copies or restricted measurements. We show an exponential advantage measuring one copy at a time, with no quantum memory kept between state copies, even when the nonadaptive learner may perform arbitrary global measurements on each copy. We construct explicit families of multi-qubit states in which a scalar parameter is encoded in one of exponentially many candidate observables, with an auxiliary marker indicating which one. An adaptive protocol identifies the relevant observable and then measures it using only single-qubit measurements and one round of adaptation. At fixed accuracy, it needs logarithmically many copies as the qubit count grows, whereas every nonadaptive protocol needs exponentially many. The same mechanism yields an exponential gap in local asymptotic minimax mean squared error constants, tight up to the exponential base. The states are separable and classical in label-dependent product bases; no entanglement or other nonclassical correlations are required. The gap comes from measurement incompatibility: the candidate observables cannot be measured jointly, so a fixed measurement must divide its information budget among them, while adaptivity selects the informative one. Accordingly, the separation disappears when the candidate observables commute, and cannot occur with fully observed classical samples. Measurement incompatibility thus emerges as a resource for quantum learning, distinct from entanglement and quantum memory, that classical feedback alone can exploit.

</details>

Abstract: Magic, a quantum resource distinct from entanglement, measures departure from states stabilized by products of Pauli operators. Such stabilizer states can be maximally entangled yet admit efficient classical description: quantum advantage requires magic. In the transverse field Ising model, we find a thermal sudden death of magic that saturates with system size, and occurs after the death of entanglement. We reach similar conclusions in generic spin chains, and in the Kitaev quantum spin liquid on the honeycomb lattice, where the threshold shows an intricate dependence on subregion shape. We then study how magic equilibrates after a quantum quench. In quenches of generic spin chains, the magic of a subregion typically dies before entanglement, contrary to thermal trajectories. An explanation is given in terms of the geometry of non-magical states. As a third type of trajectory, we examine the fate of magic with separation with an appropriate notion of non-local magic and establish a sudden death. Finally, we show how to experimentally witness a volume law of frame-robust magic in quantum materials by using neutron scattering.

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity check (QLDPC) codes may lead to order-of-magnitude reductions in the qubit overhead required for fault-tolerant quantum computation. The syndrome after surgery confirms return to the code space and the unaddressed ''spectator'' qubit is preserved with error 0.21 [0.04, 0.67]% during the three-round measurement. We further demonstrate composition of conjugate basis gadgets by sequentially measuring $\bar{X}_1$ then $\bar{Z}_2$, reusing the gadget qubits, and obtain a 1.12 [0.60, 1.94]% error in the $\bar{Z}_2$ measurement. These results constitute a hardware validation of the generalised surgery primitive underlying Pauli-based computing for QLDPC architectures.

Abstract: This paper investigates a quantum relay-assisted free-space optical (FSO) communication system in which a decode-and-forward relay is powered by time-switching optical energy harvesting and forwards the decoded information using displaced squeezed signaling (DSS). Under this channel, coherent states remain pure, whereas squeezed states are mapped to mixed Gaussian states. Under Helstrom-optimal detection, the average error of the coherent hop is derived in exact Laplace-series form. For the DSS hop, we derive the output states, a closed-form Gaussian fidelity with rigorous two sided bounds on the Helstrom error, and prove the existence of a loss-aware optimal photon partition. Furthermore, we prove that optimized squeezing preserves both the turbulence-induced diversity order and the leading asymptotic constant of coherent relaying, so that its benefit is a finite-photon-budget effect, and that an interior throughput-optimal harvesting fraction exists. A covariance-preserving benchmark with an exact series representation yields an analytical upper bound on the achievable throughput whose absolute gap vanishes as the relay photon budget grows. Numerical results based on the exact mixed-state Helstrom error quantify the benefit of squeezing and the regime in which it is useful.

Abstract: Quantum experiments without a definite causal order offer new ways to distinguish devices, estimate physical parameters, and communicate. Parallel experiments with separate reference-assisted inputs approach the bound, giving an exact equality after optimization over processes. Abstract: Small mineral patches can be spectrally distinct yet difficult to detect when diffraction mixes their light with the surrounding rock. Conventional hyperspectral detectors analyze recorded intensities, leaving an opportunity to recover target evidence through the optical measurement itself. We develop a quantum-assisted framework for exploiting this opportunity in prospective unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) hyperspectral sensing. A common radiometric and diffraction model describes direct imaging and spatial-mode measurements of the same light. The theory identifies target changes that survive background fitting and explains when mode measurements retain more detection information. It quantifies losses from mode mismatch and unresolved outputs, linking the quantum benchmark to finite receiver design. A common-basis result identifies when material spectra can share a spatial measurement at fixed geometry. An adaptive receiver uses an imaging pilot to select candidate modes and combines both stages in a full-region presence test. Simulations based on measured mineral spectra and published UAV acquisition scales demonstrate gains over complete direct imaging at a shared active-exposure budget, including pilot cost and optical loss. At matched empirical 1\% region-level false alarms, detection increases from 62.95\% to 69.65\% for a representative target. Paired analysis supports gains with registration uncertainty and, separately, under controlled nonuniform backgrounds. Calibrated mode errors reveal where measurement losses weaken or reverse the benefit. These results connect background identifiability to receiver choices and their operating conditions. They provide a framework for designing spatial measurements alongside spectral channels to detect small material features.

</details>

Abstract: This paper proposes a VLSI-like design and verification flow for quantum circuit implementation, covering four levels: behavioral modeling, register-transfer level (RTL), gate level, and full-circuit integration with quantum algorithms. Using quantum Monte Carlo methods for pi estimation as a case study, the proposed flow provides a systematic path from initial specifications to large-scale quantum circuit implementation. The paper highlights two key contributions. First, quantum digital design supports a broad range of applications beyond predefined circuit templates. This capability is particularly important for application-specific operations in algorithms such as Shor's, Grover's, and quantum amplitude estimation (QAE), and can significantly advance fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC). Second, classical simulation shows that replacing random sampling with grid sampling can achieve a quadratic improvement in this pi-estimation case without quantum computation. This suggests that the quadratic speedup may arise from the grid sampling strategy rather than from the QAE circuit itself. By adapting conventional VLSI digital design principles, this four-level flow addresses a key bottleneck in quantum circuit design and enables flexible design initiation from multiple entry points, including specifications, RTL, and gate-level descriptions, paving the way for the practical deployment of quantum computing.

</details>

Motivation: 近中期量子平台亟需高效求解计算困难问题的量子算法；NP-hard组合优化问题（编码为具有少数离散单自旋翻转跃迁能量的原子自旋哈密顿量）的高效基态制备仍具挑战，而崎岖能量景观中的局域极小会严重阻碍优化过程。

Conclusion: 腔耗散可作为一种可控资源用于量子优化，其诱导的假真空衰变机制为近中期平台上的组合优化提供了有前景的求解途径。

Abstract: Efficient quantum solvers for computationally hard problems are highly desirable for near-term quantum platforms. Here, we propose a cavity-based dissipative quantum solver for a class of NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems encoded in atomic-spin Hamiltonians with a few discrete single-spin-flip transition energies. We show that the cavity-based quantum solver exploits dissipative false-vacuum decay in configuration space to facilitate escape from metastable local minima in rugged energy landscapes. Numerical studies of MaxCut and Maximum Independent Set identify favorable parameter regions for ground-state preparation. Based on the finite-size scalings of the median mixing time and instance-averaged ground-state preparation probability, we estimate time complexities for our solver that are more favorable than those of standard Grover search. These results highlight cavity dissipation as a controllable resource for quantum optimization.

</details>


### [76] [NAKO: A Benchmarking Framework for Evaluating and Diagnosing Neutral-Atom Quantum Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39296)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可重构中性原子处理器的门调度、原子放置与并行搬运相互耦合，编译决策共同决定物理执行成本；评估此类编译器既缺少可达成的资源参考基准，也缺乏把超额成本追溯到具体编译决策的手段。

Method: NAKO 以量子比特数、逻辑深度及请求的单/双比特门密度参数化电路，为 Rydberg 阶段、路由区间与移动层设定可达成目标，并为每个基准配一个可执行参考调度；在给定执行模型下，构造保证最少 Rydberg 阶段与路由区间数，并在最小 Rydberg 阶段调度中保证最少移动。通过参考映射与中间物理状态进行受控干预，对比编译器输出以量化资源差距并归因。

Result: 在针对 Enola 与 OLSQ-DPQA 的 648 次运行评估中识别出不同瓶颈：Enola 的放置评分与跨阶段布局管理导致超额移动，诊断引导的干预在 5 个匹配电路上平均降低移动 78.9%；OLSQ-DPQA 的过早候选选择与预备状态探索不足造成可避免的纠缠阶段与路由区间，四个固定检查点续算各消除一个 Rydberg 阶段与一个路由区间且不增加移动。

Conclusion: NAKO 将具构造性最优性保证的资源目标与受控诊断、验证结合起来，可有效定位并验证编译器特定的资源节省。

Abstract: Reconfigurable neutral-atom processors couple gate scheduling, atom placement, and parallel atom transport, so compiler decisions jointly determine physical execution costs. Evaluating these compilers requires attainable resource references and a way to trace excess costs to their causes. We present NAKO, a benchmarking framework that parameterizes circuits by qubit count, logical depth, and requested one- and two-qubit gate densities, and assigns attainable targets for Rydberg stages, routing intervals, and movement layers. Each benchmark is paired with an executable reference schedule; under the stated execution model, construction guarantees minimum Rydberg-stage and routing-interval counts, and minimum movement among schedules with the minimum Rydberg-stage count. Comparing compiler outputs with these references quantifies resource gaps, while reference mappings and intermediate physical states enable controlled interventions that trace excess costs to specific compiler decisions. In a 648-run evaluation of Enola and OLSQ-DPQA, NAKO identifies distinct bottlenecks. For Enola, placement scoring and cross-stage layout management contribute to excess movement; diagnosis-guided interventions reduce mean movement by 78.9% across five matched circuits. For OLSQ-DPQA, premature candidate selection and limited exploration of preparatory states produce avoidable entangling stages and routing intervals; four fixed-checkpoint continuations each eliminate one Rydberg stage and one routing interval without increasing movement. NAKO thus links resource targets backed by constructive optimality guarantees to controlled diagnosis and validation of compiler-specific resource savings.

</details>

Conclusion: 建立了理论指导并经实验验证的量子态分类框架，可在当今可用的量子器件上实现，为利用量子数据结构的机器学习式量子系统分析提供可行路径。

Abstract: Analyzing quantum systems is essential for quantum information processing, but can require exponentially many measurements without exploiting the structure of the system or task. Quantum machine learning offers a promising way to reduce this cost by learning from quantum data. Here, we theoretically establish a framework for efficient quantum-state classification and experimentally demonstrate the resulting scheme on a programmable photonic processor. Our analysis links learning efficiency to the structure of quantum data, without relying on a physical description tailored to each task. We derive sufficient conditions for accurate classification with a number of measurements polynomial in system size. We apply this guarantee to a resource problem in continuous-variable (CV) quantum computing: classifying stellar rank, a measure of non-Gaussianity. Experimentally, we implement the classification of single-mode CV quantum states with stellar rank zero and one. This is enabled by developing a programmable CV photonic processor capable of preparing and processing diverse Gaussian and non-Gaussian states and performing virtual projection onto such states, going beyond the Gaussian-only capabilities of most existing photonic platforms. The experiment demonstrates accurate classification despite experimental imperfections. Our results establish a theory-guided and experimentally validated framework for quantum-state classification that can be implemented on quantum devices available today.

</details>


### [78] [Composable Device-Independent Quantum Digital Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39360)
*Jing-Wei Bian,Bing-Hong Li,Xu-Yang Lu,Shi-Gen Li,Hua-Lei Yin,Zeng-Bing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在构造性密码学框架下建立了首个可组合安全的量子数字签名（QDS）框架，并提出基于一次性通用哈希与多信道复用的设备无关（DI）QDS协议，同时给出LFSR-Toeplitz认证的相关密钥安全界，在有限码长下验证了城域距离可高效签署多比特文档。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将设备无关量子密码扩展到可组合地支撑全部基本信息安全目标，是构建安全量子网络的关键需求，而QDS需同时提供完整性、真实性与不可否认性。然而现有QDS协议普遍依赖对量子器件的假设，安全分析多局限于独立（stand-alone）场景，且未处理底层基于LFSR的Toeplitz认证所面临的相关密钥攻击。

Method: 在构造性密码学（constructive cryptography）中建立首个QDS可组合安全框架，并构造相应的理想QDS资源；设计采用一次性通用哈希与多信道复用的DI-QDS协议来具体实现该理想资源；推导底层LFSR-Toeplitz认证的相关密钥安全界，以在更广的伪造边界下证明不可伪造性；并在有限码长体制下开展面向真实量子网络条件的数值仿真。

Result: 给出了LFSR-Toeplitz认证的相关密钥安全界，将既有QDS安全分析的伪造边界加以扩展并据此建立不可伪造性；数值仿真表明，该DI-QDS协议在有限码长下可在城域尺度距离上高效签署多比特文档，具备实际可行性。

Conclusion: QDS由此被确立为一种可组合的密码原语，设备无关量子密码也被扩展到在未来量子网络中、以可组合安全保证支撑全部信息安全目标。

Abstract: Extending device-independent (DI) quantum cryptography to composably support all fundamental information security objectives is crucial for secure quantum networks. Achieving this goal requires composable quantum digital signatures (QDSs), which support integrity, authenticity, and nonrepudiation when integrated into quantum networks. Yet existing QDS protocols generally rely on assumptions about the underlying quantum devices, while their security analyses remain largely restricted to stand-alone settings, leaving related-key attacks on the underlying linear feedback shift register (LFSR)-based Toeplitz authentication unaddressed. Here, we establish the first composable security framework for QDS within constructive cryptography, and realize the corresponding ideal QDS resource by developing a DI-QDS protocol employing one-time universal hashing and multi-channel multiplexing. We further derive a related-key security bound for the underlying LFSR-based Toeplitz authentication, thereby establishing unforgeability while extending the forgery boundary considered in previous QDS security analyses. We also demonstrate the practical feasibility of our DI-QDS protocol under realistic quantum network conditions in the finite-size regime, with numerical simulations showing that it enables efficient signing of multibit documents over metropolitan-scale distances. Our results establish QDS as a composable cryptographic primitive and extend DI quantum cryptography to support the full range of information security objectives with composable security guarantees in future quantum networks.

</details>

Method: 从相干泵浦、真空信号与闲频光初态出发，对完全量子化三线性哈密顿量下的海森堡算符展开进行重新组织，重求和为泵浦耗尽剖面与回复剖面的解析表达式，并据此系统构建一阶、二阶、三阶关联；第一阶段耗尽的处理进一步推广至四阶关联与明亮压缩泵浦输入。

Result: 数值基准显示：在整个泵浦耗尽过程中误差量级为 10^-12；在数十个耗尽—回转换周期内、直至振荡首次完全坍缩，误差仍低于 10^-3；第一耗尽区间的处理还可给出四阶关联并兼容明亮压缩泵浦输入。

Conclusion: 该解析框架在无参量近似、无拟合参数条件下高精度复现全量子三波混频动力学，可自然扩展至更高阶矩，并揭示对未来光量子技术有意义的新现象。

Abstract: We derive analytic expressions for observable dynamics under the fully quantized trilinear Hamiltonian, governing quantum three-wave mixing and parametric amplification. Starting from a coherent pump and vacuum signal and idler, the calculation reorganizes the Heisenberg expansion into resummed depletion and return profiles, yielding directly evaluable expressions for all 1st-, 2nd- and 3rd-order correlations. The construction uses no parametric approximation or fitted parameters. Representative numerical benchmarks show errors of order $10^{-12}$ throughout the entire pump depletion, and below $10^{-3}$ across dozens of depletion-and-back-conversion cycles, up to the first total collapse of the oscillation. The first-depletion treatment additionally provides 4th-order correlations, accommodates bright squeezed-pump inputs and reveals phenomena important to future optical quantum technologies. The framework can be extended to higher moments.

</details>


### [80] [Conditional Information Flow and Neural Representability of One-Dimensional Rotated Cluster States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39392)
*Ziqi Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在一维旋转团簇态（任意旋转角下精确MPS键维恒为D=2）上，长程条件依赖（以条件互信息CMI衡量）并不需要大键维；把这一小虚拟内存直接嵌入网络架构可显著简化优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在底层量子态只携带很小内部内存（小键维）的情况下，测量结果如何在长距离上保持条件依赖，并澄清"长程CMI是否必然要求大键维"以及"学习该态时架构是否应内嵌这一小内存"两个问题。

Method: 以旋转团簇态为对象，用精确MPS收缩计算两种读取协议（纠缠交换端点读取与常规团簇态的定常体读取）的CMI随旋转角θ的变化；以自旋-1 AKLT链（CMI按I_m=(4/9)3^{-m}衰减）作精确对照；在N=64、θ=0.35的同一目标上比较学习器：正自回归GRU（学习计算基Born分布）、可精确计算的Bell基读取、复数RBM（N=8、精确目标）、通用循环变分蒙特卡洛试点，以及隐状态即键向量的MPS诱导循环模型（χ=2与χ=1对照）。

Result: 纠缠交换端点CMI由θ=0时的1比特降至θ=π/2时的0；局域等价的常规团簇态定常体读取在两个极限下均消失。AKLT链CMI长度恰等于其两点关联长度，但原因是可识别而非普适的。学习方面：GRU能用数千样本学到计算基Born分布，但可精确求解的Bell基读取表明该分布并不固定相位；复数RBM在精确目标下达到N=8基态；短程通用循环VMC试点在结束时仍在改进；MPS诱导循环模型在χ=2时以近机器精度达到基态，χ=1对照失败。

Conclusion: 长程CMI不要求大键维（D=2即可支持跨长距离的条件依赖）；把小的虚拟内存纳入架构设计可极大降低优化难度，而仅拟合Born分布的生成模型不足以确定量子相位。

Abstract: I study how measurement outcomes can remain conditionally dependent over long distances while the underlying state carries only a small internal memory, using one-dimensional rotated cluster states, whose exact matrix product state (MPS) bond dimension is $D=2$ at every rotation angle. Using exact MPS contraction, I compute the conditional mutual information (CMI) of two readout protocols: the entanglement-swapping endpoint CMI decreases from one bit at $θ=0$ to zero at $θ=π/2$, whereas a stationary bulk readout of the locally equivalent conventional cluster state vanishes at both limits. As an exact control, the spin-1 AKLT chain obeys $I_m=(4/9)\,3^{-m}$, so its CMI length equals its two-point correlation length, for an identifiable but non-generic reason. I then compare learners on the same $N=64$, $θ=0.35$ target. A positive autoregressive GRU learns the computational-basis Born distribution from a few thousand samples, but an exactly computable Bell-basis readout shows that this distribution does not fix the phases. A complex RBM reaches the $N=8$ ground state under an exact objective, while a short generic recurrent variational Monte Carlo pilot is still improving when its runs end. As a positive control, an MPS-induced recurrent model whose hidden state is the bond vector reaches the ground state to near machine precision at $χ=2$, whereas a $χ=1$ control fails. These results show that long CMI does not require a large bond dimension, and that building the small virtual memory into the architecture greatly eases optimization.

</details>

Conclusion: JC哈密顿量可视为一类更广义哈密顿量的一个特例；这类广义哈密顿量保持了JC模型的核心可解结构，因此可推广用于更广泛的原子-场相互作用问题。

Abstract: The aim of this work is to define and investigate the generalized Jaynes-Cummings (JC) Hamiltonian by means of supersymmetric (SUSY) quantum mechanics methods. We have considered trigonometric and hyperbolic Pöschl-Teller (PT) potentials as two natural ingredients to modify the standard JC model. We show that these generalized JC Hamiltonians have a list of characteristics in common with the JC Hamiltonian, for example: matrix intertwining, excitation number symmetry, Rabi frequency, formulas for atomic inversion and classical and revival times. Therefore, we conclude that JC Hamiltonians can be considered as one example of a wider a class of generalized Hamiltonians which can be useful in broader interaction problems.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对目标精度 ε，Ni–Ying 核函数族达到极小极大最优 LCHS 查询代价，其渐近值为 (2e/π)(ln(1/ε) − ln ln(1/ε) + o(1))，最优主项常数为 2e/π。

Abstract: Linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation (LCHS) is an efficient method for simulating dissipative dynamics on quantum computers. The choice of the LCHS kernel controls the cost of the algorithm. We prove that the kernel family proposed by Ni and Ying~\cite{NY26} minimizes the LCHS query cost in the minimax sense. For target accuracy $ε$, the minimum cost is asymptotically $\frac{2\mathrm{e}}π(\ln\frac1ε-\ln\ln\frac1ε+o(1))$, with optimal leading constant $\frac{2\mathrm{e}}π$. Our lower-bound proof combines weak duality with a realization lemma that approximates extremal dual functions by matrix elements of dissipative evolutions.

</details>

Abstract: This paper introduces a hybrid quantum framework for training Variational Quantum Algorithms (VQAs) aimed at addressing the fundamental problem of entanglement detection using adiabatic quantum computing. The optimization problem is formulated as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model to leverage the capabilities of quantum annealing. Preliminary results are compared against classical neural networks and conventional VQA training techniques. The findings aim to stimulate further investigation and discussion within the scientific community regarding the efficacy and applicability of the proposed approach.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic imaginary-time evolution scheme based on a compiled approximate diagonal representation of the Hamiltonian. A basis transformation is optimized offline. For a fixed compiled representation, the online circuit structure and gate count per attempt are independent of the imaginary time, and the same representation can be reused across different input states and evolution times. We derive a condition for enhanced success probability. Numerical tests show reduced online cost relative to termwise Pauli-based methods at a common accuracy threshold. We also examine how approximation errors and offline preparation costs affect the overall benefit.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统本质脆弱，易受环境噪声影响，量子态保持是实现实用量子技术的核心难题；而实际场景中往往难以事先完整获知噪声动力学，因此需要不依赖噪声先验知识的保护方案。

Method: 采用碰撞模型（collisional model）作为统一框架，设计两种与噪声无关（noise-agnostic）的保护协议：其一针对量子比特态，无需完整先验噪声信息；其二在同条件下保护编码后的逻辑量子比特。

Result: 协议可对任意单量子比特态及纠缠态在一般噪声过程下实现稳健保护；并具备保护任意量子比特数态的能力，从而提升新兴量子架构的可靠性、鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Quantum systems are intrinsically fragile and highly vulnerable to environmental noise, making the preservation of quantum states one of the fundamental challenges in realizing practical quantum technologies. We propose two noise-agnostic quantum state protection protocols based on a collisional model. The first protocol preserves quantum states of qubits without requiring complete prior knowledge of the underlying noise dynamics, and the second even protects a logical qubit under similar circumstances. These protocols provide robust protection for arbitrary single-qubit and entangled states against general noise processes. Furthermore, their capability to safeguard quantum states with an arbitrary number of qubits enhances the reliability, robustness, and scalability of emerging quantum architectures. These results establish the proposed framework as a promising approach for practical quantum information processing.

</details>


### [86] [Error-Corrected Quantum Optimization via Rydberg Atom Array Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39670)

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 证明基于Parity Architecture的Rydberg嵌入天然形成一种经典纠错码，并可推广到其他基于Rydberg阻塞的嵌入；利用superatom子结构中的精确Rydberg激发数约束作为零硬件成本的局部错误校验子，构造严格非线性码；设计专门定制的Cluster解码器，同时利用局部约束与gadget结构来纠正比特翻转错误。

Result: 数值上在不同版图几何下获得p_th≳26%的错误阈值；在QuEra Aquila QPU上，对多达107个原子的系统，Cluster解码器恢复精确优化解的平均成功率≳8%，即使裸硬件与标准贪心后处理均无法找到任何解。

Abstract: Rydberg atom quantum optimizers naturally encode the maximum independent set problem on geometric graphs. However, to solve general combinatorial optimization problems on more complex topologies, an embedding is required to overcome the limited-range native interactions. Such embeddings use gadgets, specific arrangements of atoms, which inevitably introduce ancillary atoms. Here, we show that this apparent overhead instead provides an intrinsic resource for error correction. Specifically, we demonstrate that the Parity Architecture-based Rydberg embedding naturally forms a classical error-correcting code, a principle that extends to other Rydberg blockade-based embeddings. Within superatom substructures, exact Rydberg-excitation-number constraints provide local error syndromes at no additional hardware cost and give rise to a strictly nonlinear code. To correct bit-flip errors, we introduce the precisely tailored Cluster decoder, which exploits these local constraints and the underlying gadget structure. Numerically, this approach achieves an error threshold of $p_{\text{th}}\gtrsim26 \%$ across different layout geometries. Experimentally, on the QuEra Aquila QPU, the Cluster decoder recovers exact optimization solutions with an average success rate of $\gtrsim8\%$ for systems of up to 107 atoms, even when bare hardware and standard greedy post-processing find none. A Rydberg atom array embedding is therefore not merely a hardware mapping of an optimization problem, but a co-designed architecture for quantum optimization in which embedding, computation, and error correction emerge from the same physical structure.

</details>


### [87] [Optimized Dynamical Decoupling for Spin Ensembles with Large Inhomogeneities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39693)

Method: 在环境条件下对负电荷NV色心系综，比较大量DD协议在相干保护与量子传感中的表现；识别出采用快速绝热通道(RAP)脉冲并结合相位变化的最优序列，并与广泛使用的矩形脉冲XY8进行实验对照，进而应用于弱振荡场的灵敏探测。

Result: 与标准矩形脉冲XY8相比，最优序列实验上实现了更高的信号对比度和约一个量级的存储时间(memory time)提升；该方案亦改善了对弱振荡场的传感性能。

Conclusion: 基于RAP脉冲与相位变化的最优DD协议能有效克服自旋系综中的振幅/频率非均匀性与控制误差。虽以金刚石NV色心为演示对象，该优化协议可推广至其他自旋系综及具有高振幅和/或频率非均匀性的系统。

Abstract: Spin ensembles show increasing promise for highly efficient quantum sensing and quantum technologies. However, protecting their coherent properties remains a significant challenge. Dynamical decoupling (DD) typically employs a sequence of fast pulses that aim to reduce the effect of unwanted system-environment interactions. However, the performance of the DD sequences typically suffers in spin ensembles, e.g., due to amplitude and frequency inhomogeneities and control errors. In this work, we compare the performance of an extensive number of DD protocols for coherence protection and quantum sensing in an ensemble of negatively charged NV centers under ambient conditions. We identify the optimal approach that uses rapid adiabatic passage pulses, which are robust to amplitude and frequency inhomogeneity, in combination with the variation of their phases. We experimentally demonstrate an improved signal contrast and an order-of-magnitude increase in memory time with the optimal sequence compared to the widely used XY8 with standard rectangular pulses. We also apply our approach for improved sensing of a weak oscillating field. While our particular demonstration is with NV centers in diamond, the optimized protocol is generally applicable to other spin ensembles and to systems with high amplitude and/or frequency inhomogeneities.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum Query Complexity of Tournament Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39702)
*Pragya Arora,Udit Kumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究锦标赛解（Copeland集、未覆盖集、top cycle、Condorcet winner、strong king、最大出度顶点）在比较结果需查询获得时的量子查询复杂度，给出上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典情形下锦标赛解的计算复杂度"图景统一"：Copeland集、未覆盖集、top cycle 以及寻找最大出度顶点均需 Θ(N²) 次查询。作者希望厘清量子查询模型下这些问题的复杂度是否出现分化，并刻画各自的精确量子查询界。

Method: 构造量子算法：top cycle 采用"先找哈密顿路径、再用分治定位构成 top cycle 的前缀"的策略；下界方面，利用 Ambainis 对手方法（adversary method）将计数问题归约到锦标赛问题；并给出最大出度顶点 Θ(N^{3/2}) 下界的一个自包含替代证明。

Result: top cycle 的量子查询复杂度为 Θ̃(N)；Condorcet winner 可在 Θ̃(√N) 次查询内找到；strong king 需 Θ(N^{3/2}) 次查询，与寻找最大出度顶点相同；并为 Gilani、Mande、Patro、Raja 所建立的最大出度顶点 Θ(N^{3/2}) 界提供了独立的新证明。

Conclusion: 量子查询模型打破了经典情形下的"统一图景"：不同锦标赛解的复杂度显著分化，从 Θ̃(√N)（Condorcet winner）、Θ̃(N)（top cycle）到 Θ(N^{3/2})（strong king、最大出度顶点），且计数问题的对手方法可有效移植到锦标赛问题下界证明。

Abstract: A tournament solution records a set of winners from a tournament, that is, from the outcomes of all pairwise comparisons among $N$ alternatives. We study the quantum query complexity of computing tournament solutions when the outcome of each comparison has to be learned by a query. Classically, the picture is uniform: the Copeland set, the uncovered set and the top cycle all require $Θ(N^2)$ queries, and so does finding a single vertex of maximum out-degree. We show that the quantum query complexity of the top cycle can be computed with $\tildeΘ(N)$ queries, and a Condorcet winner can be found with $\tilde Θ(\sqrt N)$ queries. Finding a strong king requires $Θ(N^{3/2})$ queries, the same as finding a vertex of maximum out-degree. We also give a self-contained alternative proof of the known $Θ(N^{3/2})$ bound for finding a vertex of maximum degree, established by Gilani, Mande, Patro, and Raja. Our lower bounds reduce counting problems to tournament problems using Ambainis's adversary method. For the top cycle, we find a Hamiltonian path and locate the prefix forming the top cycle by divide and conquer.

</details>


### [89] [Maximum-Entropy Ensemble of Quantum Subsystems with a Fixed Average State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39712)
*Charlie Shakeshaft,Christopher David White,Harry J. D. Miller*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究固定系综平均下混合态的最大熵系综，证明其本征值密度渐近收敛于关联Wishart系综（CWE）的密度，并由此导出横向场Ising模型对MP律的高温修正及测量后纠缠的有限温度Page曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Haar随机二分态约化密度矩阵对应固定迹Wishart系综，本征值密度渐近服从Marchenko–Pastur（MP）律。作者希望将这一普适性推广到更一般的、带固定系综平均约束的混合态最大熵分布，以刻画非Haar型（如热平均态、测量后态）混合系的谱密度。

Result: 建立了约束最大熵系综谱密度的普适CWE形式，并用自由乘法卷积给出闭式密度；对TFIM热态得到MP律的高温领头阶修正；证明随机POVM测量后的混合态系综渐近等价于CWE并有数值证据支撑；给出测量后系统—辅助体纠缠的有限温度Page曲线。

Abstract: The reduced density matrices of Haar-random bipartite states form a fixed-trace version of the Wishart ensemble of random matrices, with an asymptotic eigenvalue density given by the Marchenko--Pastur (MP) law. We establish a similar universal form for the maximum-entropy distribution of mixed states subject to a fixed ensemble average, arguing that its eigenvalue density converges asymptotically to that of the correlated Wishart ensemble (CWE). The density is thus obtained by a free multiplicative convolution formula, which we apply to the transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) with a thermal average state to derive a high-temperature leading-order correction to the MP law. We then consider measurement by a random POVM on the purification of the mixed reference state. We show that the resulting mixed-state ensemble is asymptotically equivalent to the CWE, and provide confirming numerical evidence. We return to the TFIM example to compute the entanglement of the system with the ancilla after measurement, and give finite-temperature Page curves for the average entanglement entropies.

</details>

Main category: quant-ph

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子假设检验的界要么依赖技术复杂的信息谱（information-spectrum）论证，要么因固定的前因子而在非渐近区域适用性受限，缺乏统一且紧致的分析框架。

Method: 利用 Nussbaum-Szkoła 映射将量子态对的假设检验问题转化为可处理的经典概率分布问题，据此构造新的下界与上界，构成统一框架，并可部分直接复用经典结果。

Result: 所得上下界统一覆盖大偏差、中偏差、小偏差渐近区域，同时给出逆定理与可达性结论；可准确近似小块长下的最优量子误差权衡函数；数值比较显示其相比基于保真度和信息谱方法的现有界更紧致。

Conclusion: Nussbaum-Szkoła 映射提供了一种简洁统一的工具，能在渐近与非渐近区域均给出紧致且适用的量子假设检验界，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Quantum hypothesis testing concerns the discrimination between quantum states. This paper introduces novel lower and upper bounds for asymmetric quantum hypothesis testing based on the Nussbaum-Szkoła mapping. Together, these bounds provide a unified recovery of both converse and achievability results across all major asymptotic regimes, including large-, moderate-, and small-deviations. Unlike existing bounds, which either rely on technically involved information-spectrum arguments or suffer from fixed prefactors and limited applicability in the non-asymptotic regime, the proposed bounds arise from a unified framework and enable, in some cases, the direct use of classical results. It is further demonstrated that these bounds provide accurate approximations to the optimal quantum error trade-off function at small blocklengths. Numerical comparisons with existing bounds, including those based on fidelity and information spectrum methods, highlight their improved tightness.

</details>

Result: 在监督图像分类与多体相检测中，长程拟设在更少DA-QNN层数或更短演化时间下即可匹配短程对应方案的表现，原因在于非局域耦合带来更快的信息传播；该资源节省降低了经典优化开销并简化实验实现。在无监督相检测中，长程拟设并非在所有区间都优于短程方案，但二者提供互补特征，可联合重构完整相图。

Abstract: Protocols combining single-qubit digital operations with analog quantum evolutions bypass large numbers of two-qubit entangling gates, offering a promising approach to variational algorithms and hybrid quantum simulation. Focusing on the digital-analog quantum neural network (DA-QNN) as a representative framework, this work demonstrates that incorporating long-range interactions enhances the efficiency of such hybrid algorithms. Within the analog blocks, an Ising model with power-law interactions is employed to generate entanglement, and this protocol is benchmarked across supervised and unsupervised learning tasks. Beyond discussing the effects of the transverse field and evolution time, the primary analysis focuses on how the interaction range governs the expressibility and trainability of the ansatz. For supervised image classification and many-body phase detection, long-range ansatzes match the performance of their short-range counterparts with fewer DA-QNN layers or shorter evolution times, owing to the rapid information propagation across the system enabled by nonlocal couplings. This resource reduction alleviates the classical optimization overhead and eases experimental implementation. In unsupervised phase detection, although the long-range ansatz does not outperform its short-range counterpart in every regime, the two ansatzes provide complementary signatures that jointly reconstruct the complete phase diagram. These findings establish long-range interacting platforms as a compelling resource for advancing practical, large-scale digital-analog quantum computing.

</details>

Abstract: Continuous-variable distributed inner product estimation (CV-DIPE) compares unknown states prepared by distant bosonic and quantum optical systems. It supports benchmarking without efficient classical descriptions or joint measurements. We develop three CV-DIPE protocols for the natural class of energy-constrained continuous-variable quantum states, based on coordinated displaced parity, coordinated Haar measurements, and independent homodyne shadows. For arbitrary multimode inputs in this class, including entangled and non-Gaussian states, we establish truncation error bounds and worst-case sample complexity guarantees for the exact inner product. The leading terms of these bounds favor coordinated Haar measurements, whose global implementation is experimentally challenging. The three protocols provide certified inner product estimates for distributed continuous-variable systems from local measurement data.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
