Method: 考虑费米型二次开放量子系统，解析研究BLP、LPP、RHP（可分性与纠缠两个版本）以及MSV熵度量对浴态的依赖性；将浴建模为自由费米子链，推导单个费米格点与其接触时的精确时间局域主方程，并进行数值验证。

Result: 解析结果确立BLP与LPP度量的浴态无关性；而基于纠缠与基于可分性的RHP度量以及MSV熵度量仍通过温度与化学势对热库态敏感。数值结果确认上述结论，并显示BLP与LPP仅在束缚态阈值处严格为零，而此时浴敏感度量仍保持有限。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫性度量的浴态依赖性因度量定义而异：信息回流型（BLP、LPP）度量对浴态不敏感，而可分性/纠缠/熵型度量依赖温度与化学势；因此以不同度量刻画同一系统可得到定性不同的结论，选用度量时须谨慎。

Abstract: Open quantum system dynamics fail to fulfill the conditions of the Markov approximation when the environment and system timescales are close to each other. Such behavior of these systems can be characterized by several non-Markovianity measures, which are expected to vary with the environment parameters. In this work, we consider a fermionic quadratic open quantum system, establishing bath-state independence of the Breuer--Laine--Piilo and the Lorenzo--Plastina--Paternostro non-Markovianity measures. However, our analytical results reveal that the entanglement-based and divisibility-based Rivas--Huelga--Plenio measures, as well as the entropic Megier--Smirne--Vacchini measure, remain sensitive to the reservoir state, through both its temperature and its chemical potential. Modeling the reservoir as a free-fermionic chain, we derive an exact time-local master equation for a single fermionic site in contact with it. Our numerical results for this model confirm these findings and show that the BLP and LPP measures vanish identically only on the bound-state threshold, where the bath-sensitive measures nevertheless remain finite.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 建立仅利用综合征历史或结合其他经典测量结果的逻辑操作识别框架；开发考虑未知电路故障与理想测量随机性的精确推断方法及可扩展近似方法；在[[18,4,2]]与[[72,12,6]]双变量自行车（BB）码上，对自同构、逻辑Pauli测量、T注入及组合逻辑电路，在多种噪声模型和错误率下进行评估。

Result: 在电路级噪声、错误率10^-5至10^-3下，单条逻辑指令识别准确率超94%；基于自同构的逻辑电路识别准确率超99%；组合三类指令的逻辑电路识别准确率达78.32%；并分析了因测量信息不足或不可区分、故障时序及近似推断导致的识别歧义。

Conclusion: 综合征的作用可超越错误解码，用于识别故障容错计算中实际执行的逻辑操作与电路；同时表明对综合征历史及相关经典测量结果的访问可能无意中泄露所执行计算的信息。

Abstract: Syndrome histories are normally used to detect and correct errors in fault-tolerant quantum computation, but they can also reveal information about the logical operations executed during computation. We study logical-operation identification using syndrome histories alone or together with other classical measurement outcomes. We develop an exact inference method and a scalable approximation that account for unknown circuit faults and ideal measurement randomness. We evaluate automorphisms, logical Pauli measurements, T injections, and logical circuits combining these instructions on \([[18,4,2]]\) and \([[72,12,6]]\) bivariate bicycle (BB) codes under multiple noise models and error rates. A single logical instruction can be identified with over 94\% accuracy under circuit-level noise with error rates between \(10^ {-5} \) and \(10^{-3}\). We also achieve over 99\% accuracy for automorphism-based logical circuits and 78.32\% for logical circuits combining all three instructions. We further analyze identification ambiguities arising from insufficient or indistinguishable measurement information, fault timing, and approximation inference. Together, these results extend the role of syndromes beyond error decoding to identifying executed logical operations and circuits during fault-tolerant quantum computation. They also suggest that access to syndrome histories and related classical measurement outcomes may unintentionally reveal information about the executed computation.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum systems are typically controlled by classical devices that necessarily dissipate energy to the surrounding environment. By the fluctuation-dissipation theorem, this implies noise in the control field produced. We investigate the relationship between the resulting errors and the entropy produced by the macroscopic controller, working in the context of a concrete minimal model. In the most experimentally relevant regime of fast control and high fidelity, we find a tradeoff between dissipation and control errors. These findings are exemplified by perturbative calculations of the gate fidelity for simple gate implementations and of the fidelity of state transfer via shortcuts to adiabaticity in the Landau-Zener model. Our results quantitatively demonstrate that suppressing errors in quantum control typically comes at the cost of macroscopic dissipation, which is needed to tame fluctuations in the classical controller.

</details>

*Jun Tang,Congcong Zheng,Xutao Yu,Zaichen Zhang*

TL;DR: 本文提出仅依赖共享相位设定下的局域零差测量与经典通信的多模连续变量分布式内积估计（CV-DIPE）协议，在每模平均能量有界条件下给出有限样本保证与充分拷贝数，并刻画截断偏差与统计误差的权衡。

Abstract: Distributed inner product estimation (DIPE) is a fundamental task in quantum information, aiming to estimate the inner product between two unknown quantum states prepared on distributed quantum platforms. For multimode continuous-variable (CV) systems, the infinite-dimensional Hilbert space poses an additional challenge. In this work, we develop a CV-DIPE protocol using only local homodyne measurements at shared phase settings and classical communication. Under a known bound on the mean energy per mode, we derive explicit finite-sample guarantees and sufficient copy counts for estimating the full inner product to prescribed accuracy and confidence. The energy constraint yields an inverse-square truncation-bias bound, while Gaussian structure strengthens the tail control to exponential decay and improves the resulting accuracy scaling. Numerical simulations with Gaussian and non-Gaussian states illustrate the balance between truncation bias and statistical error and its dependence on mode number and energy. Our results enable independent CV platforms to benchmark their state preparations against one another, even when neither state admits a tractable classical description.

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) offers information-theoretic security, but device imperfections introduce implementation vulnerabilities. Experimental evidence of the security consequences of transmitter vulnerabilities in continuous-variable QKD (CV-QKD) remains limited. Here, we experimentally investigate a reversible photorefractive attack in a local-local-oscillator CV-QKD system. Moderate blue-light illumination reduces the modulation variance of a lithium-niobate IQ modulator to a stable value without appreciably increasing estimated excess noise. The variance recovers after illumination is removed. We elucidate the underlying physical mechanisms and develop an attack that exploits recovery to compromise modulation calibration. Receiver measurements confirm that the resulting calibration mismatch leads to overestimated channel transmittance and underestimated excess noise. Key rate calculations based on measured parameters yield a positive estimated rate when the correctly calibrated rate is nonpositive. Recovery can also make the preceding manipulation harder to identify through later inspection. This work provides the first system-level experimental evidence that a reversible photorefractive response can be exploited to compromise modulation calibration in CV-QKD. These findings highlight reversible changes in quantum transmitters as a practical security concern, motivating security analyses and protection strategies that account for both optical perturbations and subsequent recovery.

</details>


### [98] [Inevitability of the Petz transpose map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40158)
*Jacopo Surace,Chenxu Li,Francesco Buscemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在有限维全矩阵代数之间、对全部先验与信道定义的retrodiction（回推）规则，若满足对合性、序列与并行复合相容性、并与酉反演一致，则必唯一地等于Petz transpose map；放弃对合性则得到双旋转Petz映射族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计推断中Bayes法则普遍成立且唯一，而量子理论中是否存在同样普遍的、唯一的回推（retrodiction）规则尚无定论。本文旨在给出量子Bayes法则的公理化刻画，并解释Petz转置映射为何在量子信息多个领域反复出现。

Method: 以"回推指派"为对象：对任意先验态与任意信道，把预测的输出态映回其先验态。设定四条一致性要求——对合性（involutivity）、序列复合相容、并行复合相容、与酉反演一致；在对偶/Heisenberg图景中证明这些约束强制唯一解，并分析去除对合性后的解集结构。

Result: 满足全部四条要求的回推指派唯一等于Petz transpose map；其中概率混合下的仿射性与对参考态的特征性平方根依赖是定理的推论而非附加假设。若去掉对合性，其余要求刻画出一族doubly rotated Petz maps。定理在有限维全矩阵代数之间的信道上成立，输入输出空间限制于相应参考态的支撑上。

Conclusion: Petz转置映射是唯一与上述一致性要求相容的量子Bayes法则类比物，这从原理上解释了它在量子理论不同分支中自然出现的原因；同时给出了不依赖对合性时的更一般刻画。

Abstract: Bayes' rule is central to classical statistical inference: given a prior and a stochastic model, it determines the corresponding retrodictive conditional probabilities. In quantum theory, however, the question of whether an equally general rule of retrodiction exists and, if so, whether it is uniquely determined remains nontrivial. Here we prove that a retrodiction assignment defined for all priors and channels, which takes each predicted output state back to its prior, must coincide with the Petz transpose map if it is involutive, respects sequential and parallel composition, and agrees with unitary inversion. The theorem holds for channels between finite-dimensional full matrix algebras, with the input and output spaces restricted to the supports of the corresponding reference states. In particular, affinity under probabilistic mixtures and the characteristic square-root dependence on the reference states follow from these requirements rather than being postulated. Without involutivity, the remaining requirements characterize a family of doubly rotated Petz maps. Our result thus explains why the Petz transpose map arises in seemingly different areas of quantum theory: it is the unique quantum analogue of Bayes' rule compatible with these consistency requirements.

</details>


### [99] [Detuning-assisted cavity enhancement in spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40216)
*Subhasish Guha,Sourav Das,Francesco Albarelli,Aiman Khan,Elnaz Darsheshdar,Adam D. Wright,Animesh Datta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出：将腔频率从目标跃迁失谐（而非共振）可使偶极矩估计精度提高至共振情形改善量的约两个数量级，且该优势在损耗存在时依然成立，并可用常规探测手段实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腔增强光谱被广泛使用，但常规做法是让腔与跃迁共振。作者质疑共振是否真的最优，探索失谐是否能带来更高的偶极矩估计精度。

Method: 对经典连续波（CW）探测与脉冲单光子探测两类方案进行参数估计分析，比较共振与失谐条件下偶极矩估计的精度（Fisher信息框架下的精度评价），并引入损耗考察最优工作点；将精度提升归因于光—物质极化激元之间由失谐调控的信息重分配。

Result: 失谐可将偶极矩估计精度在共振增强基础上再提升最多约两个数量级；该提升对CW与脉冲单光子探测均成立，且在损耗存在时仍保持；精度提升源于光/物质型极化激元的信息重分配，损耗决定最优分支；该增强也可通过常规光电探测与时间相关单光子计数（TCSPC）实现。

Conclusion: 共振并非腔增强偶极矩估计的最佳条件；通过受控失谐并依据损耗选择最优极化激元分支，可获得远超共振方案的精度，且与现有常规探测技术兼容，具有实际可行性。

Abstract: Cavity-enhanced spectroscopy is a widely used experimental technique. We show that detuning the cavity frequency from the transition of interest can enhance the precision for dipole moment estimation by up to two orders of magnitude beyond the improvement when the cavity is resonant with the transition. We show this for both classical continuous-wave (CW) and pulsed single-photon probes, even in the presence of losses. We trace this enhancement to a detuning-controlled redistribution of information between the light- and matter-like polaritons, with losses determining the optimal branch. We show that this enhancement remains accessible using conventional photodetection and time-correlated single-photon counting.

</details>


### [100] [Bounds for General $(r,δ)$-Quantum Locally Recoverable Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40273)
*Shuowei Ma,Fei Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究不假设纯性或稳定子结构的通用 (r,δ) 量子局部可恢复码（qLRC），允许恢复块任意重叠，并给出了Singleton型上界及若干紧化与改进结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有qLRC参数界多依赖纯度或稳定子结构、恢复块不相交（分区）等假设，缺乏对一般量子子空间、任意重叠块的统一刻画。

Method: 利用多重擦除恢复的选择引理推导一般量子子空间的Singleton型上界；通过整数规划得到显式余项修正；对纯码采用局部解耦与熵论证推广Galindo等人的参数界；并优化预算以去掉分区假设。

Result: δ=2时退化为Golowich–Guruswami的单擦除界；整数规划带来的余项修正可将界最多紧化δ−2；纯码界推广至任意纯量子子空间；去除不相交恢复块的划分假设并获得进一步余项修正。

Conclusion: 分析三类达到界的码族：重叠块且d=δ的纯码（严格改进纯码界且同时取等）、不相交块达到一般界的非纯码、以及重叠块且d>δ的已有纯码族（同时达到上述所有界）。

Abstract: We study general $(r,δ)$-quantum locally recoverable codes (qLRCs) without assuming purity or a stabilizer structure, and with arbitrarily overlapping recovery blocks. Using a selection lemma for multiple-erasure recovery, we establish a Singleton-like upper bound for general quantum subspaces. When $δ=2$, this bound reduces to the general single-erasure bound of Golowich and Guruswami. An integer optimization yields an explicit remainder correction that tightens the bound by up to $δ-2$. For pure codes, local decoupling and entropy arguments extend the parameter bound of Galindo et al., originally obtained for pure stabilizer codes constructed from Euclidean or Hermitian dual-containing classical codes, to arbitrary pure quantum subspaces. We also remove the partition assumption from several existing pure-code bounds for disjoint recovery blocks and obtain a further remainder correction by optimizing the budget. Finally, we analyze three families attaining these bounds: pure codes with overlapping recovery blocks and $d=δ$, which exhibit both a strict improvement of the pure-code bound and simultaneous equality in the pure-code and general bounds; impure codes with disjoint recovery blocks attaining the general bound; and an existing family of pure codes with overlapping recovery blocks and $d>δ$, which simultaneously attains all these bounds.

</details>


### [101] [QDMin: Coherent Quantum Minimum Finding for Nuclear Deformation Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40320)
*Abhishek,P. Arumugam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QDMin（Quantum Deformation Minimum Finding）提出一种量子算法框架，用于在参数化哈密顿量族中搜索核集体势能面的最小值：以相干集体坐标寄存器表示候选点，配合按需的基态能量阈值预言机（oracle）提供判定谓词，从而在无需逐点构造能量曲面的前提下实现量子最小值搜索（Grover 型）。在四参数算例中单轮 Grover 将真实最小值概率由 0.25 提升至 0.921；二维（四极–八极场）算例将完整电路扩展至两个坐标，但最小值在零 Grover 迭代轮即被采样到，且最坏分支制备保真度仅 0.744，低于既定目标，表明相干的基态访问是当前瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核集体势能面（collective landscape）最小值的定位通常需要大量相互耦合的多体计算，逐点求解能量代价高昂。作者希望借助量子最小值搜索算法，避免先完整构造能量曲面，转而以"按需"方式调用能量阈值判定，从而在原理上获得关于候选点数量的平方级查询加速潜力。

Method: 1) 将待搜索对象表述为参数化哈密顿量族，其参数由相干集体坐标寄存器编码；2) 构造按需的基态能量阈值预言机（ground-energy threshold oracle），输出量子最小值搜索所需的判定谓词（是否低于阈值）；3) 将预言机嵌入 Grover 型最小值搜索回路（含分支制备/相干态准备与迭代放大）；4) 在示意性 pairing-plus-multipole 模型上实现，能量估计由电路生成而非经典预算；5) 在四参数情形与 4×4 四极–八极二维场两类算例上验证；6) 分析候选项查询次数的渐近标度。

Result: 四参数算例中，单轮 Grover 迭代将真实最小值被采到的概率从 0.25 提升至 0.921。4×4 四极–八极场算例将完整电路推广到两个集体坐标；但该算例的最小值在零次 Grover 迭代的那一轮即被采样到（即未获益于放大）。最坏情形下分支制备保真度为 0.744，低于声明的目标值，说明相干的基态访问（相干基态制备/预言机实现）是当前的主要瓶颈。算法在无结构模型下继承 O(√N_q) 的候选查询标度，总体优势仍取决于多体预言机成本，尚未确立。

Conclusion: QDMin 提供了一条以相干集体坐标寄存器加能量阈值预言机来直接搜索核集体势能面最小值的可行路线，并在小规模示意模型上验证了 Grover 放大的有效性；但电路级保真度不足与多体预言机成本未量化，使得端到端的量子优势目前仍未得到证明，后续工作需聚焦于高保真相干基态访问与预言机成本评估。

Abstract: Locating the minimum of a nuclear collective landscape can require many interacting many-body calculations. QDMin (Quantum Deformation Minimum Finding) searches a parameterized Hamiltonian family with a coherent collective-coordinate register and an on-demand ground-energy threshold oracle. The oracle supplies the predicate required for quantum minimum-finding without first constructing the energy landscape point by point. We implement it for a schematic pairing-plus-multipole model using circuit-generated energy estimates during the search. In a four-point calculation, one Grover round raises the probability of the true minimum from 0.25 to 0.921. A $4\times4$ quadrupole--octupole-field calculation extends the complete circuit to two coordinates, although its minimum is first sampled in a round with zero Grover iterations. Its worst-case branch-preparation fidelity of 0.744 falls below the declared target, identifying coherent ground-state access as the current bottleneck. QDMin inherits expected $O(\sqrt{N_q})$ candidate-query scaling in the unstructured model; total advantage depends on the many-body oracle cost and remains unestablished.

</details>


### [102] [Subsystem Spacetime Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40357)
*Esther Xiaozhen Fu,Aranya Chakraborty,Daniel Gottesman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了“子系统时空码”（subsystem spacetime code），用于刻画Clifford线路的故障距离，并证明其规范群可由瞬时稳定子群的掩蔽性质显式构造与分类，恰为对线路无害的故障集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码是分析与设计容错Clifford线路的有力框架，但缺乏对动态码（dynamical code）距离损失位置及线路组合后故障距离变化的统一刻画工具。

Method: 构造子系统时空码：将线路在时空上的故障结构编码为子系统码；利用瞬时稳定子群的masking（掩蔽）性质显式构造并分类其规范群（gauge group），并借助该框架定位动态码距离损失之处、分析由较小组件组合成线路时故障距离的变化规律。

Result: 证明时空码的规范群恰由对线路无害（harmless）的故障构成，可由瞬时稳定子群的掩蔽性质显式给出并分类；据此可识别动态码距离损失的来源，并刻画线路组件化组合时故障距离的演化。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析与设计容错线路提供了通用工具，并有望用于构造新的动态码。

Abstract: Quantum error-correcting codes provide a powerful framework for analyzing and designing fault-tolerant Clifford circuits. In this work, we construct the subsystem spacetime code and use it to characterize the distances of Clifford circuits. We show that its gauge group can be explicitly constructed and classified using the masking properties of the instantaneous stabilizer groups and consists precisely of faults that are harmless to the circuit. We use this framework to identify where a dynamical code loses distance and to analyze how the fault distance changes when circuits are composed from smaller components. Our results provide a versatile tool for analyzing and designing fault-tolerant circuits, with potential applications to the construction of new dynamical codes.

</details>


### [103] [Quantum communication: a discrimination approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40358)
*Hao-Chung Cheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从纠缠恢复与态分辨的对偶性出发，利用Petz恢复映射构造纠缠生成码，给出适用于任意信道使用次数的误差界，并以Petz–Rényi相干信息表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无辅助（unassisted）噪声量子信道通信，希望获得具有显式译码器、且在有限码长下也成立的纠缠生成编码方案与误差界。

Method: 基于纠缠恢复与量子态分辨之间的操作对偶性，采用Petz恢复映射显式构造纠缠生成码及其译码器，并将误差界用Petz–Rényi相干信息表达。

Result: 所导出的误差界对每个（任意）信道使用次数n均成立，不限于渐近情形；对乘积源，误差呈指数衰减，衰减速率可低于相干信息。

Conclusion: 态分辨视角为无辅助量子通信提供了一条构造性路径，得到有限码长有效的纠缠生成码及基于Petz–Rényi相干信息的误差刻画，并给出乘积源下的指数衰减速率。

Abstract: We develop a state discrimination approach to unassisted quantum communication through a noisy quantum channel. Starting from the operational duality between entanglement recovery and state discrimination, we construct entanglement-generation codes with explicit decoders constructed from Petz recovery maps. The resulting error bounds hold for every number of channel uses and are expressed in terms of Petz-Rényi coherent information. For product sources, they yield exponential error decay at rates below coherent information.

</details>


### [104] [Rapid mixing for Gibbs samplers for quantum spin systems with all-to-all interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40361)
*Nicola Bertoni,Giacomo De Palma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究任意相互作用图（含全连接系统）上二体局域量子哈密顿量的吉布斯态制备，利用满足KMS细致平衡的Lindblad动力学与量子Dobrushin条件，在高温区给出与几何无关的收缩估计，混合时间为系统规模与误差容限倒数的对数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统快速热化是量子统计力学的核心问题。空间局域性为具有几何结构的系统提供了有力工具，但全连接模型需要能处理任意自旋对之间相互作用的估计方法，而既有方法难以直接适用。

Method: 构造满足Kubo–Martin–Schwinger（KMS）细致平衡的Lindblad动力学来制备吉布斯态；引入经典Dobrushin条件的量子类比以控制局域扰动对系统其余部分的影响；采用一阶量子Wasserstein距离量化扰动在动力学下的传播，并在由每格点最大总相互作用强度控制的高温区导出收缩估计。

Result: 收缩估计不依赖空间几何或图增长假设；所得混合时间相对于系统规模和逆误差容限均为对数级，且对所有初态一致成立。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典Dobrushin分析范式通过量子Wasserstein距离推广至任意图（含全连接）二体局域哈密顿量，证明高温下吉布斯态可快速、一致地制备，为无几何结构的量子多体热化提供了统一且高效的理论保证。

Abstract: Understanding when quantum many-body systems rapidly converge to thermal equilibrium is a central problem in quantum statistical mechanics. While spatial locality provides powerful tools for systems with an underlying geometry, fully connected models require estimates that accommodate interactions between every pair of spins. We study Gibbs-state preparation for two-local quantum Hamiltonians on arbitrary interaction graphs, including fully connected systems, via Lindblad dynamics that satisfies Kubo--Martin--Schwinger (KMS) detailed balance. Our approach is based on a quantum analogue of the classical Dobrushin condition, which controls the influence of local perturbations on the rest of the system. Using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order $1$, we quantify how these perturbations propagate under the dynamics and derive contraction estimates in a high-temperature regime controlled by the maximum total interaction strength per site. These estimates require no assumptions on spatial geometry or graph growth and yield mixing times logarithmic in the system size and the inverse error tolerance, uniformly over all initial states.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum Computing through Computational Imaging: Representations, Measurement, and Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40393)
*George Kourousias,Sergio Carrato,Roberto Pugliese,Panagiotis Dimitrakis,Alberto Carini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文是一篇面向计算成像与工程读者的方法论阐释，用四个区分（振幅编码的两种含义、复场干涉与未知相位推断、联合振幅矩阵化与纠缠/经典可模拟性、QAOA采样与成本算符）厘清"成像模型与量子态之间的代数对应"并不等于具体计算方法，并强调输入访问、测量与解码共同决定量子方案实际可返回的结果及其资源代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态与成像模型之间的代数对应常被误认为已给出可用的计算方案，但实际能返回什么取决于输入访问方式、测量设计与解码过程。作者旨在为计算成像和工程背景读者建立一套判断标准，避免把"数学同构"误读为"算法优势"。

Method: 提出四条概念性区分：（1）区分对输入数值的振幅编码与对完整候选标记（labeling）的振幅编码，说明振幅存储不提供全阵列读出；（2）以复场干涉说明未知相位推断需互补测量，局部Fisher信息无法察觉的模糊性可由互补测量消除；（3）将联合振幅重排为矩阵，把奇异值分解与纯态纠缠联系起来，并区分Schmidt秩与高效经典可模拟条件；（4）在四像素分割任务中把目标函数写成成本算符与采样线路，用单层QAOA与均匀采样对比。辅以归档的IBM与IQM测量数据说明六变量局部边界决策，并用低秩态重构和若干量子算法给出端到端资源核算。

Result: 四像素分割实验中，单层QAOA将平均能量从1.675降至0.686（相对均匀采样），但最优标记的概率为0.365，反而低于某个非最优标记的0.484，即能量下降不等于最优点成为最可能采样结果。归档的IBM与IQM测量数据另展示了一个六变量局部边界决策案例。

Conclusion: 代数对应只提供表示层面的等价，实际能力由输入访问、测量与解码决定；文中将每种表示与其可支持的估计/决策及所需资源对应起来，并配有可复现数值参考与"Seeing Amplitudes"交互浏览器实验室，全文自足，仅需复线性代数与概率基础。

Abstract: An algebraic correspondence between an imaging model and a quantum state does not specify a computational method: input access, measurement and decoding determine what can be returned. This exposition develops four distinctions for computational-imaging and engineering readers. It separates amplitude encodings of input values from encodings of complete candidate labelings, explaining why amplitude storage does not provide full-array readout. Complex-field interference motivates unknown-phase inference: complementary measurements resolve ambiguities that local Fisher information alone does not detect. Reshaping joint amplitudes into a matrix connects singular-value decomposition to pure-state entanglement, while distinguishing Schmidt rank from conditions for efficient classical simulation. In a four-pixel segmentation, an objective becomes a cost operator and a sampling circuit. One layer of the quantum approximate optimization algorithm (QAOA) lowers mean energy from $1.675$ to $0.686$ relative to uniform sampling, yet makes a nonoptimal labeling more probable than the optimum 0.484 versus 0.365. Archived IBM and IQM measurements illustrate a separate six-variable local boundary decision. Low-rank state reconstruction and selected quantum algorithms connect these distinctions to an end-to-end resource account. The derivations and reproducible numerical references assume complex linear algebra and probability, without prior quantum mechanics. The accompanying Seeing Amplitudes browser laboratory supports interactive exploration; the article remains self-contained and connects each representation to the estimate or decision it permits and the resources that output requires.

</details>


### [106] [Fault-Tolerant Heisenberg-Limited Quantum Metrology with Bosonic Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40405)
*Daniel G. Li,Lorcán O Conlon,Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Twesh Upadhyaya,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种针对玻色探针的容错量子传感协议：在平均光子数约束下构造最优纠错码，用受控位移将探针耦合到重复编码的量子比特寄存器，通过重复的玻色宇称测量与重置去除探针噪声，从而在退相干噪声下估计位移，实现 Fisher 信息按 NT² 标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子传感借助纠缠与相干演化可实现海森堡极限标度，但环境噪声与硬件误差会破坏敏感量子态的相干性；已有容错传感方案多针对量子比特体系，而许多实际传感器需要玻色探针，因此需要为玻色体系设计容错传感协议。

Method: 在平均光子数约束下构造最优纠错码；以受控位移操作将玻色探针耦合到重复编码的量子比特寄存器；通过反复的玻色宇称测量与重置清除探针噪声，同时保留所存储的逻辑相位；并在电路级比特翻转噪声模型下分析逻辑失效概率。

Result: 距离为 d 的编码可将逻辑失效抑制到 p^{(d+1)/2} 阶；对于衰减率 γ_A、总传感时间 T 与平均光子数预算 N，在残余信号足够小时，Fisher 信息在约 γ_A^{-(d+1)/2} 的探测时间内按 NT² 标度，显著优于仅在总时间 O(γ_A^{-1}) 内才达到海森堡标度的非容错方案。

Conclusion: 容错技术可有效扩展玻色量子传感的相干时间与精度标度：纠错码与宇称测量/重置机制在不破坏逻辑相位的前提下抑制探针噪声，使海森堡型标度在更长的探测时间范围内成立，为实用玻色传感器提供了可行的鲁棒性提升路径。

Abstract: Quantum sensors have been shown to enable more precise measurements than classical sensors. By using entanglement and coherent evolution, the estimation error can be made to scale better in interrogation time, photon count, and other resources, achieving Heisenberg-limited scaling. However, environmental noise and hardware errors typically destroy the coherence of the sensitive quantum states. This motivates the use of fault-tolerant techniques to create more robust quantum sensing protocols. Such fault-tolerant quantum sensing approaches have been shown to enhance sensitivity for qubit systems, but many practical sensors require bosonic probes. In this paper, we propose a fault-tolerant protocol using bosons to estimate displacements in the presence of dephasing noise. We construct an optimal error-correcting code under a mean photon-number constraint. Controlled displacements couple the probe to a repetition-encoded qubit register, while repeated boson-parity measurements and resets remove probe noise without erasing the stored logical phase. Under circuit-level bit-flip noise, a distance-$d$ code suppresses logical failures to order $p^{(d+1)/2}$. We show that, for a decay rate $γ_A$, a total sensing time $T$ and a mean photon number budget $N$, and for sufficiently small residual signals, the Fisher information scales as $NT^2$ over interrogation times of order $γ_A^{-(d+1)/2}$. This is a significant improvement over the non-fault-tolerant approach that achieves Heisenberg scaling only for a total time $\mathcal O(γ_A^{-1})$.

</details>


### [107] [Structural Bottlenecks in QAOA Mixer Design: Transport and Causal Reach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40424)
*Jasjyot Singh Gulati,Tharrmashastha SAPV*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文从"配置空间输运"与"有限深度因果可达性"两个结构性瓶颈出发，为浅层QAOA的mixer设计给出互补的理论与数值分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixer是QAOA的核心设计选择，但mixer的多种结构局限无法被单一指标刻画，因此需要识别并分别量化不同的结构性瓶颈。

Method: Part I：推导小β与小γ两种互补机制下的一阶、依赖mixer的目标界，并用图拉普拉斯恒等式证明连通正则mixer图下两式等价，化为代价加权的Dirichlet能量；再由逐层ℓ1扩散构造非微扰上包络，得到达到目标近似比所需的扩散必要条件。Part II：针对MaxCut，在横场X mixer上加入与问题图对齐的二体相互作用（k-hop构造，深度p时因果半径≤(k+1)p，k=1时约2p），在环、圆梯子、高围长正则二部图上与标准X mixer比较。

Result: 正则mixer图下小β与小γ主导项完全等价于同一代价加权Dirichlet能量；ℓ1扩散给出与代价分布相关的必要扩散条件。数值上X+XX与X+YY在同深度下优于X；X+YY在深度p可接近X在深度2p的近似比（差距约1个百分点）；等可达的XX与YY表现不同。

Conclusion: 配置空间输运与因果可达性是两类互补的结构瓶颈；满足结构性能力并不自动保证性能（XX vs YY），故mixer设计需同时考虑扩散能力、因果局部性与具体代价结构。

Abstract: The choice of mixer is a central design decision in the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA), yet different structural limitations of a mixer need not be captured by a single metric. We identify two complementary structural bottlenecks for shallow QAOA: configuration-space transport, which limits how strongly probability can concentrate on high-quality solutions, and finite-depth causal reach, which limits which parts of a problem instance can influence a local objective term. In Part I, we derive first-order mixer-dependent objective bounds in complementary small-$β$ and small-$γ$ regimes. For connected regular mixer graphs, the two leading-order expressions are exactly equivalent through a graph-Laplacian identity and reduce to the same cost-weighted Dirichlet energy. We then derive a non-perturbative upper envelope from the layer-wise $\ell_1$ spreading of the mixer unitary, yielding a cost-distribution-dependent necessary spreading condition for attaining a target approximation ratio. In Part II, we study the locality bottleneck for MaxCut by augmenting the transverse-field mixer with problem-graph-aligned two-local interactions. The resulting $k$-hop construction has causal radius at most $(k+1)p$ at depth $p$; for the $k=1$ architectures studied numerically, this gives radius $2p$. Across cycles, circular ladders, and high-girth regular bipartite graphs, $X{+}XX$ and $X{+}YY$ improve the best-found approximation ratios over the standard $X$ mixer at equal depth, while $X{+}YY$ at depth $p$ comes within about one approximation-ratio point of plain $X$ at depth $2p$ in the reach-matched cases examined. The unequal performance of the equal-reach $XX$ and $YY$ augmentations shows that satisfying a structural capability does not by itself guarantee performance. Together, the results provide complementary structural insights for mixer design.

</details>


### [108] [A universal gauge structure in the photon content of light-matter systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40446)
*Arka Dutta,Marcus Kollar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在截断物质希尔伯特空间的单模光-物质模型中，基态光子数的规范依赖性服从精确代数律：⟨a†a⟩_λ = ⟨a†a⟩_0 + Cλ + Qλ²，其中 C=⟨Gp⟩、Q=⟨G²⟩；其规范最小值本身是规范不变量，对量子Rabi与Dicke模型严格为正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断物质希尔伯特空间后，能谱在规范变换下不变，但基态光子数却依赖规范选择，其物理意义长期存在争议。作者希望给出这一规范依赖性的定量、解析刻画，并据此定义有物理意义的（规范不变的）光子含量。

Method: 假设规范变换等价于对耦合到物质算符 G 的场作位移，则基态光子数作为规范参数 λ 的二次多项式。解析求出两个系数：C 由 G 与场动量 p 的关联 ⟨Gp⟩ 给出，二次项系数 Q 等于 ⟨G²⟩。随后将该结果应用于量子 Rabi 模型与 Dicke 模型，并研究其最小值、正性以及不同耦合区的最优规范。

Result: 1) 得到精确的二次律 ⟨a†a⟩_λ = ⟨a†a⟩_0 + Cλ + Qλ²；2) 对量子 Rabi 模型，该最小值完全由基态能量确定；3) 在具有能级劈裂的量子 Rabi 与 Dicke 模型中，最小值严格为正，即任何规范下都残留非零光子含量；4) 弱耦合下最小光子数对应的规范为 Jaynes-Cummings 规范；5) 对 Dicke 模型，在热力学极限下该结论对所有耦合强度均成立。

Conclusion: 截断模型中光子数的规范依赖性并非无规，而是由精确二次代数律支配；取所有规范下的最小值可得到规范不变、严格正的残余光子数，从而为"基态到底有多少光子"这一争议提供可操作的不变量判据，并确立 Jaynes-Cummings 规范作为最小光子数规范的地位。

Abstract: After truncating the matter Hilbert space of a single-mode light-matter model, gauge transformations leave the spectrum invariant whereas the ground-state photon number is gauge dependent, hence, its meaning remains disputed. We show that this gauge dependence obeys an exact algebraic law whenever the gauge transformation acts as a displacement of the field coupled to a matter operator $G$. Namely, the ground-state photon number is a quadratic polynomial in the gauge parameter $λ$, $\langle a^{\dagger} a\rangle_λ$ $=$ $\langle a^{\dagger} a\rangle_{0}$ $+$ $Cλ$ $+$ $Qλ^2$. Here the coefficients are the correlator $C=\langle G\,p\rangle$ of $G$ and the field momentum $p$, and $Q=\langle G^2\rangle$. Its minimum over all gauges is naturally gauge-invariant, and for the quantum Rabi model it is determined by the ground-state energy alone. We show that it is strictly positive for the quantum Rabi and Dicke models with level splitting, so that a residual photon content survives in every gauge. At weak coupling the gauge of minimal photon number is the Jaynes-Cummings gauge, and for the Dicke model it remains so at all couplings in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>


### [109] [Process matrices on generalised theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40448)
*Matt Wilson,James Hefford,Timothée Hoffreumon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将过程矩阵框架推广到一般物理理论，以"对经典探针的自然性"重新公理化时空语境，刻画了时空语境可表示为过程矩阵的理论类，并给出超映射视角与过程矩阵视角一致的确切条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原过程矩阵框架用于定义不定因果序，但主要局限于量子理论；若要讨论一般物理理论中的高阶过程，需要一个理论无关、组合式的时空语境定义，并厘清"超映射"与"过程矩阵"两种高阶过程描述何时等价。

Method: 在广义概率理论（复合式/范畴式）框架下：（1）用经典探针上的自然性（naturality over classical probes）重新公理化时空语境，精确对应非语境赋值，并与局部实验室内的凸性及粗粒化相容；（2）利用该组合视角刻画哪些理论允许抽象时空语境具有过程矩阵表示；（3）推导超映射与过程矩阵两种视角重合的条件，并在经典理论、量子理论及类Boxworld理论上验证。

Result: 得到时空语境的简洁公理化形式；给出抽象时空语境可嵌入过程矩阵表示的清晰理论刻画；确定超映射与过程矩阵视角一致的确切条件，并证明经典理论、量子理论与某类Boxworld理论均满足该条件。

Conclusion: 过程矩阵与不定因果序的概念可在远超量子理论的范围内推广，其核心在于时空语境的自然性条件；这为在一般物理理论中统一处理高阶过程提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: We extend the process-matrix framework for defining indefinite causal orders to a broad class of generalised physical theories. We give a simple re-axiomatisation of spacetime contexts in terms of naturality over classical probes, which precisely captures non-contextual assignment together with compatibility with convexity and coarse-graining within local laboratories. Using this compositional perspective, we give a clean characterisation of theories in which abstract spacetime contexts admit a process-matrix representation, and we pin down the precise conditions (satisfied by classical theory, quantum theory, and a Boxworld-like theory) under which the supermap and process-matrix perspectives on higher-order processes coincide.

</details>


### [110] [Quantum-Walk Inspired Spectral Metrics for Evaluating QAOA Mixers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40459)
*Jasjyot Singh Gulati,Tharrmashastha SAPV*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文借鉴量子行走混合理论，提出两个与混合器哈密顿量正缩放无关的光谱启发式指标（归一化最小特征值间隔、混合酉算子长时间平均最小特征值间隔），用于刻画QAOA混合器的拓扑结构，并在完全图、超立方体、环、平衡二维环面上给出精确表达式与渐近界，最后与经验性能对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA混合器对探索指数级解空间至关重要，但混合器哈密顿量的选择通常依赖惯例、实现或电路约束，缺乏基于图拓扑的判据。作者希望建立一种与代价无关、仅由混合器拓扑光谱结构决定的启发式选择准则。

Method: 受量子行走混合理论启发，定义两个光谱启发式：混合器哈密顿量H_M的归一化最小特征值间隔，以及混合酉算子U_M(β)=e^{-iβH_M}的长时间平均最小特征值间隔；两者对H_M的正缩放不变。对完全图、超立方体、环和平衡二维环面推导精确表达式与渐近界，并将其与QAOA经验性能进行对比，分别在随机置换不变代价哈密顿量和结构化一维、二维Ising QUBO上进行验证。

Result: 四类拓扑的光谱分离度差异显著，呈现较宽的谱分离区间。对随机置换不变代价哈密顿量，QAOA经验性能排序与光谱刻画大体一致；但对结构化一维和二维Ising QUBO，该排序失效，说明混合器与代价项的对齐（mixer-cost alignment）变得重要。

Conclusion: 拓扑层面、与代价无关的光谱启发式对QAOA混合器选择既有实用价值也有局限：它们在代价结构随机时能较好预测性能，但当代价哈密顿量具有强结构时不足以解释性能差异，需要引入混合器-代价对齐等因素。

Abstract: QAOA mixers play an important role in enabling exploration of the exponentially large solution space of binary optimization problems. However, the choice of mixer Hamiltonian is typically guided by convention and implementation or circuit constraints, rather than by a graph-topology-aware criterion. In this work, inspired by quantum-walk mixing theory, we propose two complementary spectral heuristics for characterizing QAOA mixer topologies. The first is the normalized minimum eigenvalue separation of the mixer Hamiltonian $H_M$, while the second is the long-time averaged minimum eigenvalue separation of the mixer unitary $U_M(β)=e^{-iβH_M}$. Both quantities are invariant under any positive rescaling of the mixer Hamiltonian and provide Hamiltonian- and unitary-level characterizations of the mixer's spectral structure. We use them as heuristic indicators of the mixer dynamics relevant to amplitude redistribution during QAOA evolution.
  We derive exact expressions and asymptotic bounds for the complete graph, hypercube, cycle, and balanced 2-D torus, revealing a broad spectral separation between the studied topology classes. We then compare these separations with empirical QAOA performance. For random permutation-invariant cost Hamiltonians, the observed performance ordering is broadly consistent with the spectral characterization. In contrast, for structured 1-D and 2-D Ising QUBOs, this ordering breaks down, indicating that mixer-cost alignment can become important. These results illustrate both the usefulness and the limitations of topology-level, cost-agnostic spectral heuristics for QAOA mixer selection.

</details>


### [111] [Lattice moments and quantum designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40481)
*Jonathan Conrad,Kristian Holm*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 单模Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill分布的系综不构成rigged量子3-design，并量化了导致失败的重数缺陷（replica defect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验GKP（Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill）分布系综在设计理论意义上的随机性强度，判断其能否达到rigged量子3-design这一更强的高阶随机性基准。

Method: 自洽地重新推导辛格（symplectic lattice）的二阶矩公式，并据此定义、计算决定失败的replica defect。

Result: 该系综未能构成rigged量子3-design；并给出了刻画其偏离程度的replica defect的定量表达。

Conclusion: 单模GKP分布系综仅具有较弱的高阶随机性，其二阶矩公式可作为判断（rigged）k-design性质的干净工具；对连续变量量子信息中GKP资源的设计性评估具有参考价值。

Abstract: We show that the ensemble of unimodular Gottesman$-$Kitaev$-$Preskill distributions fails to form a rigged quantum $3$-design and quantify the replica defect that determines this failure. This is established by rederiving a second moment formula for symplectic lattices in a self-contained manner.

</details>


### [112] [Fast and accurate neural triage decoding of ultra-high-rate quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40490)
*Hong-Ye Hu,Zhenlai Wang,Binzhao Luo,Tao Xiang,Chen Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"neural triage decoding"框架，利用综合征一致性自适应分配神经计算，实现超高码率qLDPC码的高精度、低延迟解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高码率qLDPC码可显著降低物理量子比特开销，但实际部署需要兼顾高精度与低延迟的解码器。现有高码率qLDPC解码器精度高却延迟大，而神经网络解码器尚未在高码率qLDPC码上得到验证。

Method: 提出neural triage decoding框架：以综合征一致性为依据自适应分配神经计算——局部神经消息传递网络快速预测纠错；附加迭代消除残余综合征违例；很少触发的多样化阶段为剩余采样搜索一致解。理论分析上，对精确逐比特边缘解码器进行严格分析，揭示其存在与码距无关的综合征不一致错误地板。

Result: Triage解码器可解码编码数百个逻辑比特的超高码率qLDPC码（含lifted-product码与Kasai码），精度与近最优算法解码器相当，达到state-of-the-art水平，同时解码延迟降低约1–2个数量级。此外，神经网络解码器对误差先验失配具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应神经解码是实现近期快速、准确、可扩展量子纠错的有前景路线。

Abstract: Quantum error correction is essential for fault-tolerant quantum computing. High-rate quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes have emerged as a promising approach to reducing the substantial physical qubit overhead of quantum error correction, enabling encoding rates exceeding $1/2$ in blocks of approximately one thousand physical data qubits. However, their practical implementation requires decoders that combine high accuracy with low latency to keep pace with quantum hardware. Existing decoders of high-rate qLDPC codes can achieve high accuracy but often incur substantial computational latency, whereas neural network decoders have yet to be demonstrated for high-rate qLDPC codes. Here, we introduce \emph{neural triage decoding}, a framework that uses syndrome consistency to adaptively allocate neural computation. A local neural message-passing network rapidly predicts error corrections, additional iterations reduce residual syndrome violations, and a rarely invoked diversification stage searches for consistent corrections to the remaining shots. We demonstrate that the \texttt{Triage} decoder can decode ultra-high-rate qLDPC codes encoding a few hundred logical qubits, including lifted-product and Kasai codes, achieving state-of-the-art accuracy comparable to the near optimal algorithmic decoders while reducing decoding latency by approximately one to two orders of magnitude. Our framework is inspired by a rigorous analysis of the exact bitwise marginal decoder, revealing a distance-independent syndrome-inconsistent error floor. In addition, we show the robustness of neural network decoders to mismatched error priors. Our results establish adaptive neural decoding as a promising route towards fast, accurate, and scalable quantum error correction in the near term.

</details>


### [113] [Using domain walls to benchmark fast quantum anneals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40504)
*Shushmi Chowdhury,Asa Hopkins,Nick Chancellor,Viv Kendon*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究反平行边界条件下受挫一维Ising自旋链基态中畴壁的分布，利用D-Wave新型快速退火（短于相干时间）观测到预期的正弦平方量子分布，并将畴壁分布尾部宽度提出为表征相干时间与噪声水平的平台无关度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想链条在量子情形下应呈现正弦平方（粒子在盒中）畴壁分布，经典情形下应为均匀分布；但已有工作[Chancellor等, npj Quantum Inf. 8, 73, 2022]在实际物理实现中发现分布两者皆非，而是随机场经典模型中场平均与热平均共同作用产生的U形分布。作者希望借助更短退火时间进入相干量子区，检验量子预测并厘清分布形状的来源。

Method: 以受挫一维Ising自旋链（反平行边界自旋）为对象，使用D-Wave的快速退火（fast anneal）功能，将退火时间缩短至相干时间以下，测量基态中畴壁的分布；并与理想量子（正弦平方）、经典均匀分布及随机场经典模型（U形）进行对比分析。

Result: 在短于相干时间的快速退火下，观测到符合预期的相干量子行为，即正弦平方型畴壁分布；而在可用的最短退火时间下，动力学尚未演化至正弦平方形状，链中部呈现另一种U形分布。

Conclusion: 畴壁分布形状可作为区分量子相干、经典随机场与热效应机制的诊断工具；该最短退火时间区间内畴壁分布尾部（tail）的延展范围可作为平台无关度量，同时表征相干时间与噪声水平。

Abstract: We consider the distribution of domain walls arising in ground states of frustrated 1-dimensional Ising spin chains with anti-parallel boundary spins. For ideal chains, a sine-squared particle-in-a-box distribution of domain walls is expected in the quantum setting, and a uniform distribution expected classically. However, for chains with random imperfections, prior work [Chancellor et al., npj Quantum Inf. 8, 73, 2022] found that the distribution in a physical implementation corresponded to neither. Instead, the domain wall distribution forms a U-shaped profile arising from a classical model with random fields, where the combination of averaging over these fields and thermal averaging produces the characteristic U-shape. With D-Wave's newer fast anneal feature, we access anneal times shorter than the coherence time and observe the expected coherent quantum behavior in the form of a sine-squared distribution. For the shortest available anneal time, the dynamics have not yet reached the sine-squared shape and instead display a different U-shape in the middle of the chains. This motivates using the extent of the domain wall distribution tail in this regime as a platform independent metric that provides a signature of both coherence time and noise levels.

</details>


### [114] [Grow, Compress, and Perturb: A Hybrid Quantum-Classical Pathway to Scalable Quantum Chemistry on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40561)
*Hugo D. Nogueira,Siwar Badreddine,Olivier Adjoua,Diata Traore,Guillaume Michel,Jean-Philip Piquemal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出 VULCAN 分层 VQE 框架，通过变分双括弧流方程动态生成拟设算符，消除哈密顿量梯度测量，以更低测量代价达到化学精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VQE 变体依赖梯度驱动的算符选择，导致可扩展性受限。

Method: 基于变分双括弧流（VDBF）方程动态生成拟设算符；采用线性规模的最小完备池（比 QEB 池小 4-12 倍）；整层同时追加以彻底消除哈密顿量梯度测量；以"先增长后压缩"方案结合高效态重叠测量控制电路深度；将哈密顿量投影到主行列式子空间以降低量子比特需求。

Result: 在最高 32 个逻辑比特的高性能模拟中，对氢链、BeH2 和 H2O，仅用批处理 ADAPT-VQE 测量成本的 5-20% 即达到化学精度；对 32 比特 C3H6，单层 VULCAN 配合 GPU 加速的随机 Epstein-Nesbet（ENPT2）修正即可达到化学精度，而批处理 ADAPT-VQE 无法达到。

Conclusion: VULCAN 以无梯度、分层构建方式显著降低测量开销与电路深度，随机 ENPT2 后处理支持在达到化学精度时提前终止，为量子化学计算提供了更可扩展的方案。

Abstract: While Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) variants are among leading candidates for electronic structure calculations on quantum computers, gradient-based operator selection limits their scalability. To solve this, we introduce the Hamiltonian-gradient-free VULCAN (Variational Upbuilding, Layered Compression, Adaptive N-body perturbation) layered VQE framework. Our method dynamically generates ansatz operators using Variational Double Bracket Flow (VDBF) equations. Combined with a linearly scaling minimal complete pool, it creates a pool 4-12 times smaller than QEB (Qubit-Excitation-Based) pools. By appending entire layers simultaneously, Hamiltonian gradient measurements are completely eliminated. Circuit depth is managed via a grow-then-compress scheme using efficient state-overlap measurements, and qubit requirements are reduced by projecting the Hamiltonian onto a dominant-determinant subspace. Using high-performance emulation up to 32 logical qubits, we achieve chemical accuracy at just 5-20% of the measurement cost of batched ADAPT-VQE for hydrogen chains, BeH2 and H2O. For the 32-qubit C3H6, where batched ADAPT-VQE does not reach chemical accuracy, a single VULCAN layer combined with a GPU-accelerated stochastic Epstein-Nesbet (ENPT2) correction does. This stochastic ENPT2 post-processing step enables early termination at chemical accuracy.

</details>


### [115] [Algebraic strong-to-weak symmetry breaking and partial decodability in the surface code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40567)
*Stephen W. Yan,Yimu Bao,Sagar Vijay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 相干噪声可诱导非常规的量子信息恢复与混合态序。本文通过对偶的三角晶格表面码（Pauli-Z旋转+校验测量）与Z2顺磁体（强对称退相干），揭示从可解码相到部分可解码临界相的转变，以及对应的代数型强弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）；用SO(n)非线性sigma模型（n→1复制极限）统一描述，解析导出临界相中普适的最优Pauli解码保真度（仅依赖长宽比）与幂律衰减的对偶保真度关联子，并用大规模数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干（而非随机）噪声下量子纠错码的信息恢复行为与混合态序分类尚不清楚；同时，混合态中"代数型"强弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）是否存在、能否由经典Pauli退相干实现，是开放问题。作者希望用一个统一框架刻画相干噪声下的解码相变与对偶的混合态序，并区分其"非经典"性质。

Method: 1) 选取Wegner对偶的两套模型：三角晶格表面码受单比特Pauli-Z旋转后做syndrome测量；对偶的Z2顺磁体受强对称退相干。2) 以旋转角为控制参量扫描相图。3) 构造弱耦合SO(n)非线性sigma模型，并在复制极限n→1下解析求解。4) 将可观测量（最优Pauli解码保真度、对偶保真度关联子）映射为sigma模型中twist算符的关联。5) 用大规模数值模拟检验解析预言。

Result: 随旋转角增大，码从可解码相转变为部分可解码临界相，对偶顺磁体从强对称相进入具有代数型Z2 SWSSB的相。解析得到临界相的普适特征：热力学极限下最优Pauli解码保真度趋于仅依赖系统长宽比的普适函数；对偶保真度关联子呈普适幂律衰减。数值模拟与解析预言一致。此外论证代数SWSSB相无法由任何作用在Z2顺磁体上的Pauli退相干信道实现，并通过初态中相距很远的Z2磁通插入下输出态的敏感性，揭示不同Z2电荷轨迹之间在长距离上仍保持相干。

Conclusion: 相干噪声可实现部分可解码的临界相与代数型SWSSB，二者由同一SO(n) sigma模型在n→1极限下统一描述，其普适行为由twist算符关联决定。代数SWSSB具有非经典性：不能由Pauli退相干信道产生，并表现为对远距Z2磁通插入的持续长程相干。

Abstract: Coherent noise can give rise to unconventional regimes of quantum information recovery and mixed-state order. We study these phenomena in two settings related by Wegner duality: the triangular-lattice surface code subject to single-qubit Pauli-$Z$ rotations followed by syndrome measurements, and a $\mathbb{Z}_2$ paramagnet subject to strongly symmetric decoherence. Increasing the rotation angle drives a transition from a decodable phase to a \emph{partially decodable} critical phase in the code, and from a strongly symmetric phase to a phase with \emph{algebraic} $\mathbb{Z}_2$ strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) in the dual paramagnet. We develop a common description of these phenomena in terms of a weakly coupled $\mathrm{SO}(n)$ nonlinear sigma model in the replica limit $n\to1$. Taking this replica limit analytically, we derive universal signatures of the critical phase: an optimal Pauli decoding fidelity that approaches a universal function of the system's aspect ratio in the thermodynamic limit, and a dual fidelity correlator that decays as a universal power law. These observables relate to correlations of twist operators in the sigma model description. Large-scale numerical simulations confirm these analytical predictions. We also argue that the algebraic SWSSB phase cannot be realized by any Pauli dephasing channel acting on the $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ paramagnet. We probe this "non-classical" character through the output state's sensitivity to well-separated $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ fluxes inserted in the initial state, which reveals persistent coherence between distinct $\mathbb{Z}_{2}$ charge trajectories at long distances.

</details>


### [116] [On the Quantum Chromatic Gap II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40571)
*Lorenzo Ciardo,Antoine Mottet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证实了第一作者在SODA'26提出的猜想：对每个d≥4、ε>0、k∈N，存在经典值<ε的d-to-1 label-cover game具有使用有限维纠缠、测量上下文半径≥k的完美量子策略；由此得到χ_q=3而χ任意大的图，首次改进BCS'99以来的量子色数间隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Brassard–Cleve–Tapp（PRL'99）以来，量子色数χ_q与经典色数χ之间的间隙改进一直停滞，本文旨在构造更强的伪心灵感应（pseudo-telepathy）型分离。

Method: 先构造"拉伸"版Mermin–Peres魔方（拉开方程间距并用长方程线连接），提升完美量子策略的测量上下文半径并保持经典不可满足性；再做并行重复压低经典值；接着用Dinur–Guruswami–Khot–Regev（STOC'03）的PCP构造转化为伪心灵感应的多层Kneser超图；最后输入Fei–Minzer–Wang的从超图着色到4-to-1游戏的归约，证明该归约的量子完备性并说明其保持上下文半径。

Result: 对任意d≥4、ε>0、k，构造出经典值<ε但具有限维纠缠完美量子策略（测量上下文半径≥k）的d-to-1 label-cover game；并得到χ_q=3、χ任意大的图族。

Conclusion: 证实了Khot d-to-1游戏强形式伪心灵感应的猜想，并给出量子色数间隙首次超越BCS'99结果的构造。

Abstract: We confirm a conjecture of the first author [SODA'26] on a strong form of pseudo-telepathy for Khot's $d$-to-$1$ games: for every $d\geq 4$, every $ε>0$, and every $k\in\mathbb N$, there is a $d$-to-$1$ label-cover game of classical value less than $ε$ that admits a perfect quantum strategy using finite-dimensional shared entanglement with measurement contexts of radius at least $k$. As a consequence, there exist graphs whose quantum chromatic number $χ_q$ is $3$ and whose classical chromatic number $χ$ is arbitrarily large. This is the first improvement on the quantum chromatic gap since the work of Brassard, Cleve, and Tapp [Phys. Rev. Lett.'99], which produced graphs for which $χ$ grows exponentially in $χ_q$.
  To prove the conjecture, we proceed as follows. We start with a ``stretched'' version of the Mermin--Peres magic square, obtained by spacing out its equations and linking them with additional long equation wires. This operation increases the radius of the initial measurement contexts of a perfect quantum strategy for the game, while preserving classical unsatisfiability. We then apply parallel repetition to make the classical value small, and we turn the system into a pseudo-telepathic multi-layered Kneser hypergraph via a PCP construction of Dinur, Guruswami, Khot, and Regev [STOC'03]. Finally, we feed the resulting hypergraph into the reduction of Fei, Minzer, and Wang from hypergraph colouring to $4$-to-$1$ games, we establish quantum completeness of this reduction, and we show that it preserves context radius.

</details>


### [117] [What Can Be Computed with Indefinite Causal Structure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40573)
*Omri Shmueli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在不预设确定因果序的 process matrix 形式下，作者给出两类多项式时间算法：一般 process matrix 计算可解 NP 完全问题，纯 process matrix 可解 unique-sink orientation 问题，从而表明不定因果序在该形式下可超越 BQP（在 Uso 无量子多项式算法的假设下）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"操作无需遵循确定因果序"是否能带来额外计算能力。已有定性结果（如 Oreshkov 等的 process matrix 形式、Araújo 等的 purification postulate）缺少以电路规模为代价度量的算法层面刻画，本文希望在该计算模型中量化不定因果序的计算优势。

Method: 采用 Oreshkov 等的 process matrix 形式定义计算模型：以量子电路简洁描述一个 process matrix，算法访问其生成的 channel，计算代价以生成电路规模衡量。构造两个设置下的显式高效酉生成器：(1) 一般 process matrix 计算，所用有效过程满足 purification postulate，生成的 channel 为酉的（尽管生成电路非酉）；(2) 要求 process matrix 本身为纯的，构造对 unique-sink orientation 的多项式时间算法。

Result: 第一设置下，一般 process matrix 计算足以求解 NP 中的每个问题；第二设置下，得到 unique-sink orientation 的多项式时间算法，该问题统一刻画了若干长期存在的优化计算问题；生成的酉算子是显式且可高效构造的。

Conclusion: 若 unique-sink orientation 不存在量子多项式时间算法，则不定因果序在 process matrix 形式内能提供超出 BQP 的计算能力，且这一优势可在保持酉性的条件下实现。

Abstract: Can quantum computation become more powerful when operations do not need to follow a definite causal order? We study this question in the process matrix formalism of Oreshkov et al. (Nature Communications, 2012), which allows quantum operations to be connected without a fixed global order. In the computational model, a quantum circuit succinctly describes a process matrix, and the algorithm is given access to its generated channel, with computational cost measured by the size of the generating circuit.
  We give polynomial-time algorithms in two settings. First, general process matrix computation suffices to solve every problem in $\textbf{NP}$. The valid processes used by our algorithm satisfy the purification postulate of Araújo et al. (Quantum, 2017), and their generated channels are unitary, although their generating circuits are not. Second, requiring the process matrices themselves to be pure, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for unique-sink orientation, a general problem capturing several longstanding computational problems in optimization. Our unitary generators are constructed explicitly and efficiently. Assuming that unique-sink orientation has no quantum polynomial-time algorithm, our result shows that indefinite causal order can provide computational power beyond $\textbf{BQP}$ within the process matrix formalism, even while preserving unitarity.

</details>


### [118] [Classical communication over quantum channels: does entanglement help in nature?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40585)
*Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一般的Schur加权块熵不等式，证明所有相位协变信道（含多能级振幅阻尼、广义退相位、阻尼-退相位信道）不存在纠缠对经典通信速率的增益，并顺带重新导出"所有量子比特信道无纠缠优势"的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典猜测认为纠缠码字跨信道独立使用不会提升经典通信速率，Hastings等工作虽否证该猜测，但构造反例需要极大维度，暗示物理信道或低维信道可能无纠缠优势；受(广义)振幅阻尼信道近期进展启发，希望刻画更广信道类别的无纠缠优势性质。

Method: 提出一般的Schur加权块熵不等式（Schur-weighted block entropy inequality），并以此为核心工具分析信道的经典容量可加性/纠缠辅助增益。

Result: 证明所有相位协变信道均无纠缠优势，涵盖多能级振幅阻尼信道、广义退相位信道与阻尼-退相位信道；作为副产品恢复了所有量子比特信道无纠缠优势的已知结论。

Conclusion: 相位协变信道这一类广泛的物理相关信道不存在纠缠对经典通信的增益，为"低维/物理信道无纠缠优势"提供了有力证据，并统一覆盖了此前零散的结果。

Abstract: Can entangling codewords across independent uses of a quantum channel improve the rate of classical communication? An old conjecture states that there is no such entanglement advantage for all channels. Although Hastings's result and follow-up works disproved this conjecture, it turns out that enormous dimension is required to construct a channel where entanglement enhances classical communication. This indicates that there might be no entanglement advantage for many physical channels, or for all low dimensional channels. Motivated by recent progress on (generalized) amplitude damping channels, we propose a general Schur-weighted block entropy inequality, and show that there is no entanglement advantage for all phase-covariant channels including multi-level amplitude damping channels, generalized dephasing channels, and damping-dephasing channels. As a by-product, we also recover the recently established result that there is no entanglement advantage for all qubit channels.

</details>


### [119] [Toric codes all the way down: decoupling translation-invariant topological CSS codes in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40592)
*Mingxin He,Yifei Wang,Hao Song,Yingfei Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了高维平移不变 p 元拓扑 CSS 码可"解耦"（即通过有限深度局域 CSS 保持的 Clifford 电路等价于若干高阶环面码的堆叠）的充要判据：粗粒化后各激发层的平移作用平凡且层间"粘合"消失，并给出显式构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维以上的拓扑 CSS 码具备横截非 Clifford 门、单次译码等容错优势，但缺乏系统理解；解耦为高阶环面码堆叠是统一刻画这些码的手段，而高维情形下激发按维数分层的层间粘合现象使二维的判据不再充分。

Method: 针对素 p 的平移不变 p 元 CSS 码，要求电路与超晶格的平移对易。先在超晶格上将码解耦为高阶环面码堆叠；分析激发按维度划分的超选择层，证明层间粘合是二维判据之外的唯一剩余阻碍；并给出由 SUM（qudit CNOT）与 SWAP 门构成的显式构造算法。

Result: 证明了码可在超晶格上解耦当且仅当：粗粒化后平移在每一层上作用平凡且粘合消失。进一步，若每层激发类型有限且非零层数为 m，则一旦平移在每层上作用平凡，再沿各方向粗粒化 p^{m-1} 倍即可消除粘合。物理上该判据同时决定码在有限环面上是否编码最大数量的逻辑 qudit。

Conclusion: 高维拓扑 CSS 码的解耦障碍完全由"层内平移平凡性 + 层间粘合"刻画，粘合可通过有限倍粗粒化消除，并可由 SUM/SWAP 电路算法显式实现；这统一了容错性质与逻辑 qudit 数最大化的问题。

Abstract: Topological Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes in more than two spatial dimensions offer features useful for fault-tolerant quantum computation, such as transversal non-Clifford gates and single-shot decoding, but a systematic understanding of them is still lacking. One approach is to decouple a code: map it to a stack of higher-form toric codes by a finite-depth local CSS-preserving Clifford circuit, possibly after adding ancillas. For translation-invariant $p$-ary CSS codes, where $p$ is prime ($p=2$ for qubits), we require the circuit to commute with the translations of a superlattice. In two dimensions, such a decoupling exists once these translations act trivially on every anyon type. In higher dimensions, excitations are sorted by their dimension into superselection layers, which can remain glued together even when translations act trivially on each layer. We show that this gluing is the only remaining obstruction: a code can be decoupled on a superlattice exactly when, after coarse-graining to it, translations act trivially on every layer and the gluing vanishes. Moreover, for a code with finitely many excitation types in each layer and $m$ nonzero layers, once translations act trivially on every layer, further coarse-graining by a factor of $p^{m-1}$ in every direction removes the aforementioned gluing. Physically, the same criterion determines when the code encodes the maximal number of logical qudits on a finite torus. Finally, we give an explicit algorithm that constructs the decoupling as a circuit of SUM (qudit CNOT) and SWAP gates.

</details>


### [120] [Probing fluctuations in photon emission statistics of spin injected quantum dots with time dependent electronic excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40593)
*Chiran Wijesundara,Vincent Iglesias-Cardinale,Yuan Lu,Herbert Fotso,Bo Xu,Tim Thomay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 电驱动CoFeB/MgO自旋注入InGaAs/GaAs量子点实现零磁场下圆偏振单光子发射，四探测器HBT测量显示n=|2⟩态统计涨落随自旋去磁而消失，揭示自旋注入同时调控光子螺旋度与高阶时间关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无需外部泵浦激光、微电子兼容的电驱动量子光源，并探究自旋注入除控制光子偏振（螺旋度）外是否影响发射的高阶时间关联特性。

Method: 构建含垂直磁各向异性CoFeB铁磁自旋注入层与MgO隧道势垒的器件，向InGaAs/GaAs量子点有源区注入自旋极化电子；用四探测器扩展Hanbury Brown–Twiss干涉仪与时间标记读出获取时间分辨光子数统计；并以含可变激发机制与多发射体collapse算符的随机波函数演化进行唯象建模。

Result: 零外加磁场下获得圆偏振光子发射；单激发周期内可分辨至|2⟩ Fock态；n=|1⟩发射脉冲行为符合电激发预期，而n=|2⟩统计在脉冲内多个时间位置出现显著涨落，且该涨落在自旋注入层去磁后消失。

Conclusion: 自旋注入不仅决定发射光子螺旋度，还调控发射的高阶时间关联；该平台为电驱动、自旋控制的单/多光子Fock态源提供路径，并可作为固态量子光源自旋条件多发射体动力学的测试平台。

Abstract: Electrically spin-injected ensembles of InGaAs/GaAs quantum dots emit circularly polarized single photons, with the emission helicity determined by the spin polarization of the injected electrons. A ferromagnetic CoFeB spin-injection layer with perpendicular magnetic anisotropy, combined with an MgO tunnel barrier, enables spin-polarized electron injection into the quantum-dot active region, resulting in circularly polarized photon emission at zero applied magnetic field. These devices realize direct spin-charge-photon conversion in an electrically driven, microelectronics-compatible quantum light source that operates without an external pump laser. Using an extended Hanbury Brown-Twiss interferometer with four detectors and time-tagged readout, we measure time-resolved photon-number statistics that resolve up to $|2\rangle$ Fock states within a single excitation cycle. While single-photon ($n=|1\rangle$) emission tracks the electrical excitation pulse as expected, the two-photon ($n=|2\rangle$) statistics exhibit pronounced fluctuations at multiple temporal positions within the pulse. These fluctuations vanish upon demagnetization of the spin-injection layer, establishing a direct link between the multi-photon emission dynamics and the spin polarization of the injected carriers. The observations indicate that spin injection controls not only the helicity of the emitted photons but also higher-order temporal correlations of the emission. The behavior is phenomenologically captured by inserting both a variable excitation mechanism and a set of multi-emitter collapse operators within a stochastic wave function evolution. The platform thus opens a route to electrically driven, spin-controlled single and multi-photon Fock-state sources and provides a testbed for spin-conditioned multi-emitter dynamics in solid-state quantum light sources.

</details>


### [121] [Optimal detuning and gate time for strongly driven single-qubit gates beyond the rotating-wave approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40595)
*Alejandro D. Bendersky,Sergio S. Gomez,Rodolfo H. Romero*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文研究超越旋转波近似（RWA）的单量子比特门保真度问题，利用二阶有效Magnus-Taylor哈密顿量理论分析失效机制，并通过引入Bloch-Siegert型幅度依赖失谐与操作时间重标定来修正RWA门条件，数值上使门失真度降低数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RWA在弱耦合下给出闭式门参数，但提高驱动幅度以加快动力学时，被忽略的反旋项会导致门不精确，并使共振发生Bloch-Siegert移动。因此需要在大幅度下仍保持高保真门设计的理论框架，以扩展可用幅度范围。

Method: 在驱动频率倒数二阶精度内构造有效Magnus-Taylor哈密顿量，理论计算超越RWA的门失真度；对驱动频率施加幅度依赖的Bloch-Siegert型失谐，并重标定操作时间，从而在修正参数下恢复RWA门条件；再以薛定谔方程数值解验证修正效果。

Result: 数值求解薛定谔方程表明，所提出的修正可将门失真度降低数个数量级，从而显著扩展单量子比特门的可用驱动幅度范围。

Conclusion: 通过二阶有效哈密顿量分析与Bloch-Siegert型失谐补偿及时间重标定，可在较大驱动幅度下恢复RWA门条件的精度，为快速、高保真单量子比特门设计提供解析可行的修正方案。

Abstract: Single-qubit quantum gates are implemented as the time-controlled unitary evolution of a two-level system under harmonic driving. The rotating-wave approximation (RWA) provides closed-form gate parameters for weak coupling. Larger amplitudes, desirable to speed up the dynamics, make the RWA-designed gates inaccurate because of the neglected counter-rotating term, which also shifts the resonance (Bloch-Siegert shift). Here we study theoretically the infidelity of gates beyond the RWA, using the effective Magnus-Taylor Hamiltonian through second order in the inverse of the driving frequency. By detuning the driving frequency by an amplitude-dependent shift of Bloch-Siegert type and rescaling the operation time, the RWA gate conditions are recovered with corrected parameters. Numerical solutions of the Schrodinger equation show that these corrections lower the gate infidelity by several orders of magnitude, extending the range of usable amplitudes

</details>


### [122] [Exceptional Points as Witnesses of Non-Gaussian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40612)
*Aritra Ghosh,Subhashish Banerjee,M. Bhattacharya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文指出，噪声平均退相干响应中的奇异点（EP）可暴露非高斯噪声统计的可观测特征，并据此区分具有相同低阶统计量的环境；同时提出以相同独立噪声源构成退相干模型，从EP阶数推断源数目并预测复合噪声的高阶统计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子器件的高效控制需要了解环境噪声，但常用的功率谱无法完整刻画真实环境中的非高斯涨落，因此需要能够区分具有相同低阶统计量的不同环境并揭示高阶统计特征的探测手段。

Method: 研究噪声平均退相干的非厄米动力学，利用其中出现的奇异点（EP）阶数作为统计指纹；构建由相同且相互独立的噪声源组成的理论模型（保持功率谱不变），在单源模型标定的前提下，由实测退相干响应提取最大EP阶数来推断噪声源数目；并设计原子干涉型（atomtronic）读出方案，把不同EP特征映射到集合平均原子干涉图样的演化上。

Result: 表明EP可在退相干响应中给出非高斯统计的可观测信号，即使不同环境具有相同的低阶统计量也能被区分；最大EP阶数与噪声源数目对应，并可据此预测复合噪声的高阶统计量；提出了可实施的原子干涉读出方案，将不同EP签名转化为可测量的干涉图样差异。

Conclusion: 该工作将非厄米动力学与环境噪声统计联系起来，为计算、计量与精密测量中量子器件的噪声表征提供了一条可行途径。

Abstract: Efficient control of quantum devices requires the knowledge of their environmental noise, yet the commonly measured power spectrum does not fully characterize non-Gaussian fluctuations of realistic environments. Here we show that exceptional points (EPs) in the noise-averaged dephasing response can expose observable signatures of non-Gaussian statistics, distinguishing environments with identical lower-order statistics. We also introduce a theoretical model that describes dephasing environments in terms of identical independent noise sources while preserving the power spectrum. Given a calibrated single-source model, the largest EP order extracted from the measured dephasing response determines the number of sources and predicts the higher-order statistics of the combined noise. We illustrate an atomtronic readout that maps distinct EP signatures onto an evolving ensemble-averaged atomic interference pattern. Our results connect non-Hermitian dynamics to environmental noise statistics, providing a route to characterize noise in quantum devices used in computing, metrology, and precision measurement.

</details>


### [123] [Optimal Diamond Distance Estimation for Unitary Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40623)
*Jinge Bao,Qisheng Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文给出估计两个未知 $d$ 维酉信道的钻石距离（diamond distance）的量子算法，达到加性误差 $\varepsilon$，查询复杂度为 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$，并证明该复杂度因匹配 Jeon 与 Oh 的下界而最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻石距离刻画两个量子信道在单次使用下可被最优区分的概率，是信道比较的核心度量；但未知酉信道的钻石距离估计缺乏紧致的查询复杂度刻画，需要给出达到下界的最优算法。

Method: 设计量子算法，通过对未知的 $d$ 维酉信道及其逆信道进行查询，对二者的钻石距离进行加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 的估计；查询次数为 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$，并与 Jeon 与 Oh 给出的下界进行对比以确立最优性。

Result: 在加性误差 $\varepsilon$ 下，算法以 $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ 次对酉信道及其逆的查询完成钻石距离估计；该查询复杂度与已有下界匹配，因而在常数因子意义下最优。

Conclusion: 未知 $d$ 维酉信道的钻石距离估计问题具有 $\Theta(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ 的查询复杂度，本文算法达到该最优上界，闭合了该问题的复杂度刻画。

Abstract: The diamond distance between two quantum channels determines the optimal probability of distinguishing them from a single use. We give a quantum algorithm that estimates the diamond distance between two unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channels to within additive error $\varepsilon$, using $O(\sqrt{d}/\varepsilon)$ queries to the unitary channels and their inverses. The query complexity is optimal, as it matches the lower bound due to Jeon and Oh (npj Quantum Inf. 2026).

</details>


### [124] [Left-Handed Transmission Lines for the Dynamical Casimir Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40624)
*Valentin E. Salari,Fernando C. Lombardo,Paula I. Villar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究以串联电容和并联电感构成的手性超导传输线（LH线）中、由通量调制SQUID终端驱动的动力学卡西米尔效应，并与同等元件的右手（RH）线及共面波导对比，表明LH谱（非零下带边、高频模式稀疏）与耦合的模式依赖性共同决定哪些模式被激发及激发速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 左手传输线具有非零下带边、高频模式稀疏的特殊谱结构，而动力学卡西米尔效应在其中的表现尚无系统研究；同时需要明确LH线相较常规RH线和共面波导是否具有产生目标模式的优势。

Method: 将LH线终端接通量调制SQUID，推导量子哈密顿量；证明两模式间的耦合在SQUID处可因子化为各模式振幅的乘积；结合LH谱与耦合的模式依赖分析模式布居与增长速率；并与同等元件的RH线、同SQUID终端的共面波导进行对照，包括同时泵浦两个模式的情形。

Result: 等元件下LH线耦合随频率增长，在由单元电感与电容设定的交叉泵浦频率以上超过RH线；仅LH线在RH带边以上存在共振；双模泵浦可使弱耦合模式"借用"强耦合模式的耦合，共振线交叉可布居三或四个模式但不提高增长率；与共面波导相比耦合相近，但LH线把产生的粒子保持在目标模式，而近等距波导将粒子分散到多对模式且需更高泵浦能量。

Conclusion: LH谱与模式依赖耦合的组合，使左手超导传输线成为动力学卡西米尔效应中模式选择性更优的平台：可在更低泵浦能量下将粒子集中于目标模式，并具备RH带边以上的额外共振通道与模式借用效应。

Abstract: Left-handed (LH) superconducting transmission lines, built with series capacitors and shunt inductors instead of the series inductors and shunt capacitors of a conventional right-handed (RH) line, have a spectrum with a nonzero lower band edge and modes that become sparse at high frequencies. We study the dynamical Casimir effect in a LH line terminated by a flux-modulated SQUID and compare it with an RH line built from the same elements and with a long coplanar waveguide terminated by the same SQUID. We derive the quantum Hamiltonian and show that the coupling between two modes factorizes into their amplitudes at the SQUID. Together, the LH spectrum and the mode dependence of this coupling determine which modes are populated and how fast. With equal elements, the LH coupling grows with frequency and exceeds the RH one above a crossover pump frequency set by the cell inductance and capacitance, and only the LH line has resonances above the RH band edge. Pumping two modes at once lets a weakly coupled mode borrow coupling from a strongly coupled one, while crossings of resonance lines populate three or four modes without increasing the growth rate. Compared with the coplanar waveguide, the LH line has a similar coupling but keeps the created particles in the target mode, whereas the nearly equidistant waveguide spreads them over many pairs and needs more pump energy.

</details>


### [125] [Locality in Dissipative Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40628)
*Qicheng Tang,Ruhanshi Barad,Xueda Wen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究自由费米子局域哈密顿量的基态能否由局域耗散制备。作者用带线性跳跃算符的二次Lindbladian，对平移不变目标借助Fourier对应把实空间局域性与动量空间解析性联系起来，给出存在指数局域线性跳跃算符的完整谱判据，并证明变号费米面交叉构成不可行定理；一维中该交叉即为全部障碍，高维还有额外障碍，短程基态关联则是局域耗散制备的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是回答"局域哈密顿量的基态能否由局域耗散制备"这一基本问题，特别针对自由费米子体系，厘清目标哈密顿量的谱结构如何决定耗散制备所需的局域性。

Method: 采用含线性跳跃算符的二次Lindbladian进行耗散基态制备；对平移不变目标，利用Fourier对应建立实空间局域性与动量空间解析性的联系，导出指数局域线性跳跃算符存在的完整谱判据；并以两个具有代数衰减基态关联的二次能带接触模型说明高维额外障碍，另外分析短程基态关联在无平移不变性时的充分性。

Result: 得到完整谱判据与不可行定理：变号费米面交叉排除指数局域线性跳跃算符；一维中此类交叉是唯一且完整的障碍，高维则一般存在额外障碍（由两个能带接触模型例示）；短程基态关联虽非必要，但足以保证（即便无平移不变性时）局域耗散制备。

Conclusion: 目标哈密顿量的谱结构决定耗散基态制备的局域性，为局域耗散制备基态的可行性提供了清晰的谱学刻画。

Abstract: Can the ground state of a local Hamiltonian be prepared by local dissipation? We address this question for free-fermion Hamiltonians, using a quadratic Lindbladian with linear jump operators for the dissipative ground-state preparation. For translation-invariant targets, Fourier correspondence relates spatial locality to momentum-space analyticity, allowing us to derive a complete spectral criterion for the existence of exponentially local linear jump operators that prepare the ground state. This criterion leads to a no-go theorem that, sign-changing Fermi-level crossings exclude exponentially local linear jump operators for preparing the ground state. While these crossings are the complete obstruction in one dimension, additional obstructions generally arise in higher dimensions. We illustrate these additional obstructions using two quadratic-band-touching models with algebraically decaying ground-state correlations. We further show that short-range ground-state correlations, although not necessary, are sufficient for local dissipative preparation even without translation invariance. Our results establish how the spectral structure of the target Hamiltonian governs the locality of dissipative ground-state preparation.

</details>


### [126] [Entanglement diagnostics of stochastic-resetting steady states: theory and measurement on superconducting qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40638)
*Jakob Murauer,Sabine Tornow,Manas Kulkarni,Eli Barkai,Satya N. Majumdar,Monalisa Singh Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究横向场Ising链在随机重置下的量子非平衡稳态：区分"重置历史未分辨"与"已分辨"两种纠缠熵，给出两比特闭式解、12比特精确计算，并在IBM超导处理器上实验验证；两类熵之差（Jensen gap）等于子系统保留的关于"距上次重置时间"的Holevo信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置可将量子系统驱动至幺正演化无法达到的非平衡稳态，而该稳态的纠缠依赖于是否对重置历史做分辨。作者希望厘清这一"历史分辨/平均"的相互作用机制，并利用重置时间被经典记录这一特点，规避监测线路中需指数稀有后选择才能获得轨迹分辨纠缠的困难。

Method: 以横向场Ising链为模型：对两比特给出纠缠熵与concurrence的封闭解析解，并精确数值计算至12比特链；在IBM超导处理器上对重置前后的熵与concurrence进行测量，与无拟合参数的无噪声预言对比，并用单参数去极化模型解释concurrence的压低；进一步以Jensen gap与Holevo信息建立解析联系。

Result: 两比特解析解显示能级本征态间的相位匹配产生共振，其在稳态中的可见度由重置态的对称性决定。硬件测量中，重置平均前后的熵在无任何拟合参数下紧贴无噪声预言，而concurrence在多数测量中低于预言；两者差异源于读取同一重构态的不同分量，去极化模型以单拟合参数复现了concurrence的压低。两熵之差等于子系统关于"距上次重置时间"所保留的Holevo信息；在Bell态重置下，该信息随链长在半链切割处增长，在所研究尺度内未见饱和（较小重置概率下）。

Conclusion: 重置历史的分辨与否对应两类可测量的纠缠诊断，其差（Jensen gap）具有明确的Holevo信息意义；由于重置时间可经典采样并记录，轨迹平均熵无需后选择即可获得，为研究开放量子多体纠缠提供了相较监测线路更高效、可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting drives quantum systems into nonequilibrium stationary states unreachable under uninterrupted unitary evolution, and their entanglement depends on whether the reset history is resolved or averaged over. We study this interplay in the transverse-field Ising chain, deriving closed-form results for two qubits and evaluating chains of up to 12 qubits exactly. In the two-qubit system, these closed-form results show that phase matching between energy eigenstates produces resonances, and their visibility in the stationary state is determined by the symmetry of the reset state. Measurements on an IBM superconducting processor show the entropies evaluated before and after the reset average closely following noiseless predictions with no fitted parameter, while concurrence lies below prediction across most of the hardware measurements. The two diagnostics respond differently because they read different components of the same reconstructed state, and a depolarizing model captures the concurrence suppression with one fitted parameter. The difference between the two entropies, i. e., the Jensen gap of entanglement entropy, equals the Holevo information that a subsystem retains about the time since the last reset. With a Bell-state reset, this information grows with chain length across a half-chain cut over the sizes studied, without visible saturation at the smaller reset probabilities. Since reset times are sampled and recorded classically, the trajectory-averaged entropy, and with it the Jensen gap, is obtained without postselection, in contrast to monitored circuits, where trajectory-resolved entanglement requires sampling exponentially rare measurement records.

</details>


### [127] [Comparison of entangling strategies for unitary synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40642)
*Jonas Wunder,Hendrik Weimer,Ricardo Costa de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文以酉合成（unitary synthesis）作为与具体应用无关的基准任务，构建了一套通用工具集，用于评估由原生Mølmer–Sørensen多比特门构成的门的集合的性能，并识别多比特门真正带来优势所需的硬件条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子平台具备长相干时间、近全连接和潜在多比特门等优势，但随着门集合更灵活，工程复杂度上升；目前尚不清楚这种复杂度增加与实际运行量子算法性能提升之间的权衡关系。

Method: 开发多功能的工具集（versatile toolset），用于评估由原生Mølmer–Sørensen多比特门组成的门集合的性能；采用与具体应用无关的酉合成作为基准目标，并据此推导多比特门具备优势所需的特定硬件要求。

Result: 通过系统性评估，识别出使多比特门相对二比特门方案具有优势的具体硬件参数条件（如门保真度、多比特门规模与开销等），量化了增加门集合灵活性带来的收益与工程复杂度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 原生多比特Mølmer–Sørensen门并非无条件优于二比特门方案；只有在满足特定硬件要求时，其灵活门集合才能在算法层面带来实际收益。该工具集为囚禁离子硬件路线的设计取舍提供了可量化的评估框架。

Abstract: Trapped-ion quantum computers are promising as a platform for realizing quantum computational advantage. They promise long coherence times, near all-to-all connectivity, and potential multiqubit gates as distinguishing advantages compared to competing platforms. Regarding these advantages, it is crucial to understand the trade-offs between increasing engineering complexity and actual improvements in running quantum algorithms with a more flexible gate set. In this work, we investigate this question and develop a versatile toolset to evaluate the performance of gate sets composed of native Mølmer--Sørensen multiqubit gates. To this end, we employ unitary synthesis as an application-agnostic benchmark target and identify specific hardware requirements for multiqubit gates to be advantageous.

</details>


### [128] [Reconstructing hidden observables via quantum snapshot thermometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40652)
*Jie Ren,Andrew Hallam,Luheng Zhao,Huanqian Loh,Zlatko Papić*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"量子快照温度计"（quantum snapshot thermometry）：利用局域热化与独立标定的平衡态物态方程，将仅对角可测的投影快照转化为隐藏守恒密度分布，可恢复能量前沿、扩散常数并区分自旋输运与能量扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影快照是量子模拟器的标准读出手段，但只能直接探测在测量基下对角的可观测量，隐藏的守恒密度分布无法直接获得；而扩展测量基或量子态层析代价高昂。

Method: 基于局域平衡（局域热化）假设，将固定基下的投影快照与独立标定的平衡态物态方程结合，把测得的对角布居反演为局域温度/化学势，从而重建守恒密度剖面；并分析对称性导致的读出歧义，指出利用空间结构的先验知识可加以消解。

Result: 数值上成功恢复能量前沿与扩散常数，区分自旋输运与能量扩散，并揭示初始测量中不可见的守恒电荷剖面；同时识别出对称性引起的读出歧义并给出解决方案。

Conclusion: 固定基快照温度计无需额外测量基或量子态层析，即可提供超越输运指数的定量流体动力学信息。

Abstract: Projective snapshots are a standard readout tool for quantum simulators, but they directly probe only observables that are diagonal in the measurement basis. We show how local equilibration and an independently calibrated equilibrium equation of state can reveal otherwise hidden conserved-density profiles. We demonstrate numerically that such ``quantum snapshot thermometry'' can recover energy fronts and a diffusion constant, distinguish spin transport from energy spreading, and reveal conserved-charge profiles invisible in the initial measurements. We also identify symmetry-imposed ambiguities in the readout and show how prior knowledge of the spatial structure can resolve them. Fixed-basis snapshot thermometry thus provides quantitative hydrodynamic information beyond transport exponents, bypassing additional measurement bases and quantum state tomography.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

### [129] [Validity Domains for Semiclassical Time in Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37696)
*Gianpaolo Rosati,Sergio L. Cacciatori*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将引力WKB流作为从属量子自由度的受控时间参考，在引力构型空间上用Hamilton–Jacobi向量场直接表述Born–Oppenheimer绝热条件，得到有效域 $D_t=D_{\mathrm{WKB}}\cap D_{\mathrm{ad}}\cap D_{\mathrm{sharp}}$，并在FRW微超空间耦合均匀大质量标量模中证明绝热域与WKB有效域并不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半经典WKB时间常被默认为可用的时间参考，但缺乏对"何时该时间参考在定量上可靠"的系统判据。作者希望区分三类彼此独立的要求：引力分支的WKB有效性、从属自由度的绝热控制、参考流相对量子涨落的锐度，并检验它们是否定义同一区域。

Method: 不预设WKB时间，而在引力构型空间上以Hamilton–Jacobi向量场定义绝热条件，将绝热控制与局部WKB有效性、参考锐度解耦；对单一选定半经典分支给出有效域为 $D_{\mathrm{WKB}}\cap D_{\mathrm{ad}}\cap D_{\mathrm{sharp}}$，多相干分支共存时再加退相干条件。随后在闭合FRW微超空间耦合齐次大质量标量模的模型中，比较普通WKB控制参数与构型空间绝热参数在转折点附近的行为，并用转折点的均匀Airy处理消除WKB形式发散。

Result: 从振荡区逼近引力转折点时，普通WKB控制参数发散，而构型空间绝热参数趋于零；均匀Airy处理消除WKB形式发散但保留不等价性——局部控制参数仍为 $O(1)$，构型空间绝热参数则按 $O(M^{-1/3})$ 参数化压低（$M$ 为大质量/半经典参数）。

Conclusion: 即便在最简单的微超空间模型中，Born–Oppenheimer绝热性与引力WKB有效性也定义不等价的有效域。存在振荡半经典分支并不足以保证可靠的WKB时间描述；半经典时间可靠性由支撑参考流的各有效域之交集决定。

Abstract: We investigate the domain in which a semiclassical gravitational WKB flow can serve as a quantitatively controlled temporal reference for subordinate quantum degrees of freedom. Rather than introducing WKB time at the outset, we formulate the Born--Oppenheimer adiabatic condition directly on gravitational configuration space using the Hamilton--Jacobi vector field. This separates adiabatic control from the local WKB validity of the gravitational branch and from the sharpness of the candidate reference against quantum fluctuations. For a single selected semiclassical branch, these requirements define the validity domain $D_t = D_{\mathrm{WKB}} \cap D_{\mathrm{ad}} \cap D_{\mathrm{sharp}}$, with an additional decoherence condition when coherent branches coexist.In a closed Friedmann--Robertson--Walker minisuperspace coupled to a homogeneous massive scalar mode, the distinction is explicit: approaching the gravitational turning point from the oscillatory region, the ordinary WKB control parameter diverges while the configuration-space adiabatic parameter tends to zero. A uniform Airy treatment of the turning point removes the formal WKB divergence while preserving the inequivalence: the corresponding local control parameter remains of order unity, whereas the configuration-space adiabatic parameter is parametrically suppressed as $O(M^{-1/3})$.Thus Born--Oppenheimer adiabaticity and gravitational WKB validity define inequivalent domains even within the simplest minisuperspace model. We conclude that the existence of an oscillatory semiclassical branch is not sufficient for a reliable WKB temporal description: semiclassical temporal reliability is instead determined by the overlap of the validity domains supporting the reference flow.

</details>


### [130] [Classical traversability of a carbon BTZ wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37704)
*Valerie Tynianska,Alfredo Iorio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在二维弯曲碳膜上实现的空间虫洞模拟几何中，无质量Dirac准粒子的经典可穿越性由守恒角动量的尖锐二值阈值决定，喉部最小半径充当离心势垒，临界轨迹呈对数发散并多次绕喉。该结果为后续介观研究提供几何骨架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在类比引力平台上模拟空间虫洞的可穿越性，验证能否用二维弯曲碳膜（如石墨烯类膜）复现虫洞喉部的零测地线结构，并为未来介观尺度（如量子输运）实验建立几何基础。

Method: 对非旋转BTZ黑洞的空间外部扇区施加反射对称加倍技术，构造虫洞背景，以光滑喉部替代事件视界；将该几何置于二维弯曲碳膜上。无质量Dirac准粒子按旋量光线传播，运动学仅由度规曲率决定，沿超静态时空的零测地线运动。通过求解精确测地线方程分析轨迹分类。

Result: 经典可穿越性由守恒角动量上的尖锐二值阈值控制：低于阈值的轨迹穿越喉部，高于阈值的被反射；喉部最小半径表现为离心势垒。近临界轨迹的角向演化呈对数发散，准粒子在逃逸前多次空间绕行喉部。

Conclusion: 该类比虫洞几何支持清晰的经典二分穿越行为，并提供绕行修正的精确光线追踪构型；这些经典结果构成后续介观（波动/量子）研究的必要几何骨架。

Abstract: We investigate the classical traversability of an analogue spatial wormhole geometry realized on a two-dimensional curved carbon membrane. The background is obtained by implementing a reflection-symmetric doubling technique on the exterior spatial sector of a non-rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli black hole, effectively replacing the event horizon with a smooth traversable throat. Within this framework, massless Dirac quasiparticles propagate as spinorial light rays whose kinematics are governed exclusively by the metric curvature, forcing them to track the null geodesics of the ultrastatic spacetime. By evaluating the exact geodesic equations, we demonstrate that classical traversability is dictated by a sharp binary threshold in the conserved angular momentum, where the minimum throat radius acts as a centrifugal potential barrier that separates traversing paths from reflecting ones. We show that near-critical trajectories experience a logarithmic divergence in their angular evolution, causing the quasiparticles to execute multiple spatial windings around the throat before escaping. These classical ray-tracing configurations establish the necessary geometric backbones for future mesoscopic investigations.

</details>


### [131] [Thermodynamic and structural properties of the thin atmosphere of a white dwarf](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37708)
*Riccardo Fantoni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 采用路径积分蒙特卡罗方法，在广义相对论恒星模型中确定白矮星稀薄大气内库仑等离子体（jellium）的热力学与结构性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 白矮星稀薄大气中的库仑等离子体处于强耦合、部分简并及强引力场环境，其热力学与结构性质对恒星大气建模至关重要，而相对论引力效应与量子多体效应的联合处理此前研究不足。

Method: 以路径积分蒙特卡罗（PIMC）方法直接模拟库仑等离子体（jellium，即均匀正电荷背景下的电子/离子体系），并将模拟体系嵌入广义相对论描述的恒星背景中，计入引力对局域热力学参量（如密度、温度分布）的影响。

Result: 给出了白矮星大气条件下库仑等离子体的热力学量（如能量、压强、比热等状态方程相关量）与结构性质（如粒子间关联、径向分布函数），并显式体现广义相对论修正带来的差异。

Conclusion: PIMC 与广义相对论恒星模型相结合，可作为刻画白矮星大气等离子体状态方程与结构的可靠途径，为致密星大气建模提供定量依据。

Abstract: We determine thermodynamic and structural properties of the Coulomb plasma (jellium) in the thin atmosphere of a white dwarf using the path integral Monte Carlo method within a general relativity description of the star.

</details>


### [132] [Draining Without Emptying a Stringy Black Hole: Repetitive Electric Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37713)
*Huai-Fan Li,Xiong-Ying Guo,Ke Wang,Hong Lei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究GMGHS黑洞在最大能量提取最优条件下的重复电场Penrose过程，给出迭代终止条件，并通过数值模拟分析能量提取的回报率、能量利用率及其对衰变半径、粒子荷质比、入射粒子比能和黑洞初始电荷的依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在GMGHS黑洞电荷无法被完全消耗的情形下，重复电场Penrose过程能否持续提取能量及其效率，并与RN黑洞进行对比。

Method: 在最大能量提取的最优条件下建立GMGHS黑洞重复电场Penrose过程的迭代框架；分析入射粒子有效势在迭代中的行为、逃逸条件与负能粒子存在条件；进行数值模拟，考察回报率与能量利用率随衰变半径、粒子1荷质比、入射粒子比能及初始电荷的变化。

Result: GMGHS黑洞中入射粒子的有效势曲线在迭代过程中保持不变（不同于RN黑洞）；逃逸条件与黑洞电荷无关，负能粒子1的存在条件要求电荷足够大，故过程不能将电荷消耗至零。能量提取回报率可超过100%，但能量利用率不超过50%，大部分可提取能量转化为不可约质量的增加。较小衰变半径与较大|荷质比|提升回报率和利用率；入射粒子比能越大回报率越小而利用率不变；初始非极端黑洞下回报率随初始电荷单调增加，利用率先增后减。

Conclusion: GMGHS黑洞的重复电场Penrose过程无法耗尽黑洞电荷与质量，迭代结束后仍保留可提取能量；其能量提取效率受衰变半径、粒子荷质比、入射粒子比能与初始电荷显著影响。

Abstract: This paper studies the repetitive electric Penrose process in the Gibbons-Maeda-Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GMGHS) black hole under the optimal condition for maximum energy extraction. Unlike the Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole, the effective potential curve of the incident particle in the GMGHS black hole remains unchanged during the iterations. The iteration termination conditions are determined by two aspects: the escape condition requires that particle 0 can overcome the effective potential peak and escape to infinity, and this condition is independent of the black hole charge; the existence condition for negative-energy particle 1 requires the black hole charge to be sufficiently large, indicating that the repetitive process cannot completely consume the charge of the GMGHS black hole to zero.
