We explore the thermodynamic topology of the anisotropic Kerr-Newman-AdS black hole using Duan's $φ$-mapping theory and a complex residue approach. By creating the generalized off-shell free energy, we propose a thermodynamic vector field in the parameter space with zero points corresponding to equilibrium black hole states. The winding numbers associated with these defects are utilized to calculate the black hole's global topological number. We begin with an analysis of the entire anisotropic Kerr-Newman-AdS black hole, followed by two limiting examples. Setting the anisotropic parameter $K = 0$ produces the Kerr-Newman-AdS black hole, while eliminating the AdS contribution produces the Kerr-Newman black hole. The anisotropic Kerr-Newman-AdS and Kerr-Newman-AdS black holes have a single thermodynamic defect with winding number $+1$, which results in $W = +1$. In contrast, the Kerr-Newman black hole has two defects with winding numbers $+1$ and $-1$, which result in a vanishing global topological number, $W = 0$. These findings show that the AdS contribution alters the global topological classification, but the anisotropic matter studied here preserves the topological class of the Kerr-Newman-AdS black hole. As an analogous limiting instance, we further study a Kiselev-type black hole, analyzing the circumstances in which its metric reduces to a charged black-hole geometry and how the thermodynamic topology reflects this reduction. Finally, we use the complex residue approach on the anisotropic Kerr-Newman-AdS black hole.

</details>


### [169] [HWF Analysis of TRN Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39757)
*Ashfaque Ahmad,Sudhir Kumar Gupta,Abbas Ali*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 将视界波函数(HWF)形式推广到D+1维球对称带电无自旋量子源，构造TRN黑洞内外视界的波函数，计算黑洞形成概率并分析其对电荷、质量与维数的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HWF形式此前多用于无电荷、固定维数的情形，缺乏对带电源与额外维度的系统处理；本文意在考察电荷与时空维数如何影响视界量子化与黑洞形成概率。

Method: 在D+1维时空构造球对称带电无自旋源的HWF，分别得到Tangherlini–Reissner–Nordström黑洞内、外视界对应的波函数，据此计算形成概率P_BH^±，并以无量纲参数α（极端极限α→1）、m/Λ_D及维数D为变量做参数扫描。

Result: 内、外视界概率比P_BH^-/P_BH^+在源偏离极端极限时由1急剧变化；高维情形下形成概率显著压低，尤其当m≲Λ_D时。例如α=0.5时，P_BH^+(D=5)/P_BH^+(D=10)在m/Λ_D=0.5、1、2时分别约为2.95×10^3、117、3.35。

Conclusion: HWF可自洽推广至带电高维黑洞；电荷偏离极端程度与空间维数D是控制内外视界形成概率及其比值的关键参数，高维与轻质量源下黑洞形成被强烈抑制。

Abstract: We extend the horizon wave-function (HWF) formalism to a spherically symmetric, charged, spinless quantum source in $\mathcal{D} + 1$ dimensional spacetime geometry and construct the HWFs associated with inner- as well as outer-horizons of the Tangherlini--Reissner--Nordström (TRN) black hole. The corresponding black hole formation probabilities, $P_{\rm BH}^{\pm}$, are also calculated and their sensitivities due to various source parameters including charge, mass, and $\mathcal{D}$ are analysed as well. Our analysis reveals that the ratio of inner- to outer-horizon probabilities, $P_{\rm BH}^{-}/P_{\rm BH}^{+}$, changes drastically from being unity as the source departs away from being in the extremal limit defined by $α$ ($\to 1$ for extremal case). Furthermore, black hole formation probabilities are found to be highly suppressed for higher values of $\mathcal{D}$, particularly for the source mass, $m \lesssim Λ_{\mathcal{D}}$, e.g., for $α= 0.5$, the ratio, $P_{\rm BH}^{+}(\mathcal{D} = 5)/P_{\rm BH}^{+}(\mathcal{D}=10)$ turn out to be approximately $2.95\times10^3$, $117$, and $3.35$ respectively for $m/Λ_{\mathcal{D}} = 0.5, 1$ and $2$, with $Λ_{\mathcal{D}}$ being the reduced gravity scale associated with $\mathcal{D}$ spatial-dimensions.

</details>


### [170] [Looking for gravitational waves with orbital dynamics: GUEST mission and the $μ$Hz band of Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39881)
*Diego Blas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该会议论文综述了利用引力束缚系统的轨道作为微赫兹引力波与超轻暗物质的共振探测器，并介绍了新提出的GUEST任务概念：两颗覆盖后向反射器的致密无源球体在高偏心地球轨道上由全球激光测距网跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在脉冲星计时阵列与LISA之间的微赫兹频段尚属空白，探测该频段有望揭示脉冲星计时阵列信号的延伸、早期宇宙信号、原初黑洞或暗物质等新现象。

Method: 将引力束缚系统的轨道视为微赫兹引力波与超轻暗物质的共振探测器进行理论综述；并描述GUEST任务概念的设计：两颗密集无源球体表面覆盖后向反射器，置于高偏心地球轨道，由全球激光测距网络跟踪。

Result: 给出轨道共振探测微赫兹引力波与超轻暗物质的物理框架，并提出可实现该探测的GUEST卫星任务方案。

Conclusion: 微赫兹频段是引力波天文学极具吸引力的新窗口，轨道共振探测结合激光测距跟踪的卫星概念为填补该频段提供可行途径。

Abstract: `Adding colour'' to the gravitational-wave sky by probing unexplored frequency bands has the potential to unveil new and unexpected phenomena in the Universe. The microhertz band, between pulsar timing arrays and LISA, is a particularly attractive target: it should host the extension of the signal recently found by pulsar timing arrays (if it is generated by a family of sources of gravitational waves), and it may harbour signals from the early Universe, primordial black holes or dark matter. In these proceedings, we review how the orbits of gravitationally bound systems act as resonant detectors of microhertz gravitational waves and ultra-light dark matter. We then describe GUEST (Gravitational Universe Exploration with Satellite Tracking), a mission concept recently proposed to the ESA F-class call, consisting of two dense passive spheres covered with retroreflectors, placed in highly eccentric Earth orbits and tracked by a global laser-ranging network.

</details>


### [171] [Self-Bound Anisotropic Stars in $\mathcal{R}+α\mathcal{R}^2$ Gravity with a Hayward Exterior: Junction Conditions and NICER Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39916)
*Furqan Nazeer,Tooba Feroze*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在$\mathcal{F}(\mathcal{R})=\mathcal{R}+\alpha\mathcal{R}^2$引力下构建解析致密星模型：内部采用嵌入类一Durgapal-Bannerji解，外部采用Hayward度规，并用修正引力的联合条件（而非GR的）以闭式确定全部常数；模型可同时拟合四颗脉冲星并提供$R_{1.4}=11.5$ km、$M_{\rm max}=2.9M_\odot$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有修正引力解析星模型普遍直接套用广义相对论的联合条件（junction conditions），并用某个特定恒星模型给出的半径来定常数，做法不自洽且缺乏普适性。本文旨在摆脱这两点依赖，在完全使用二次$\mathcal{F}(\mathcal{R})$引力自身联合条件的前提下，自洽确定模型常数，并量化"外部解非Birkhoff假设"带来的影响。

Method: (1) 内部取嵌入类一（Karmarkar条件）的Durgapal-Bannerji解析解；外部取Hayward度规，以长度尺度$\ell$相对Schwarzschild做形变，作为无Birkhoff定理时的外部假设。(2) 采用$\mathcal{F}(\mathcal{R})=\mathcal{R}+\alpha\mathcal{R}^2$引力的联合条件，将全部常数以$M$、$R$、$\ell$的闭式表达式定出；不再强制$p_r(R)=0$，改由外曲率迹的连续性替代。(3) 在表面处Ricci标量发生跃变，由此分析薄壳（负表面能密度、引力双层、径向应力），并约束$\alpha$与$\ell$使表面应力低于中心压强的1%。

Result: 表面处$\mathcal{R}$跳变导致薄壳结构：$\alpha>0$时表面能密度为负，并伴随引力双层与很小的径向应力；要求该应力小于中心压强1%给出$\alpha\lesssim0.2$ km$^2$、$\ell\lesssim0.3M$。内部表现为自束缚星。单一表面能密度$\rho_s=(1.37\pm0.16)\rho_0$可同时再现PSR J0740+6620、J0437-4715、J0030+0451与J0614-3329；取$\alpha=0.1$ km$^2$时给出$R_{1.4}=11.5$ km、$M_{\rm max}=2.9M_\odot$。由于$\ell$仅以$(\ell/M)^2(M/R)^3$阶进入表面观测量，结果可直接过渡到Schwarzschild外部。若改用食X射线脉冲星的奇异星半径标定，则得$R_{1.4}\approx9$ km，被现有数据排除。

Conclusion: 二次$\mathcal{F}(\mathcal{R})$引力的联合条件足以在无Birkhoff定理的情况下自洽地闭合解析星模型，且Hayward形变对表面观测量的影响为高阶小量，故模型实质不依赖外部解的选取。所得模型与多颗脉冲星观测相容（$R_{1.4}=11.5$ km、$M_{\rm max}=2.9M_\odot$），同时预言表面薄壳（负表面能密度、引力双层）；而以奇异星半径标定的分支被观测否决。

Abstract: Analytic compact-star models in modified gravity usually borrow the junction conditions of general relativity and fix their constants with radii derived for a particular stellar model. Here we avoid both. We study $\mathcal{F}(\mathcal{R})=\mathcal{R}+α\mathcal{R}^{2}$ gravity with a Durgapal-Bannerji interior of embedding class one (Karmarkar condition) and a Hayward exterior. Without Birkhoff's theorem, the exterior is an assumption, and we measure its effect with the Hayward metric, which deforms Schwarzschild by a length $\ell$. The junction conditions of quadratic $\mathcal{F}(\mathcal{R})$ gravity fix all constants in closed form in terms of $M$, $R$ and $\ell$. The condition $p_r(R)=0$ is replaced by continuity of the trace of the extrinsic curvature. The Ricci scalar jumps at the surface, so there is a thin shell with negative surface energy density for $α>0$, a gravitational double layer, and a small radial stress. For this stress to be below 1% of the central pressure we need $α\lesssim0.2$ km$^{2}$ and $\ell\lesssim0.3M$. The interior behaves as a self-bound star. A single surface energy density, $ρ_s=(1.37\pm0.16)ρ_0$, reproduces PSR J0740+6620, J0437-4715, J0030+0451 and J0614-3329, and for $α=0.1$ km$^{2}$ the model predicts $R_{1.4}=11.5$ km and a maximum mass of $2.9 M_\odot$. Because $\ell$ enters surface observables only at order $(\ell/M)^{2}(M/R)^{3}$, the results carry over to a Schwarzschild exterior. Calibrating instead with strange-star radii of eclipsing X-ray pulsars gives $R_{1.4}\approx9$ km, which current data rule out.

</details>


### [172] [Diamonds in the sky: the shape of gravitational waves from cosmic string cusps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39983)
*Pierre Auclair,Chams Gharib,Danièle Steer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文深入分析宇宙弦尖点（cusp）的引力波辐射，超越Damour–Vilenkin的经典工作，指出两个此前文献未描述的特征：辐射在天空中呈菱形而非锥形分布，且最大辐射方向并非尖点速度方向而是两个略微偏斜的方向，并研究辐射功率增强的两种极限情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于宇宙弦尖点引力波辐射的认识主要基于Damour与Vilenkin的经典结果，其认为辐射集中在以尖点速度方向为轴的锥内且在该方向最强。作者希望检验这一图像是否完整，并量化可能被忽略的几何与功率特征对观测预言的影响。

Method: 对尖点引力波辐射进行解析分析，推导修正后的角分布结构，并以简化算例加以说明；随后考察两种使总辐射功率增强的极限情形（菱形在特定方向被拉伸、尖点处高阶导数消失）；最后对奇次谐波弦环（odd-harmonic loops）群体进行统计估计，评估这些效应的重要性。

Result: 发现在任意给定频率下，尖点的引力波辐射在天空中描绘出菱形而非锥形；辐射峰值出现于两个相对尖点速度方向略有偏斜的方向。当菱形在某一方向被拉伸或尖点处高阶导数消失时，总辐射功率增强。

Conclusion: Damour–Vilenkin的锥形、速度方向最强的图像需要修正；菱形角分布与偏斜峰值方向为弦网络引力波预言提供了新的可观测特征，并可能在奇次谐波弦环群体中具有统计上不可忽略的重要性。

Abstract: We carry an in-depth analysis of the gravitational wave (GW) emission of cosmic string cusps, going beyond the seminal work of Damour and Vilenkin. We identify two features that, to the best of our knowledge, have not been described in the literature: first the GWs emission from a cusp, at any given frequency, is not concentrated inside a cone but instead draws a diamond-like shape in the sky. Second, the emission is not maximal in the direction of the cusp velocity, but in two slightly offset directions. We explain these effects in detail, and illustrate them with simplified examples. Then, we study two limiting cases for which the overall radiated power is enhanced: when the diamond is stretched in a particular direction, and when higher order derivatives vanish at the cusp. We statistically estimate the importance of these effects in a population of odd-harmonic loops.

</details>


### [173] [Inverse Gertsenshteǐn-Zel'dovich Effect in Time-Independent Electromagnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40024)
*Rajendra Prasad Bhatt,Rudraksh Verma,R. Prasad,Sudipta Sarkar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文为逆Gertsenshteǐn-Zel'dovich效应（引力波在背景电磁场中转化为电磁波）建立了适用于空间变化、时间无关背景电磁场的通用形式体系，涵盖引力波全部六种偏振模式，并应用于天体物理磁场的电磁信号研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对逆Gertsenshteǐn-Zel'dovich效应的研究主要局限于恒定背景电磁场，缺乏对空间变化背景场及任意偏振模式的系统处理，限制了该效应在引力波探测与天体物理观测中的应用。

Method: 构建计算引力波穿过空间变化、时间无关背景电磁场时所诱导电场与磁场分量的通用形式化框架，该框架可容纳引力波全部六种偏振模式；通过恒定场与空间变化场等多个示例展示其适用性，并进一步分析引力波穿过中子星与磁星相关天体物理磁场时产生的电磁信号。

Result: 形式体系成功给出各类背景场（恒定与空间变化）下引力波诱导电磁场的分量表达式，并刻画了引力波穿越中子星、磁星等天体磁场所生成的电磁信号特征。

Conclusion: 逆Gertsenshteǐn-Zel'dovich效应连接引力波与电磁波两种多信使天文信使，为引力波探测与天体物理观测提供了有前景的框架；所发展的形式体系可应用于高频引力波探测器设计及高能天体物理电磁信号研究。

Abstract: The inverse Gertsenshteǐn-Zel'dovich effect describes the conversion of gravitational waves into electromagnetic waves in the presence of a background electromagnetic field. This effect has primarily been studied for constant background electromagnetic fields. In this work, we develop a general formalism for calculating the components of the electric and magnetic fields induced by the passage of a gravitational wave through a spatially varying, time-independent background electromagnetic field. The formalism accommodates all six possible polarization modes of gravitational waves. We illustrate its applicability through several examples, including gravitational waves propagating through constant and spatially varying electromagnetic fields. We further investigate the electromagnetic signals generated by the passage of gravitational waves through astrophysical magnetic fields, particularly those associated with neutron stars and magnetars. By connecting gravitational waves and electromagnetic waves--two essential messengers of multi-messenger astronomy--the inverse Gertsenshteǐn-Zel'dovich effect offers a promising framework for both gravitational-wave detection and astrophysical observations. The formalism developed here may therefore find applications in the design of high-frequency gravitational-wave detectors and in the investigation of high-energy astrophysical electromagnetic signals.

</details>


### [174] [Curvature scaling and linear area response in charged black-hole interiors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40043)
*Fu-Guo Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文识别了带电黑洞内部两个标度律的机制与适用区间：质量膨胀形成大内部质量台地后，标量修正与电修正分别导致K≈48m²/r⁶和K与(A*-A)⁻³的联合反立方律，且电修正可被大台地质量推迟至r~q²/m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电黑洞内部Kretschmann标度律此前被报道但缺乏机制解释与适用性界定，特别是r⁻⁶律与已知精确下界是否相容、标量振幅响应律何时成立均不明确。

Method: 在球对称、中性无质量标量场演化中分析质量膨胀与台地形成；解析推导标量与小电荷修正下的K≃48m²/r⁶及局域指数趋近6的条件，证明精确下界K≥28q⁴/(5r⁸)排除有限系数r⁻⁶渐近，估计电修正延迟尺度r~q²/m；对固定坐标与初始规范下的线性响应ρ=r²∝A推导半幂半径律，并给出联合K/m²∝(A*-A)⁻³；以完整数值演化验证。

Result: 解析上得K≈48m²/r⁶及其成立条件、K≥28q⁴/(5r⁸)下界与电修正延迟尺度；线性响应给出简单零点附近的半幂律与联合反立方律。数值演化确认电修正、在多个观测时刻支撑半幂律，并检验联合反立方关系。

Conclusion: 两标度律的物理机制与有效区间被厘清；大台地质量可使经典区在电修正显著前达到普朗克曲率，为黑洞内部奇异结构及量子引力效应进入时机的判断提供约束。

Abstract: We identify the mechanisms and range of validity of two scaling laws reported in charged black-hole interiors. In spherical evolutions with a neutral massless scalar, mass inflation produces a large internal mass $m$, followed by a plateau. Small scalar and electric corrections give $K\simeq48m^2/r^6$, where $r$ is the areal radius and $K$ the Kretschmann scalar. The local exponent is near six when the mass and relative curvature correction also vary slowly with radius. The exact bound $K\geq28q^4/(5r^8)$, with $q$ the charge, excludes a finite-coefficient $r^{-6}$ asymptote, while a large plateau mass delays electric corrections to $r\sim q^2/m$. For sufficiently large plateau mass, this classical regime can reach Planckian curvature before electric corrections become significant. At fixed coordinates and initial gauge, a linear response of $ρ=r^2$ to the initial scalar amplitude $A$ gives the half-power radius law near a simple zero. Where both mechanisms apply, $K/m^2\propto(A_*-A)^{-3}$, with $A_*$ the area zero. Full evolutions confirm the electric correction, support the half-power law at several observation times, and test the joint inverse-cube law.

</details>


### [175] [Photon Rings and Mass-Independent Observables of a Five-Dimensional Charged AdS Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40069)
*Amrillokhon Mamatkhonov,Javokhir Sharipov,Tursunali Xamidov,Chen Zhou,Sanjar Shaymatov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究五维带电AdS黑洞的光学性质，提出两个质量无关的几何观测量（子环指数γ与临界碰撞参数同ISCO直接像碰撞参数之比），推导γ的闭式表达并给出MCMC参数约束预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdS时空中黑洞阴影的视大小依赖观测者且依赖质量标度，难以作为干净的判别量，因此需要构造不依赖质量标度、不依赖观测者的几何观测量来刻画黑洞阴影结构。

Method: 利用零测地线方程确定五维带电AdS黑洞的事件视界、光子球半径与临界碰撞参数；构造质量无关观测量——子环指数γ及临界碰撞参数与ISCO直接像碰撞参数之比，给出γ的闭式解；基于无噪声模拟数据（取5%量级分数不确定度）开展MCMC预报。

Result: 视界、光子球半径与临界碰撞参数均随电荷增大而减小，光子球半径与AdS半径无关；γ与AdS半径无关并满足2π/√3<γ≤π√2，高于四维Schwarzschild与Reissner–Nordström黑洞的对应值；MCMC表明无量纲AdS半径可约束到16–19%相对不确定度，电荷仅在近极端基准模型下可与零区分；γ对四维Schwarzschild黑洞给出3.4–5.9σ的高斯分离。

Conclusion: 子环指数γ是区分五维带电AdS黑洞与四维Schwarzschild/R-N黑洞的有效、质量无关探针，而电荷的可测性受参数简并限制，仅在近极端情形可行。

Abstract: We investigate the optical properties of a five-dimensional charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) black hole. Using the null geodesic equations, we determine the event horizon and photon sphere radii and the critical impact parameter, showing that all decrease with increasing charge, while the photon sphere radius remains independent of the AdS radius. To avoid dependence on the mass scale and the observer-dependent apparent size of the shadow in an AdS spacetime, we introduce two mass-independent geometric observables: the subring exponent $γ$ and the ratio of the critical impact parameter to that of the direct image of the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO). We derive $γ$ in closed form and show that it is independent of the AdS radius and satisfies $2π/\sqrt{3}<γ\leqπ\sqrt{2}$, above the values for four-dimensional Schwarzschild and Reissner--Nordström black holes. Using a Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) forecast based on noiseless mock data with illustrative fractional uncertainties of $5\%$, we find that the dimensionless AdS radius can be constrained with a relative uncertainty of $16$--$19\%$, whereas the charge can be distinguished from zero only for the near-extremal fiducial model considered. Under these assumptions, the subring exponent provides a Gaussian separation of $3.4$--$5.9σ$ from the four-dimensional Schwarzschild black hole.

</details>


### [176] [Disentangling Spin-induced Precession vs. Eccentricity in GW190521 and GW231123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40077)
*Patricia McMillin,Giuseppe Ficarra,Carlos O. Lousto,Richard O'Shaughnessy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用RIT第五版双黑洞波形目录中逾2000个完全数值相对论模拟，结合RIFT对GW190521与GW231123做贝叶斯参数估计，确认前者同时具有偏心率与自旋进动，后者则偏好自旋进动但偏心率可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GW190521已被指认为在进入探测频段（20 Hz）时可能具有较大偏心率，需要更严格的波形模型加以检验；同时需检验当前信号能否区分偏心率与自旋进动两类波形效应。

Method: 使用RIT第五版双黑洞波形目录中2000余个完全数值相对论模拟，通过快速迭代拟合算法（RIFT）对GW190521与GW231123进行贝叶斯参数估计；并作为对照，仅使用非进动（偏心与准圆轨道）模拟重新分析两个事件。

Result: GW190521最高似然来自同时偏心且自旋进动的波形，e20=0.207(+0.192/-0.147)，χ1⊥=0.717(+0.194/-0.406)；GW231123偏好自旋进动，χ1⊥=0.678(+0.218/-0.462)，但偏心率可忽略，e10=0.097(+0.124/-0.073)、e20=0.056(+0.059/-0.045)。仅用非进动模拟时两事件似然均更低。最高似然的数值相对论模拟给出合并后残余黑洞反冲速度约3965 km/s（GW190521）与2059 km/s（GW231123）。

Conclusion: 该方法能够区分当前引力波信号中偏心率与自旋进动的效应；两事件均可由完全数值相对论波形高效完成参数估计，并给出高反冲速度的残余黑洞预测。

Abstract: We use the more than 2000 full numerical-relativity simulations included in the fifth release of the RIT binary-black-hole waveform catalog to perform Bayesian parameter estimation, via the rapid iterative fitting algorithm (RIFT), for the gravitational-wave events GW190521 and GW231123. We revisit GW190521, which has previously been identified as potentially possessing large eccentricity upon entering the detector band at $20~\mathrm{Hz}$, and confirm this finding: the highest likelihood is obtained from waveforms that are simultaneously eccentric and spin-precessing, yielding $e_{20} =0.207_{-0.147}^{+0.192}$ and $χ_{1,\perp}=0.717_{-0.406}^{+0.194}$. Applying the same simulation pool to GW231123, we likewise find a preference for spin precession where at 20 Hz $χ_{1,\perp}=0.678_{-0.462}^{+0.218}$, but in this case with negligible eccentricity at both $10~\mathrm{Hz}$ and $20~\mathrm{Hz}$, with $e_{10}=0.097_{-0.073}^{+0.124}$ and $e_{20}=0.056_{-0.045}^{+0.059}$. For comparison, both events are also analyzed using exclusively non-precessing (eccentric and quasi-circular) simulations, which yield lower likelihoods in each case. These results indicate that our approach is capable of discriminating between the effects of eccentricity and spin precession in current gravitational-wave signals. The singled out top peak likelihood NR simulations give estimates of the recoil velocities of the merged final holes of 3965km/s for GW190521, and of 2059km/s for GW231123.

</details>


### [177] [Nonminimal Scalar-curvature Coupling Noncanonical Warm Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40159)
*Zhi-Peng Peng,Shi-Chao Zhang,Xiao-Pei Xu,Ruan-Jing Zhang,Xiao-Min Zhang,Xi-Bin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究非最小耦合非正统暖暴胀模型：在Jordan框架下推导修正的背景方程与慢滚条件，用随机方法导出原初标量与张量功率谱，结果表明标量谱近标度不变，张标比被进一步压低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察非最小耦合（暴胀场与标量曲率耦合）、非正统动力学与热耗散三者的联合效应能否拓展传统暖暴胀的适用范围，尤其是使较陡势也能慢滚、弱耗散区仍可维持暖暴胀，并兼容亚普朗克场位移。

Method: 在Jordan框架下推导修正背景方程；通过线性稳定性分析建立慢滚条件；采用随机方法（stochastic approach）导出暴胀场涨落的Langevin方程，进而计算原初标量与张量功率谱。

Result: 非最小耦合增强有效阻尼并修正势梯度，使较陡势亦可慢滚；标量谱近标度不变；非最小耦合、非正统动力学与热耗散的联合效应可进一步压低张标比；模型在弱耗散区仍能维持暖暴胀，并在合适参数区容纳亚普朗克场位移。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为传统暖暴胀的可行扩展；但摘要未给出具体的谱指数、张标比数值及参数区间的定量约束，尚需与观测数据（如n_s、r上限）对比以确认可行性。

Abstract: We investigate a noncanonical warm inflationary model with a nonminimal coupling between the inflaton field and scalar curvature. Within the Jordan frame, we derive the modified background equations and establish the slow-roll conditions through a linear stability analysis. We find that the nonminimal coupling enhances the effective damping and modifies the potential gradient, facilitating slow-roll evolution even for relatively steep potentials. Using the stochastic approach, we derive the Langevin equation for inflaton fluctuations and obtain the primordial scalar and tensor power spectra. Our results show that the scalar spectrum is nearly scale-invariant, while the combined effects of nonminimal coupling, noncanonical dynamics, and thermal dissipation can further suppress the tensor-to-scalar ratio. Moreover, the model can sustain warm inflation even in the weak-dissipation regime and accommodate sub-Planckian field excursions in suitable parameter regions. These findings highlight the potential of this framework as a viable extension of conventional warm inflation.

</details>


### [178] [Rotation overcharges vectorized black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40179)
*Shengjun Guo,Guangzai Ye,Peng Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Einstein–Maxwell–Vector (EMV)模型中首次构造了转动矢量毛黑洞解，发现矢量毛的质量不是自由参数，而是由旋转响应固定的二阶不变量（满足 M_V=2Ω_H J_V）；超过临界耦合后视界存在域突破极值Kerr–Newman界，达到 q=Q/M>1，且毛发在阴影椭圆率上留下可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在球对称情形下，矢量化Reissner–Nordström黑洞严格欠带电（矢量毛以电荷为代价），但由于球对称使毛的Komar质量恒为零、恒等式 M_V=2Ω_H J_V 退化为 0=0，因此"极值上限是矢量场本身的属性还是球对称的假象"一直无法判定。作者通过引入转动来同时解除这两个简并。

Method: 在EMV模型（无质量矢量场与电磁不变量非最小耦合）中构造转动的矢量化黑洞解（该设定下首个转动解），利用旋转引入的非零角动量与视界角速度，使Komar质量与恒等式不再退化，从而确定毛发质量；并计算阴影的椭圆率以量化毛发的观测效应。

Result: 毛发质量由 M_V=2Ω_H J_V 固定，是旋转响应的二阶不变量而非自由参数；超过耦合阈值时存在域越过极值Kerr–Newman界，可达 q>1，q²+χ² 最高约1.84，而 α=5 时最大电荷仍低于该界。在 (q,χ)=(0.9947,0.10) 附近，毛发贡献椭圆率 8.30%，远大于电荷的 0.44% 与旋转的 0.06%（约19倍）；在过带电解 (q,χ)=(1.2527,0.10) 处总椭圆率达 25.6%。

Conclusion: 极值带电上限并非矢量场的普适属性，而是球对称导致的；转动解表明高耦合下矢量毛可使黑洞过带电，并在阴影中产生无法由单纯给Kerr黑洞充电模仿的显著信号。该分支是否存在鬼（ghost）仍无定论，现有no-go判据因所假定的背景与色散关系在此不适用而无法裁定。

Abstract: A charged black hole has a ceiling: extremality bounds the charge that a given mass and spin can support. Vector hair has so far only lowered it. In the Einstein--Maxwell--Vector (EMV) model, where a massless vector field couples nonminimally to the electromagnetic invariant, the spherically symmetric vectorized Reissner--Nordström black holes are strictly undercharged: vector hair is paid for in charge. Whether this ceiling is a property of the vector field or merely of spherical symmetry has remained open, because spherical symmetry hides the hair's mass as well: its Komar mass vanishes identically and the exact identity $M_V=2Ω_HJ_V$ degenerates to $0=0$. Rotation lifts both degeneracies at once. We construct the rotating solutions of the model, the first in this setting, and find that the hair's mass is not a free parameter but a second-order invariant of the rotational response, fixed by $M_V=2Ω_HJ_V$. Above a coupling threshold the ceiling gives way: the domain of existence crosses the extremal Kerr--Newman bound, reaching $q=Q/M>1$ with $q^2+χ^2$ up to $1.84$, while at $α=5$ the maximum charge stays below it. The shadow is where the hair becomes visible. At $(q,χ)=(0.9947,0.10)$, near the extremal bound, the hair contributes an ellipticity of $8.30\%$, against $0.44\%$ from the charge and $0.06\%$ from rotation --- a factor of $19$ that charging a Kerr black hole cannot mimic; at the overcharged solution $(q,χ)=(1.2527,0.10)$ the total ellipticity reaches $25.6\%$. Whether the branch is ghost-free remains open, but the existing no-go criteria do not settle it: they assume a background and a dispersion relation that do not hold here.

</details>


### [179] [Gravitational waves away from a Schwarzschild black hole: full spectrum analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40181)
*Marco Refuto,Giampiero Esposito*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究史瓦西零无穷附近的引力波，将其视为无质量标量场的自旋加权推广，计算其完整谱并与LIGO/VIRGO观测结果一致，延续作者此前在黑洞微扰论中利用庞加莱渐近展开的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前发展了基于庞加莱渐近展开的黑洞微扰论数学形式，但尚未与引力波现象学建立联系；本文旨在将该形式体系与引力波物理对接，为刻画引力波特征提供新的数学工具。

Method: 在史瓦西时空零无穷附近研究引力波，把引力波作为无质量标量场的自旋加权（spin-weighted）推广处理，并借助已有的庞加莱渐近展开框架计算其完整频谱。

Result: 得到了引力波的完整谱，且与LIGO与VIRGO实验观测相符。

Conclusion: 所发展的数学形式与引力波现象学紧密关联，可提供研究引力波特征的新数学工具。

Abstract: Gravitational waves as one approaches Schwarzschild null infinity are the core of our investigation, treated as the spin-weighted generalisation of a massless scalar field. In particular, we compute their full spectrum, consistent with observations coming from LIGO and VIRGO experiments. This paper continues recent work by the authors, where the Poincaré asymptotic expansion was exploited in black hole perturbation theory. The mathematical formalism we developed is now intimately connected with gravitational wave phenomenology, providing new mathematical tools to investigate their features.

</details>


### [180] [Scalar--photon coupling in cosmology: Radiation evolution, recombination, and CMB signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40319)
*Y Bisabr*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在CLASS中实现标量场与光子流体的有效耦合，系统推导背景与线性扰动方程及绝热初始条件，并利用Planck 2018条件似然评估其CMB与物质功率谱的可观测印记；结果显示弱耦合下似然较平坦，最佳取样点相对ΛCDM略被不利（Δχ²≈+0.30），尚未构成观测偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量场与辐射的相互作用可改变复合前膨胀历史，从而在CMB各向异性中留下印记，但目前缺乏对该有效标量—光子流体耦合从背景到扰动的完整、可计算的实现与观测检验。

Method: 对指数势正则标量场推导耦合的背景方程、线性扰动方程及领头阶绝热初始条件；从辐射期标度解出发，将动力学相互作用植入CLASS，计算复合历史、CMB各向异性与线性物质功率谱；光子热力学与碰撞作唯象处理并假设普朗克谱守恒；采用固定的六个标准宇宙学参数、仅对定标参数A_Planck进行profile的条件Planck 2018温度、偏振与透镜似然进行检验。

Result: 相互作用对CMB与物质谱产生尺度依赖的修正，但弱耦合下条件似然较浅；最佳取样点(ε_i,σ)=(4×10⁻⁵,0.10)相对匹配的无相互作用ΛCDM参考略被不利，Δχ²≈+0.30。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了唯象的宇宙学印记，但未证明观测上对相互作用的偏好；确定性约束需进一步验证光子热化、碰撞动力学与光子密度归一化。

Abstract: Interactions between a cosmological scalar field and radiation can modify the pre-recombination expansion history and leave observable signatures in cosmic microwave background (CMB) anisotropies. We investigate an effective scalar-photon-fluid coupling, deriving the coupled background and linear perturbation equations and leading-order adiabatic initial conditions for a canonical scalar field with an exponential potential. Starting from a radiation-era scaling solution, we implement the dynamical interaction in CLASS and compute the resulting recombination history, CMB anisotropies, and linear matter power spectrum. Photon thermodynamics and collisions are treated phenomenologically, assuming preservation of a Planckian spectrum. We assess observational compatibility using conditional Planck 2018 temperature, polarization, and lensing likelihoods, holding the six standard cosmological parameters fixed while profiling the calibration parameter $A_{\rm Planck}$.
  The interaction produces scale-dependent modifications to CMB and matter spectra, but the conditional likelihood remains shallow at weak coupling. The best sampled interacting point, $(ε_i,σ)=(4\times10^{-5},0.10)$, is slightly disfavored relative to the matched interaction-off $Λ$CDM reference ($Δχ^2\simeq+0.30$). These results establish phenomenological cosmological signatures without demonstrating an observational preference for the interaction. Definitive constraints require further validation of photon thermalization, collision dynamics, and photon-density normalization.

</details>


### [181] [Quasinormal-mode encounters: Pseudospectra near Kerr--Newman exceptional points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40323)
*Kyriakos Destounis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 利用伪谱与条件数研究Kerr–Newman时空中近异常点处黑洞准正规模谱不稳定性被显著放大，频率劈裂按平方根律、条件数按距离的逆平方根增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常点(EP)处两个准正规模频率与本征矢合并，理论上会强烈放大黑洞准正规模的谱不稳定性；作者希望在Kerr–Newman时空中量化这一效应，并检验其对ringdown的潜在贡献。

Method: 对中性无质量标量场沿(ℓ,m)=(1,1)椭球分支、由能动张量构造能量范数；对耦合引力电磁(GE)扰动采用带正辐射能量的四场耦合系统；在选定的近EP背景上计算伪谱与条件数，并考察匹配GE参数路径上的频率劈裂与条件数标度。

Result: 在近EP背景上，近距离模式对中两个模式的条件数均高于同一背景下更阻尼的两个模式；沿匹配GE参数路径，频率劈裂服从平方根律，条件数按到EP距离的逆平方根增长；伪谱轮廓清晰呈现从单模敏感性到模式对集体响应的过渡。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的离散化问题与能量范数框架内，结果量化了EP附近谱不稳定性的放大，并为研究其对黑洞ringdown的贡献提供了基础。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs), at which two quasinormal frequencies and their eigenvectors coalesce, can strongly enhance the spectral instability of black hole quasinormal modes. We investigate this behavior in Kerr--Newman spacetimes through pseudospectra and condition numbers for neutral massless scalar fields and coupled gravitoelectromagnetic (GE) perturbations. For the scalar field, we follow the $(\ell,m)=(1,1)$ spheroidal branch and derive the energy norm from the stress-energy tensor. For GE perturbations, we use a coupled four-field system equipped with a positive radiative energy. At the selected near-EP backgrounds, the condition numbers of both modes in the close pair exceed those of the two more-damped modes shown for comparison at the same background. Along a matched GE parameter path, the frequency splitting follows a square-root law and the condition numbers grow as the inverse square root of the distance to the EP. The pseudospectral contours resolve the transition from individual-mode sensitivity to the collective response of the pair. Within the discretized problems and energy norms considered here, these results quantify the amplification of spectral instability near EPs and provide a basis for studying their contribution to black-hole ringdown.

</details>


### [182] [Curvature Blindness: Material Inference in Stars and Black Hole Candidates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40501)
*Jacob M. Krebs*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出广义相对论中"性质层面可辨识性判据",系统组织两类导致物性无法确定的逆问题：不同状态可具有相同总源，不同时空历史可具有相同保留观测；并以理想气体与黑洞候选体为例给出说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力观测可能以两种方式留下物性不确定性：①不同物态具有相同的总源（等源模糊性）；②不同时空历史在保留观测上不可区分。现有框架缺少统一判据来判断保留数据能否确定材料性质，作者因此提出性质层面的可辨识性准则来组织这类逆问题。

Method: 建立性质层面可辨识性判据，将逆问题分解为三个层次：瞬时源相等、历史范围内源相等与观测投影。用两个理想无质量气体构造等源模糊性示例（应力能量张量相同而温度相差因子 (8/29)^{1/4}、熵不同）；另构造因果流体示例：初始源与几何数据相同但本构响应不同，比较二者曲率历史（在固有时线性阶分离）与标度因子（三次阶分离）。对黑洞候选体，区分定常外部参数、扰动通量与组分历史。

Result: 两个理想无质量气体给出等源但温度、熵不同的显式构造；因果流体两模型在曲率历史的固有时线性阶、标度因子的三次阶出现分离；框架澄清黑洞候选体中定常外部参数、扰动通量与组分历史之间的差别，并给出检验保留数据是否确定某一物性的方法。

Conclusion: 性质层面可辨识性判据把"观测是否确定材料性质"分解为瞬时源相等、历史源相等与观测投影三个层次，为恒星与黑洞候选体的引力逆问题提供统一检验框架，并表明等源或等历史观测并不蕴含物性相同。

Abstract: Gravitational observations can leave material properties undetermined in two different ways: distinct states can have the same total source, and distinct spacetime histories can have the same retained observations. We organize these inverse problems in general relativity through a property-level identifiability criterion, with applications to stars and black hole candidates. Two ideal massless gases illustrate equal-source ambiguity: their temperatures differ by a factor of $(8/29)^{1/4}$ and their entropies differ while their stress-energy tensors agree. A separate causal-fluid example starts with identical sources and geometric data but different constitutive responses; the two models' curvature histories separate at linear order in proper time and their scale factors at cubic order. For black hole candidates, the relevant distinction is between stationary exterior parameters, perturbing fluxes and constituent history. The framework provides a test for whether retained data identify a material property, separating instantaneous source equality, history-wide source equality and observational projection.

</details>


### [183] [Anisotropic overlap reduction functions for arbitrary equal-arm TDI channels: A fully analytic framework and efficient evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40548)
*Kun Zhou,Jin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文建立了静态、互易、等臂等边星座中任意有限延迟TDI通道各向异性重叠约化函数（ORF）的统一解析框架，将通道生成与角度积分分离，并给出多精度稳定算法，较天区数值积分加速约50–5000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道TDI探测的各向异性引力波背景建模需要大量各向异性ORF，而逐一天区积分计算代价高，且延迟点存在可去奇异性，缺乏统一、可复用的解析与稳定数值方案。

Method: 构造 admissible channel module 的自由基，联合激光噪声抵消的多项式约束、星座对称性与方向投影积分；用两个初等角度核族生成所有通道对的强度ORF；通道代数与偏振无关，故强度核约化适用于张量、矢量、呼吸和纵向扇区；在任意固定有限角度阶（示例至ℓ=5）保留完整传播效应；采用预备频率批次、共享多精度表达式及可去延迟点局部展开实现稳定求值。

Result: 在匹配误差容限下，预备批次基准中解析实现比优化的天区求积快约50至5000倍；独立多精度天区积分验证了测试算例中低至10^-100量级的混合误差目标。

Conclusion: 所得核函数与生成规则为各向异性多通道背景模型提供了可复用的解析输入，构成统一的ORF计算框架。

Abstract: We establish a unified analytic framework for anisotropic overlap reduction functions (ORFs) of arbitrary finite-delay time-delay interferometry (TDI) channels in a static, reciprocal, equal-arm equilateral constellation. The construction combines the polynomial constraints for laser-noise cancellation, constellation symmetries, and directional-projection integration. A free basis of the admissible channel module separates channel generation from angular integration; two families of elementary angular kernels then generate all channel-pair intensity ORFs. The channel algebra is independent of gravitational-wave polarization, and the intensity-kernel reduction applies to tensor, vector, breathing, and longitudinal sectors. Tensor multipoles illustrate the framework here. Complete propagation effects are retained at arbitrary fixed finite angular order, with explicit examples through $\ell=5$. Prepared frequency batches, shared multiprecision expressions, and local expansions at removable delay points provide stable evaluation. In the prepared-batch benchmark, the analytic implementation is approximately 50 to 5000 times faster than optimized sky quadrature at matched error tolerances. Independent multiprecision sky integrations also validate prescribed mixed error targets down to $10^{-100}$ in the tested cases. The resulting kernels and generation rules supply reusable inputs for anisotropic multichannel background models.

</details>


### [184] [Gravitational-Wave Echoes and Greybody Cutoff from Logarithmic Throats at a Marginal-Trapping Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40631)
*Jahed Abedi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究近临界（准黑洞）长喉中轴向引力波的传播：喉内两端的回声延迟随临界的偏离按 Δ^{-s/2} 标度增长，时间域演化验证了三个量级的标度关系，且势垒给出不依赖任何视界表面重力的灰体截断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态球对称位形逼近边缘俘获阈值时会形成长喉：当 F=1-2m/r 的非退化极小趋近零，喉的固有长度按 -lnΔ/√β 增长而局部曲率保持有限。这类"封顶喉"是研究无视界微态几何的简化模型，作者希望弄清引力波在这种喉中如何传播、回声延迟如何随距离阈值的偏离标度，并与薄壳引力星/虫洞等模型对比。

Method: 采用有效模型，将 lapse 参数化为 N∝F^{s/2}，在角动量势垒之后构造"腔体"；同时研究四维、由物质支撑的准黑洞，其近-AdS₂ 喉由正则中心封顶（类比封顶微态几何）。对多种 s 值做时间域演化，测量回声间隔 T_echo≃2Λ+δ 随 Δ 的变化，并分析势垒对反射谱的灰体截断。

Result: s>0 时喉起腔体作用，回波以 T_echo≃2Λ+δ∝Δ^{-s/2} 的间隔返回（δ 与 Δ 无关）；时间域演化在 Δ 上跨越三个量级证实该标度。s=0 时喉并入势垒，无独立回声。势垒导致反射谱灰体截断 1-Γ≃e^{-(ω-f)/T}，T 由势垒曲率决定，不依赖其背后视界的表面重力。

Conclusion: 该模型中回声延迟由喉底附近红移的倒数控制（与封顶微态几何一致），不同于薄壳引力星和虫洞仅按到"应有视界"距离的对数增长。模型中的瞬发信号在有视界与有反射喉时相同，但无视界情形下透射波以回声返回，补全了全反射谱的高频部分，可据此区分视界与反射喉。

Abstract: When a static, spherically symmetric configuration is brought close to a marginal-trapping threshold, it develops a long throat. As the nondegenerate minimum of $F=1-2m/r$ approaches zero, $F\simeqΔ+β(r-R_M)^2$, the proper length across the throat grows as $-\lnΔ/\sqrtβ$, even though the local curvature stays finite. We study how axial gravitational waves propagate in an effective model of this throat, in which the lapse is parametrized as $N\propto F^{s/2}$. For $s>0$ the throat acts as a cavity behind the angular-momentum barrier, and waves come back out as echoes spaced by $T_{\rm echo}\simeq2Λ+δ\proptoΔ^{-s/2}$, where $Λ$ is the optical length of the throat and $δ$ does not depend on $Δ$. We study four-dimensional, matter-supported quasi-black holes in which a regular centre caps the near-AdS$_2$ throat. These configurations provide a simplified model for investigating capped throats in horizonless microstate geometries. The echo delay in this model is controlled by the inverse redshift near the bottom of the throat, as in capped microstate geometries. This differs from thin-shell gravastars and wormholes, where the delay grows only logarithmically with the distance to the would-be horizon. Time-domain evolutions confirm the $Δ^{-s/2}$ scaling over three decades in $Δ$; for $s=0$ the throat becomes part of the barrier and no distinct echo appears. The barrier also imposes a greybody cutoff on the reflected spectrum, $1-Γ\simeq e^{-(ω-f)/T}$, where $T$ is fixed by the curvature of the barrier and does not depend on the surface gravity of any horizon behind it. In this model, the prompt signal is the same whether the interior contains a horizon or a reflecting throat. In the horizonless case, however, the transmitted wave returns as echoes, restoring the high-frequency part of the full reflected spectrum.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Exploring Fractional Quantum Hall States with Deep Learning: From Abelian to Non-Abelian Phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38533)
*Xiangyu Ma,Kehuang Chen,Yuntian Gu,Weiluo Ren,Ji Chen,Di Luo,Fengcheng Wu,Xiang Li*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种对称适配神经网络波函数，可同时描述阿贝尔与非阿贝尔分数量子霍尔态并纳入所有朗道能级，用于识别竞争拓扑序的微观基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔系统的基态识别困难：竞争相常近简并，且朗道能级混合难以处理，亟需能同时处理多朗道能级与多种拓扑序的灵活波函数框架。

Method: 构造对称适配（symmetry-adapted）神经网络波函数，兼容阿贝尔与非阿贝尔态，并包含所有朗道能级的贡献；通过有限尺寸重叠（overlaps）与纠缠谱（entanglement spectra）与候选分数量子霍尔态进行比较，涵盖复合费米液体、Wigner 结晶及分数量子霍尔态。

Result: 准确刻画复合费米液体及低填充下的 Wigner 结晶；第一朗道能级部分填充时，ν=4/3 与 7/5 的重叠与纠缠谱证据与 $\bar{2}^{k}1^{k+1}$ 型 parton 相一致，ν=3/2 呈反 Pfaffian 型纠缠结构。

Conclusion: 神经网络波函数为研究分数量子霍尔系统中竞争拓扑序提供了灵活且有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall systems host strongly correlated topological phases with potential applications in topological quantum computation, yet identifying their microscopic ground states remains challenging because competing phases are often nearly degenerate and Landau-level mixing is difficult to treat. Here, we introduce a symmetry-adapted neural-network wavefunction that describes Abelian and non-Abelian states while incorporating all Landau levels. The method accurately captures composite-fermion liquids and the emergence of Wigner crystallization at low filling. When the first Landau level is partially filled, finite-size overlaps and entanglement spectra provide evidence consistent with $\bar{2}^{k}1^{k+1}$-type parton phases at $ν=4/3$ and $7/5$, while the $ν=3/2$ state exhibits anti-Pfaffian-like entanglement structure. These results demonstrate that neural-network wavefunctions offer a flexible framework for studying competing topological orders in fractional quantum Hall systems.

</details>


### [186] [Dual Area-Volume-of-Fluid (DAVOF): co-advecting volume and face-area fractions for a closed-form, cell-local interface normal and position on unstructured meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38863)
*Tomislav Marić*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于"面润湿面积分数"输运的几何VOF新方法：利用散度定理在单元自身面上直接闭合求解界面法向与位置，无需邻居模板或迭代，在多面体网格上保持平面精确性并显著改善曲率与毛细流稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非结构多面体网格上的几何VOF依赖邻居模板的体分数重构界面方向，在畸变单元与存在wisp（破碎小液滴）时精度退化，由此得到的曲率会驱动毛细流的寄生电流。

Method: 与每个单元的体分数一致地输运每个面面积的润湿分数；由面积分数与体分数用两个闭合恒等式分别解出界面法向（对单元自身面求和+液体子体积上的散度定理）与平面位置，无需邻居与迭代；以平面裁剪输运，并用单元环并集上的同一恒等式得到的"环法向"稳定状态；初步输运实现中每单元一个抛物面片，其坐标框架取自法向、曲率张量取自环、位置取自体分数。

Result: 两个恒等式在六面体、扰动及多面体单元上对平面精确；球与椭球上法向达到面分数阶精度，位置在所有状态与网格下均二阶；穿过一环重构法向的二次曲面给出收敛曲率，参考模型则无法给出。球体输运超3.3个直径保持有界守恒，面分数为一阶，位置与形状误差与plicRDF相当。抛物面片初步实现下，N=32–128、每周期面分数约2.8（精确法向）与2.1（环二次曲面法向）时球体有界守恒。

Conclusion: 整体精度受所输入法向的阶数限制，二阶输运法向是后续研究目标；本文档作为该方法进一步开发的记录基础。

Abstract: Geometric Volume-of-Fluid (VOF) on unstructured polyhedral meshes reconstructs the interface orientation from the volume fractions of a stencil of neighbours; on irregular cells and in the presence of wisps it degrades, and the curvature built on it drives the parasitic currents of capillary flows. We transport, consistently to the volume fraction of every cell, the wetted fraction of every face area. From the area and volume fractions, closed-form equations recover the interface normal and position of a cell, for the first time in VOF: the normal from one sum over the cell's own faces, the divergence theorem on the liquid sub-volume, and the plane's position from a second, without neighbours or iteration. Both identities are exact for a plane on hexahedral, perturbed and polyhedral cells. On a sphere and an ellipsoid the normal has the order of the face fractions, the position is second order for every state and mesh, and a quadric through one ring of recovered normals gives a converging curvature where the reference models show none. Transported with the plane clipping, the ring normal, the same identity on the union of the cell's ring, stabilises the state and carries a sphere over 3.3 diameters bounded and conservative, first order in the face fractions as the clipping law predicts, at plicRDF's position and shape errors. A preliminary transport implementation with one paraboloid per cell, its frame from the normal, its curvature tensor from the ring and its position from the volume fraction, carries the sphere bounded and conservative over the resolution ladder with face fractions of order 2.8 per period with the exact normal and 2.1 with the ring quadric's normal over N = 32 to 128. The loop has the order of the normal it is fed; a transported normal of second order is the research goal. This document is the basis on which the further development of the method is documented.

</details>


### [187] [PhysicsNeMo-Mesh: GPU-Native Meshes and Simulation Cases for Physics AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39072)
*Peter D. Sharpe,Corey Adams,Negin Sobhani,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: PhysicsNeMo-Mesh 是开源 PhysicsNeMo 库的网格模块，在 GPU 上、批处理、且在 PyTorch 自动微分内提供类似 PyVista 的网格处理能力，并像 xarray 之于非结构网格那样为点/单元/整体网格数据赋予名称与形状语义。它将预处理从"策展时一次性完成"推进到"使用点按需完成"，兼顾中间数组存储的速度与 VTK 等归档格式的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习用于物理仿真面临三个痛点：(1) 样本相对单次前向计算过大，GPU 利用率受 I/O 瓶颈制约；(2) 传统预处理开销高，只能在数据策展阶段做一次，无法针对每个模型/流程在使用点差异化处理；(3) 等变与网格原生架构需要策展过程通常丢弃的信息（如场秩、命名边界）。

Method: 构建 GPU 批处理网格处理模块：提供点、单元和整体网格上的命名字段与形状语义；仿真案例对象保存完整 PDE 问题描述（含边界条件及其给定值）；在使用点进行快速 GPU 预处理，并与训练重叠执行；保留可用自动微分的数据通路。

Result: 单张 GB200 GPU 上网格运算比 72 线程 VTK 快 6.2–1068×；整案例加载比读取其 VTK 文件快 8.0–88×（字段相同时为 8.0×）；与训练重叠时，对 127 ms 的 Transolver 步骤仅损失 2.1% 吞吐。PhysicsNeMo 公开配方中一份 DrivAerML 数据集同时供给 Transolver、GeoTransolver 与 GLOBE。形状与壁面条件同时变化时，按边界设置条件将热传导误差从 87% 降至 6–9%。

Conclusion: PhysicsNeMo-Mesh 结合了中间数组存储的速度与 VTK 类归档格式的灵活性，使一份数据集可服务多种模型与流程；通过保留场秩与命名边界，保留了下一代架构所需的语义结构。

Abstract: PhysicsNeMo-Mesh is the mesh module of the open-source PhysicsNeMo library. It provides mesh processing similar to PyVista, but on the GPU, in batches, and inside PyTorch's automatic differentiation. Like xarray for unstructured meshes, it attaches semantics to data: fields on points, cells, and whole meshes have names and shapes, and a simulation case keeps the complete PDE problem specification, including boundary conditions and their prescribed values. It addresses three problems that plague machine learning for physical simulation. First, GPU utilization is bottlenecked by I/O, because samples are very large relative to the compute per forward pass. Second, because traditional preprocessing is expensive, it is necessarily done once, during curation, rather than differently for each model and workflow at the point of use. Finally, advanced equivariant and mesh-native architectures require information that curation ordinarily discards. Fast GPU mesh processing makes it practical to preprocess at the point of use: on one GB200 GPU, mesh operations run $6.2\text{--}1{,}068\times$ faster than VTK with 72 threads, and loading a whole case is $8.0\text{--}88\times$ faster than reading its VTK files ($8.0\times$ when both read the same fields). Overlapped with training, this preprocessing costs $2.1\%$ of throughput for a $127\,\text{ms}$ Transolver step. So one dataset serves many models and workflows: in PhysicsNeMo's public recipe, one stored DrivAerML dataset feeds Transolver, GeoTransolver, and GLOBE. PhysicsNeMo-Mesh combines the speed of an intermediate array store with the flexibility of an archival format such as VTK. When shapes and wall conditions both vary, per-boundary conditions cut a heat-conduction error from $87\%$ to $6\text{--}9\%$. And by keeping field ranks and named boundaries, it retains the semantic structure that next-generation architectures will need.

</details>


### [188] [A Gaussian-Process Surrogate for Control Rod Worth with Calibrated Uncertainty: Application to VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40389)
*Sean Gohlke,Jilang Miao*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该文用高斯过程代理模型拟合VERA基准题4的反应性-控制棒位置曲线，从核函数导数闭式给出微分与积分棒价值及其不确定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统棒价值通过对离散棒位的本征值差分获得，导数分辨率差且无不确定度量化。

Method: 在OpenMC(ENDF/B-VIII.0)建模的3×3组件集群(混合AIC/B4C棒束)上构建GP代理：参数化S形均值函数承载动态范围，Matérn-5/2核GP拟合残差，各次计算的蒙特卡洛不确定度作为已知观测噪声；利用微分线性性由核函数导数闭式得到微分棒价值及其方差。43次训练(三种粒子数)、11次独立测试、129次留一验证。

Result: 代理模型在测试噪声之上满足10 pcm精度目标；标准化LOO残差方差1.07，95%区间[0.85,1.39]，区间校准良好。总价值与OpenMC端点直接差分在合成不确定度内一致，微分价值峰值定位误差在一步以内。三种拟合质量差异很大的均值函数给出差异<0.1 pcm/step的微分价值，但其带宽宽度随核平滑度变化达4倍。

Conclusion: GP代理可给出校准良好的棒价值及其不确定度，结果对均值函数选择稳健、对核平滑度敏感；文中报告了所需计算次数与残余近似，并展望扩展到棒位与可溶硼联合建模。

Abstract: Differential and integral control rod worth are usually obtained by differencing eigenvalue calculations at discrete rod positions, which resolves the derivative poorly and gives it no uncertainty. We build a Gaussian-process (GP) surrogate for reactivity as a function of rod position on VERA Core Physics Benchmark Problem 4, a $3\times3$ assembly cluster with a hybrid AIC/B$_4$C rod cluster control assembly, modelled in OpenMC with ENDF/B-VIII.0. A parametric sigmoidal mean carries the dynamic range, a Matern-5/2 GP carries the residual, and the reported Monte Carlo uncertainty of each run enters as known observation noise. Because differentiation is linear, the differential rod worth and its variance follow in closed form from the kernel derivatives. Trained on 43 runs at three particle counts and tested on 11 independent runs, the surrogate meets a fixed 10 pcm accuracy target above the test-run noise, and its intervals are calibrated: the standardized leave-one-out residuals over 129 independent runs have variance 1.07 with 95% interval [0.85, 1.39]. The total worth agrees with the direct OpenMC end-point difference within combined uncertainty, and the differential worth peak is located to within one step. Three mean functions of very different fit quality give the same differential worth to within 0.1 pcm/step, while the width of its band depends on the kernel smoothness by a factor of four. The number of runs required and the remaining approximations are reported, and the extension to rod position and soluble boron is outlined.

</details>


### [189] [ML-COHP: predicting bond descriptors using latent space of a universal interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40445)
*Daniil S. Alekseev,Ilya V. Chepkasov,Alexander G. Kvashnin*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出用MACE基础模型冻结描述符预测ICOHP的对称成对头模型，误差较GP模型减半，秒级重现晶界LOBPSTER结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICOHP是DFT中衡量键强度的标准量，但每个结构都需单独做DFT单点加LOBSTER投影，百原子级晶胞（如晶界、催化纳米颗粒）需耗时数天，成本过高。

Method: 从MACE基础模型的冻结描述符出发进行预测：设计对称成对头，读取两原子的标量通道与键投影矢量通道，并对由同一模型元素嵌入构建的成对基线进行修正；在高熵二硼化物(Hf,Zr,Ti,Nb,Ta)B2、缺陷石墨烯上的Pt团簇及吸附O2的Pt/Pd纳米颗粒上测试。

Result: 强于0.5 eV的键误差为1.6–4.1%，约为Ferreira等在同一数据上训练的GP模型的一半；仅用高温HEB2快照训练即可将弛豫晶界晶胞的LOBSTER ICOHP重现到0.06 eV，耗时约1秒（LOBBSTER需5–51小时）；矢量通道使误差减半，基线使模型仅需两个训练结构即可用；结合微调MACE势的几何，给出连续O2覆盖下Pt/Pd颗粒的成键图像，DFT显示O2使Pt富集壳层损失的金属—金属键是Pd壳层的数倍。

Conclusion: 冻结的MACE描述符可高效、准确地代理ICOHP计算，显著降低键分析成本，并支持大尺度催化剂与缺陷结构的高通量成键分析。

Abstract: The integrated crystal orbital Hamilton population (ICOHP) is the standard measure of bond strength in a DFT calculation, but every structure needs its own DFT single point and LOBSTER projection, which takes days for a cell of a hundred atoms, such as a grain boundary or a catalytic nanoparticle. We predict ICOHP from the frozen descriptors of a MACE foundation model. A symmetric pair head reads the scalar and bond-projected vector channels of the two atoms and corrects a pair baseline built from the element embeddings of the same model. We tested the model on the high-entropy diboride (Hf,Zr,Ti,Nb,Ta)B$_2$, Pt clusters on defective graphene and Pt/Pd nanoparticles with adsorbed O$_2$. The error on bonds stronger than 0.5 eV is 1.6-4.1%, about half that of the Gaussian-process model of Ferreira et al. trained on the same data. Trained only on high-temperature HEB$_2$ snapshots, the model reproduces the LOBSTER ICOHP of relaxed grain boundary cells to 0.06 eV in about a second, against 5-51 hours of LOBSTER. The vector channels halve the error compared to the scalar ones, and the baseline keeps the model usable with two training structures. With geometries from a fine-tuned MACE potential, the model gives the bonding of Pt/Pd particles over a continuous O$_2$ coverage, and DFT on these particles shows that O$_2$ costs a Pt-rich shell several times more metal--metal bonding than a Pd shell.

</details>


### [190] [Predicting Weight Windows for Duct-Streaming Shielding Geometries with a Convolutional Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40457)
*Jilang Miao,Ansel Kneier,Jonathan Porrazzo*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出用U-Net从二维管道几何与源位置直接预测前向通量权重窗下界，毫秒级替代MAGIC迭代MC计算；在Protvino迷宫中未重训练即达0.96相关，出口探测器品质因子为模拟值54倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向通量权重窗能对管道穿透屏蔽问题实现全局方差缩减，但每种新几何都需重新做迭代蒙特卡罗计算才能构造权重窗，代价高、周期长，亟需快速生成手段。

Method: 在Protvino迷宫包围盒内随机生成250个混凝土管道（三类弯曲与矩形族），以Cf-252点源在OpenMC中运行并生成MAGIC权重窗下界；训练一个190万参数U-Net学习从二维几何与源位置到下界对数的映射；以留出几何评估误差，并在重训练之外测试矩形Protvino迷宫的迁移与权重窗应用效果，比较品质因子(FOM)。

Result: 留出管道中承载通量的网格误差为0.14–0.51个数量级，而重复MAGIC运行的可复现性仅数个百分之一数量级，故残差属模型误差（低于1e-6处参考本身为噪声）。直接迁移至矩形Protvino迷宫时预测与MAGIC相关0.96；经最深单元截断后作权重窗，出口探测器FOM为模拟值54倍、同几何MAGIC运行1.7倍、复用MAGIC权重窗的0.80倍。并表明任一权重窗的运行成本由其最深单元决定，可由粒子群代理量预测。

Conclusion: CNN可毫秒级近似MAGIC权重窗生成，具备跨几何迁移能力并带来显著方差缩减收益，但受最深单元成本约束暂未超越复用MAGIC窗；以种群代理量预测成本为部署阶段的截断策略与推广提供了依据。

Abstract: Forward-flux-based weight windows give global variance reduction for duct-streaming shielding problems, but each new geometry needs an iterative Monte Carlo calculation to build them. We report preliminary results on a convolutional neural network (CNN) that learns the map from a two-dimensional duct geometry and source position to the lower window bound of such an iteration, here the MAGIC generator in OpenMC, in milliseconds. A set of 250 random concrete ducts of three curved and rectangular families on the Protvino labyrinth bounding box was run in OpenMC with a Cf-252 point source and MAGIC generation, and a 1.9-million-parameter U-Net was trained on the logarithm of the lower bound. On held-out ducts the error in the cells that carry the flux is 0.14 to 0.51 decades; replicate MAGIC runs show this part of the map to be reproducible to a few hundredths of a decade, so the residual is model error, while below $10^{-6}$ the reference itself is noise. Applied without retraining to the rectangular Protvino labyrinth, the prediction correlates with MAGIC at 0.96. Used as weight windows there, with its deepest cells clamped at deployment, it gives an exit-detector figure of merit 54 times the analog value, 1.7 times that of the MAGIC generation run for the same geometry, and 0.80 of that of reused MAGIC windows whose construction it does not pay for. The run cost of any window set is shown to be set by its deepest cells and predictable from a population proxy.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [191] [Moment-Guided Optimizers for Noisy Deep Learning: MGO-SC as a Simple Consistency Gate for Adam-Style Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37712)
*Alicem Koyun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MGO-SC——一种基于符号 EMA 幅值的坐标级"符号一致性门控"的 Adam 类轻量改进，并将朴素门控改为"保均值"变体以补偿步长损失；在 CIFAR-10/ResNet-18 上匹配学习率与解耦权重衰减后，在干净、对称与非对称标签噪声下均稳定优于 AdamW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应优化器在强随机性与标签噪声下会退化，原因是梯度符号与幅值不稳定。作者希望在不再引入二阶信息或逐样本过滤的前提下，用极低开销的机制区分"符号稳定的可信坐标"与"符号抖动的不可信坐标"，从而提升噪声鲁棒性。

Method: 以符号的指数滑动平均（EMA）幅值构造坐标级一致性门控：符号稳定坐标被信任、抖动坐标被降权。作者指出朴素门控只能收缩更新，导致有效步长损失、在匹配学习率下劣于基线；因此提出保均值（mean-preserving）变体，在坐标间重新分配信任而不改变每张量净步长尺度。与 AdamW 结合，配合学习率匹配与解耦权重衰减。

Result: CIFAR-10 + ResNet-18，匹配学习率与解耦权重衰减条件下，保均值 MGO-SC 在干净、对称（如 40% 对称噪声下 +1.4 点）与非对称标签噪声下均一致优于 AdamW；配对 bootstrap 95% 置信区间不含零，Wilcoxon 检验 p<0.01。论文还报告了匹配随机种子的效应量、非参数 bootstrap 置信区间与配对显著性检验，并公开代码与原始日志。

Conclusion: 符号一致性门控本身并非"越多收缩越好"：必须保持净步长尺度才有效。保均值 MGO-SC 是一种零额外二阶开销、无需逐样本过滤的轻量鲁棒性增强，在标签噪声场景下稳定改善 AdamW，且实证报告规范（效应量、置信区间、配对检验、代码开放）较为扎实。

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers are widely used in deep learning, yet their behavior can degrade under heavy stochasticity and label noise where the sign and magnitude of gradients can be unstable. We study MGO-SC (Moment-Guided Optimizer with Sign-Consistency), a lightweight modification of Adam-style methods that derives a coordinate-wise consistency gate from the magnitude of a sign exponential moving average (EMA): coordinates whose gradient sign is stable are trusted, while coordinates whose sign fluctuates are down-weighted. We show that the naive form of this gate, which can only shrink updates, loses effective step size and underperforms when the learning rate is matched to the baseline. We therefore introduce a mean-preserving variant that reallocates trust across coordinates without changing the net per-tensor step magnitude. On CIFAR-10 with ResNet-18, under a matched learning rate and decoupled weight decay, mean-preserving MGO-SC consistently improves over its AdamW baseline across clean, symmetric, and asymmetric label noise (e.g., +1.4 points at 40% symmetric noise; paired bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero; Wilcoxon p<0.01), while adding no second-order information or per-sample filtering. We report matched-seed effect sizes, nonparametric bootstrap confidence intervals, and paired significance tests, and we release code and raw logs.

</details>


### [192] [What a Weight Update Buys: Leverage Across Training Stages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37719)
*Shikhar Shiromani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"杠杆率"(leverage)——以Fisher二次型衡量单位权重更新带来的实际行为收益，并用以区分预训练、SFT、RL、DPO等阶段的更新效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练仅移动约千分之一权重的范数却剧烈改变模型行为，而预训练移动约三分之一范数却只带来缓慢漂移；现有的权重变化范数等指标无法解释这种巨大差异，因此需要一个能刻画"权重更新效率"的精确度量。

Method: 定义leverage为更新方向的Fisher二次型，除以同检查点同尺寸随机方向的同一二次型（每个方向一次前向模式自动微分即可计算）；在Olmo-3、Tülu-3、Zephyr、OLMo-2检查点上测量；结合SGD噪声二次模型、bf16/fp32精度对照实验、以及DARE随机删除-重缩放的理论与实验分析；并用两个受控复现实验验证读数可靠性。

Result: leverage在四个数量级上对检查点排序：预训练窗口约等于随机水平，SFT约5倍，RL-Zero与RLVR为400–1,500倍，DPO为400–23,000倍。预训练在30k–1M步窗口内对散文的效果不超随机方向，符合含噪二次模型的预测。无fp32主副本的bf16训练冻结36–99%坐标、leverage最多下降7倍，并产生更新幅度与对角Fisher的对齐。推导并验证复用律：符号翻转零假设预测DARE在保留率p下的相对损伤为(1−p)/(p·ρ_sign)，在DARE有效处与实测相差不超过2倍。控制实验：fp32主权重下SFT/DPO轨迹随训练推进leverage下降；从锚点复跑已发布DPO阶段，位移在1%内、leverage在4%内吻合，而普通配方读数低5–6倍；消融显示该水平由长度归一化损失与其batch size 128共同决定。

Conclusion: leverage是一个精确、可复现的更新效率度量，能统一解释预训练与各类后训练阶段的差异，揭示训练精度与损失/批大小配置对更新的影响，并为DARE等权重复用方法给出可验证的定量规律。

Abstract: Post-training moves a language model's weights by about a thousandth of their norm and changes its behavior sharply; pretraining moves them by a third and buys a slow drift. We introduce leverage, an exact measure of what a unit of weight change buys: the Fisher quadratic form of an update, computed with one forward-mode pass per direction, divided by the same form for random directions of the same size at the same checkpoint. Leverage orders released Olmo-3, Tülu-3, Zephyr, and OLMo-2 checkpoints over four orders of magnitude: pretraining windows sit at the random level on prose, SFT five times above it, RL-Zero and RLVR at 400-1,500, and DPO at 400-23,000. With this instrument we find that pretraining accumulates into noise: windows of 30k to 1M steps are no more consequential on prose than random directions of their size, as a noisy quadratic model of SGD predicts. We find that training precision changes what an update looks like: training bf16 parameters without an fp32 master copy freezes 36-99% of coordinates, lowers leverage up to sevenfold, and creates an alignment between update magnitudes and the diagonal Fisher that fp32 training does not show. We derive and verify a law for reuse: the sign-flip null predicts the relative damage of random drop-and-rescale (DARE) at keep rate $p$ as $(1-p)/(p\,ρ_{\mathrm{sign}})$, within a factor of two of measurement wherever DARE succeeds. Two controls make these readings reliable: every controlled SFT and DPO trajectory with fp32 master weights loses leverage as it moves, and two re-runs of a released DPO stage from its anchor with its published recipe land within 1% of the released displacement and 4% of its leverage, where a plain recipe reads five to six times lower; leaving one component out traces the level to the length-normalized loss and its batch size of 128 together.

</details>


### [193] [Threshold Compatibility and Support-Selection Diagnostics in Neural Discovery of Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37726)
*Zeen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过有限诊断实验表明：在神经偏微分方程（PDE）发现中，即便系数回归精确到数值精度，支持集选择也可能出错；阈值不兼容、重拟合损伤与评分拒绝是三类可区分的失效机制，但本文不主张自动修正或一般性恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经PDE发现流程通常假定"精确回归即得正确方程"，但支持选择（support selection）环节可能破坏这一假设。作者希望系统刻画"回归精确却方程错误"这一现象，并厘清其背后的具体机制。

Method: 针对中位数/样本标准差阈值准则，推导精确的双信号保留边界与全向量扰动界；在精确对流-扩散（advection-diffusion）库上检验系数恢复；对40个已保存稀疏状态做真支持重拟合；设置固定序贯阈值岭回归对照（60个理想控制）；开展条件验证与事后能量池化；在4条未使用解析轨迹上做240次物理残差比较；在系数、观测点与权重固定条件下进行精确库/目标替换实验；并测试损失加权在各预算下是否满足Burgers扩散收益准则。

Result: 精确对流-扩散库在系数恢复至数值精度时仍丢失扩散项；真支持重拟合降低扩散误差但不能恢复全部导数精度；序贯阈值岭回归在60个理想控制中全部恢复精确支持，却在每个学习状态中多选项；全折正确支持候选被条件验证拒绝，事后能量池化在60个状态中均保留其更低的极小损失；240次物理残差比较中，给定支持估计全部优于未修正的选中方程；在59/60状态下精确替换库与目标会反转学习行序；损失加权在各测试预算下均未满足指定的Burgers扩散收益准则。

Conclusion: 有限诊断可区分阈值不兼容、重拟合损伤与评分拒绝三类失效；但本文不建立自动修正方法，也不声称一般性恢复或预测稳定性。

Abstract: Accurate regression need not yield the correct equation after support selection. For a median/sample-standard-deviation threshold in neural PDE discovery, we derive an exact two-signal retention boundary and a complete-vector perturbation bound. Exact advection-diffusion libraries lose diffusion despite coefficient recovery to numerical precision. True-support refits of forty saved sparse states reduce diffusion error without restoring all derivative accuracy. A fixed sequential threshold ridge comparator recovers exact support in sixty ideal controls but selects extra terms in every learned state. All-fold correct-support candidates are rejected by conditional validation; post hoc energy pooling preserves their higher minimum loss in all sixty states. Supplied-support estimates outperform unchanged selected equations in all 240 physical-residual comparisons on four unused analytic trajectories. With coefficients, observation points and weights fixed, exact replacement of the learned library and target reverses their learned-row ordering in 59/60 states. Weighting both losses also fails the specified Burgers diffusion-benefit criterion at every tested budget. These finite diagnostics distinguish threshold incompatibility, refit damage and score rejection; they establish neither automatic correction nor general recovery or predictive stability.

</details>


### [194] [Predicate Neuron: Differentiable Quantization of Predicate Relations in Gradient-based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37727)
*Ioannis Konstantoulas,Iliana Loi,Kyriakos Sgarbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Native Predicate Neuron（谓词神经元），一种可微分的逻辑推理神经模块，通过一阶逻辑量化把符号推理原生嵌入标准神经网络，在 FC/CNN/RNN/Transformer 上加速收敛、提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号推理方法多依赖静态架构，难以与深度神经网络及梯度优化框架融合，因此需要一个可原生嵌入标准网络、可端到端训练的逻辑推理模块。

Method: 设计 Native Predicate Neuron：用单一可训练参数学习在一阶逻辑运算之间交替选择，从而建模关系逻辑；采用"双重性质"梯度机制——训练时执行硬逻辑运算但保持可反传的可微性；通过对输入与选择状态同时量化，使其能逼近连续与非连续函数并处理不连续性。

Result: 在 MNIST、Wikitext 等基准上，于全连接、卷积、循环、Transformer 四类架构中验证：加入谓词神经元总体加速收敛、提升精度、降低损失，在需要单调推理或处理不连续性的任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 谓词神经元可原生集成进标准神经网络以实现模糊逻辑运算，并在 Keras 与 PyTorch 中开源可用。

Abstract: In machine learning, symbolic reasoning approaches often rely on static architectures that hinder integration into deep neural networks and gradient-based optimization. To address this, we introduce the Native Predicate Neuron, a differentiable logic-reasoning neural module that enriches standard architectures with symbolic reasoning capabilities via first-order logic quantization. The proposed neuron models relational logic by learning to alternate between operations using a single trainable parameter. It features a tailored gradient mechanism with a dual-nature formulation: executing hard logical operations during training while maintaining differentiability for backpropagation. By quantizing both its input and choice state, the Predicate Neuron approximates continuous and non-continuous functions, effectively resolving discontinuities that standard deep learning architectures struggle with. We evaluate the Predicate Neuron across Fully-Connected, Convolutional, Recurrent, and Transformer architectures on benchmarks like MNIST and Wikitext. Results show that incorporating Predicate Neurons accelerates convergence, boosts accuracy, and lowers loss in general, with particularly strong improvements in tasks requiring monotonic reasoning or handling discontinuities. Overall, the Predicate Neuron natively integrates into standard neural networks to enable fuzzy logic operations and is available in Keras and PyTorch.

</details>


### [195] [Selective Transfer Learning for Groundwater Level Anomaly Forecasting: Accuracy-Risk Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37733)
*Yaxuan Wu,Ali Mohammed Mansoor Alsahag,Seyed Sahand Mohammadi Ziabari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作研究荷兰多井地下水异常预测中的上下文感知选择性迁移学习；冻结源域预训练常损害部分异构井，CASL 通过校准训练的凸混合在"无迁移/固定迁移"两个冻结父模型间做上下文加权，共评估 457 口井、13,718 个测试窗口，仅带来 0.424–1.084% 的 RMSE 改善，且与全局凸混合互有胜负。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源域预训练虽能提升平均精度，但在异构井上表现不一致：固定迁移会显著损害相当比例的井（18.38–51.86%），因此需要一种能按上下文选择性利用迁移、规避负迁移的方法，并厘清"部分救回"究竟来自凸混合的结构性效应还是真正的上下文优势。

Method: CASL（Context-Aware Selective Learning）：以校准集训练、带约束的上下文混合，在两个冻结父模型（无迁移模型 No-TL 与固定迁移模型 Fixed-TL）之间做逐上下文凸组合；对照方法 Global Convex Blend 用同一对父模型但每 seed 仅拟合一个全局权重。任务设定为九个月连续观测输入、精确三个月历月目标且不做插值；评价覆盖 457 口井、13,718 个测试窗口、5 个预定义种子，四单元 maxsim0.60 源域池仅凭 Always-TL 验证 RMSE 冻结。

Result: 固定迁移损害 18.38–51.86% 的井。CASL 相比 No-TL 的 RMSE 低 0.424–1.084%，在受损井中部分救回 97.53–99.50%、完全救回 23.46–43.78%。全局凸混合在 4 个种子上保护更多井，CASL 在 4 个种子上汇总 RMSE 略低，二者互不支配。

Conclusion: 部分救回可由凸混合的结构性性质产生，并不能证明上下文选择本身优越。结果支持固定划分上的"精度—风险权衡"结论，而非滚动部署有效性；CASL 的实际增益幅度有限。

Abstract: This work studies context-aware selective transfer learning for multi-well groundwater anomaly forecasting in the Netherlands. Source-domain pretraining can raise average accuracy but is not uniformly reliable: it degrades performance on a substantial fraction of heterogeneous wells. Context-Aware Selective Learning (CASL) uses calibration-trained, constrained contextual blending of frozen no-transfer and fixed-transfer parents. Global Convex Blend uses the same parents with one calibration-fitted weight per seed. The corrected task requires nine consecutive observed monthly inputs and an exact three-calendar-month target, without interpolation. Evaluation covers 457 wells and 13,718 test windows under five predefined seeds, with the four-cell maxsim0.60 source pool frozen using Always-TL validation RMSE alone. Fixed transfer harms 18.38-51.86% of wells. CASL has 0.424-1.084% lower RMSE than No TL, partially rescuing 97.53-99.50% and fully rescuing 23.46-43.78% of fixed-harmed wells. Partial rescue can arise structurally from convex blending and does not establish contextual superiority. Global Convex Blend protects more wells in four seeds, while CASL has slightly lower pooled RMSE in four. Neither dominates: the results support an accuracy-risk trade-off on the fixed split, not rolling deployment validity.

</details>


### [196] [Anisotropic Trust Region Warping for Prior-Data Fitted Networks in Bayesian Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37737)
*Samuel Younger,Tinkle Chugh,George De Ath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出先验-数据拟合网络（PFN）在贝叶斯优化中的预测似然会随目标函数长度尺度偏离元训练先验模式而下降，提出无需重训练的各向异性信赖域（ATR）warping：用核目标对齐估计逐维长度尺度，并对输入空间作仿射重缩放以匹配PFN期望的长度尺度，性能随之提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFN以单次前向传播替代高斯过程（GP）代理模型中的超参数重拟合，但代价是先验在元训练阶段被固定。实验表明，当目标函数长度尺度超出PFN元训练先验的众数时，PFN的预测对数似然逐渐落后于重拟合的GP，且该缺陷会传递到优化性能。作者希望在不重训练网络的前提下修正这一先验不匹配。

Method: 提出各向异性信赖域warping：1）通过核目标对齐（kernel target alignment）估计逐维长度尺度；2）对输入空间作仿射重缩放，将目标映射到PFN所期望的长尺度；3）以锚点为中心构造信赖域，决定保留哪些观测点；4）该方法不改变PFN的O(d·N²)推断开销。

Result: 在从Matérn-3/2核采样的函数上，ATR warping在长度尺度范围的两端都提升了PFN的优化性能。

Conclusion: PFN固定先验导致的长度尺度失配可通过输入空间warping在推断阶段予以纠正，无需重训练；ATR warping在极端长度尺度下均改善优化效果，且不增加推断复杂度。代码已公开。

Abstract: Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) replace the hyperparameter refitting of Gaussian Process (GP) surrogates in Bayesian optimisation with a single forward pass, at the cost of a prior that is fixed at meta-training time. We show empirically that a PFN's predictive log-likelihood falls increasingly behind that of a refitted GP as the objective function's lengthscale grows beyond the mode of the PFN's meta-training prior, and that this deficit carries over to optimisation performance. To correct the mismatch without retraining the network, we introduce Anisotropic Trust Region (ATR) warping. Per-dimension lengthscales are estimated by kernel target alignment, and an affine rescaling of the input space maps the objective onto the lengthscale the PFN expects, with a trust region around the anchor determining which observations are retained, leaving the PFN's $\mathcal{O}(d \cdot N^2)$ inference cost unchanged. On functions drawn from a Matérn-3/2 kernel, ATR warping improves optimisation performance of the PFN at both extremes of the lengthscale range. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/youngers2006/Trust-Region-Warping-For-PFNs.

</details>


### [197] [Spokes, not a Ring: Revisiting the Weekday Manifold in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37796)
*Diksha Gupta,Rieke Fruengel,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验"连续流形假设"，发现星期表示虽呈显著环形几何，但实际更像圆上排列的七个离散状态而非连续环，进而质疑对表示流形的连续解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作假设大语言模型将某些概念表示为多维流形而非单一方向，并常据此假设沿流形移动对应概念的连续变化；但这一"连续流形假设"缺乏直接检验。

Method: 以典型的星期流形为对象：(1) 测度沿流形方向的局部敏感度；(2) 检验流形上离散状态之间的中间空间是否被其他概念（一天中的时间、不确定性、自然文本）所占据。

Result: 局部敏感度存在七个与星期对齐的极小值，产生阶梯式的表示—行为映射；中间空间既不由时间、不确定性填充，也不由自然文本填充。

Conclusion: 星期流形应理解为七个离散状态而非连续环，连续解读不成立；并建议系统检验其他表示流形上的移动是否对应有意义的表示与行为变化。

Abstract: Recent work has hypothesised that large language models may represent some concepts as multi-dimensional manifolds instead of single directions. Using these manifolds to interpret or steer representations often assumes that moving along them corresponds to a continuous change in the represented concept. In this work, we test this continuous manifold assumption on the prototypical weekday manifold. Despite its striking circular geometry, we find that the weekday representations behave more like seven discrete states arranged in a circle than a continuous ring. First, we show that local sensitivity has seven minima aligned with the weekdays, producing a step-like mapping between representation and behaviour under on-manifold steering. Second, we test whether any concepts are represented along the manifold outside the seven discrete states, and find neither time of day, uncertainty, nor natural text populate the intermediate space. Together, these results challenge the continuous interpretation of the weekday manifold and motivate testing whether movement along other representation manifolds correspond to meaningful changes in model representations and behaviour.

</details>


### [198] [Responsible Quantum Machine Learning for Medical Imaging: A Fairness Audit Across Sensitive Subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37819)
*Giovanni dos Santos Franco,Felipe Mahlow,Ellison Fernando Cardoso,José Adenaldo Santos Bittencourt,Felipe Fanchini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对医学影像任务中的经典与量子分类器进行实证公平性审计，检验量子机器学习（QML）在下述假设下是否公平：QML并非天然公平。研究在DDI皮肤病损（敏感属性：Fitzpatrick皮肤类型）与CheXpert来源胸片（敏感属性：年龄组）两个场景中，基于ResNet-18嵌入训练SVM、MLP、QSVM、QCNN，并在基线及三种公平性缓解策略（可微Equalized Odds正则、梯度反转GRL、二者结合）下比较。结果显示量子分类器在总体性能上具竞争力但仍存在亚组差异，且差异缩小可能源于最优组性能退化；多个亚组差异最小的配置（如DDI上的QCNN+GRL）因整体判别力损失而不满足预设非劣性界，临床不可接受。公平性效应依模型与数据集而异，需进行亚组感知的实证评估；所有量子模型均为经典模拟，不主张量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QML在医学分类中的探索日益增多，但其在亚组感知公平性准则下的行为缺乏研究；同时存在将量子模型默认为"天然公平"的隐含假设，需要实证检验。

Method: 在两个数据集（DDI皮肤病损，敏感属性为Fitzpatrick皮肤类型；CheXpert衍生胸片，敏感属性为年龄组）上，使用ResNet-18嵌入，训练SVM、MLP、QSVM与QCNN；设置基线及三种缓解策略：可微Equalized Odds正则、Gradient Reversal Learning（GRL）及二者联合；以基础模型PanDerm、EVA-X作为外部参照。除亚组AUC差距外，还对每种缓解策略相对基线设定预设非劣性界，以排除"通过降低最优组而非提升最差组"造成的虚假差异缩小。

Result: 量子分类器可取得具竞争力的总体性能，但仍表现出亚组差异。按非劣性准则，若干亚组差异最小的配置（尤以DDI上的QCNN+GRL为代表）因整体判别力损失而不可临床接受。DDI上QSVM+GRL+EO在性能/公平权衡上最佳并接近PanDerm；CheXpert上QCNN+GRL总体性能最高但差异增大，EVA-X仍更强。

Conclusion: 公平性效应依赖于模型与数据集，QML需要亚组感知的实证评估，不能假定其内在公平；仅以亚组差距缩小衡量公平性可能产生误导。所有量子模型均在经典模拟下运行，作者不主张量子优势。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning (QML) is increasingly explored for medical classification, yet its behaviour under subgroup-aware fairness criteria remains understudied. We present an empirical fairness audit of classical and quantum classifiers for medical imaging, assuming QML is not inherently fair. Two scenarios are evaluated: dermatological lesions on DDI (sensitive attribute: Fitzpatrick skin type) and chest X-rays on CheXpert-derived data (sensitive attribute: age group). Using ResNet-18 embeddings, we train SVM, MLP, QSVM and QCNN under a baseline and three mitigation settings: differentiable Equalized Odds (EO) regularisation, Gradient Reversal Learning (GRL), and both combined. Foundation models (PanDerm, EVA-X) serve as external references. Quantum classifiers may achieve competitive aggregate performance while still exhibiting subgroup disparities. Since a smaller subgroup AUC gap may stem from degrading the best group rather than improving the worst, each mitigation is also tested against a pre-specified non-inferiority margin relative to its baseline. Under this criterion, several configurations with the smallest disparity, notably QCNN with GRL on DDI, are not clinically acceptable due to loss of global discrimination. On DDI, QSVM with GRL+EO performed best and approached PanDerm in the performance/fairness trade-off; on CheXpert, QCNN with GRL achieved the highest aggregate performance but increased disparity, and EVA-X remained stronger. Fairness effects are model- and dataset-dependent, so QML requires empirical subgroup-aware evaluation. All quantum models run in classical simulation; we claim no quantum advantage.

</details>


### [199] [Learning Without Clocks: Partition-Invariant Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37823)
*Han Wang,Jingyuan Bao,Juntao Wu,Saiao Zhou,Yuman Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究事件驱动传感器（事件相机、脉冲耳蜗）中"时间切片不变性"问题，提出事件更新两两对易是输出与同时事件处理顺序无关、且对时间戳连续的充要条件；由此定义一类时间介导非对易（TM）模型，构造最小TM层——可学习的HR相关器推广，并实例化为线性复杂度的Clock-Free Event（CFE）层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数学习系统先把异步事件分帧/打包，输出依赖于流的切分方式；而事件间以闭式演化的逐事件更新网络天然具有切片不变性。作者进一步追问：还需要什么条件才能（i）不受同时事件处理顺序影响、（ii）对时间戳连续（即使两事件时间交叉），同时（iii）仍对时序顺序敏感。

Method: 对任意非线性事件模型证明：(i)与(ii)各自等价于事件更新的两两对易；一旦对易，所有顺序依赖来自更新与事件间动力学的非对易，且随时间滞后线性消失，从而给出分辨率鲁棒性界。据此构造最小TM层（HR相关器的可学习Lipschitz推广），证明单个加性层无法一致表示有序对交互而三维TM状态可以；每层TM比加性层多两级时间嵌套；给出高阶构造TM-k与保持三性质的堆叠规则；实例化为事件数线性的CFE层，并给出无拓扑时序点过程变体、封闭形式似然，以及预注册的时钟鲁棒性协议与物理时间威胁模型。

Result: 理论：给出对易性充要刻画、分辨率鲁棒性界、加性层不可表示性与三性质保持的堆叠规则。实验（初步开发集）：TM状态较同等加性状态在DVS128 Gesture上提升3.5–9.1个点，在SHD上提升24.5个点，在Speech Commands上提升30.6个点。

Conclusion: 事件更新的对易性是实现顺序无关与时间戳连续的关键结构性条件，时间介导的非对易状态可在保持时序敏感性的同时提供分辨率鲁棒性；CFE层以线性复杂度实现该设计，并在初步实验中显著优于加性基线，同时配套预注册的时钟鲁棒性评估协议。

Abstract: Event cameras, spiking cochleas and similar sensors emit asynchronous events, yet most learning systems first bin them into frames or packets, so the output depends on how the stream was sliced. Networks whose state is updated exactly at every event and evolves in closed form between events are invariant to this slicing. We ask what more makes the output (i) independent of the processing order of simultaneous events and (ii) continuous in the time stamps, even when two events cross in time, while (iii) staying sensitive to temporal order. For arbitrary nonlinear event models we prove that (i) and (ii) are each equivalent to pairwise commutation of the event updates, and that once updates commute, all order dependence comes from their non-commutation with the inter-event dynamics and vanishes linearly in the time lag. This identifies a class of time-mediated non-commutative (TM) models and yields a resolution-robustness bound for any model. We construct the minimal TM layer, a learnable, provably Lipschitz generalization of the Hassenstein-Reichardt correlator; prove that no single additive layer can uniformly represent ordered pair interactions, whereas a three-dimensional TM state can; show that each TM layer adds two levels of temporal nesting versus one for additive layers; and give a single-layer higher-order construction (TM-k) and a stacking rule preserving all three properties. We instantiate these results as the Clock-Free Event (CFE) layer, linear in the number of events, give a topology-free variant for temporal point processes with closed-form likelihoods, and specify a preregistered clock-robustness protocol with a physical-time threat model for spike-retiming attacks. In preliminary development-set experiments, the TM state beats an otherwise identical additive state by 3.5-9.1 points on DVS128 Gesture and by 24.5 and 30.6 points on SHD and Speech Commands.

</details>


### [200] [Learning Lotteries with Minimal Stakeholder Violations from Membership Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37828)
*Nicholas Teh,Paul W. Goldberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将"A/B测试式混发"形式化为在 m 个候选系统上选择彩票、面向 n 个仅有二元成员查询的未知半空间代理人、最小化拒绝代理人数的问题，给出 APX-困难性与多组精确/近似算法及下界，并用合成实验说明最坏情况界偏保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者常可部署多个系统版本的随机混合（如 A/B 测试），而每个利益相关方（安全团队、监管方、私有测试数据的客户）只在其期望性能（如有害回复率）满足自身标准时接受该混合，但该标准只能通过"该混合是否可接受"的二元询问获知。Choo 等（ICML 2026）研究的是让所有代理人都接受的彩票，但当安全与有用性要求冲突时可能不存在这样的彩票，因此需要转向最小化拒绝者人数这一更现实的目标。

Method: 1) 模型：在 m 个备选上选彩票，n 个代理人各自接受一个未知半空间内的彩票，仅回答二元成员查询（接受/拒绝）。2) 复杂度：归约证明目标问题的 APX-困难性，即便所有半空间已知、效用均为 0/1。3) 精确算法：m 为常数时多项式时间；存在某最优彩票使任何接受者距其阈值至少 η（η 为常数）时，拟多项式时间。4) 部分代理可查询时：对代理人的均匀样本做优化，得到对总体的加性与相对保证，样本量不依赖 n；线性规划给出任意 m 下的多项式时间方法。5) 确定性下界：m≥3 时仅查询 K 个不同代理人的确定性算法无法保证少于 n−K 的额外拒绝数，且该界可达。

Result: APX-困难性（即使半空间已知、效用为 0/1）；m 常数时的多项式精确算法与间隙条件下的拟多项式精确算法；依赖 n 无关样本量的加性/相对泛化保证及任意 m 的 LP 多项式算法；m≥3 时确定性仅查询 K 个代理人至少产生 n−K 额外拒绝的下界且可达（即对未询问者无任何保证）；合成实验显示最坏情况界在随机实例上偏保守。

Conclusion: 最小化拒绝代理人的彩票选择在一般情况下是 APX-难的，但在 m 小或最优解带阈值间隙时可由精确算法高效求解；部分可查询设定下可用不依赖 n 的样本获得总体保证、或用 LP 处理任意 m；而查询预算受限的确定性算法存在 n−K 的强下界，说明覆盖"未被询问的代理人"本质上需要随机化或额外假设。

Abstract: An AI developer often has several candidate versions of a system and can deploy a mixture of them, sending each request to a randomly chosen version, as in A/B testing. Each stakeholder (e.g., a safety team, a regulator, or a customer with private test data) accepts a mixture exactly when its expected performance (e.g., its rate of harmful replies) meets the stakeholder's own standard, which the developer can learn only by asking whether a proposed mixture is acceptable. We model this as choosing a lottery over $m$ alternatives for $n$ agents, each of whom accepts exactly the lotteries in an unknown halfspace and answers such binary membership queries. While Choo et al. (ICML 2026) sought a lottery that every agent accepts, none may exist when, say, safety and helpfulness requirements conflict, so we minimize the number of rejecting agents. We show that this problem is APX-hard even when all halfspaces are known and all utilities are $0$ or $1$. We then give exact algorithms that run in polynomial time when $m$ is constant, and in quasi-polynomial time when, for a constant $η$, some optimal lottery has no accepting agent within $η$ of its threshold. When only some agents can be queried, optimizing over a uniform sample of agents gives additive and relative guarantees for the whole population, with a sample size that does not depend on $n$, and a linear program gives a polynomial-time method for any $m$. On the other hand, for $m\ge 3$, a deterministic algorithm that queries only $K$ distinct agents cannot guarantee fewer than $n-K$ extra rejections, and this is achievable; in other words, it can guarantee nothing for the agents it does not ask. Small synthetic experiments indicate that our worst-case bounds are conservative on random instances.

</details>


### [201] [Depth through Recurrence: Towards Ultra-Efficient On-Device ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37833)
*Chen Feng,Tianyi Xu,Ramchalam Kinattinkara Ramakrishnan,Yicheng Lin,Shaojie Zhuo,Zhaocong Yuan,Chenzheng Su,Xiaopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出块循环（block-recurrent）ASR架构，用少量权重共享的循环块替代参数化深度，在参数量减少一个数量级的情况下保持大型ASR模型的准确率，并支持高效端侧个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ASR系统靠加深加深模型深度、扩大容量提升准确率，但内存、能耗与计算开销大；在手表、眼镜等资源极度受限的边缘设备上无法部署。核心问题是：能否在不扩大参数量的前提下获得高表征能力。

Method: 重新审视深度的作用，发现"逐层表征动态"现象——多数层学习功能相似的变换；据此用一组由权重共享层构成的循环块替代参数化深度，保留有效深度但大幅压缩模型；结合表征引导的分组与知识蒸馏；紧凑骨干上再用轻量MoE-LoRA实现设备端个性化。

Result: 仅用两个共享块的模型在Open ASR Leaderboard基准上平均恢复大型模型97.4%的准确率；MoE-LoRA个性化可用户特定模型达到或超过通用ASR系统准确率且推理成本显著更低；跨循环步复用权重降低内存访问开销与推理能耗。

Conclusion: 无需大参数量即可实现高质量ASR；结构化循环为边缘端超压缩、高能效、个性化的语音识别提供了有原则的路径。

Abstract: Modern automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems have achieved remarkable accuracy by scaling model depth and capacity, but at the cost of increased memory, energy, and computational demands. On edge devices where ASR is often most needed, such as watches and glasses, deploying such large models is infeasible due to extremely constrained resources. This raises a fundamental question: can we achieve high representational power in deep ASR models without scaling up parameterization? In this work, we revisit the role of depth and identify layer wise representational dynamics, in which most layers learn functionally similar transformations. Motivated by this insight, we propose a block-recurrent ASR architecture that replaces parameterized depth with a small set of recurrent blocks, each consisting of weight-shared layers, thereby preserving effective depth while drastically reducing model size. Through representation-guided grouping and knowledge distillation, block-recurrent models retain the accuracy of large reference models while using an order of magnitude fewer parameters. Across Open ASR Leaderboard benchmarks, a model with only two shared blocks recovers 97.4% of the accuracy of large models on average. The compact backbone further enables efficient on-device personalization through lightweight MoE-LoRA adaptation, allowing user-specific models to match or surpass the accuracy of general-purpose ASR systems with significantly lower inference cost. By reusing weights across recurrent steps, the proposed architecture reduces memory-access overhead and inference energy consumption. Together, these results show that high quality ASR can be achieved without large parameterization, and that structured recurrence provides a principled path towards ultra-compressed, energy-efficient, and personalized speech recognition on the edge.

</details>


### [202] [Improving Data-Constrained Scaling with Hyperparameter Transfer and Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37842)
*Ethan Baron,Shmuel Berman,Anirudh Satheesh,Akshay Vegesna,Samip Dahal,Andrew Gordon Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对数据受限语言模型训练，提出随规模迁移超参数（含权重衰减）的幂律规律，并结合集成（ensembling）的算力分配缩放律，在FineWeb 1亿token上验证损失降低逾0.15 nats（困惑度下降14%），且在小模型拟合、最大12倍算力外推时均单调改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算力预算增长快于高质量训练数据增长，数据受限缩放成为必需；但既有方案存在两点缺陷：一是将权重衰减等关键超参固定不随规模调整，而最优超参随模型规模与训练epoch数变化剧烈；二是忽略集成这一数据受限下的重要扩展维度。其结果是先前配方的损失可能随算力预算增加反而上升。

Method: 同时解决两处缺陷：(1) 拟合幂律，刻画超参数（如权重衰减）随规模的迁移规律；(2) 拟合包含集成的缩放律，规定如何分配算力（模型规模、数据量、训练epoch与集成规模的权衡）。配方仅在较小规模运行上拟合。

Result: 在FineWeb的1亿token上训练，相比先前配方验证损失降低超过0.15 nats，困惑度下降14%；通过集成实现随算力单调改善。增益配方由小规模run拟合，并在最高12倍算力预算下完成验证。

Conclusion: 数据受限场景下，超参数随规模迁移的幂律与含集成的算力分配缩放律能够修正既有配方中损失随算力上升的反常现象，并获得稳定且可外推的单调增益。
