Abstract: As growing compute budgets outpace the growth of high-quality training data, large language models must be trained with both compute and data constraints in mind. Unfortunately, existing prescriptions for data-constrained scaling suffer from two important limitations. First, past works hold key hyperparameters such as weight decay fixed with scale, despite the fact that optimal hyperparameters vary wildly with model size and number of epochs. Second, past works neglect ensembling, a promising axis for scaling under data constraints. As a surprising consequence, prior prescriptions can lead to loss that increases with compute budget. We address both limitations by fitting power laws that prescribe how to transfer hyperparameters with scale and scaling laws that prescribe how to allocate compute with ensembling. Training on 100M tokens from FineWeb, our recipe reduces validation loss by over 0.15 nats compared to prior recipes, a 14% decrease in perplexity, and achieves monotonic improvements with compute through ensembling. These gains are achieved using prescriptions fit to smaller runs and validated at up to 12$\times$ larger compute budgets.

</details>


### [203] [Interpretable neural equation learning for high-dimensional materials data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37880)
*Philip Jacobson,Prasad Iyer,Remi Dingreville,Saaketh Desai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 nn-EQL，一个通过在带有可定制神经元级激活函数的神经网络上迭代训练、剪枝与再训练来生成候选方程的方程学习框架；用受 MDL 启发的评分（平衡误差与稀疏性）对候选排序，并对项贡献、输入缩放敏感性和不确定性进行诊断；在 AI-Feynman 基准及高维材料数据集（多相材料有限元模拟、高通量薄膜沉积实验）上完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方程学习方法多停留在中等维度、较干净的回归问题上，缺乏在稀疏、高维材料数据集中"发现并解释"所学方程的工作流；同时需要兼顾可解释性与稀疏性筛选，并能诊断各输入项的作用与不确定性。

Method: nn-EQL 框架：1）构建带用户可定制、神经元级激活函数的神经网络；2）通过迭代训练—剪枝—再训练生成候选方程；3）采用受最小描述长度（MDL）启发的评分对候选方程排序，权衡拟合误差与稀疏性；4）对学得方程做诊断分析，包括逐项贡献、输入缩放敏感性及不确定性；5）对表格与高维数据借助标准编码器模型接入，实现端到端方程学习。

Result: 在标准 AI-Feynman 基准（中等维度干净回归）以及多尺度高维材料数据集（异质材料有限元模拟、高通量薄膜沉积实验数据）上展示了 nn-EQL 的有效性；框架能借助标准编码器处理高维输入并学得方程，同时提供对学得项的洞察。

Conclusion: nn-EQL 将方程学习扩展到稀疏、高维材料数据场景，兼具稀疏性与可解释性诊断能力；作者展望，未来结合物理可解释表征、多模态与多保真度数据以及自适应数据采集，有望使方程学习用于提出并检验机理假设。

Abstract: Equation learning seeks to discover compact, interpretable equations from data using various machine-learning techniques. However, few workflows discover and interpret learned equations in sparse, high-dimensional materials datasets. We present nn-EQL, an equation-learning framework that generates candidate equations through iterative training, pruning, and retraining of a neural network with customizable, user-specified neuron-level activation functions. Candidate equations are ranked using a minimum description length (MDL)-inspired score, balancing error and sparsity, and diagnosed to assess term-wise contributions, input-scaled sensitivities, and uncertainties. We demonstrate nn-EQL on standard AI-Feynman benchmarks as well as multiscale high-dimensional materials datasets such as finite-element simulations of heterogeneous materials, and high-throughput experimental thin-film deposition data. nn-EQL integrates with tabular data and high-dimensional data via standard encoder models, learning equations and providing insight into learned terms. Looking forward, integrating physically interpretable representations, multimodal and multi-fidelity data, and adaptive data acquisition could enable equation learning to formulate and test mechanistic hypotheses.

</details>


### [204] [SAC-GCL: Structure-Aware Compression for Graph Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37898)
*Muhammad Ifte khairul Islam,Tanvir Hossain,Esra Akbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAC-GCL：用结构分解与图压缩（凝聚子图→超节点）生成视图，替代随机扰动，提升图分类的鲁棒性与判别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GCL的视图生成依赖随机增广（边扰动、节点掩码），缺乏结构感知，易破坏关键拓扑或引入语义不一致视图；近期自适应方法仅在局部节点/边邻域操作，无法捕捉对图分类至关重要的高阶、宏观结构组织。

Method: 提出结构感知压缩框架SAC-GCL：采用结构分解提取凝聚子图并压缩为超节点，构建保持局部稠密度、捕捉高层结构模式并降低噪声与冗余的粗化图视图；使用多种凝聚子图模型（如k-core、k-truss类）得到互补视图；采用分层视图编码器，以共享权重的凝聚子图嵌入初始化超节点特征，实现多分辨率信息融合。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，结构感知压缩可作为随机扰动的有力替代，为图分类任务生成更鲁棒、更具判别性的图表示。

Conclusion: 以结构分解与压缩为核心的视图生成策略优于随机增广与局部自适应方法，验证了宏观结构信息在GCL中的价值；摘要未给出具体精度提升数值，需结合全文实验表进一步核验。

Abstract: Graph Contrastive Learning (GCL) has achieved remarkable success in self-supervised representation learning by maximizing agreement between augmented views of a graph. Although augmented view generation is the central step in GCL, existing models rely on stochastic augmentation of the input graph, such as edge perturbation and node masking, which lack structural awareness and frequently destroy critical topology or introduce semantically inconsistent views. While recent adaptive methods improve upon random perturbations, they primarily operate on localized node or edge neighborhoods and fail to capture higher-order, macro-level structural organization crucial for graph classification. To address this limitation, we propose Structure-aware Compression for Graph Contrastive Learning (SAC-GCL), a novel framework that employs structural decomposition and graph compression as a principled view generation strategy. Moving beyond random augmentations, SAC-GCL leverages structural decomposition techniques to extract cohesive subgraphs and condense them into supernodes, constructing coarsened graph views that preserve local density and capture higher-level structural patterns while reducing noise and redundancy. We leverage multiple cohesive subgraph models to derive complementary views of the graph, each capturing distinct structural properties. To capture multi-resolution information, a hierarchical view encoder processes these compressed views by initializing super-node features with shared-weight cohesive subgraph embeddings. Our results from extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that structure-aware compression provides a powerful alternative to random perturbations, yielding more robust and discriminative graph representations for graph classification tasks.

</details>


### [205] [Think Twice: The Metis-1.6 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37903)
*Giulianno Vollmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Metis-1.6 报告了两个从零训练的语言模型 Praxis（2.94B/0.46B 激活）与 Logos（10.23B/1.18B 激活），采用 Mamba-2 与注意力混合的 MoE 堆叠并每 token 计算两次；各自训练 1.02T tokens。Logos 在 40 项后训练基准上平均 52.93，处于数据量与激活参数的 Pareto 前沿，数学能力强但代码、指令跟随和长上下文较弱。报告核心价值在于失败复盘与 MoRE 消融。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者主张"更好的数据、训练方法与新架构可以买到更大参数量买不到的效果，且成本仅为零头"，Metis 是对该假设的下注，Metis-1.6 是这一路线的最新一步，目标并非挑战前沿模型，而是验证在极小训练数据与极少激活参数下的效率前沿。

Method: 单研究者从零构建两个模型：Praxis（2.94B 总参/0.46B 激活）与 Logos（10.23B/1.18B 激活）。架构为 Mamba-2 与注意力混合的 MoE 堆叠，对每个 token 执行两次（"再看一眼"）。训练总量各 1.02T tokens：804.76B 预训练、18B 上下文扩展至 131,072 tokens、200B 有监督微调。另包含一个 1.77B 参数的 n-gram 记忆模块实验，以及支撑 MoRE（递归专家混合）的 10B-token 消融实验。

Result: 40 项后训练基准上 Logos 平均 52.93，在 17 个对比模型中排第 7，与 Qwen3-4B 相差不到 1 分；其训练数据约为披露数据的对比模型的中位数数据的 1/12，且每 token 激活参数少于任何优于它的模型，因而在数据量与激活参数两维上均处于 Pareto 前沿。数学能力突出：30 个模型中排第 2，AIME 2025 得 67.50%，HMMT February 2026 得 63.64%。代码、指令跟随与长上下文为弱项。失败发现包括：预设优化器从未生效，整个预训练期间注意力部分用 AdamW 训练；1.77B 参数的 n-gram 记忆学会自我关闭；五项微调选择损害了能力；组均值归一化下的长度奖励在组通过率超过 2/3 后惩罚正确但冗长的解，作者证明该符号翻转并用一行 clamp 修复。

Conclusion: Metis-1.6 表明在极小数据与低激活参数预算下可达效率前沿并获得较强数学能力，但不具备全面通用性；报告的主要价值在于对训练故障、微调取舍与 reward 设计缺陷的坦诚复盘，这些经验直接指导了 Metis-1.7 的循环式 MoRE 架构。

Abstract: Metis is a bet that better data, better training methods and new architectures can buy what a bigger parameter count cannot, at a fraction of the cost. Metis-1.6 is its latest step: two language models built from scratch by a single researcher, Praxis (2.94B parameters, 0.46B active per pass) and Logos (10.23B parameters, 1.18B active per pass). Both run a hybrid Mamba-2 and attention mixture-of-experts stack twice for every token, so each token gets a second look. Each was trained on 1.02T tokens in total: 804.76B of pretraining, 18B of context extension to 131,072 tokens, and 200B of supervised fine-tuning. Metis-1.6 was never meant to challenge frontier models, and across 40 post-trained benchmarks it mostly does not: Logos averages 52.93, seventh of 17 models in its comparison set and within a point of Qwen3-4B. But it gets there on roughly a twelfth of the median training data of the comparators that disclose it, and with fewer active parameters per token than any model that beats it, which puts it on the Pareto frontier for both. In mathematics it ranks second of 30 models, with 67.50% on AIME 2025 and 63.64% on HMMT February 2026. It is weak at code, instruction following and long context. The rest of the report is the part I found most valuable: what went wrong. The optimizer I configured never ran, and attention trained with AdamW for all of pretraining. A 1.77B-parameter n-gram memory learned to switch itself off. Five fine-tuning choices cost capability. And a length reward under group-mean normalization punished correct solutions for being long once a group's pass rate passed 2/3, a sign inversion I prove and fix with a one-line clamp. I close with the 10B-token ablations behind MoRE (Mixture of Recursive Experts), the looped architecture of Metis-1.7.

</details>


### [206] [TERRA: Task-Oriented Edge AI for Regional Fire Detection with Identity-Free Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37909)
*Mohammad Kazemi,Julie A. McCann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TERRA 是一种面向区域火情识别的边缘 AI 框架，结合任务导向本地推理、无标识区域标签上报与置换不变区域证据聚合，提供 TERRA-Score（6 位量化概率）与 TERRA-Feature（24 位学习表示）两种上报模式，在 EN54 数据集上以 6~24 位负载即可达到 AUPRC 0.987~0.990、FAR≤1% 时 MDR 1.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式火情监测系统中大量传感设备共享上行链路，但运维目标通常是判定哪些区域存在火情证据，而非恢复每个设备的具体测量值；传统逐设备数据回传方式在带宽与鲁棒性上效率不高，因此需要任务导向的上报与聚合机制。

Method: 提出 TERRA 框架：设备端执行任务导向本地推理，采用去除设备标识、仅携带区域标签的上报格式，接收端进行置换不变的区域证据聚合。两种模式：TERRA-Score 上报量化后的本地火情概率；TERRA-Feature 上报紧凑学习表示，接收端用置换不变的 DeepSets 模型聚合。以 EN54 分布式室内火灾报警数据集全部 15 种数值传感模态为概念验证平台，针对回合级火灾场景目标，采用重复不相交三折交叉验证，考察表示维度、任务负载量化、上报可得性以及对消息丢失与虚假恢复消息的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 9 条上报且消息完美恢复条件下，TERRA-Feature 以 24 位任务负载取得平均 AUPRC 0.990，TERRA-Score 仅用 6 位取得 0.987；两者在 FAR≤1% 时 MDR 均为 1.81%。可靠恢复时 TERRA-Feature 的 AUPRC 略高，而更紧凑的 TERRA-Score 对虚假消息污染的敏感性更低。

Conclusion: 任务导向的边缘表示与免标识区域聚合可在极低负载下实现高精度区域火情推断；表示精度与传输鲁棒性之间存在权衡，紧凑评分型上报更适合存在虚假消息污染的场景，特征型上报更适合恢复可靠、追求更高精度的场景。

Abstract: Distributed fire-monitoring systems may involve many sensing devices reporting over a shared uplink, even though the operational objective is often to identify regions exhibiting evidence of fire rather than to recover device-specific measurements. We introduce TERRA (Task-Oriented Edge Representation and Regional Aggregation), an edge-AI framework that combines task-oriented local inference, identity-free region-tagged reporting, and permutation-invariant regional evidence aggregation. TERRA supports two reporting modes: TERRA-Score, in which each device communicates a quantized local fire probability, and TERRA-Feature, in which it communicates a compact learned representation for receiver-side aggregation using a permutation-invariant DeepSets model. Using all 15 numerical sensing modalities of the distributed indoor EN54 fire-alarm dataset as a proof-of-concept testbed, we evaluate regional inference for an episode-level fire-scenario target under repetition-disjoint three-fold cross-validation. We study representation size, task-payload quantization, report availability, and robustness to missed and false recovered messages. With nine reports and perfect message recovery, TERRA-Feature achieves mean AUPRC 0.990 using a 24-bit task payload, while TERRA-Score achieves 0.987 using only 6 bits; both attain an MDR of 1.81% at FAR $\leq$1%. TERRA-Feature achieves slightly higher AUPRC under reliable recovery, whereas the more compact TERRA-Score is less sensitive to increasing false-message contamination.

</details>


### [207] [Generative modeling for fusion: a large-scale benchmark of stellarator fast-ion simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37910)
*Misha Padidar,Teresa Huang,Mark Goldstein,Michael Czekanski,Jeffrey Soules*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布BoSS数据集：18万个仿星器MHD平衡配置及其导心模拟结果与粒子轨迹，并给出生成建模与代理建模两类基线任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿星器设计依赖高保真仿真评估性能，导心模拟得到的关键指标计算昂贵，难以纳入优化循环，通常仅作诊断使用；而机器学习需要能同时覆盖设计多样性与高保真输出的规模化数据集。

Method: 构建Benchmark of Stellarator Simulations（BoSS）数据集，将仿星器设计与导心仿真结果及轨迹配对，覆盖广泛的磁几何与约束性能；在此基础上定义并评估两类任务：面向良好粒子约束的生成式设计建模，以及预测热沉积模式与粒子轨迹的代理模型。

Result: BoSS为首个大规模导心模拟数据集，包含180,000个MHD平衡的仿真数据；基线模型能生成多样且性能良好的仿星器，并提供有用的代理预测。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了ML加速仿星器设计的潜力，并指出长时段轨迹预测与分布外生成是未来关键方向；数据集通过 https://boss.flatironinstitute.org/ 开放。

Abstract: Stellarators offer a promising magnetic confinement approach for achieving controlled nuclear fusion. However, stellarator design remains challenging, as evaluating configuration performance requires computationally expensive, high-fidelity simulations. In particular, key performance metrics derived from guiding center simulations are costly to include directly in the design optimization loop, and are almost exclusively used as diagnostics. Machine learning has the potential to advance fusion research by enabling rapid generation and evaluation of new stellarator configurations. Effectively applying ML for fusion demands large-scale datasets that capture both design diversity and high-fidelity simulation outputs. In this work, we introduce the Benchmark of Stellarator Simulations (BoSS), a dataset of stellarator designs paired with guiding center simulation results and trajectories. The BoSS is the first large-scale dataset of guiding center simulations, containing simulation data for 180,000 distinct magnetohydrodynamic equilibria spanning a wide range of magnetic geometries and confinement properties. To exemplify the utility of ML to accelerate stellarator design, we formulate and address two sets of modeling tasks: generative modeling for designing stellarators with good particle confinement, and surrogate modeling for predicting stellarator heat deposition patterns and particle trajectories. The tasks reveal the potential impact ML can have in the stellarator field and important opportunities to improve the applicability of ML models in the sciences. Our baselines generate diverse, performant stellarators and provide useful surrogate predictions, and point to long-horizon trajectory forecasting and out-of-distribution generation as key directions for future work. The BoSS can be accessed through https://boss.flatironinstitute.org/.

</details>


### [208] [Muon Makes the First Mixture-of-Experts Layer Harder to Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37914)
*Marcos Hernanz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在55M与108M激活参数MoE模型中，论文测量了优化器对专家路由偏置"跨度"的影响：Muon（而非AdamW）作为网络其余部分优化器时，会使第一层MoE偏置跨度平均约翻倍，导致最忙专家负载达公平份额的4–7倍；对第一路由器输入做去均值可消除过载而几乎不损loss，小模型上降低Muon学习率亦可缓解，而单独将路由器换为AdamW反而略增loss。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源MoE语言模型多用Muon而非AdamW训练，负载均衡依靠每步调整的专家路由分偏置实现；但各配方对"路由器是否也由Muon训练"意见不一，缺乏对优化器如何影响负载均衡难度的系统测量。

Method: 定义层内路由偏置的最大值与最小值之差为"span"作为层均衡难度的指标；在55M和108M激活参数模型上，于短程调优得到的学习率下，跨多个随机种子比较Muon与AdamW配置，分别控制"路由器优化器"与"网络其余部分优化器"，并分析第一路由器输入的共享方向、warmup后专家负载分布，以及输入去均值、降低Muon学习率、仅路由器换AdamW三种干预的效果。

Result: Muon在所有种子下均抬高第一层MoE的span，平均约翻倍，其他层变化很小；该上升源于网络其余部分使用Muon，与路由器优化器无关。此时第一路由器输入共享同一方向，warmup后不久最忙专家平均承担4–7倍公平份额。前3000步内对输入减去batch均值可消除过载且几乎不改变loss；小模型上将Muon学习率减半可消除大部分上升并略改进loss；仅把路由器换成AdamW会使负载在warmup期间波动更大并略增loss。

Conclusion: 优化器选择显著影响MoE路由负载均衡：Muon训练主干会通过输入共同方向加剧首层路由偏置跨度与专家过载。在该规模下，结果支持继续用Muon训练路由器，并提示可通过输入去均值或调低Muon学习率来缓解首层均衡问题。

Abstract: Many open mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models train with Muon instead of AdamW. Most balance the load with a bias on each expert's routing score, adjusted after every step. Their recipes disagree on whether Muon should also train the router. In models with 55M and 108M active parameters, we measure how the optimizer changes the span of these biases, the largest minus the smallest in a layer. The span grows when all tokens favor the same experts, so it shows how hard a layer is to balance. At learning rates tuned on shorter runs, Muon raised the span of the first MoE layer at every seed, roughly doubling it on average, while the other layers changed much less. The rise came from Muon on the rest of the network, whichever optimizer trained the router. In those runs, the inputs to the first router shared a direction, and soon after the warmup its busiest expert averaged four to seven times its fair share. Over the first 3,000 steps, subtracting the batch mean from these inputs removed this overload while barely changing the loss. On the smaller model, halving Muon's learning rate removed most of the rise and slightly improved the loss. Moving only the router to AdamW made the loads shift more during the warmup and slightly raised the loss, so at this scale the results favor keeping the router on Muon.

</details>


### [209] [Bi-Phase Training: Learning Efficiently From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37934)
*Abel Gurung,Joseph Campbell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bi-Phase Training (BPT)，一种参数高效的预训练方法：通过可训练对角矩阵与低秩适配器组合构成高秩左右变换来参数化权重更新，并周期性合并进冻结基座权重，从而在显著减少可训练参数的同时保持全参数优化的高秩学习动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型预训练随规模增长计算代价高昂，需要参数高效的预训练方法；但现有低秩类方法（如LoRA）受限秩瓶颈，难以复现全参数优化中的高秩学习动态。

Method: BPT将每个权重更新参数化为约束性高秩左/右变换的组合，由可训练对角矩阵和低秩适配器构成；适配器更新周期性合并（merge）进冻结的基座权重，使可及方向在训练过程中实现复合式扩张。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu（10B与100B tokens）上从零预训练Qwen2.5（100M–1.5B）与OLMo-2-1B：验证困惑度接近全参数基线而可训练参数远少；1.5B规模下可训练参数减少66%，验证困惑度仅有小幅差距；多下游任务评估显示保留大部分全参数模型的下游性能。

Conclusion: BPT在预训练阶段以显著更低的参数开销逼近甚至基本匹配全参数预训练的效果，兼顾高秩学习动态与参数效率，为大规模预训练提供可行替代方案。

Abstract: Pre-training foundational models is computationally expensive as models scale. In this work, we introduce Bi-Phase Training (BPT), a parameter-efficient pre-training method that preserves the high-rank learning dynamics observed in full-parameter optimization with substantially reduced trainable parameters. BPT parameterizes each weight update as a composition of constrained high-rank left/right transformations via trainable diagonal matrices and low-rank adapters whose updates are periodically merged into the frozen base weights, resulting in a compounding expansion of accessible directions during training. Across language-model pre-training from scratch (Qwen2.5 models from 100M to 1.5B parameters and OLMo-2-1B) on FineWeb-Edu (10B and 100B tokens), BPT achieves validation perplexity close to full-parameter baselines while using far fewer trainable parameters; e.g., at the 1.5B scale, BPT uses 66% fewer trainable parameters with only a small validation-perplexity gap. Additionally, a comprehensive evaluation across diverse downstream tasks shows that BPT preserves much of the full-parameterized model's downstream performance.

</details>


### [210] [Finite-Step Geometry of Marginal-Preserving Velocity Fields in Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37936)
*Ewsey Obzherin,Valentin Khrulkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究生成流（generative flows）在有限步数值离散下的非等价性：速度场不同但边际路径相同，经离散化后行为却不同。作者在固定边际路径与数值方法的前提下，用投影修正方程刻画改变边际律的局部积分误差分量，并针对仿射固定形状分布给出精确有限步可达性、谱障碍与尖锐的最近可达Wasserstein误差；对扩散概率流ODE构造保持精确边际路径的skew-score扰动并量化其在分数误差下的泄漏。高斯与混合实验验证了所预测的阶数、可达性与障碍现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成流中多个速度场可诱导相同的连续边际演化，但经数值离散化后产生不同结果；现有理论多关注连续时间等价性，缺乏对有限步离散下非等价性的系统刻画。作者旨在固定边际路径与数值方法，理解离散化如何破坏等价性、误差如何影响边际分布，以及哪些边际分布实际可达。

Method: 1）提出"投影修正方程"（projected modified equations），分离出局部积分误差中改变边际律的分量，并给出边际收敛阶提升的充分条件；2）针对仿射固定形状分布，刻画精确有限步可达集，推导谱障碍（spectral obstructions）并给出尖锐的最近可达Wasserstein误差；3）对扩散概率流ODE，构造skew-score扰动，在精确分数下保持边际路径不变，并量化分数误差下的泄漏（leakage）。

Result: 投影修正方程能够识别改变边际律的误差分量，并给出高阶边际收敛的充分条件；仿射情形下得到有限步可达性的精确刻画、谱障碍及最近可达Wasserstein误差的尖锐界；skew-score扰动在精确分数下保持边际路径，且其泄漏量可被显式量化。受控高斯与混合分布实验验证了所预测的收敛阶、可达性与障碍现象。

Conclusion: 有限步离散下生成流的等价性会失效，同一边际路径的不同速度场在离散后行为显著不同；通过投影修正方程、谱分析与skew-score扰动，可在固定边际路径与数值方法下系统刻画这种非等价性，并为离散化设计提供理论指导。

Abstract: Generative flows can admit different velocity fields that induce the same continuous marginal evolution but behave differently after numerical discretization. We study this finite-step non-equivalence while keeping the marginal path and numerical method fixed. Projected modified equations characterize the component of local integration error that changes the marginal law and give sufficient conditions for higher marginal convergence order. For affine fixed-shape distributions, we characterize exact finite-step reachability and derive spectral obstructions and sharp nearest-reachable Wasserstein errors. For diffusion probability-flow ODEs, we construct skew-score perturbations that preserve the exact marginal path under exact scores and quantify their leakage under score error. Controlled Gaussian and mixture experiments recover the predicted order, reachability, and obstruction phenomena.

</details>


### [211] [SAG-Sep: Sparse Augmented Graphs and Onion-Guided Search for Rounded Capacity Cut Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37952)
*Haoran Liu,Guanyi Wang,Yu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SAG-Sep 分离器：用单次前向的稀疏增强图编码 LP 解并对节点打分，配合免训练解码器 Onion-Sweep 生成嵌套 RCC 候选，在 N=1000 实例上把平均根间隙相对最强 NeuralSEP 基线降低 17.7%，同时神经网络分离每轮约提速 30 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCC（取整容量割）对 CVRP 松弛效果好，但精确分离在大规模下计算上不可行；现有启发式与神经分离器虽快，却难以在有限分离预算内产出足够多的高效割。此外 NeuralSEP 类方法依赖迭代式 predict-and-coarsen 推理，开销大。

Method: 1) 将分数型 LP 解编码为稀疏增强图（SAG），加入由流信息引导的多跳边以刻画长程路径结构，避免注意力稠密化；2) 单次编码器前向即可输出节点在 RCC 诱导子集中的隶属分数（覆盖不同车辆数层级），省去迭代推理；3) 观察到高价值精确 RCC 通常呈嵌套的"洋葱式"子集族，且节点分数与嵌套深度相关，据此设计免训练解码器 Onion-Sweep：按分数排序客户，一次性向量化评估所有前缀的 RCC 违反度，输出每个副本最违反的前缀及跨副本的嵌套候选族，无需调阈值或探测带。

Result: 在 NeuralSEP 基准、N=1000 客户实例上，SAG-Sep+OS 相比最强 NeuralSEP 基线将平均根间隙降低 17.7%；单次前向的 SAG-Sep 分离器实现约 30 倍的每轮神经分离加速。

Conclusion: LP 感知的稀疏编码与高价值割的嵌套结构是神经分离中值得更广泛应用的结构性规律。

Abstract: Rounded capacity cuts (RCCs) are among the most effective cuts for capacitated vehicle-routing relaxations, but exact separation is computationally prohibitive at scale. Existing heuristic and neural separators can be fast, but often fail to produce sufficiently many effective cuts within practical separation budgets. We introduce SAG-Sep, a separator that scores node membership in RCC-inducing subsets across vehicle-count levels from a fractional LP solution in a single encoder forward pass, avoiding the iterative predict-and-coarsen inference used by NeuralSEP-style separators. SAG-Sep encodes the LP solution as a Sparse Augmented Graph (SAG), adding flow-informed multi-hop edges that expose long-range routing structure without densifying attention. Beyond LP-aware sparse encoding, we observe that high-value exact RCCs are typically nested, organizing into onion-like subset families, and SAG-Sep's node scores track subset nesting depth. This motivates Onion-Sweep (OS), a training-free decoder that ranks customers by their scores, evaluates the RCC violation of every prefix of the ranking in one vectorized pass, and emits the most violated prefix of each replica together with a cross-replica family of nested proposals, converting independent node scores into structurally coordinated RCC candidates without a tuned threshold or probing band. On the NeuralSEP benchmark with N=1000 customer instances, SAG-Sep with OS reduces the average root gap by 17.7% relative to the strongest NeuralSEP baseline, while the single-pass SAG-Sep separator achieves a roughly 30-fold per-iteration neural separation speedup. Our findings suggest that LP-aware sparse encoding and the nested structure of high-value cuts are structural patterns worth leveraging in neural separation more broadly.

</details>


### [212] [Self-Supervised Latent Action-Conditioned World Modeling for Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37963)
*Chia-Han Kuo,Chengshuai Zhao,Huan Liu,Lo Pang-Yun Ting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACTS-WM，一个自监督的、以潜在动作为条件的世界模型框架，用于时间序列表示学习。它先无监督地从时间序列迁移中推断潜在动作，再用 JEPA 编码器与动作条件世界模型预测目标表示，在 22 个数据集、4 类任务上优于预训练的 JEPA 编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 JEPA 的时间序列编码器只做潜空间预测，忽略了支配时间序列观测之间迁移的潜在因素（underlying factors）。作者假设：显式建模时间序列变化背后的信息能促进时间序列建模。

Method: 1) 潜在动作建模：无需显式动作标注，从时间序列的相邻观测迁移中推断潜在动作（latent actions）；2) 构建基于 JEPA 的编码器；3) 构建以潜在动作与任务信息为条件的世界模型，预测目标时间序列的表示；整体为自监督训练框架。

Result: 在来自多个领域的 22 个数据集、4 类代表性任务（单变量与多变量预测、分类）上进行评测，ACTS-WM 通过学习互补表示，在下游任务上优于预训练的 JEPA 编码器。

Conclusion: 显式建模时间序列变化背后的潜在动作可有效提升时间序列表示学习；潜在动作条件化的世界模型能与 JEPA 编码器形成互补表示，带来稳定的下游性能增益。

Abstract: Time-series data play an important role in various tasks, such as weather forecasting, web traffic analysis, and speech recognition. Recently, pioneering studies have increasingly adopted Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) based encoders to learn time-series representations through latent-space prediction. However, they often overlook the underlying factors governing transitions between time-series observations. In this work, we hypothesize that explicitly modeling the information behind changes in time series can facilitate time-series modeling. Building on this hypothesis, we propose ACTS-WM, a self-supervised latent action-conditioned world model framework for time-series analysis. First, we introduce a latent action modeling approach that infers the underlying information behind time-series transitions as latent actions, without requiring explicit action annotations. We then develop a JEPA-based encoder and an action-conditioned world model to predict target time-series representations conditioned on the learned latent actions and task information. We evaluate ACTS-WM across 4 representative tasks on 22 datasets from diverse domains, covering univariate and multivariate forecasting and classification. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate that ACTS-WM can improve downstream performance over pretrained JEPA-based encoders by learning complementary representations.

</details>


### [213] [Application of PDE emulators for a multi-material, multi-physics simulation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37972)
*Diane Oyen,Morgan Jones,Adithan Kathirgamaraju,Abu Bucker Siddik,Alexander Most,David Schodt,Christine Sweeney,Kyle Hickmann,Michael McCann,Jemma Stachelek,Alexius Wadell,Michal Kucer,Mahindra Rautela,Siddharth Mansingh,James Amarel,Gerd J. Kunde,Emily Casleton Taylor,Soumi De,Ayan Biswas,Aimee Hungerford,Nathan Debardeleben,Earl Lawrence*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文评估多种基于计算机视觉的代理模型架构在多材料计算物理任务上的表现，指出在多物理多材料数据集上，预训练模型（含Transformer类）适配存在显著实现困难，并强调稀疏不平衡数据学习、空间编码与物理一致性度量适配、降低训练数据生成成本的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习架构被用于快速替代数值模拟器以加速科学发现与工程设计，但现有验证多集中于流行的PDE基准，缺乏对更贴近工业与工程应用数据集的评估，因此需在更具挑战性的真实场景中检验其性能。

Method: 将多种基于计算机视觉的架构作为代理模型，在多材料、多物理的计算物理任务上训练与评估，并分析适配预训练模型（尤其是基于Transformer的架构）时遇到的实现挑战。

Result: 在多材料多物理数据集上，预训练模型（包括Transformer类架构）的适配存在显著实现困难；结果表明针对稀疏与不平衡数据设计的算法、空间编码与物理一致性度量的适配技术，以及降低充足训练数据生成成本的方法至关重要。

Conclusion: 面向工业级应用时，代理模型研究应优先关注稀疏/不平衡数据学习、空间编码与物理一致性度量适配，以及高效训练数据生成，而非仅依赖常见PDE基准上的表现。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) architectures are being developed for fast emulation of numerical simulators to accelerate both scientific discovery and engineering design. While these approaches are well-tested on popular PDE (partial differential equation) benchmarks, it is necessary to evaluate their performance on datasets that more accurately reflect industrial and engineering applications. We evaluate several computer vision-based architectures trained as surrogate models on a challenging computational physics task involving multiple materials, revealing significant implementation challenges for adapting certain pre-trained models, including transformer-based architectures. For such multi-physics multi-material datasets, our results underscore the importance of learning algorithms designed for sparse and imbalanced data, techniques for adapting spatial encodings and physical consistency metrics, in addition to methods for reducing the cost of generating sufficient training data.

</details>


### [214] [Sometimes less is more: Investigating inductive biases of MLPs and Transformers on noisy k-parity features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37976)
*Alasdair Paren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用含噪声k-parity特征（一个或多个）的合成数据，系统比较MLP与Transformer的训练动力学与归纳偏置差异：Transformer对噪声特征更鲁棒但难以学习低可用性复杂特征，MLP反之且随数据量稳步提升；固定训练预算下增加唯一训练样本数反而可能拖慢Transformer优化、降低最终精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际数据中特征的可预测性与"可用性"（availability）差异很大，但缺乏可精确控制这些属性的实验平台，导致难以解释Transformer与MLP在不同数据条件下的性能差异。

Method: 构建以噪声k-parity编码特征的合成测试台（noisy k-parity），可独立控制每个特征的可预测性与可用性；在多组数据分布、数据规模和模型规模下训练MLP与Transformer，比较训练动力学、归纳偏置及固定训练预算下数据量与精度的关系。

Result: 两架构训练动力学与归纳偏置差异显著：Transformer对噪声特征更鲁棒，但学习复杂且低可用性特征困难；MLP相反，且随数据量增加稳定提升。固定训练预算下，增加唯一训练样本数会减慢Transformer优化，甚至使最终精度下降（更小数据集反而更好）；训练精度表明这是优化速度效应，预算随数据规模放大时该效应缩小但不消失。

Conclusion: noisy k-parity测试台通过精确控制特征可预测性与可用性，为理解Transformer与MLP何时以及为何学习方式不同提供了清晰起点，并可用于设计能利用这些差异的数据与训练预算配置。

Abstract: We empirically investigate how MLPs and Transformers differ when trained on data sets containing one or more features encoded by noisy k-parity. Noisy k-parity gives us hard synthetic data sets in which we can control the predictivity and availability of every feature. Across a range of data distributions, data scales and model sizes, we find large differences in the training dynamics and inductive biases of the two architectures: Transformers are more robust to noisy features but struggle to learn complex, low-availability features, whereas MLPs show the opposite trend and improve steadily with more data. We also find that, under a fixed training budget, increasing the number of unique training examples can slow Transformer optimisation enough to reduce final accuracy, so that a smaller data set performs better. Training-accuracy results show this is an optimisation-speed effect; it shrinks, but does not vanish, when the budget is scaled with the data set size. By giving precise control over feature predictivity and availability, this testbed offers a clear starting point for understanding when and why Transformers and MLPs learn differently, and for designing data and training budgets that exploit those differences.

</details>


### [215] [Separability Is Not Alignability: Representations for Spectrum-to-Molecule Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37979)
*Karol Musielinski,Fanurs C. E. Teh,Takahiko Koyama,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文审慎检验"质谱基础模型+两模态对齐"这一缓解配对数据稀缺的路线：在MassSpecGym上，用无测试折污染的谱编码器重训后，发现对齐目标存在理论与实证缺陷，真正有效的是模拟谱数据而非冻结表征几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从小分子串联质谱谱图鉴定分子受限于成对(谱图-结构)数据的稀缺。基础模型范式被寄望绕开稀缺：在无标注谱上预训练谱编码器、在大规模结构语料上预训练分子编码器，再对齐两个冻结表征(类似视觉-语言模型及"柏拉图表征假说")。作者要审查这一路线是否真的成立。

Method: 在MassSpecGym基准上进行审计，并重训谱编码器以排除测试折数据污染；理论分析对齐目标与分子度量间的关系；识别并隔离候选集目标(candidate-based objective)所隐含的、与谱无关的诱饵(decoy)先验；最后对比模拟谱(simulated spectra)与冻结几何对齐的效果。

Result: 理论与实证两点负面发现：(1)产生谱编码器的预测类目标从未见过分子结构，可证明某些预测目标在不固定分子间度量(metric relating distinct molecules)的情况下就固定了分子身份，而对齐恰恰需要一个刻画不同分子关系的度量；(2)候选集目标会奖励一个与谱无关的、针对基准诱饵的先验，作者将其单独隔离出来。

Conclusion: 解决数据稀缺的关键不在架构层面：模拟谱优于冻结表征几何的对齐方案。这提示质谱基础模型的对齐路线在方法学上需重新审视，数据扩充(模拟谱)是更有效的方向。

Abstract: Identifying a small molecule from its tandem mass spectrum is limited by paired data. Foundation models offer a way around this scarcity: pretrain a spectrum encoder on unlabeled spectra and a molecule encoder on a large corpus of structures, then align the two frozen representations, as in vision-language and as the Platonic Representation Hypothesis suggests. We audit this route on MassSpecGym with spectrum encoders retrained free of test-fold contamination. Alignment needs a metric relating distinct molecules, but the objectives that produce spectrum encoders never see molecular structure: we prove that some predictive objectives fix molecular identity without fixing that metric. Moreover, we find that a candidate-based objective rewards a spectrum-independent prior over the benchmark's decoys, which we isolate. What ultimately addresses data scarcity is not architectural: simulated spectra surpass frozen geometry.

</details>


### [216] [Predicting Sparse Observables in Parameterized Unsteady Flows Using Coarse-Mesh Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37986)
*Brener Ramos,William R. Wolf,Tarcísio C. D. Oliveira,Scott T. M. Dawson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出直接在粗网格上部署图神经网络（GNN）的降阶模型，跳过全状态重构，仅预测所选自由度；在参数化圆柱绕流与不均匀加热方腔问题上，粗网格GNN可媲美甚至优于细网格代理，并结合动力学自适应采样与噪声增强展开训练提升精度与长期预测稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全阶高维非线性流动代理模型在应用只需部分自由度时往往不必要，传统降阶方法仍需全状态重构，代价高。作者希望避免求解器分辨率下的重构，直接预测稀疏探针量与积分量。

Method: 在粗网格上直接训练图神经网络作为降阶代理；测试对象为参数化2D/3D受限圆柱流动及不均匀加热方腔，覆盖不同雷诺数/瑞利数区间；提出由频谱变化引导的动力学信息自适应采样策略；借助粗网格训练的低成本，实现噪声增强的多步展开（unrolling）训练。

Result: 模型可泛化到未见工况，准确预测稀疏探针位置与积分量；粗网格GNN匹配或优于细网格代理，并减少训练时间与内存，甚至适用于3D混沌动力学；自适应采样相比均匀采样将最大误差降低一个数量级且成本可忽略；噪声增强展开训练改善长期自回归预测；在更低成本下优于参数化DMD、稀疏MLP、U-Net和傅里叶神经算子，并支持稀疏观测下的系统辨识与状态估计。

Conclusion: 粗网格表示、自适应采样与噪声增强展开训练共同构成无需求解器分辨率重构的参数化非定常流降阶建模高效框架。

Abstract: Full-order surrogates for high-dimensional nonlinear flows are often unnecessary when applications require only selected degrees of freedom. We propose reduced-order models that bypass full-state reconstruction by deploying graph neural networks (GNNs) directly on coarse meshes. Applied to parameterized 2D/3D confined cylinder flows and an unevenly heated cavity over Reynolds/Rayleigh-number ranges, the models generalize to unseen conditions and accurately predict sparse probe locations and integrated quantities. Coarse-mesh GNNs match or outperform finer-mesh surrogates while reducing training time and memory, even for 3D chaotic dynamics. A dynamics-informed adaptive sampling strategy guided by frequency-spectrum variations reduces maximum errors by an order of magnitude versus uniform sampling at negligible cost. Coarse-mesh training also makes noise-augmented multi-step unrolling practical, improving long-horizon autoregressive predictions. The approach outperforms parametric DMD, sparse MLPs, U-Nets, and Fourier neural operators at lower cost, and enables system identification and state estimation from sparse observations. These results show that coarse-mesh representations, adaptive sampling, and noise-augmented unrolled training form an efficient framework for reduced-order modeling of parameterized unsteady flows without solver-resolution reconstruction.

</details>


### [217] [Deep learning for non-transvenous ICD screening: tailored neural network architectures, extended experiments, and explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37993)
*Bartosz Morawski,Samuel J. Ward,Alex Touze,Lin-Thiri Toon,Paul R. Roberts,John Paisey,Alain B. Zemkoho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出针对皮下ICD（S-ICD）与血管外ICD（EV-ICD）的24小时动态筛查框架，并推出直接在ECG信号上运算的1D CNN系列模型AmpRatioNet，用于预测T:R与P:R比值异常（过感知风险）。在89名患者、4605段标注数据上采用患者级10折交叉验证，AmpRatioNet-SE在所有指标上全面优于基于2D相空间重构图像的现有最佳方法，且无需按设备或比值类型重新设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-ICD与EV-ICD避免了经静脉导线的并发症，但易发生过感知并引发不当放电，与死亡率升高相关。过感知风险由T:R、P:R振幅比预测，而该比值在24小时内波动；然而S-ICD筛查仅用单段10秒ECG，EV-ICD则完全缺乏正式筛查流程。因此需要覆盖全天的动态筛查方法，并填补EV-ICD队列T:R分析与P:R预测的空白。

Method: 构建24小时动态筛查框架：提出AmpRatioNet系列1D卷积神经网络，直接以原始ECG信号为输入；与基于2D相空间重构图像的最佳现有方法进行基准比较；数据为89名患者、4605段经临床医生标注的10秒ECG片段，采用患者级10折交叉验证与bootstrap置信区间；并进行可解释性分析，与可解释的经典峰值标注算法对比。

Result: AmpRatioNet-SE在各设置、各指标上均取得最优性能，且无需针对设备或比值类型做专门改造。在过感知易发片段上，AmpRatioNet-SE相对经典峰值标注算法的误差增幅最多小14倍，说明其对困难片段更稳健。

Conclusion: 将片段级预测聚合为24小时时间线，可将S-ICD筛查从10秒拓展至全天，并为EV-ICD提供可行的筛查路径（尚待设备级验证）。

Abstract: This paper studies two non-transvenous implantable cardioverter-defibrillators (ICDs), namely the Subcutaneous ICD (S-ICD) and the Extravascular ICD (EV-ICD). Both avoid the complications of transvenous leads but are prone to oversensing, which can trigger inappropriate shocks associated with increased mortality. Oversensing risk is predicted by the ratio of T- or P-wave amplitude to R-wave amplitude (T:R, P:R), and these ratios vary over a 24-hour period. Yet S-ICD screening uses a single 10-second electrocardiogram (ECG) segment, and the EV-ICD has no formal screening protocol whatsoever. We present a 24-hour ambulatory screening framework for both devices which includes the first T:R analysis in an EV-ICD cohort and the first full evaluation of P:R prediction. We introduce AmpRatioNet, a family of 1D convolutional neural network architectures operating directly on the ECG signal, and benchmark it against the best existing approach - based on 2D phase space reconstruction images. Evaluation uses 4,605 clinician-annotated 10-second segments from 89 patients, with patient-level 10-fold cross-validation and bootstrap confidence intervals. AmpRatioNet-SE demonstrates superior performance on every metric in each setting with no device- or ratio-specific redesign. An explainability analysis against interpretable classical peak labelling algorithms shows that AmpRatioNet-SE's increase in error on oversensing-prone segments is up to 14 times smaller than theirs. Segment-level predictions are aggregated into a 24-hour timeline, extending S-ICD screening beyond 10 seconds and offering a plausible EV-ICD screening pathway pending device-level validation.

</details>


### [218] [Towards Generalizing Pretrained All-Atom Representations to Unified Binder Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37994)
*Maojiang Su,Jiale Chen,Qijie Zhu,Shang Wu,Jiayi Wang,Jihai Zhao,Guo Ye,Yibo Wen,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PairFuse，通过轻量层级适配器将预训练全原子表征融合进几何隐扩散模型，以参数高效方式实现抗体、多肽、小分子三类模态的统一binder设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全原子模型（如PairFormer）在特定模态binder设计中表现强，但只服务于单一模态；几何隐扩散模型可在共享隐空间做统一设计，二者优势互补。核心问题是如何把预训练全原子表征泛化用于统一binder设计。

Method: 参数高效融合框架：(1) 轻量层级适配器逐层注入预训练全原子表征；(2) 双机制融合——丰富扩散模型的分子块(molecular block)特征，以及将成对(pairwise)结构信息注入几何扩散denoiser；(3) 理论上证明增强后的denoiser保持几何性质，且pairwise注意力融合比仅特征融合具有额外注意力表达力。

Result: 在抗体、多肽、小分子三类binder设计基准上，PairFuse优于原始预训练扩散模型及现有表征融合基线。

Conclusion: 预训练全原子表征可经层级适配器有效迁移至统一几何隐扩散建模，特征融合与成对结构融合协同提升统一binder设计的性能与表达力。

Abstract: PairFormer-based all-atom models demonstrate strong capabilities in biomolecular binder design. However, existing methods often use all-atom representations for modality-specific binder design, such as protein and peptide design. In contrast, recent geometric latent diffusion models pursue unified binder design by representing molecular blocks from different modalities in a shared latent space. These complementary strengths motivate us to investigate how to generalize the use of pretrained all-atom representations to unified binder design. We introduce PairFuse, a parameter-efficient method that enables this generalization through representation fusion. Specifically, we use lightweight layerwise adapters to integrate pretrained all-atom representations into an independently pretrained geometric latent diffusion model. Our adapters integrate pretrained all-atom representations through two mechanisms: enriching the diffusion model's molecular block features and incorporating pairwise structural information into the geometric diffusion model's denoiser. Theoretically, we prove that the augmented denoiser preserves geometry and pairwise attention fusion provides additional attention expressivity beyond feature-only fusion. Experimentally, across antibody, peptide, and small molecule binder-design benchmarks, PairFuse achieves stronger performance than the pretrained diffusion model and existing representation-fusion baselines.

</details>


### [219] [Preserving Safety During Sequential Fine-Tuning: A Factorial and Geometric Analysis of Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38001)
*George Ruellan,Diana Benavides-Prado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将"监督式拒答回放"与"logit蒸馏"两种安全保持策略解耦，在Mistral-7B与Llama-3-8B的顺序LoRA微调中比较其行为与几何效应。结果显示：蒸馏以极小过度拒答代价（ASR降5.4、过度拒答升5.1）几乎阻止安全侵蚀，而监督回放虽大幅降ASR（51.1）却带来严重过度拒答（53.4）；且两模型的拒答方向几何表现出现分离，表明"保留基座拒答方向"并非行为安全的可靠代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 良性微调会侵蚀大模型的安全对齐，顺序适配会进一步累积该风险。现有缓解手段主要有两类：回放有害拒答/良性遵从样本（监督暴露）与匹配基座logits的蒸馏，但二者在顺序适配过程中对安全行为和拒答几何的各自贡献尚不清楚，缺乏解耦分析。

Method: 将监督暴露与logit蒸馏两种策略因子化分离，在Mistral-7B与Llama-3-8B上进行顺序LoRA微调，任务序列涵盖代码生成、德语摘要与科学问答。行为层面用HarmBench攻击成功率（ASR）与OR-Bench-hard过度拒答率评估；几何层面提取拒答方向，并比较蒸馏与暴露对其保留程度，以及拒答方向消融后的拒答行为存留情况。

Result: 行为上，监督回放在Mistral上ASR平均降低51.1分（低于基座），但过度拒答平均上升53.4分；纯logit蒸馏（无监督回放损失）仅使ASR平均降5.4分、过度拒答升5.1分，即以极小代价基本阻止安全侵蚀。Llama呈现相同的不对称性。两者结合可缓和暴露带来的过度拒答，且基本不影响下游任务得分。几何上两模型分离：Mistral中蒸馏比暴露更贴近地保留基座拒答方向；Llama中暴露后的拒答行为在消融提取出的拒答方向后仍大量存留。

Conclusion: 监督暴露与蒸馏对安全的贡献机制不同：蒸馏以低过度拒答代价高效保持安全，暴露则以高过度拒答为代价换取更低ASR。两模型间的几何—行为解离表明，保留基座拒答方向不能作为行为安全的可靠代理指标，安全评估须以行为指标为核心。

Abstract: Benign fine-tuning can erode safety alignment in large language models, and sequential adaptation substantially compounds this risk. Prior work mitigates this erosion by replaying harmful-prompt refusals and benign-compliance examples, while other work instead matches base-model logits via distillation. However, the individual contributions of supervised exposure and distillation to safety behavior and refusal geometry during sequential adaptation remain unclear. We factor these two strategies apart and study their behavioral and geometric effects during sequential LoRA fine-tuning of Mistral-7B and Llama-3-8B across diverse task sequences (code generation, German-language summarization, and science question-answering). Behaviorally, supervised replay exposure yields the largest reduction in HarmBench attack success rate (ASR) on Mistral, averaging 51.1 points below base, but at a substantial over-refusal cost, averaging 53.4 points above base on OR-Bench-hard. Logit distillation, without any supervised replay loss, largely prevents safety erosion at a fraction of this cost: an average 5.4-point ASR reduction against only a 5.1-point rise in over-refusal. Llama shows the same asymmetry between the two strategies. Combining exposure and distillation moderates exposure's over-refusal while leaving downstream task scores largely unaffected. Geometrically, the two models diverge: on Mistral, distillation preserves the base refusal direction more closely than exposure does, whereas on Llama, refusal behavior after exposure largely survives ablation of the extracted refusal direction. This dissociation between models indicates that preserving the base refusal direction is not a reliable proxy for behavioral safety.

</details>


### [220] [Are Seismic Foundation Models More Than Glorified Feature Extractors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38003)
*Laura Laurenti,David Liu,Brian Kulis,Men-Andrin Meier,Christopher Johnson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 地震序列在"秒—分钟级波形传播"与"秒—月级事件发生过程"两个分离时间尺度上随机；现有地震波形基础模型沿用音频/视觉的单窗口自监督目标，只覆盖前者。作者用共享Mamba编码器-解码器训练9种设计（3M窗口），发现改变目标只是"重新分配"而非提升下游能力，故认为受限因素在于学习目标，需跨事件定义目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震数据具有双时间尺度随机性：单次地震波形在秒至分钟内传播，而地震事件本身构成稀疏、自激发的发生过程，间隔从秒到月。要在单一训练目标中同时尊重两个尺度是公开难题。现有地震波形基础模型（FMs）直接借用音频与视觉的学习目标，这些目标均由单一固定长度窗口计算，窗口内只包含一次波传播实现（一条地震波形），永远不包含决定地震何时发生的发生过程。因此需要系统检验：目标函数本身是否才是制约因素。

Method: 构建九个模型设计，共享同一Mamba编码器-解码器，在固定语料3M窗口上训练，系统地变化自监督目标、量化瓶颈与混合器；其中两组为架构匹配对照，仅改变学习目标。每个检查点以冻结方式在三项下游任务上评估（小样本P波拾取、分类、地震动预测）。并做容量、架构、领域邻近性三方面对照实验，以排除混杂解释。

Result: 改变目标并未普遍提升下游性能，而是重新分配技能，且无任何检查点在全任务上占优。在最强分类器上叠加掩码压力会使其再特化而非泛化：小样本P波拾取精度+6.9点，小样本分类精度−9.6点。容量、架构与领域邻近性对照均无法解释"技能迁移而非提升"。与任务专用基线相比优势依赖数据体制：小样本下分类与地震动预测优于基线，全数据下则落后。按语言/视觉领域标准，这些模型更接近特征提取器而非基础模型。

Conclusion: 关键瓶颈在于学习目标本身，而非容量、架构或预训练语料规模；要真正解决双尺度问题，必须定义跨事件（event-to-event）的学习目标，即对应使地震活动性随机化的那个时间尺度。

Abstract: Earthquake sequences are stochastic on two separated timescales: the seismic waves of any individual earthquake propagate over seconds to minutes, while the earthquakes themselves arrive as a sparse, self-exciting occurrence process, with inter-event intervals from seconds to months. Respecting both scales in one training objective is the open challenge. Foundation models (FMs) for seismic waveforms instead import their learning targets from audio and vision, where every target is computed from a single fixed-length window. Such a window holds one realization of the wave propagation (an earthquake trace), never the occurrence process that decides when earthquakes happen. We develop nine model designs sharing one Mamba encoder-decoder, trained on a fixed corpus of 3M windows, varying the self-supervised objective, quantization bottleneck and mixer; the two key comparisons are architecture-matched, varying the objective alone. Each checkpoint is evaluated frozen on three downstream tasks. Varying the target redistributes downstream skill rather than raising it, and no checkpoint wins everywhere. Adding masking pressure to our strongest classifier re-specializes it rather than making it a generalist: it gains 6.9 points of few-shot P-wave picking precision and loses 9.6 points of few-shot classification accuracy. By the standard imported from language and vision, these models are feature extractors rather than FMs. Controls on capacity, architecture, and domain proximity do not explain why skill moves rather than rises. Against task-specific baselines the advantage is regime-dependent: our checkpoints beat them few-shot on classification and ground-motion prediction, and trail them on full data. We argue the binding constraint is the target, and that settling the question requires a learning target defined across events, the timescale that makes seismicity stochastic in the first place.

</details>


### [221] [Measured or Real? When Model-Derived Measurements Can Be Corrected or Compared](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38015)
*Yifeng Li,Ziang Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型测量量（如样本难度、模型基准领先度）随随机种子与提示变化；该文给出可靠性的显著性检验修正与跨提示设置的模型比较方法，可缓解相关性超过1、提示导致排名反转等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从训练模型算出的数值（样本难度、基准领先度）会随种子和提示改变，因此需要判断何时可对这类测量做修正或稳健比较。教科书式的衰减修正（除以两可靠度平方根）会失效，在CIFAR-100两类难度分数上使100个类别中70个相关性超过1。

Method: 证明教科书修正的均方误差在相关性与可靠度使用不同种子时仍为无穷，故提出新规则：仅当两可靠度均通过显著性检验时才修正，并除以可靠度的上置信界；输出有界，覆盖率保证基于高斯噪声假设。对提示效应，在每个提示设置内比较模型，以提示网格固定、项目数为渐近维度控制族错误率；并通过跨种子对齐嵌入坐标提升逐维可靠度。

Result: 新规则输出始终有界；在可靠度估计良好后显著降低误差，500个模拟单元下误差减半以上。提示可反转排名：ARC 上 Qwen2-1.5B 在零样本提示下落后 Llama-3.2-3B 3.8 分，在两样本提示下反超 16.6 分；四个基准上对两种设置取平均，在16次反转中15次给出单一胜者。对齐嵌入坐标使留出逐维可靠度由0.003升至0.813。

Conclusion: 测量修正需以可靠度的显著性检验为前提并使用上置信界，方可保证有界与稳定；跨提示设置的比较宜按设置分别进行并控制族错误率，而非简单合并或直接比较原始差值。

Abstract: Numbers computed from trained models, such as an example's difficulty or a model's benchmark lead, change with seed and prompt. We ask when such measurements can be corrected or compared. Noise shrinks correlations, and the textbook correction divides by the square root of two estimated reliabilities. On two CIFAR-100 difficulty scores, it returned correlations above one in 70 of 100 classes. We prove that its mean squared error is infinite even when the correlation and the reliabilities use separate seeds. Our rule corrects only when both reliabilities pass a significance test, then divides by their upper confidence bounds. Its output is always bounded, and its coverage guarantees assume Gaussian noise. It helps once reliability is well estimated, more than halving the error with 500 simulated units. Prompt settings can reverse rankings. On ARC, Qwen2-1.5B trails Llama-3.2-3B by 3.8 points with no examples in the prompt and leads by 16.6 with two. Across four benchmarks, averaging the two settings names one winner for 15 of 16 such reversals. We compare models within each setting, controlling family-wise error asymptotically in the number of items for a fixed prompt grid. Aligning embedding coordinates across seeds raises held-out per-dimension reliability from 0.003 to 0.813.

</details>


### [222] [Detecting Brain Cancer Progression Through Signatures of Wearable Accelerometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38021)
*Rishit Das,Barbora Barancikova,Matt Williams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用粗糙路径签名（rough path signature）从腕戴加速度计数据中提取行走路径特征，并以签名核MMD（SigMMD）量化患者步行行为变化，经患者个体化归一化后可区分高级别胶质瘤（HGG）患者的稳定期与进展期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级别胶质瘤预后差且伴功能进行性下降；现行评估疾病进展的金标准为增强MRI，费用高、易致幽闭恐惧且仅每2–3个月一次，缺乏连续监测手段。可穿戴加速度计可非侵入连续监测，但数据噪声大、高度个体特异，传统时间序列方法与汇总统计难以捕捉临床有意义的变化。

Method: 将BrainWear研究中HGG患者的腕戴加速度计记录转换为步行路径（walking-path）片段；采用粗糙路径签名做特征提取，并用签名核最大均值差异（SigMMD）量化患者步行行为的变化；针对患者间异质性，以患者自身肿瘤稳定期的自然波动做个体化归一化。

Result: 患者个体化归一化显著提升了稳定期与进展期状态的分离度，形成候选的肿瘤进展生物标志物；结果提供了基于签名的可穿戴加速度分析可区分疾病状态的原理性验证证据。

Conclusion: 基于签名核的可穿戴加速度计分析有望成为神经肿瘤学中的数字生物标志物，支持开展更大规模的纵向连续监测研究。

Abstract: Glioblastoma and other high-grade gliomas (HGG) have a poor prognosis and are associated with progressive functional decline. The standard of care to assess progressive disease is contrast-enhanced MRI, which is expensive, claustrophobic, and performed only once every 2-3 months. Wearable accelerometers offer a non-invasive alternative for continuously monitoring patient function between clinic visits. However, accelerometry data streams are typically noisy and highly patient-specific, making conventional time series methods and summary statistics poorly suited to detecting clinically meaningful change. This work aims to classify stable versus progressive disease states by applying rough path signatures for feature extraction from wrist-worn accelerometer data. We convert accelerometer recordings from HGG patients enrolled in the BrainWear study into walking-path segments and use signature kernel maximum mean discrepancy (SigMMD) to quantify change in patients' walking behaviour. We overcome the challenge of inter-patient heterogeneity by adjusting for the natural variation in a patient's walking during stable tumour periods. This patient-specific normalisation substantially improves separation, yielding a candidate biomarker of tumour progression. Our results provide proof-of-concept evidence that signature-based analysis of wearable accelerometry can distinguish stable from progressive disease states, motivating larger longitudinal studies of continuous wearable monitoring as a digital biomarker in neuro-oncology.

</details>


### [223] [Learning from Relative Action Credit for Escaping Local Minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38034)
*Giovanni Minelli,Giulio Turrisi,Lorenzo Amatucci,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出PPO在长时程接触密集任务中停滞的根源是状态价值critic缺乏动作判别能力，提出用动作条件价值critic（比较已执行动作与同状态采样替代动作）扩展PPO，结合GAE与探索噪声上界，在相同训练预算下完成精细peg insertion，而普通PPO失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习有望仅靠交互学习操作技能，无需预训练先验或示范数据。但ON-POLICY方法（如PPO）在长时程、接触密集任务上常停滞：密集奖励塑形可带策略走过各阶段，但阶段拼接与稀疏奖励事件发现难以成功。作者将失败归因于状态价值信用分配中动作判别信息的缺失——状态价值critic仅凭episode结果评估进展，当策略收敛到部分解时所有结果趋于一致，无法证明某动作优于另一动作，改进停止。

Method: 将PPO扩展为带动作条件价值critic的算法：critic可在同一状态下将已执行动作与采样的替代动作进行比较，提供额外学习信号；并与广义优势估计（GAE）及探索噪声上界相结合，以逃离平台期。

Result: 在相同的训练预算与相同探索设置下，扩展算法成功逃离性能平台并完成精细的peg insertion任务，而PPO失败。

Conclusion: 状态价值信用分配缺乏动作判别是on-policy RL在接触密集长时程任务中停滞的关键原因；引入动作条件价值critic（配合GAE与噪声上界）可有效打破平台期，实现高精度接触操作。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds the promise of robots that learn manipulation skills from interaction alone, without pretrained priors or demonstration data. In practice, on-policy methods such as PPO routinely stall on long-horizon, contact-rich tasks: dense reward shaping carries the policy through stages, yet stitching them together and discovering sparsely rewarded events rarely succeeds. We trace this failure to the missing action discrimination in state-value credit assignment: a state-value critic judges progress only by episode outcomes, so when the policy converges to a partial solution all outcomes coincide, and improvement stops because no action can prove better than another. We therefore extend PPO with an action conditioned value critic which, can compare the executed action with alternatives sampled at the same state and aid the learning signal. Combined with Generalized Advantage Estimation and a bound on exploration noise, we show how the extended algorithm escapes the plateau and completes precise peg insertion within a training budget where PPO with identical exploration fails.

</details>


### [224] [Integral Probability Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38036)
*Bogdan Kulynych,Shahab Asoodeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出积分概率边界（IPBs），非线性推广积分概率度量（IPMs），使界限在极端情形下紧致，并给出实用估算算法，应用于对齐、无评判性能估计与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPMs 将分布差异压缩为期望间的单一最坏情形间隙，这种压缩代价高：对稀有事件、安全失效或代表性不足群体，基于 IPM 的界限松散甚至空洞无效。

Method: 提出 IPMs 的非线性推广——积分概率边界（IPBs），推导其基本性质，并针对实际相关设定设计估计它们的算法。

Result: 在理论与实际应用（对齐、无评判性能估计、公平性）中验证 IPBs，可得到性能与公平性差距的紧致界限；在真实生成建模任务中，对稀有群体公平性差距的估计比最先进方法精确约15个百分点。

Conclusion: IPBs 克服了 IPM 在极端/稀有情形下界限松散的问题，提供极端处紧致的界限，具有理论与实用价值。

Abstract: Bounds based on Integral Probability Metrics (IPMs) are ubiquitous in machine learning and statistics, appearing, e.g., in generalization, alignment, fairness, and privacy. IPMs reduce the discrepancy between two distributions to a single worst-case gap between expectations. This compression is costly: for rare events, safety failures, or under-represented groups, IPM-based bounds are loose or vacuous. We introduce a non-linear generalization of IPMs, which we term Integral Probability Boundaries (IPBs). Unlike IPMs, IPBs provide bounds that are tight at the extremes. We derive their basic properties and present algorithms for estimating them in practically relevant settings. We demonstrate the usage of IPBs in both theoretical and practical applications for alignment, judge-free performance estimation, and fairness, where they enable us to obtain sharp bounds on performance and fairness gaps, e.g., 15 p.p. more precise estimates on fairness gaps for rare groups in a realistic generative modeling task, compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [225] [Measuring Deep Learning Performance - An Empirical Study of Performance Distributions Across Architectures and Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38037)
*Kevin L Coakley,Odd Erik Gundersen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过18,600次实验揭示深度学习性能分布的非高斯性、尾部风险及其与模型规模、训练时长、均值表现的解耦关系，主张以分布性指标替代单一均值评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习算法设计与实现中的非确定性导致性能并非单值而是分布，但该性能分布对鲁棒性的影响长期被忽视；高可靠性应用需要评估模型在随机性下的稳定性而非仅均值表现。

Method: 在图像分类与时间序列预测架构上开展186组实验，每组以100个不同随机种子重复运行以构建性能分布（共18,600次运行），并用离散度、对称性与尾部风险三类指标量化鲁棒性。

Result: 性能分布常呈非高斯（时间序列尤为明显）；模型规模对鲁棒性无系统影响——较大图像分类模型离群值更少但离散度未降，较小时序模型离散度更低但极端低表现更多；训练时长非线性缩放，早停可平衡性能与鲁棒性；均值性能无法预测鲁棒性（时序中等相关、图像分类无相关）；时序模型的低表现离群值约为图像分类的三倍，尾部风险显著更高。

Conclusion: 尾部风险对高风险场景的可信AI构成严重隐患，均值优异的模型可能存在仅通过分布分析才能暴露的长尾与极端离群值；模型选择不应仅依赖均值性能，离散度、对称性与尾部风险应纳入可靠性评估体系。

Abstract: Context: Non-determinism in deep learning algorithm design and implementation leads to performance variation, meaning model performance is not a single value, but rather a distribution. These model performance distributions are underexplored despite their impact on robustness. Objective: We investigate the robustness of deep learning performance to sources of non-determinism, specifically focusing on how performance distributions differ across various architectures and tasks. Method: We conducted 186 experiments on image classification and time series forecasting architectures. Each experiment was run 100 times with different random seeds to generate performance distributions, resulting in 18,600 runs. Robustness was quantified using metrics for spread, symmetry, and tail risk. Results: Performance distributions are frequently non-Gaussian, particularly in time series forecasting. Model size does not systematically affect robustness -- larger image classification models show fewer outliers but not lower spread, while smaller time series models show lower spread but more extreme underperformers. Training duration does not scale linearly; early stopping effectively balances performance and robustness. Mean performance does not predict robustness - time series forecasting shows moderate correlation while image classification shows none. Time series models produce nearly three times more underperforming outliers than image classification models, indicating substantially higher tail risk. Conclusions: Tail risk poses serious concerns for Trustworthy AI in high-stakes applications. Models performing well on average may exhibit long tails and extreme outliers revealed only through distributional analysis. Mean performance alone should not guide model selection; assessment of spread, symmetry, and tail risk is essential for reliable model assessment where consistent performance is critical.

</details>


### [226] [CADENCE: Distributional Next-Transaction Forecasting for Business Cash-Flow Underwriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38084)
*Alexander Wu,Jingchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CADENCE——一个日历感知的分布事件网络，将每笔交易视为token，用紧凑的因果Transformer预测每类现金流下次支付的发生金额与完整非参数时间分布，替代生产中的FFT周期性特征，并在真实信贷场景中显著提升上游审批与风险指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经常性现金流是现金流授信的核心：它区分可持续收入与一次性入账、揭示固定负债、决定还款能力。但小微企业收入多为语义模糊的转账、支付流普遍很短、且遵循日历约定（如月内某日、发薪日、周末顺延），频域方法（FFT）会把这些日历规律当作噪声，导致周期性估计困难。

Method: 采用紧凑的因果Transformer，把每笔交易作为一个token；从外部标注器与冻结文本编码器继承交易语义，以在长尾交易对手上保持紧凑与泛化；输出为下次支付金额加一个完整、非参数化的时间分布（而非不可解释的嵌入）；引入日历输入与"无事件"监督信号。设计上进行消融验证各模块作用。

Result: 消融显示：交易对手语义主要驱动短历史下的召回，日历输入驱动时间精度，无事件监督驱动精确率。相比生产FFT基线，单日时间精度提升93.3%，下一事件F1提升6.7%，且输出分布经过校准，可直接作为下游决策的置信门控。端到端信贷评估中，以CADENCE特征替换FFT周期性特征后：KS提升4.8%，固定损失率下通过率提升18.2%，固定通过率下损失率下降13.1%。

Conclusion: 经常性现金流估计可从频域建模转向"事件+分布"的因果建模：显式的日历结构与交易语义比FFT谱特征更契合小微企业短而稀疏的支付流；输出校准的分布既提升精度，又提供可解释的置信信号，可直接嵌入既有授信模型并带来实质性业务增益。

Abstract: Recurring cash flows are the backbone of cash-flow underwriting: they separate durable revenue from one-off deposits, expose standing obligations, and ultimately determine ability to pay. For small businesses, recurrence is both indispensable and hard to estimate, because much of their income arrives as ambiguous transfers, most payment streams are short, and schedules follow calendar conventions that frequency-domain methods treat as noise. We present CADENCE, Calendar-Aware Distributional Event Network for Cash-Flow Estimation, a compact causal Transformer that treats each transaction as a token and predicts the amount and a full, nonparametric timing distribution of every cash flow's next payment. Inheriting transaction semantics from an external labeler and a frozen text encoder keeps CADENCE compact yet general across the long tail of small-business counterparties, while predicting a calibrated distribution rather than an opaque embedding makes its outputs interpretable enough to feed existing underwriting models and power credit decisions. Ablations confirm the role of each design choice, with counterparty semantics driving recall on short histories, calendar inputs driving timing accuracy, and no-event supervision driving precision. Against the production FFT baseline, CADENCE improves one-day timing accuracy by 93.3% and next-event F1 by 6.7%, while producing calibrated distributions that serve directly as a confidence gate for downstream decisions. In an end-to-end credit evaluation on production applications, replacing FFT recurrence features with CADENCE features raises KS by 4.8%, raises the approval rate at a fixed loss rate by 18.2%, and lowers the loss rate at a fixed approval rate by 13.1%.

</details>


### [227] [Jailbreak Connectivity: Towards Diverse, Transferable, and Universal Multimodal Large Language Model Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38091)
*Xu Zhang,Ziqing Hu,Shuo Han,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Jailbreak Connectivity（JC）框架，利用图像空间中有效对抗样本可被低损失区域连通的特性，沿连续对抗路径优化，实现多样化、可迁移、通用的多模态大模型视觉越狱攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型视觉越狱方法通常针对孤立对抗样本进行单独优化，导致攻击多样性不足、跨模型迁移性差、对未见有害查询的泛化能力有限。

Method: 基于"有效对抗输入可由图像空间低损失区域连通"的观察构建连续对抗路径：通过路径采样生成多样化越狱图像；引入轻量级代理分类器引导跨模型迁移；设计分布级目标函数，实现对多种有害查询的通用攻击。

Result: 在MM-SafetyBench上，对Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct、InternVL3.5-8B、Gemma-3-12B-IT的平均攻击成功率分别为84.01%、85.77%、97.95%，最高超出最强基线68.36个百分点；黑盒评测中在GPT-5.6与Gemini 3.1 Pro上较FigStep分别提升24.69与27.34个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明MLLM安全对齐存在结构化脆弱性，可为更全面的安全性评估提供依据。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are vulnerable to visual jailbreak attacks that bypass safety safeguards. Existing methods typically optimize isolated adversarial examples, limiting attack diversity, transferability, and generalization. We propose Jailbreak Connectivity (JC), a framework based on the observation that effective adversarial inputs can be connected through low-loss regions in image space. JC optimizes continuous adversarial paths: path sampling yields diverse jailbreak images, lightweight surrogate classifiers guide cross-model transfer, and a distribution-level objective enables universal attacks across harmful queries. On MM-SafetyBench, JC achieves average attack success rates of 84.01%, 85.77%, and 97.95% on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, InternVL3.5-8B, and Gemma-3-12B-IT, respectively, exceeding the strongest baselines by up to 68.36 percentage points. In black-box evaluations, JC improves attack success rates over FigStep by 24.69 and 27.34 percentage points on GPT-5.6 and Gemini 3.1 Pro, respectively. These findings reveal structured vulnerabilities in MLLM safety alignment and inform more comprehensive security evaluation.

</details>


### [228] [TGTV: An Interpretable Framework for UAV Fault Detection and Traceability via Spatiotemporal Graph Learning and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38106)
*Zixuan Wang,Changbo Shi,Ji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TGTV：将时空图学习与时序Transformer/VAE异常检测、连续时间Shapley归因与证据约束的大语言模型诊断相结合的可解释故障检测与溯源框架，在ALFA固定翼数据集上取得窗口级AUPRC 0.976、F1 0.920及飞行级F1 0.944。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机存在工况切换、传感器异步采样与控制补偿，会掩盖故障特征；现有时间序列检测方法难以表征跨子系统耦合故障、难以跟踪故障演化，且语言模型诊断缺乏定量证据约束。

Method: TCN+GAT（加权融合邻接）提取多尺度时序模式与传感器空间依赖；时序Transformer与VAE混合模型基于预测误差与重构概率做窗口级异常评估；连续时间Shapley归因识别关键传感器与高贡献时段并保持故障演化连续性；构建包含检测结果、图关系、时序归因、传感器归因与工况上下文的结构化证据包，以证据约束协议驱动LLM生成带可追溯证据标识的飞行级结论。

Result: ALFA固定翼飞行数据集跨飞行留出实验：窗口级AUPRC 0.976、准确率0.973、召回率0.902、F1 0.920；飞行级故障诊断召回率0.943、F1 0.944。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了准确的异常检测与有定量支撑的故障溯源线索，为可解释的飞行后故障复查与分析提供基础。

Abstract: Fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicles experience operating-condition transitions, asynchronous sensor sampling, and control-system compensation that can obscure fault signatures. Existing temporal detection methods have limited capacity to represent coupled faults across subsystems, track their evolution, and constrain language-model diagnoses with quantitative evidence. This paper proposes TGTV, an interpretable fault detection and traceability framework that integrates spatiotemporal graph learning with a large language model. A temporal convolutional network and a graph attention network with weighted fused adjacency extract multiscale temporal patterns and spatial dependencies among sensors. A hybrid temporal Transformer and variational autoencoder then evaluates window-level anomalies using prediction errors and reconstruction probabilities. Continuous-time Shapley attribution identifies critical sensors and high-contribution intervals while preserving the temporal continuity of fault evolution. A structured evidence package combines detection results, graph relationships, temporal attribution, sensor attribution, and operating context. An evidence-constrained diagnosis protocol uses this package to generate flight-level conclusions with traceable evidence identifiers. Cross-flight holdout experiments on the public ALFA fixed-wing flight dataset yield a window-level area under the precision-recall curve of 0.976, accuracy of 0.973, recall of 0.902, and F1 score of 0.920. Flight-level fault diagnosis achieves recall of 0.943 and an F1 score of 0.944. These results demonstrate accurate anomaly detection and quantitatively supported fault-tracing cues, providing a basis for interpretable post-flight fault review and analysis.

</details>


### [229] [A Separation Principle for Adversarial Attacks and Defense of Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38127)
*Farid Mafi,Saber Jafarpour,Mohammad Pirani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出结构驱动的GNN特征攻击框架SepAttack：在攻击目标松弛下，最优特征扰动为秩一矩阵，可分解为仅依赖图的度比例分量与仅依赖权重的顶奇异向量分量；该攻击无需梯度、有解析保证、速度快至约70倍且跨架构迁移性极强，并由闭式最坏扰动推出SepAT对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有GNN对抗攻击多被建模为以模型为中心的梯度/策略/元学习优化问题，把图视为被动基底，忽视了GNN沿图结构消息传递这一本质特征——脆弱性同样编码在图几何中。作者希望从归一化邻接算子的谱性质出发重新刻画特征攻击。

Method: 基于归一化邻接算子的谱性质构造结构驱动攻击框架；对攻击目标做自然松弛后得到分离原理：最优特征扰动为秩一，分解为图相关分量（与度成正比的空间分配）与权重相关分量（复合权重的顶奇异向量）；据此提出无需梯度的SepAttack，并推导出闭式最坏攻击对应的分离原理对抗训练SepAT。

Result: SepAttack无需梯度计算且具解析保证；在定向攻击设定下相较Nettack、IG-Attack等基线取得相当或更优效果，加速最高约70倍；由于其图相关分量按构造具架构不变性，在连续特征图上跨架构迁移近乎完美；SepAT在二值特征基准上报告了鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: 节点度是支配GNN特征扰动对抗脆弱性的根本图量；将攻击由模型中心视角转为结构/谱视角可同时获得效率、可解释性与架构无关的迁移性，并自然导出与之匹配的对抗训练方案。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on graph neural networks (GNNs) have been studied as a model-centric optimization problem driven by gradients, learned policies, or meta-learning, with the graph treated as a passive substrate. This view overlooks a defining feature of GNNs: their predictions are produced by structured message passing along the graph, so their adversarial vulnerabilities are encoded as much in the geometry of the graph as in the learned weights. We develop a structure-driven framework for feature attacks in which the attack is derived from the spectral properties of the normalized adjacency operator. Its central result is a separation principle: under a natural relaxation of the attack objective, the optimal feature perturbation is rank one and factorizes into a graph-dependent component (degree-proportional spatial allocation, depending only on the graph) and a weight-dependent component (top singular vector of the composed weights, depending only on the weights). Node degree thus emerges as the fundamental graph quantity governing adversarial vulnerability to feature perturbations. The resulting attack, which we call SepAttack, requires no gradient computation, carries analytical guarantees, achieves competitive or superior performance against established baselines such as Nettack and IG-Attack with up to ${\sim}70\times$ speedup in the targeted setting, and transfers near-perfectly across architectures on continuous-feature graphs because its graph-dependent component is architecture-invariant by construction. We further derive a separation-principle adversarial training procedure (SepAT) directly from the closed-form worst-case attack and report robustness gains on binary-feature benchmarks.

</details>


### [230] [Estimating Implicit Expected Log Density Ratios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38151)
*Avi Amalanshu,Chandler Squires,Yizhou Lu,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"期望对数密度比(ELDR)"概念，指出许多隐式密度问题实为ELDR估计任务，证明其有限样本下的根本性局限，并给出针对性的估计器改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度比估计(DRE)在无法精确计算概率时是实验设计、生成建模、强化学习等应用的核心子程序。但许多涉及隐式密度的问题中DRE被朴素误用，需要明确其真实任务性质并厘清可行性与局限。

Method: 形式化定义ELDR——在某个第三个（可能不同）测度下积分的对数密度比；证明有限样本ELDR估计的基本限制；论证常规DRE方法不适用于ELDR；提出对部分DRE方法的简单修改，使收敛到积分分布的样本可出现在训练数据中。

Result: 理论分析表明标准DRE式方法在ELDR任务上存在内在缺陷；基准实验显示所提修改能降低ELDR估计误差。

Conclusion: 既有DRE方法不宜直接用于ELDR估计，需发展专门的ELDR估计器；本文的初步修改与实验为这一方向提供了基础性动机与可行性证据。

Abstract: When exact probabilities cannot be computed, \emph{density ratio estimation (DRE)} is a key subroutine in the wide range of applications where density ratios appear, from experimental design to generative modelling and reinforcement learning. In this paper, we introduce the concept of an \emph{expected log density ratio (ELDR)}: log density ratios that are integrated under some third, possibly distinct measure. We show many problems with implicit densities where DRE is naïvely used are actually ELDR estimation tasks, and prove fundamental limitations on finite-sample ELDR estimation. Based on these results, we argue that the typical DRE-based approaches are unsuitable for ELDR estimation, and provide some foundational motivation towards bespoke ELDR estimators. To demonstrate, we propose a simple modification to some DRE methods that allows samples convergent to the integral distribution to appear in training data. We show through benchmarks that this can improve ELDR estimation error.

</details>


### [231] [MASS: Multi-mask Selective Self-Distillation for Leakage-Resilient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38154)
*Xiuqi Liu,Oveys Delafrooz Noroozi,Upamanyu Madhow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASS：用同一已验证解的多个独立掩码视图近似参考解边缘分布，聚合互补教师预测并选择性迁移可靠 token 级修正，在数学推理上优于自蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy 自蒸馏中教师模型可访问特权信息，但若仅以单一参考解为条件，教师预测会集中于某一条推理轨迹，导致给学生模型的迁移效果受限。

Method: 提出 Multi-Mask Selective Self-Distillation (MASS)：对同一已验证解构造多个独立掩码视图，用以近似无法获得的“多样参考实现上的边缘分布”；聚合这些互补的教师预测，选择性地只迁移可靠的 token 级修正，并自适应地把掩码集中于对推理影响较大的参考片段。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准与多个模型族上，MASS 一致优于强自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 多视图掩码 + 选择性迁移 + 自适应掩码聚焦，可缓解单参考条件导致的轨迹坍缩，提升 on-policy 自蒸馏的蒸馏效率与泛化。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation enables the same model to act as student and teacher, with dense token-level supervision obtained by the teacher being given access to privileged information. However, conditioning the teacher on a single reference can concentrate its predictions on one reasoning trajectory and limit transfer to the student. We propose Multi-Mask Selective Self-Distillation (MASS), which approximates the unavailable marginal over diverse reference realizations using multiple independently masked views of one verified solution. MASS aggregates these complementary teacher predictions, selectively transfers reliable token-level corrections, and adaptively focuses masking on influential reference spans. Across mathematical reasoning benchmarks and model families, MASS consistently outperforms strong self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [232] [Floor It, Then Fill It: Simulating Creator Ecosystems for Fair Video-Content Recommendation Systems at Billion User Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38155)
*Sohini Roychowdhury,Long Sha,Sunand Agarwal,Payal Kamboj,Shuyang Chen,Junlin Zhang,Scott Chen,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个基于真实平台数据校准的用户模拟框架（约42K创作者、12周耦合交互），用以在预算化多臂老虎机框架下评估流量分配的公平性干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与度优化的推荐系统存在"富者愈富"动态，结构性不利于新兴创作者；而大规模评估去偏干预需要高保真的创作者—观众生态模拟器，此类模拟器的保真度、多样性与有效性仍缺乏系统检验。

Method: 构建接地气的用户模拟框架，从三个行为维度建模：(i) 创作者多样性——基于真实平台创作者级分布聚类；(ii) 行为保真度——耦合的创作者—内容状态机，刻画成长轨迹、互动积累与毕业动态；(iii) 群体级有效性——与观测平台指标进行标定。并在此环境上以预算化多臂老虎机（MAB）形式化公平流量分配策略，扫描内容—观看目标定义、奖励函数与"小创作者保留配额"杠杆等配置。

Result: 最优配置（带保底的内容级观看目标 + 观看均衡差距奖励）使90%的观看目标每周达成，小创作者自然触达每周提升约+33%，分配稳定性高（获得资助流量的创作者集合Jaccard=0.819）。同时分析了Sim2Real差距并提出验证协议。

Conclusion: 模拟驱动的公平性结论具备条件性可信度：需通过多维度标定与Sim2Real验证协议界定其适用边界；保底+均衡奖励的MAB策略可在不显著牺牲稳定性的前提下改善新兴创作者曝光。

Abstract: Engagement-optimized content recommendation systems exhibit a well-documented rich-get-richer dynamic that structurally disadvantages emerging creators on large-scale platforms. Evaluating debiasing interventions at scale demands faithful simulation of creator-viewer ecosystems, yet the fidelity, diversity, and validity of such simulators remain underexplored. We present a grounded user-simulation framework that models a heterogeneous population of ${\sim}42$K creators and their coupled viewer interactions over 12~weeks, and use it to evaluate equitable view-allocation policies formulated as a budgeted multi-armed bandit (MAB). The simulator captures three behavioral dimensions: (i)~\textbf{creator diversity}, modeled via a clustering approach grounded in real platform creator-level distributions; (ii)~\textbf{behavioral fidelity}, captured through coupled creator-content state machines that represent growth trajectories, engagement accumulation, and graduation dynamics; and (iii)~\textbf{population-level validity}, measured by calibration against observed platform metrics. We evaluate simulative-configurations that vary content-viewership target definitions, reward functions, and a small-creator reserve lever to maximize creator-content recommendation equity. A floor-protected per content-view-target with a view-parity-gap reward guarantees 90\% of view targets being met by creators every week, grows small-creator organic reach ${\sim}{+}33\%$ per week, and achieves high distribution stability (set of creators with funded-views Jaccard $= 0.819$). We analyze the Sim2Real gap and propose validation protocols, contributing evidence for when simulation-derived fairness conclusions can be trusted.

</details>


### [233] [Replaying the Same Gradients at a Different $\varepsilon$: A Registered Test of the Fixed-Trajectory Account of Adam's $\varepsilon$ for One Rare Row](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38169)
*Lngel Kyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文用"重放"实验检验一个朴素读法——Adam中提高ε等价于按算术比例收缩稀有token输出嵌入行的更新步长。结果显示：ε提高四个数量级时，重放预测在三颗种子上均被判为错误，真实位移比预测大8.9–11.4倍（主头）与5.0–7.7倍（全softmax头）；三个数量级时则不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam将每次更新除以梯度平方滑动平均的平方根加小常数ε。直观上，对稀有token的输出嵌入行（"探针行"），抬升ε可被纯算术地理解为缩小该行步长。作者希望直接检验这一"算术读法"是否正确，即ε的作用是否可仅由分母缩放来刻画。

Method: 在标准配置的小型因果Transformer上训练。训练过程中对探针行的更新用不同ε重新相除，且使用该run自身的动量估计，从而在真实梯度上做精确算术重放并固定轨迹。事先书面规定"重放算错"的判定规则。检验ε抬升3–4个数量级；用主头（采样负例）与全softmax头作第二仪器；另设两组结果提交后登记的种子；并做两组真实run对照：仅对探针行抬ε、仅对其它所有参数抬ε，以及只缩放该行学习率而不动分母。

Result: ε升四个数量级时，三颗登记种子上重放均判为错误：探针行保留的真实位移是预测的8.9–11.4倍（主头）、5.0–7.7倍（全softmax头）。ε升三个数量级时（仅主头），重放未被判错，但边际很窄；结果提交后登记、独立评分的另外两颗种子呈现同样模式。真实run中，仅对探针行抬ε足以复现该行的变化；仅对其余参数抬ε也能部分推动该行；仅缩放该行学习率时，重放以相同方向误判。

Conclusion: 在单一模型家族、单一语料的一个嵌入行上，ε不能仅被读作对该行步长的算术收缩，重放的偏差有方向性且可复现。作者明确不作关于变化速率、嵌入行的一般性（其它行仅作单轨迹面板观察）以及随模型规模如何变化的任何主张，结论范围严格限于此设定。

Abstract: Adam divides every update by the square root of a running average of squared gradients plus a small constant, epsilon. For an output-embedding row whose token is rare, which we call the probe row, raising epsilon can be read as shrinking that row's steps by arithmetic alone. We test that reading directly. While a training run proceeds, we also divide the probe row's update by a different epsilon, using the run's own moment estimates; this replay is exact arithmetic on the gradients the run actually saw, so it holds the run's trajectory fixed. A rule for when the replay counts as wrong was written down before the runs.
  We train a small causal transformer in a standard configuration. When epsilon rises by four orders of magnitude, the replay counts as wrong on each of the three seeds registered for the test: the probe row keeps 8.9--11.4 times more of its displacement than predicted with the main, sampled-negative head, and 5.0--7.7 times more with a full-softmax head scored as a second instrument. At three orders of magnitude, tested with the main head only, it does not, by narrow margins; a further two seeds, registered after this result had been committed and scored on their own, follow the same pattern. In separate real runs, raising epsilon on the probe row alone is enough to reproduce the row's change and raising it on every other parameter alone also moves the row part of the way --- readings about the runs that do not rescue the replay; scaling the row's learning rate instead, with the denominator untouched, is mispredicted by the replay in the same direction.
  This is one embedding row of one model family on one corpus; we make no claim about rates, about rows in general (other rows were read only as a panel on one trajectory) or about how the effect changes with model size.

</details>


### [234] [B2P-Corr: Batch-to-Population Gradient Estimators for Non-Decomposable Correlation Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38171)
*Jingquan Yan,Yuwei Miao,Peiran Yu,Junzhou Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出B2P-Corr框架，通过全局矩草图将不可分解的相关系数目标转化为可分解伪损失，缩小小批量与总体梯度间的"批到总体"（B2P）差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCC、CCC等相关系数指标依赖数据集级矩（均值、方差、协方差），具有不可分解性，直接用小批量局部统计训练会产生有偏、高方差的梯度，其期望与总体梯度不一致，从而阻碍随机优化。

Method: 将相关目标转化为由轻量全局"矩草图"驱动的可分解伪损失，提出两个O(B)复杂度变体：B2P-EMA用指数移动平均与stop-gradient实现渐近梯度跟踪；B2P-U用跨批采样的U统计量估计并配合蓄水池式稳定化。理论上量化了朴素小批量优化的O(B^{-1})偏差，证明B2P-EMA在"快草图/慢参数"机制下的移动目标跟踪结果，并对B2P-U给出理想化控制变量分析。

Result: 在2个合成数据集与3个真实数据集上，B2P-Corr实现稳定优化，相关系数高于朴素小批量优化，且额外开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 以全局矩草图替代局部批统计可有效弥合B2P梯度偏差，为不可分解相关系数目标提供高效、理论可证的随机优化方案。

Abstract: Correlation metrics such as the Pearson Correlation Coefficient (PCC) and Concordance Correlation Coefficient (CCC) are standard regression metrics and learning objectives, but their non-decomposable nature poses a challenge for stochastic optimization. Since these metrics depend on dataset-level moments (mean, variance, covariance), naive mini-batch training with local statistics yields biased, high-variance gradients whose expectation does not align with the population gradient. To bridge this ``batch-to-population'' (B2P) gap, we propose \textbf{B2P-Corr}, a novel framework that transforms correlation objectives into decomposable pseudo-losses driven by lightweight global ``moment sketches''. We introduce two efficient variants: \textbf{B2P-EMA}, which utilizes exponential moving averages and stop-gradient operators to ensure asymptotic gradient tracking with $O(B)$ cost ($B$ is batch size); and \textbf{B2P-U}, which uses sampled cross-batch U-statistic estimators and a reservoir-based stabilization with an $O(B)$ implementation. Theoretically, we quantify the $O(B^{-1})$ bias of naive mini-batch optimization, prove a moving-target tracking result for B2P-EMA under a fast-sketch / slow-parameter regime, and provide an idealized control-variate analysis for B2P-U. Experiments on two synthetic and three real-world datasets demonstrate B2P-Corr achieves stable optimization and higher correlation than naive mini-batch optimization with negligible overhead.

</details>


### [235] [Data Amount Heterogeneity Improves Performance in Federated Learning for Neuroimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38172)
*Paul Kurian,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Abhijith Shaji,Greg Ver Steeg,Paul M. Thompson,Jose Luis Ambite*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究联邦学习（FL）在神经影像任务中不同站点数据量分布的影响，发现"异构联盟"（1个大数据站点+多个小数据站点）优于各站点数据量相同的"同构联盟"，尤其在低数据量情形下，并能接近集中式训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL已被成功用于神经影像任务（如基于结构MRI的阿尔茨海默病分类），但大型联盟（如ENIGMA）中各站点数据量差异极大。作者希望考察站点间数据量异质性（1大站+若干小站 vs. 各站数据相同）对FL收敛速度与最终精度的影响，以指导实际联盟的组织方式。

Method: 采用FL范式：各站点本地训练深度学习模型，对本地模型参数加密后上传至联邦控制器，在同态加密下聚合得到社区模型并回传各站点继续训练，多轮迭代。数据不出站点，模型参数受加密等机制保护。在此基础上对比异构联盟（1个大数据站点+多个小数据站点）与同构联盟（各站点数据量相同）在低数据量等不同数据量情形下的学习表现。

Result: 异构联盟优于同构联盟，在低数据量情形下尤为明显，且性能接近集中式训练；含一个大数据站点通常带来更快的收敛速度和更高的最终精度；异构联盟能更充分地利用小数据站点。

Conclusion: 数据量异构的联盟结构（1大站+多小站）在FL神经影像分析中更有效，可更好利用小数据站点资源，这对ENIGMA等大型联盟的站点组织具有实践指导意义。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) allows for secure analysis of distributed data without data sharing. We and others have successfully applied FL to neuroimaging tasks, such as Alzheimer's disease classification from structural magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). FL trains a deep learning model locally at each site, encrypts the local model parameters and sends them to a federation controller that aggregates the local models under homomorphic encryption, the resulting community model is sent back to the sites for further training, and the process repeats for a number of rounds. FL often achieves learning performance (e.g., accuracy) close to centralized training, but no data leaves the sites and model parameters are protected through encryption and other measures.
  In this paper, we show that federations composed of sites with heterogeneous amounts of data (1 large site plus small sites) perform better than homogeneous (same data per site) federations, particularly in low data regimes, while reaching performance close to centralized training. Having a big site often results in faster convergence and higher final accuracy. Heterogeneous federations better exploit sites with small amounts of data, a common situation in large consortia such as ENIGMA.

</details>


### [236] [A Gradient based Adversarial Augmentation Framework for Mitigating Unlearnable Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38173)
*Long Dang,Satpal Sura,Kaiqi Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于梯度的对抗性数据增强框架，通过可微增强管线与随机梯度上升学习增强策略，从最多18种增强技术中搜索有效子集以破坏"不可学习数据"（ULD）的捷径，从而恢复模型干净测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可学习数据方法通过不可感知扰动制造学习捷径来阻止未授权训练。要压力测试这类防护，需要找到能显著提高模型损失的对抗变体，迫使模型学习泛化特征。但现有基于数据增强的攻击依赖启发式搜索或对候选增强集的随机采样，当候选操作数量庞大时难以扩展。

Method: 提出名为techname的梯度式对抗增强框架：利用可微数据增强管线，以随机梯度上升端到端学习增强策略，在最多18种增强技术的空间中高效搜索，并用网格搜索选出保护方案专属的增强子集；该子集以最大化代理模型的分类损失为目标训练；同时引入感知+像素双重正则化，使学到的操作参数保持图像语义不变。
