Result: 在受控消融实验中，techname稳定优于等价的随机采样基线：EMIN保护的CIFAR-10上，ResNet-18干净测试精度80.0% vs. 77.29%；NTGA保护的CIFAR-10上，MobileNet-V2为83.28% vs. 66.45%。这表明干净测试精度的可恢复性来自梯度引导，而非扩大的搜索空间。

Conclusion: techname为压力测试ULD方法提供了一种有效且可扩展的对抗增强框架，是开发鲁棒ULD方法的关键工具。

Abstract: Unlearnable data methods (ULD) protect public data from unauthorized model training by adding imperceptible perturbations that create a learning shortcut. A deep neural network can exploit the shortcut learning path to minimize training loss without learning generalizable features. Intuitively, finding adversarial versions of perturbed images that can substantially increase the model's loss can force the model to learn generalizable features. Existing data augmentation based attacks rely on heuristic search strategies/random sampling over a curated augmentation set to identify adversarial augmentation operations, an approach that often does not scale when the number of candidate operations is large. To address the challenge of a large search space, we propose {\techname}, a gradient based adversarial augmentation framework that learns an augmentation policy via stochastic gradient ascent. {\techname} leverages a differentiable data augmentation pipeline to efficiently search a set of up to 18 augmentation techniques, from which a grid search selects a protection-specific subset. The augmentation subset is trained to maximize a surrogate model's classification loss. We introduce a perceptual-plus-pixel regularization to learn operation parameters that preserve image semantics. Under a controlled ablation, {\techname} consistently outperforms an equivalent random sampling baseline (80.0\% vs. 77.29\% clean test accuracy on EMIN-protected CIFAR-10 for ResNet-18 and 83.28\% vs. 66.45\% on NTGA-protected CIFAR-10 for MobileNet-V2), demonstrating that gradient guidance drives the clean test accuracy recoverability rather than the expanded search space. {\techname} offers an effective and scalable adversarial augmentation framework for stress-testing ULD methods, a critical tool for developing robust ULD methods.

</details>


### [237] [Convex-SKM-Net: Low-Latency Block Halfspace Corrections for Neural Convex Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38197)
*Haoyu Zhu,Xiaochen Zhang,Qingchun Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Convex-SKM-Net——一个无需约束专用不等式投影器的凸约束满足层，用残差与（次）梯度 oracle 生成线性化半空间，并以固定深度对偶投影梯度下降（dual PGD）求解最小位移块问题；同时联合学习预测器与正定 mobility 以自适应修正几何。理论上证明外层迭代收敛及 Slater 条件下的距离线性收敛，实验中在 SOC-MPC 与电力系统凸松弛上全部输出满足 1e-3 可行性容差，推理加速最高 144.3×（SOC-MPC）与 7.7×（QCQP），QCQP 平均目标间隙 0.012% 且在 1e-4 下全可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的决策需要快速推理与严格约束满足，但现有方法依赖约束专用投影或可行集参数化，迭代修复代价高，缺乏通用且高效的约束满足机制。

Method: 由残差与（次）梯度 oracle 构造线性化半空间，避免约束专用不等式投影器；采用固定深度对偶投影梯度下降求解最小位移块问题，实现 GPU 友好的矩阵运算；联合学习预测器与正定 mobility，自适应调整修正几何以提升目标质量；并给出固定对偶深度下外层迭代收敛、以及 Slater 条件与有界次梯度下距离线性收敛的理论证明。

Result: 在三个 SOC-MPC 视界与六类电力系统凸松弛上，全部测试输出满足 1e-3 可行性容差；相较最新约束满足方法，SOC-MPC 推理加速最高 144.3×，联合训练的凸 QCQP 基准加速 7.7×；QCQP 平均目标间隙 0.012%，1e-4 下全测试可行；学习 mobility 使五种子仿射任务平均间隙降低 22.4%，并在匹配的电力系统比较中改善间隙。

Conclusion: Convex-SKM-Net 以 oracle 驱动的线性化半空间与定点深度对偶 PGD 实现了通用、可微、GPU 高效的凸约束满足层，兼顾严格可行性与推理速度，且通过可学习 mobility 进一步改善目标质量，为学习式决策提供可证明收敛的约束满足方案。

Abstract: Fast inference and strict constraint satisfaction are essential for learning-based decision-making. Existing methods often rely on constraint-specific projections or feasible-set parameterizations, while iterative repair can be costly. We introduce Convex-SKM-Net, a convex constraint-satisfaction layer that forms linearization halfspaces from residual and (sub)gradient oracles without constraint-specific inequality projectors. It applies fixed-depth dual projected-gradient descent (dual PGD) to minimum-displacement block problems using GPU-friendly matrix operations. We jointly learn the predictor and a positive-definite mobility, adapting the correction geometry to improve objective quality. We prove convergence of the outer iterates at fixed dual depth and linear convergence in distance under Slater's condition and bounded subgradients. All our test outputs on three second-order-cone model-predictive control (SOC-MPC) horizons and six power-system convex relaxations satisfy a $10^{-3}$ feasibility tolerance. Comparisons with the latest constraint-satisfaction methods show inference speedups of up to $144.3\times$ on SOC-MPC and $7.7\times$ on a convex quadratically constrained quadratic programming (QCQP) benchmark with joint training. On QCQP, the mean objective gap is $0.012\%$, with full test feasibility at $10^{-4}$. Learning mobility reduces the five-seed affine-task mean gap by $22.4\%$ and improves gaps in matched power-system comparisons.

</details>


### [238] [Averaged Annealed Markov Chains Achieve the Algorithmic Threshold for Tensor PCA, Part II: Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38206)
*Aniket Das,Tselil Schramm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究球面上的张量主成分分析（tensor PCA）问题：从第 $k$ 阶张量幂的含噪观测中恢复未知单位球面向量（固定 $k\ge3$）。作者证明带退火与遍历平均的球面 Langevin 动力学可从均匀随机初始化出发，在算法阈值以上实现多项式时间的强恢复，从而表明局部马尔可夫链可从未知冷启动一路成功至算法阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tensor PCA 存在著名的信息—计算鸿沟：谱方法与平方和等全局算法在算法阈值以上有效，阈值以下有匹配的下界；而马尔可夫链等局部算法此前仅在随机初始化下、于多项式更大的信噪比处才能成功，普遍被认为劣于全局算法。本文延续 Part I 的工作，旨在检验局部算法能否在冷启动下达到算法阈值。

Method: 采用球面 Langevin 动力学，并引入两个关键机制：模拟退火（annealing）以逐步提升有效信噪比，以及遍历平均（ergodic averaging）以降低估计方差；从 $\mathcal{S}_N$ 上的均匀随机初始化出发进行采样与估计，并进行多项式时间复杂度的理论分析。

Result: 证明该算法在算法阈值以上的信噪比区间内，从均匀随机初始化即可实现多项式时间的强恢复（strong recovery），突破了此前局部算法需多项式更大信噪比的限制，且与全局算法的阈值相匹配。

Conclusion: 结合 Part I，本文表明局部马尔可夫链在无信息冷启动条件下也能成功恢复至算法阈值，改变了"局部算法在 tensor PCA 中劣于全局算法"的既有认识。

Abstract: We consider the tensor principal components analysis (tensor PCA) problem on the sphere, in which we are asked to recover an unknown vector in $\mathcal{S}_N=\{x\in\mathbb{R}^N:\lVert x\rVert^2=N\}$ from a noisy observation of its $k$-th tensor power, for a fixed $k\ge3$. Tensor PCA exhibits a well-studied information-computation gap: global algorithms such as spectral methods and sum-of-squares work above an algorithmic threshold, below which we know matching lower bounds (against these and some other restricted models of computation). In contrast, local algorithms such as Markov chains were only known to succeed from a random initialization at a polynomially larger signal-to-noise ratio, and were believed to be inferior to global algorithms for tensor PCA. Continuing the program initiated in Part I [arXiv:2610.16705], we show that spherical Langevin dynamics, when supplemented with annealing and ergodic averaging, achieves polynomial-time strong recovery above the algorithmic threshold from a uniformly random initialization. Together with Part I, our results demonstrate that local Markov chains can succeed all the way down to the algorithmic threshold from an uninformative cold start.

</details>


### [239] [Progressive Coupling Method for Diffusion Posterior Sampling in Ptychography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38207)
*Yihan Qiu,Mingtao Huang,Tianyuan Li,Shudong Li,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出渐进耦合后验采样（PCPS）方法，利用扩散模型先验在弱测量约束下提升低重叠扫描的叠层成像重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠层成像依赖重叠衍射测量的冗余信息实现稳健重建，但密集扫描增加采集时间与数据量；降低扫描重叠可提高效率却削弱重建的物理约束。扩散模型能提供强学习先验以补偿冗余损失，但在弱测量约束下有效利用该先验仍是难题。

Method: PCPS在采样过程中渐进耦合扩散先验与测量一致性：重建初期不确定性高时允许更宽的解空间探索，随重建逐步精细化施加更强的扩散引导一致性约束。

Result: 在多种扫描重叠的真实X射线叠层成像数据上，PCPS相比传统迭代方法与现有扩散后验采样器均持续提升重建精度，低重叠条件下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 扩散先验可降低精确叠层成像重建所需的测量冗余，为高效低重叠扫描提供可行路径。

Abstract: Ptychography is a computational imaging method that reconstructs a sample from overlapping diffraction measurements. This technique relies on these overlapping measurements to provide the redundancy information needed for robust reconstruction, but dense scanning increases acquisition time and data volume. Reducing scan overlaps can improve measurement efficiency but weakens the physical constraints available for reconstruction. Diffusion models provide strong learned prior information, offering a promising approach to compensate for the loss of measurement redundancy. However, effectively utilizing this prior information under weak measurement constraints remains a challenge. We propose a progressive coupling posterior sampling (PCPS) method, which allows for a broader exploration of the solution space under high uncertainty while maintaining stronger diffusion-guided consistency as the reconstruction becomes more refined. Experiments on real X-ray ptychographic data at various scan overlaps demonstrate that PCPS consistently improves reconstruction accuracy compared to traditional iterative methods and existing diffusion posterior samplers, especially at low overlaps. These results show that diffusion priors can reduce the measurement redundancy required for accurate ptychography reconstruction.

</details>


### [240] [RainCheck: Harnessing Historical Weather Data with Machine Learning for Rainfall Forecasting in Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38211)
*Shahran Rahman Alve,Muhammad Zawad Mahmud,Samiha Islam,Al Jubayer Pial*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统量化了站点气象降雨预测中"随机划分泄漏"与"气候态基线缺失"两类评估缺陷，提出并释放防泄漏评估协议，指出机器学习模型在时空外推下几乎无法超越简单气候态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习降雨预测文献普遍采用将站点记录随机打乱为训练/测试集的评估方式，导致模型在预测当天可见邻近站点的同期观测（信息泄漏），同时掩盖了季节查找表已能解释大部分方差的基线事实，使模型技能被系统性高估。

Method: 基于孟加拉国气象局35个站点1948–2014年共618,650站点-日数据，对比三种评估划分（随机、时间划分训练≤1999/测试2005–2014、时空划分含未见站点与未见年份）、两类特征（仅日历特征 vs 含前期降雨特征），并将六种学习器与气候态、持续性、SARIMA基线对比；同时检验GRU序列模型与梯度提升，并给出Brier技能分数及95%置信区间。

Result: (1) 仅站点-月气候态对高雨量月份分类准确率即达88.4%（AUC 0.950），时间评估下无学习器提升超过0.8个百分点；(2) 仅含日历特征的随机森林在随机划分下次日湿日AUC达0.889，高于时间评估下任何含真实预测因子的模型，但在未来年份上降至0.835，低于气候态（0.859）；(3) 含滞后降雨的模型具有真实但有限的技能：相对气候态的Brier技能分数0.074（95% CI 0.064–0.088），去除站点与年份输入后升至0.091，加入他站同日观测后升至0.131；GRU与梯度提升持平但未超越，技能在两天以上预见期消失；月总量预测中无学习器显著优于气候态。

Conclusion: 随机划分会严重高估降雨预测模型的技能，且气候态是必须报告的强基线；建议未来基于站点的研究在留出年份上、以防泄漏协议报告相对气候态的技能，作者已公开协议与代码。

Abstract: Machine-learning rainfall forecasts are often evaluated by shuffling station records into random training and test sets. For rainfall, this lets a model see what happened at neighbouring stations on the very day it is asked to forecast, and it hides the fact that a seasonal lookup table already explains most of the variance. We quantify both effects on 618,650 station-days from 35 Bangladesh Meteorological Department stations (1948-2014). We compare random, temporal (train <=1999, test 2005-2014) and spatio-temporal (unseen stations and unseen years) evaluation, calendar-only versus antecedent-rainfall features, and six learners against climatology, persistence and SARIMA. Three findings stand out. First, a station-month climatology alone classifies high-rainfall months with 88.4% accuracy (AUC 0.950), and no learner improved on it by more than 0.8 points under temporal evaluation. Second, a calendar-only random forest reached a next-day wet-day AUC of 0.889 under a random split, higher than any model with real predictors under temporal evaluation, yet fell to 0.835, below climatology (0.859), on future years. Third, models with lagged rainfall add genuine but modest skill for next-day forecasts: a Brier skill score of 0.074 (95% CI 0.064-0.088) over climatology, rising to 0.091 when station and year inputs are removed and to 0.131 when other stations' same-day observations are added. A GRU sequence model matches but does not exceed gradient boosting, and skill disappears beyond a two-day lead. For monthly totals, no learner beat climatology significantly. We release a leakage-aware protocol and code so that future station-based studies report skill against climatology on held-out years.

</details>


### [241] [HotPOT: Operator Transformer with Heterogeneous Physics Experts for Large-Scale PDE Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38218)
*Jiayi Li,Guancheng Zhou,Yisi Luo,Ruihua Chen,Penghao Jiang,Deyu Meng,Flora D. Salim,Piotr Koniusz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 HotPOT——一种面向大规模 PDE 预训练的异构物理专家算子 Transformer，用共享编码器 + 每族 PDE 专属「可学习预测器 + 无可训练参数固定数值更新」的神经-数值专家替代同构 MoE，在 24 组同规模对比中全面优于 MoE-POT，平均降低 rollout 误差 50.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多 PDE 联合预训练中，混合异质 PDE（控制方程、物理变量、变量交互均不同）并不总能提升各任务性能，存在跨任务干扰。现有 PDE MoE 通过学习路由缓解干扰，但专家架构同构，难以直接体现不同方程在状态演化上的差异。

Method: HotPOT 采用共享编码器提取跨 PDE 的共性信息；每个 PDE 族配备一个神经-数值专家：可学习预测器与固定的、不含可训练参数的数值更新算子配对，预测器输出该数值更新所需的量，再由数值更新计算下一状态。由此各 PDE 共享编码器但保留各自的预测器与数值更新，实现异构物理专家。

Result: 在 5 个 PDE 族、6 种设置下，HotPOT 在微调前后全部 24 组同规模对比中均优于 MoE-POT，平均降低 rollout 误差 50.7%；17M 参数的模型甚至超过显著更大的 MoE-POT 模型，且增益可延伸至未见物理条件下的零样本预测。

Conclusion: 将数值求解结构显式嵌入专家设计、以异构专家承载不同 PDE 的状态演化规律，是提升大规模 PDE 预训练跨任务泛化与抗干扰能力的有效途径，兼具参数效率。

Abstract: Large-scale PDE pre-training trains a single model on the data from multiple partial differential equations (PDEs). However, adding heterogeneous PDEs with different governing equations, physical variables, and variable interactions does not always improve predictive performance across tasks. Existing PDE mixture-of-experts models mitigate cross-task interference through learned routing, but typically use homogeneous expert architectures, making it difficult to directly reflect differences in state evolution across equations. We introduce HotPOT, an operator transformer with heterogeneous physics experts for large-scale PDE pre-training. HotPOT uses a shared encoder to capture information common across PDEs, while each PDE family uses a neural-numerical expert that pairs a learnable predictor with a fixed numerical update containing no trainable parameters. Each predictor outputs the quantities required by its paired numerical update, which then computes the next state. This design allows different PDEs to share a common encoder while retaining their own learnable predictors and numerical updates. Experiments across five PDE families and six settings show that HotPOT outperforms MoE-POT in all 24 matched-scale comparisons before and after fine-tuning, reducing rollout error by 50.7% on average. Notably, the 17M model also surpasses substantially larger MoE-POT models, and the performance gains extend to zero-shot prediction under unseen physical conditions. Code is available at https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/HotPOT-code.

</details>


### [242] [Variational Consequence-Driven Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38238)
*Wen Jiang,Ke Jiang,Xiaoyang Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结构行为正则化（StBR），用闭式潜在散度替代不可行的"后果分布"约束，在动力学敏感区域自适应收紧行为约束，防止策略偏离离线数据，在多个离线RL基准上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL主要通过对学习策略与行为策略的动作分布施加散度约束来缓解分布偏移，但该范式忽略了动力学一致性结构：即使动作偏差很小，也可能导致截然不同的状态转移，使智能体偏离离线数据集。作者因此希望在"状态后果分布"层面施加策略约束，而非单纯匹配动作分布。

Method: 直接约束后果分布需要真实动力学模型、计算不可行。StBR将不可解目标替换为闭式（closed-form）潜在散度；通过将潜在空间结构化以反映转移动力学，使该潜在散度成为真实后果分布散度的理论上界替身（bounded surrogate）；再用该潜在散度正则化学习策略。

Result: 潜在散度可作为真实后果分布散度的有界代理；正则在动力学敏感区域自适应收紧行为约束，有效防止策略偏离离线数据集；在多样化离线RL基准上性能优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 以"后果感知"的潜在散度替代动作分布匹配，是一种兼顾理论可解性与实证有效性的离线RL行为正则化思路。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) typically mitigates distribution shift by imposing divergence constraints on the action distributions of the learned and behavior policies. However, this paradigm cannot ensure the underlying dynamic consistency structure: even minor action deviations can lead to drastically different transitions, inadvertently driving the agent away from the offline dataset. To address this, we explore policy constraints through their induced state-consequence distributions rather than relying on pure action-distribution matching. Nevertheless, directly enforcing such consequence-aware constraints is computationally intractable without access to the true underlying dynamics model. To bypass this issue, we propose Structural Behavior Regularization (StBR), which replaces the intractable objective with a closed-form latent divergence. Structuring this latent space to reflect transition dynamics renders the latent divergence a theoretically bounded surrogate for the true consequence-distribution divergence. By regularizing the learned policy with this latent divergence, StBR adaptively tightens the behavior constraint in dynamics-sensitive regions, effectively preventing deviations outside the offline dataset. Empirically, we demonstrate that StBR achieves superior performance across diverse offline RL benchmarks.

</details>


### [243] [Nearly Minimax Optimal Regret for Adversarial Linear Bandits with Strong Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38243)
*Qiwei Di,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CRW-FTRL 算法，在强腐败（strong corruption）下的对抗式线性 bandit 中达到 $\tilde O(\sqrt{dT\log K}+dC)$ 的高概率 regret，首次在主导项上匹配无腐败最优速率，并解决 Liu et al. (2024) 留下的 $\tilde O(d\sqrt{T}+dC)$ 开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强腐败对手在看到学习者动作后篡改奖励，会击穿对抗 bandit 常用的重要性加权（importance-weighted）估计：对手只污染低频动作被采样的轮次，即以采样概率的倒数放大每单位腐败的影响。已有最好界 $\tilde O(\sqrt{d^3T}+dC)$ 在主导项上比无腐败速率多出 $\sqrt{d}$ 因子，$\tilde O(d\sqrt{T}+dC)$ 是否可达悬而未决。

Method: 提出 CRW-FTRL：(1) 采用半径加权熵正则的 FTRL（follow-the-regularized-leader）；(2) 用归一化核估计器（normalized kernel estimator）估计奖励，使其对腐败的敏感度由每个动作的半径而非逆采样概率控制；(3) 半径非递减，并与采样分布通过单调 log-determinant 障碍问题联合优化，使正则项中的负线性项同时抵消腐败偏差与每个比较器的估计误差。

Result: 对 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中 $K$ 个动作，CRW-FTRL 对抗自适应对手以高概率取得 $\tilde O(\sqrt{dT\log K}+dC)$ regret，主导项匹配已知最优无腐败速率，且与 $\Omega(dC)$ 下界在忽略对数因子意义下吻合。对一般紧致动作集作离散化后，得到 $\tilde O(d\sqrt{T}+dC)$ regret。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了强腐败下对抗式线性 bandit 的统计速率：$\tilde O(d\sqrt{T}+dC)$ 可达且最优（至对数因子），回答了既有开问题。核心洞见在于用"半径加权"替代"逆采样概率加权"的敏感性控制，并通过与采样分布的联合设计实现偏差与估计误差的统一抵消。

Abstract: We study adversarial linear bandits with strong corruption, in which an adaptive adversary chooses the reward vectors and a strong adversary corrupts each observed reward after seeing the learner's action, subject to a known total corruption budget $C$. Strong corruption breaks the importance-weighted reward estimates used by adversarial bandit algorithms: by corrupting only the rounds in which a rarely sampled action is drawn, the adversary amplifies each unit of corruption by the inverse of its sampling probability. The best known bound for this problem, $\tilde O(\sqrt{d^3T} + dC)$ by Liu et al. (2024), is a factor $\sqrt{d}$ above the corruption-free rate in its leading term, and whether $\tilde O(d\sqrt{T} + dC)$ regret is achievable was left open. We propose CRW-FTRL, which runs follow-the-regularized-leader with a radius-weighted entropy regularizer and estimates rewards with a normalized kernel estimator whose sensitivity to corruption is governed by per-action radii instead of inverse sampling probabilities. The radii are nondecreasing and are chosen jointly with the sampling distribution through a monotone log-determinant barrier problem, so that a negative linear term in the regularizer cancels both the corruption bias and the estimation error of every comparator. For $K$ actions in $\mathbb{R}^d$, CRW-FTRL achieves $\tilde O(\sqrt{dT\log K} + dC)$ regret with high probability against an adaptive adversary, which matches the best known corruption-free rate and the $Ω(dC)$ lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Applied to a discretization of a general compact action set, it yields $\tilde O(d\sqrt{T} + dC)$ regret, which settles the statistical rate of corrupted adversarial linear bandits under strong corruption.

</details>


### [244] [Transportable Offline Reinforcement Learning in Presence of Unmeasured Confounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38253)
*Junyi Liao,Hangqi Ren,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出因果校正的可迁移性框架（CAT），在域偏移与未测量混杂并存的条件下，利用多源异构数据联合识别目标域因果量，并据此进行悲观策略优化，给出有限样本次优性保证（CCB情形可达 N^{-1/2}）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习从历史数据学习策略时，部署环境常与数据采集域不一致（域偏移），且日志变量可能遗漏同时影响动作与结果的混杂因子（未测量混杂）。单一源数据集往往不足以同时解决这两类问题，因此需要利用多个异构源数据集的互补信息来识别目标域的因果量并优化策略。

Method: 将可迁移性理论与工具变量、负对照、代理变量相结合，构建因果校正的可迁移性框架。对含混杂的上下文赌博机，识别问题归结为桥函数积分方程组，进而实现有限样本极小极大估计；以识别量的置信集做悲观策略优化，得到 CAT-CCB。进一步推广到分幕含混杂马尔可夫决策过程，在结构与完备性条件下由互补源识别目标奖励与转移机制，在高斯动力学与线性函数逼近下得到 CAT-CMDP。

Result: 建立高概率有限样本次优性界，显式刻画逼近误差、估计误差、源到目标偏移、离线覆盖与样本量的影响；对有界有限维线性函数类，上下文赌博机保证恢复到标准 Õ(N^{-1/2}) 速率；数值实验验证了从异构含混杂数据学习目标域策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 当没有任何单一源数据集足以完成因果识别与策略优化时，多源互补结合因果校正的可迁移性分析提供了有理论保证的原则性方法，适用于域偏移与未测量混杂并存的离线策略学习。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning seeks to learn decision policies from previously collected data, but deployment environments may differ from the domains in which the data were collected, and logged variables may omit confounders that affected both historical actions and outcomes. We study offline policy learning under the joint challenges of domain discrepancy and unmeasured confounding, where multiple heterogeneous source datasets provide complementary information about a target domain. We develop a causally adjusted transportability framework combining transportability theory with instrumental variables, negative controls, and proxies to identify target-domain causal quantities. For confounded contextual bandits, identification reduces to systems of bridge-function integral equations, enabling minimax estimation from finite samples. Confidence sets for the identified quantities are then used for pessimistic policy optimization, yielding CAT-CCB. We extend the framework to episodic confounded Markov decision processes, where complementary sources identify target reward and transition mechanisms under structural and completeness conditions. Under Gaussian dynamics and linear function approximation, this yields CAT-CMDP. We establish high-probability finite-sample suboptimality guarantees that quantify the effects of approximation, estimation, source-to-target shift, offline coverage, and sample size. For bounded finite-dimensional linear classes, the contextual-bandit guarantee recovers the standard $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(N^{-1/2})$ rate. Numerical experiments support the effectiveness of the framework for learning target-domain policies from heterogeneous confounded data. Together, the results provide a principled approach when no single source dataset suffices for causal identification and policy optimization.

</details>


### [245] [ParaTTT: Parallelizing Test-Time Training Layers with Deep Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38255)
*Noel Loo,Neehal Tumma,T. Konstantin Rusch,Mathias Lechner,Alexander Amini,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParaTTT (PTTT) 通过迭代细化内层梯度轨迹，将任意深度的非线性 TTT 记忆并行化，推理更快、精度更高且性能不弱于线性循环与 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTT 层用小网络在线梯度下降替代线性快权重记忆，表达能力更强，但现有实现（如 TTT-MLP）依赖顺序块式权重更新，速度远慢于分块并行的线性递推，且块式执行只是对逐 token 在线递推的近似；而线性递推可精确分块并行。因此目标是既保留非线性记忆的表达力，又获得精确、可并行的计算。

Method: 提出 PTTT 算法：从隐藏层梯度的初始估计出发，每次细化由三次分块并行操作组成——前向评估特征轨迹、精确 gated-delta-rule 计算读出轨迹及其特征梯度、反向传播得到新的梯度估计；精确在线梯度下降递推是该迭代的不动点，解码时无需细化迭代。理论上对两层变体在充分收缩条件下证明几何收敛。

Result: 实践中两次迭代即足够；4K 上下文下两次细化使内层轨迹评估比使用块近似的 TTT-MLP 快 32×（误差匹配），完整 340M 模型训练快 4.9×。340M 与 1.3B 模型在语言建模困惑度和零样本准确率上匹配或超过 Gated DeltaNet 与 Mamba-2，与同规模 Transformer 相当，且召回能力优于线性递推。

Conclusion: PTTT 把非线性 TTT 记忆转化为精确的分块并行迭代，兼顾表达力、精度与效率，可作为线性循环架构的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) layers replace the linear fast-weight memory of modern recurrent architectures with a small neural network trained online by gradient descent. This nonlinear memory is more expressive than the linear memories of Gated DeltaNet and Mamba-2, but is difficult to parallelize: existing implementations such as TTT-MLP rely on sequential, block-wise weight updates that run several times slower than chunkwise-parallel linear recurrences. Moreover, their block-wise execution only approximates the token-by-token online recurrence, whereas linear recurrences admit an exact chunkwise-parallel evaluation. We introduce ParaTTT (PTTT), an algorithm for parallelizing arbitrarily deep nonlinear memories by iteratively refining estimates of the inner-loop gradient trajectory. Starting from a guess of the hidden-layer gradients, each refinement consists of three chunkwise-parallel passes: a forward pass that evaluates the resulting feature trajectory, an exact gated-delta-rule pass that computes the readout trajectory and its feature gradients, and a backward pass that propagates these gradients into a new estimate. The exact online gradient descent recurrence is a fixed point of the iteration, while decoding requires no refinement iterations. We prove geometric convergence for a two-layer variant under sufficient contraction conditions. In practice, two iterations suffice. With two refinement steps at 4K context, PTTT evaluates the inner-loop trajectory $32\times$ faster than TTT-MLP with the block approximation at matched error, while the full 340M model trains $4.9\times$ faster. Models trained at 340M and 1.3B parameters match or exceed Gated DeltaNet and Mamba-2 and remain competitive with same-size Transformers on language-modeling perplexity and zero-shot accuracy, with better recall than the linear recurrences.

</details>


### [246] [A Hybrid Physics-informed holomorphic neural networks Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38269)
*Zhijie Su,Qiaolin He,Zhiping Mao,Shiquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合物理信息全纯神经网络(HPIHNN)框架，通过将解分解为特解与齐次解两部分，把全纯表示方法推广到非齐次PDE问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息全纯神经网络(PIHNN)通过复解析表示可"按构造"满足齐次PDE，但其向非齐次问题的推广较为困难，而实际工程问题多涉及非齐次方程与复杂几何。

Method: 将解分解为特解与齐次解两部分：特解在简单扩展区域上用谱方法计算；齐次解由仅在边界上训练（使用修正后的边界数据）的PIHNN逼近。该构造避免了对复杂几何的网格划分，并保留了全纯表示的降维优势。

Result: 在非齐次Poisson、Stokes、双调和、线弹性与Helmholtz方程上测试，涵盖复杂几何、角点奇异性和近不可压缩情形，方法均能准确求解；在所考察的算例中普遍取得比标准PINN更低的误差和更短的训练时间。

Conclusion: 将谱特解与全纯神经网络逼近相耦合，为非齐次PDE在复杂区域上的求解提供了一种高效途径。

Abstract: Physics-informed holomorphic neural networks (PIHNNs) exploit complex-analytic representations to satisfy homogeneous PDEs by construction, but their extension to nonhomogeneous problems is nontrivial. We propose a hybrid physics-informed holomorphic neural network (HPIHNN) framework based on a decomposition of the solution into particular and homogeneous components. The particular component is computed by a spectral method on a simple extended domain, while the homogeneous component is approximated by a PIHNN trained only on the boundary with modified boundary data. The resulting formulation avoids meshing complex geometries and retains the dimensionality-reduction advantage of holomorphic representations. The method is applied to nonhomogeneous Poisson, Stokes, biharmonic, linear elasticity, and Helmholtz equations. Numerical experiments covering complex geometries, corner singularities, and nearly incompressible regimes demonstrate that the proposed method accurately resolves the corresponding solutions and, in the considered tests, generally achieves lower errors and shorter training times than standard PINNs. The results indicate that coupling spectral particular solutions with holomorphic neural approximations provides an efficient approach to nonhomogeneous PDEs on complex domains.

</details>


### [247] [NAGO: Noise-Aware Generative Operator via Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38273)
*Guanyu Chen,Shengze Xu,Pengwei Liu,Xingyu Ren,Pengkai Wang,Antonios Armaou,Tieyong Zeng,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出噪声感知生成算子NAGO，将生成模型的源测度作为可设计变量而非固定高斯，实现SPDE不变测度的高效函数空间采样，可将精确RK4步数从约30步降至3–5步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对SPDE不变测度做函数空间采样时，现有生成式流方法（如高斯泛函流匹配）默认使用固定高斯源测度，导致边际速度场谱刚度高、数值积分代价大。作者认为源测度的选择本身是一个尚未被利用的设计自由度。

Method: 1) 将源测度视为设计变量；2) 建立无穷维流良定义的容许性（admissibility）条件；3) 理论分析表明有限带宽下的源—目标协方差比控制边际速度场的谱刚度；4) 据此给出"容许性约束下的谱匹配"规则：先选取足够粗糙以满足适定性的源，再在已解析的Fourier模态上最小化协方差失配。

Result: 在三个代表性随机场基准上，源设计提升了少步谱精度及多项分布统计与物理统计指标；在受控的Matérn场消融中，达到精确RK4所需步数由高斯泛函流匹配的约30步降至3–5步，神经ODE评估量减少6–10倍。增益在平稳多尺度场上最显著，并依赖于度量、分辨率与引导协议。

Conclusion: 源测度的谱匹配设计是提升函数空间生成采样效率的有效手段，可在保证适定性的前提下大幅降低ODE求解成本；但其收益具有场景依赖性，需结合度量、分辨率与引导策略综合评估。

Abstract: We introduce NAGO, a noise-aware generative operator for efficient sampling from invariant measures of stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs) in function space. Our central idea is to treat the source measure as a design variable rather than a fixed Gaussian default. We establish admissibility conditions for well-defined infinite-dimensional flows and show that the finite-band source--target covariance ratio controls the spectral stiffness of the marginal velocity field. This analysis yields an admissibility-constrained spectral-matching rule: choose a source that is sufficiently rough for well-posedness, then minimize covariance mismatch over the resolved Fourier modes. On three representative stochastic-field benchmarks, this source design improves few-step spectral accuracy and several complementary distributional and physical statistics. In a controlled Matern-field ablation, it reduces the accurate RK4 budget from about 30 steps for Gaussian functional flow matching to 3--5 steps, a $6$--$10\times$ reduction in neural ODE evaluations. These gains are strongest for stationary multiscale fields and depend on the metric, resolution, and guidance protocol.

</details>


### [248] [The Role of Quantization in Loss Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38277)
*Zaiqian Chen,Aditya Somasundaram,Sophie Li,Sean McLeish,John Kirchenbauer,Tom Goldstein,Micah Goldblum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示语言模型预训练中的"定时炸弹式损失尖峰"（time-bomb spikes）由LM head输出logit增长导致的有限精度/量化误差累积驱动，提出以SGD+动量替代LM head上的AdamW来抑制logit增长并提升长程训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练中的损失尖峰浪费大量算力并破坏原本健康的训练。其主因之一是最终LM head输出logit的增长与发散。其中一类代价高昂的失稳——仅在长时间训练后才出现的延迟性、终局性发散——其触发机制尚不清楚，需要识别其前兆并给出缓解手段。

Method: 1）追踪LM head输出logit增长与量化误差随训练的变化；2）通过调节数值精度（降低/提高精度）进行干预实验，检验精度与尖峰发生时间/稳定性的因果关系；3）提出缓解方法：将LM head上的AdamW替换为带动量的SGD；4）对文献中已有缓解技术与本文方法做对比分析，检验其是否共同降低量化误差。

Result: 1）"定时炸弹式"尖峰发生前，LM head中的量化误差快速增大，最终演变为损失尖峰；2）降低数值精度会加速此类尖峰出现，提高精度则改善稳定性；3）用带动量的SGD替代LM head上的AdamW可抑制logit增长，在长训练周期下提升稳定性；4）现有各类缓解技术与本文方法均能降低量化误差。

Conclusion: 有限精度误差是"定时炸弹式"损失尖峰的隐藏驱动因素；抑制该误差是稳定长程预训练的关键，且多种缓解技术的有效性可归因于共同的数值机制（降低量化误差）。

Abstract: Loss spikes in language model pretraining can waste substantial compute and derail otherwise healthy training runs. A primary driver of these instabilities is the growth and divergence of output logits in the final language modeling (LM) head. In this work, we study a particularly costly form of this instability associated with output logit growth: delayed, terminal divergences that emerge only after prolonged training, which we call time-bomb spikes. We find that these instabilities are preceded by rapidly growing quantization error in the LM head, which eventually culminates in a loss spike. Crucially, reducing numerical precision accelerates time-bomb spikes, while increasing precision improves stability. We then propose a simple yet effective mitigation that replaces AdamW on the LM head with SGD with momentum, suppressing logit growth and improving stability over long training horizons. Finally, we find that existing mitigation techniques from the literature (along with our own) all reduce quantization error, suggesting a shared numerical mechanism behind their effectiveness. Together, our results identify finite precision error as a hidden driver of time-bomb spikes and show that suppressing this error is key to stable long-horizon pretraining.

</details>


### [249] [A Case Study of a Multi-Agent Kernel-Optimization Loop That Learns a New AI Accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38329)
*Byonggon Chun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对在一个架构与语言均鲜见的加速器（FuriosaAI RNGD NPU，使用新近且文档稀疏的 Rust DSL）上运行的 LLM Agent 循环进行案例研究，12天内优化了三个 Gemma-4-12B 解码内核，最终达到 9.293× 几何平均加速，且各内核耗时均在仅搬运权重所需周期的 1.25× 以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型在单个算子基准上能写出正确的 CUDA 内核，但面对其很少见过的加速器架构与编程语言时准确率骤降。因此需要研究如何让 LLM Agent 在陌生且文档匮乏的硬件/语言环境下仍能有效生成并优化内核。

Method: 构建面向陌生加速器的 Agent 循环：(1) 自建目标知识——编写并核验 DSL 文档摘要、用微基准测量芯片、记录编译器导出的合法性规则，并在工具链变更时复核；(2) 在每内核的 Design Tree 上进行基于角色的搜索，既探索整体设计也累积小幅被接受的改动；(3) 由于设备时间稀缺且读数有噪声，先用低成本检查在设备运行前筛选新设计，运行后期仅在与最优内核反复对比后才能接受改动。

Result: 最终三个内核相对官方基线达到 9.293× 几何平均加速，每个内核的周期数都在仅搬运其权重所需周期的 1.25× 以内，接近内存带宽上限。

Conclusion: 通过“自建知识 + Design Tree 搜索 + 廉价预筛与严格验收”的 Agent 循环，LLM 可在文档稀疏的陌生加速器上实现接近带宽极限的内核优化；提示词、profiler 与验收测试已开源。

Abstract: Frontier large language models write correct CUDA kernels for most single-operator benchmark tasks, yet their accuracy collapses on accelerators whose architecture and programming language they have rarely seen. We present a case study of an LLM agent loop built for such accelerators. Our example is the FuriosaAI RNGD NPU, programmed in a recent, sparsely documented Rust DSL, where the loop optimized three Gemma-4-12B decode kernels over 12 days on a shared, scarce device. The loop builds its own target knowledge: it writes and checks a summary of the DSL documentation, measures the chip with microbenchmarks, records compiler-derived legality rules, and revisits this knowledge when the toolchain changes, rechecking rules and measurements where it can. A role-based search over per-kernel Design Trees explores whole-kernel designs and accumulates small accepted changes in the best version of each kernel. Because device time is scarce and readings are noisy, cheap checks screen new designs before any device run, and late in the run a change was accepted only after repeated comparisons with the best kernel on the device. The final kernels reach a 9.293x geomean speedup over the official baseline, each within 1.25x of the cycles needed just to move its weights. Our prompts, profiler and acceptance test are available at https://github.com/bgchun-fs/multi-agent-kernel-gen-loop.

</details>


### [250] [Multi-Token Prediction Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38331)
*Jayden Teoh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出标准多Token预测（MTP）训练中MTP交叉熵梯度可精确分解为有益的KL自蒸馏项与低信噪比的残差项，残差会随规模增大而干扰NTP与多Token学习；提出KL梯度恢复技巧（利用共享线性输出头，通过直通梯度路由由交叉熵梯度精确恢复KL梯度），实现Soft-MTP，一行代码改动、零新增参数、无可测开销，在各规模上优于标准MTP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MTP已成为语言模型预训练的常见组件，能提升下游性能并借助投机解码加速推理。但作者发现标准MTP训练存在缺陷：MTP梯度中的残差分量同时干扰下一Token与多Token学习，且随模型规模增大而加剧；该残差信噪比低，会淹没真正有用的KL自蒸馏信号。

Method: 理论分析：将MTP交叉熵梯度精确分解为KL自蒸馏项（把各MTP头拉向模型自身的NTP分布，改善投机解码）与低信噪比残差项。方法：提出KL梯度恢复技巧——当两个分布共享线性输出头时，其KL散度梯度可通过直通梯度路由（straight-through gradient routing）组合两者的交叉熵梯度被精确恢复；相比直接最小化KL，无需词表规模logit矩阵，且兼容标准融合交叉熵kernel。据此构建Soft-MTP：仅一行代码改动，复用已有NTP与MTP交叉熵计算，去除噪声残差、只反向传播KL自蒸馏信号；同时兼容DeepSeek式与Meta式MTP，不增加参数。

Result: 在167M至1.2B参数范围内，Soft-MTP在各规模均优于标准MTP：更低的NTP困惑度、更快的投机解码、更高的零样本准确率；从689M起MTP困惑度更低；1.2B微调后数学与代码推理更强；在多数指标上达到或超过直接KL散度最小化。

Conclusion: 标准MTP梯度中的残差分量是性能瓶颈，去除该残差、仅保留KL自蒸馏信号的Soft-MTP以极低实现成本（一行改动、无参数、无开销）带来稳定且随规模一致的收益，堪称MTP优化的"免费午餐"。

Abstract: Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) is now a common ingredient in language-model pre-training, as it improves downstream performance and enables faster inference through speculative decoding. We identify a flaw in standard MTP training, in which a residual component of the MTP gradient interferes with both next-token and multi-token learning, increasingly so with scale. Specifically, the MTP cross-entropy gradient can be decomposed exactly into a KL self-distillation term that pulls each MTP head toward the model's own next-token prediction (NTP) distribution and improves speculative decoding, and a residual that has a low signal-to-noise ratio and buries the useful KL signal. We introduce the KL gradient-recovery trick: when two distributions share a linear output head, the gradient of their KL divergence can be recovered exactly by combining their cross-entropy gradients through straight-through gradient routing. Unlike minimizing the KL directly, this trick requires no vocabulary-sized logit matrices and is compatible with standard fused cross-entropy kernels. This enables Soft Multi-Token Prediction (Soft-MTP), a one-line code change to MTP that removes the noisy residual and backpropagates only the KL self-distillation signal by reusing the existing NTP and MTP cross-entropy computations. Soft-MTP supports both DeepSeek- and Meta-style MTP, adds no parameters, and incurs no measurable overhead. From 167M to 1.2B parameters, Soft-MTP improves over standard MTP with lower NTP perplexity, faster speculative decoding and higher zero-shot accuracy at every scale, lower MTP perplexity from 689M onward, and stronger math and code reasoning after fine-tuning at 1.2B. Soft-MTP also matches, if not exceeds, direct KL divergence minimization on most metrics. Soft-MTP is, in effect, a free lunch for MTP optimization. Our code can be found at https://github.com/JaydenTeoh/Soft-MTP.

</details>


### [251] [MUSE: Multi-Level Memory with Selective Runtime Injection for Scientific Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38332)
*Haoran Jin,Defu Lian,Xiting Wang,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MUSE：将历史经验组织为全局、方法级、实现级三层记忆，并在执行过程中按需选择性注入，用于科学编程智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史经验可在多个层面指导科学编程，但仅做任务级检索会遗漏那些在执行阶段才显现相关性的实现层面经验。

Method: 从历史轨迹构建全局、方法级、实现级三级记忆；初始化时注入全局记忆与问题匹配的方法级记忆；执行过程中依据当前交互检索实现级记忆；由LLM门控判断是否注入，并生成具体的审查建议或跳过注入。

Result: 在数值问题求解、内核优化、科学软件修复等四个基准上，MUSE在所有评价指标上优于检索与记忆基线；消融实验支持多级记忆设计与门控注入；迁移实验显示记忆在内核基准间仍有效。

Conclusion: 多级记忆结合运行时选择性注入，能够有效实现科学编程中经验的可复用，代码已开源。

Abstract: Historical experience can guide scientific coding at multiple levels, but task-level retrieval may miss implementation lessons that become relevant during execution. We introduce MUSE, a memory management framework combining multi-level memory with selective runtime injection for scientific coding agents. MUSE builds global, approach-level, and implementation-level memory from historical trajectories. At initialization, the agent receives global memory and problem-matched approach-level memory. During execution, current interactions are used to retrieve implementation-level memory. An LLM gate selects relevant experience and generates specific review advice, or skips injection. Across four benchmarks spanning numerical problem solving, kernel optimization, and scientific software repair, MUSE outperforms retrieval and memory baselines on all evaluation metrics. Ablations support the multi-level design and gated injection, while transfer experiments show that memory remains useful across kernel benchmarks. These results show that multi-level memory and selective runtime injection enable effective reuse of experience in scientific coding. Code is available at https://github.com/hr-jin/MUSE.

</details>


### [252] [Optimal Regret for Communication-Efficient Decentralized Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38334)
*Sifan Yang,Wenhao Yang,Wei Jiang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带压缩通信的去中心化在线凸优化问题，提出 Ascent-gossip（在线压缩+Chebyshev递推，共识速率更快），并据此给出达到最优 regret 界的在线算法：凸情形 $\widetilde O(\omega^{-1/2}n\rho^{-1/4}\sqrt{T})$、强凸情形 $\widetilde O(\omega^{-1}n\rho^{-1/2}\ln T)$，在 $T,n,\rho,\omega$ 上与下界匹配（至多对数因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有最优上界 $O(\omega^{-1/2}n\rho^{-1}\sqrt{T})$ 与 $O(\omega^{-1}n\rho^{-2}\ln n\ln T)$ 相比下界 $\Omega(\omega^{-1/2}n\rho^{-1/4}\sqrt{T})$ 与 $\Omega(\omega^{-1}n\rho^{-1/2}\ln T)$，在 $\rho^{-1}$ 依赖上存在多项式级差距，需要消除这一差距。

Method: 提出 Ascent-gossip：将在线压缩策略与 Chebyshev 递推结合，获得比现有压缩 gossip 更快的共识速率；再将其嵌入去中心化在线算法框架，用于处理仅依赖本地损失与邻居压缩消息的全局损失序列最小化。

Result: 在凸与强凸损失下分别取得 $\widetilde O(\omega^{-1/2}n\rho^{-1/4}\sqrt{T})$ 与 $\widetilde O(\omega^{-1}n\rho^{-1/2}\ln T)$ 的 regret，在 $T,n,\rho,\omega$ 上（至多 $\ln n$、$\ln\rho^{-1}$ 对数因子）与下界匹配。

Conclusion: 通过改进压缩 gossip 的共识速率，可在压缩通信约束下实现去中心化在线凸优化的最优 regret 阶，闭合了与下界在 $\rho^{-1}$ 依赖上的差距。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate decentralized online convex optimization with compressed communication, where $n$ learners over a network jointly minimize a sequence of global loss functions based only on their local losses and compressed messages from their neighbors. The best known regret bounds for this problem are $O(ω^{-1/2}nρ^{-1}\sqrt{T\ln n})$ and $O(ω^{-1}nρ^{-2}\ln n\ln T)$ for convex and strongly convex loss functions, respectively, where $ρ\in(0,1)$ is the spectral gap of the communication matrix and $ω\in(0,1]$ is the compression quality factor. Compared with the corresponding lower bounds of $Ω(ω^{-1/2}nρ^{-1/4}\sqrt{T})$ and $Ω(ω^{-1}nρ^{-1/2}\ln T)$, these upper bounds exhibit polynomial gaps in their dependence on $ρ^{-1}$. To close these gaps, we first develop Ascent-gossip, which combines an online compression strategy with a Chebyshev recursion and attains a faster consensus rate than existing compressed gossip methods. Furthermore, by adopting Ascent-gossip, we propose an online algorithm that achieves optimal regret bounds of $\widetilde O(ω^{-1/2}nρ^{-1/4}\sqrt{T})$ and $\widetilde O(ω^{-1}nρ^{-1/2}\ln T)$ for convex and strongly convex loss functions, respectively. Up to logarithmic factors in $n$ and $ρ^{-1}$, these results match the lower bounds in terms of $T$, $n$, $ρ$, and $ω$.

</details>


### [253] [Learning a Prior-Data Fitted Network for Graph Foundation Models via Structural Causal Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38335)
*Ziwei Zhang,Tianyin Liao,Chunyu Hu,Ge Lan,Nuo Xu,Yi Huang,Qingyun Sun,Xingxuan Zhang,Peng Cui,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GSCM-PFN：一种基于结构因果模型（SCM）先验生成的合成数据预训练的图 PFN，联合建模节点与拓扑，在23个真实基准上超越传统 GFM 与既有图 PFN，且预训练不使用真实图数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型（GFM）受限于预训练规模不足，或依赖与 LLM 文本信息对齐；借用先验数据拟合网络（PFN）思路的图 PFN 又难以建模节点/边集合内部及之间的因果关系、难以生成多样图结构、缺乏合适架构处理复杂合成数据。

Method: (1) 设计联合特征-边-标签的结构因果模型（SCM）指导合成图生成，刻画节点集与边集内部及跨集合的因果关系；(2) 提出先验引导的节点-边依赖建模，实例化多样化图生成策略，并证明该 SCM 生成先验比现有先验更具表达力；(3) 提出特征置换不变的 dual-path 图网络，并给出从多样合成图学习的理论保证。

Result: 在23个真实世界基准上的实验表明，GSCM-PFN 优于传统 GFM 及基于 PFN 的 GFM，且预训练阶段完全不使用真实图数据。

Conclusion: SCM 引导的合成先验配合置换不变双路径架构，可作为无需真实数据预训练的跨域图预测有效范式，为图基础模型提供新路径。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable representations across different graphs and domains. However, existing GFMs face notable limitations, including limited pre-training scale or reliance on aligning with textual information in Large Language Models (LLMs). To overcome these limitations, recent studies have drawn inspiration from prior-data fitted networks (PFNs), which learn from large synthetic datasets and support multi-domain prediction backed by causal learning. However, existing graph PFNs encounter severe difficulties in modeling causal relationships within and across node and edge sets, generating diverse graph structures, and designing suitable architectures that can learn from complex synthetic datasets. To address these challenges, we propose **GSCM-PFN** (Graph SCM-based Prior-Data Fitted Network), a graph PFN pre-trained on synthetic data generated from an SCM-based prior that jointly models nodes and graph topology. Specifically, we (1) design a joint feature-edge-label structural causal model (SCM) to guide synthetic graph generation and capture causal relationships within and across node and edge sets; (2) introduce prior-guided node-edge dependency modeling to instantiate diverse graph generation strategies, and prove that our SCM-based generation prior is more expressive than existing priors; and (3) propose a feature-permutation-invariant dual-path graph network with theoretical guarantees for learning from diverse synthetic graphs. Experiments on 23 real-world benchmarks show that GSCM-PFN outperforms both conventional GFMs and PFN-based GFMs, without utilizing real-world graph data for pre-training. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GSCM-PFN-A3A3/

</details>


### [254] [How Much Do Teacher Bridges Matter in Interleaved On-Policy Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38383)
*Leran Chen,Zhiyuan Yao,Lingnan Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在相同轨迹上比较 on-policy 蒸馏中对教师文本（bridge）token 的三种处理（无损失、前向KL、反向KL），预注册实验未发现教师文本作为上下文或训练目标带来额外准确率收益，三种损失处理在准确率上统计等价（±5个百分点内）；结果受限于BF16推理与训练步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy 蒸馏中用教师预测在学生生成的回答上训练学生，部分方法会插入教师撰写的片段（bridge），但对这些 token 该如何训练存在分歧——是否加损失、用前向还是反向KL，缺乏受控比较。

Method: 以 Qwen3-1.7B 为学生、Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 为教师，在完全相同的轨迹上对比无损失、前向KL、反向KL三种处理；按每个 token 由哪个模型生成来拆分梯度；预注册（pre-specified）检验与重复实验，并做探索性试点（把 bridge 放在失败回答与教师分歧最大处）及四倍加权实验；另做了训练步数与在线训练变体的稳定性考察。

Result: 短训练、BF16 推理下，教师文本无论是作为上下文还是训练目标，预注册检验均未检测到准确率增益。学生首段后的一段 bridge 仅占 2.7% token，其梯度很小且近似与学生 token 梯度正交；三种损失处理梯度方向相近，准确率在 ±5 个百分点内统计等价。探索性试点显示教师 token 损失可能有收益，但预注册重复未能证实，四倍加权也无显著准确率提升。固定回答训练中准确率在第 4 次更新后见顶，随后学生逐渐无法停止生成；所测在线训练变体均未同时满足稳定停止与准确率提升。FP32 精确评估下重复实验模型频繁触及回答长度上限。

Conclusion: 在 BF16 推理与短训练设定下，教师 token 监督（作为上下文或损失目标）未显示必要性与增益，三种损失处理效果相当；但这不能推广为"教师 token 监督普遍不必要"，需在更长训练与更高精度推理下重新检验，尤其是指标被 BF16 舍入掩盖的小权重变化。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student using teacher predictions on student-generated responses. Some methods insert teacher-written segments, or bridges, but disagree on how to train on those tokens. We compare no loss, forward KL and reverse KL on identical trajectories using Qwen3-1.7B as student and Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 as teacher. We separate gradients by which model wrote each token. Under our short training runs and BF16 inference, pre-specified tests detected no added accuracy benefit from teacher text as context or as a training target. A one-paragraph bridge after the student's first paragraph is only 2.7% of the tokens and contributes a small gradient nearly orthogonal to the student-token gradient. In this main comparison, the three loss treatments gave similar gradient directions and were statistically equivalent in accuracy within a +/-5 percentage-point margin. An exploratory pilot placing the bridge where failed responses disagreed most with the teacher suggested a benefit from teacher-token loss. A pre-specified replication did not confirm this, and fourfold weighting showed no significant accuracy benefit. Two limitations bound these findings. Among the tested amounts of training on fixed responses, gains peaked after four updates before the student increasingly failed to stop generating. None of the tested online training variants met the criteria for stable stopping and improved accuracy. Our evaluations used BF16 inference, which rounds away most of the small weight changes. When evaluated in exact FP32, the tested replication models frequently reached the response-length limit. Our accuracy findings therefore concern BF16 inference; they do not establish that teacher-token supervision is generally unnecessary.

</details>


### [255] [Continual Adaptation Across Modalities: Learning and Forgetting in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38436)
*Xinrui Wang,Zhihao Chen,Jia Tang,Alexandra Gomez-Villa,Bartlomiej Twardowski,Joost van de Weijer,Songcan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究统一多模态模型（UMM）在多模态多任务持续学习中的学习与遗忘动态，发现跨模态干扰随参数隔离程度降低而减弱、生成侧比理解侧更脆弱、后训练获得的能力最先被遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型已能单框架处理与生成多种模态，但其在不同模态多任务上持续学习时的学习与遗忘规律尚不清楚，缺乏对其机制的理解阻碍了可持续演化的UMM发展。

Method: 对UMM进行跨模态多任务持续学习实验，系统考察架构设计（模态专属参数隔离程度）与适配方法如何影响模态内学习及生成侧与理解侧之间的跨模态干扰，并对不同来源能力（预训练vs后训练）的遗忘进行对比测量。

Result: 三点发现：(1) 跨模态干扰的严重程度随模态专属参数隔离程度提高而下降；(2) 模型内存在不对称性，生成侧显著比理解侧脆弱；(3) "后训练获得的能力最先被遗忘"，纯后训练能力（如个性化生成）崩溃84%–90%，而预训练能力保持稳定。

Conclusion: 参数隔离与后训练脆弱性共同塑造UMM的学习与遗忘动态，为构建可持续演化的统一多模态模型提供了起点与实证依据。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs), which can process and generate diverse modalities within a single framework, have recently emerged as a powerful paradigm. However, when these models undergo continual learning on multiple tasks across different modalities, their learning and forgetting dynamics remain poorly understood. In this paper, we systematically investigate how architectural design and adaptation method shape intra-modal learning and cross-modal interference between the generation and understanding sides. Specifically, we report three findings. First, the severity of cross-modal interference decreases with the degree of modality-specific parameter isolation. Second, a within-model asymmetry: the generation side of a UMM is markedly more fragile than its understanding side. Third, what post-training adds is the first to be forgotten: purely post-training capabilities such as personalized generation collapse by 84\% to 90\%, while pretraining capabilities remain stable. Together, these results clarify how parameter isolation and post-training fragility shape UMMs' learning and forgetting dynamics, offering a starting point for developing continually evolving UMMs.

</details>


### [256] [AtomSteer: Test-Time Constraint Steering for Atomistic Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38517)
*Pengfei Cai,Katharina S. C. Jäger,Utkarsh Utkarsh,Nofit Segal,Bowen Deng,Akshay Subramanian,Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AtomSteer：一种在采样过程（test-time）中对预训练原子生成模型施加结构约束的统一推理框架，通过有界局部投影、图刚性立体化学变换与周期对称投影修正中间预测，在无需重训与改架构的前提下同时提升晶体学、几何与立体化学准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散与流匹配模型已广泛用于晶体材料、分子构象与蛋白-配体复合物生成，但难以满足晶体学对称性、结构几何与精确立体化学要求。作者希望在不重训、不改架构的情况下，用保几何的修正方式增强已训练模型的约束满足能力。

Method: 提出 AtomSteer 测试时推理框架：在采样过程中对中间结构预测施加三类修正——有界局部投影（bounded local projection）、图刚性立体化学变换（graph-rigid stereochemical transformation）、周期对称性投影（periodic symmetry projection）；采用"接受—拒绝"准则，仅保留能提升约束满足度且对结构其余部分改动受限的更新。在 FlowMM、ET-Flow、Boltz-2 三个骨干上验证，覆盖从头晶体生成、晶体结构预测、分子构象生成与蛋白-配体复合物预测。

Result: 从头晶体生成中空间群分布覆盖度提升；晶体结构预测中目标空间群回收率提升；分子构象生成中有效性以及 E/Z 立体化学准确率提升，同时保持构象集合保真度；蛋白-配体复合物中相较实验参考结构降低配体 E/Z 误差，并保持有竞争力的对接成功率与 PoseBusters 有效性。

Conclusion: AtomSteer 为现有原子生成模型提供统一的化学与晶体学控制手段，作为模型无关的推理期插件，可在不重训、不修改架构的情况下扩展其约束满足能力。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching models offer scalable approaches to generating crystalline materials, molecular conformers, and protein-ligand complexes. However, satisfying crystallographic symmetry, structural geometry, and accurate stereochemistry remains challenging. Satisfying these requirements calls for corrections that preserve the geometry already captured by the pretrained model. In this work, we propose AtomSteer, a test-time inference framework for steering pretrained atomistic generative models with structural constraints. During sampling, AtomSteer corrects intermediate structure predictions with bounded local projections, graph-rigid stereochemical transformations, and projections over periodic symmetries, accepting updates that improve constraint satisfaction while limiting changes to the rest of the structure. We demonstrate AtomSteer across three pretrained backbones, FlowMM, ET-Flow, and Boltz-2, spanning de novo crystal generation and crystal structure prediction, molecular conformer generation, and protein-ligand complex structure prediction. AtomSteer improves space-group distribution coverage in de novo crystal generation and improves target space-group recovery in crystal structure prediction. AtomSteer improves molecular validity and E/Z stereochemical accuracy while retaining conformer ensemble fidelity. For protein-ligand complexes, AtomSteer reduces ligand E/Z errors relative to experimental reference structures while achieving competitive docking success and PoseBusters validity. Our method provides a unified framework for adding chemical and crystallographic control to existing atomistic generative models without retraining or architectural changes.

</details>


### [257] [REVBRAIN: Real-time Probabilistic EEG Forecasting Using Residual Vector Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38558)
*Hanna Pankka,Jaakko Lehtinen,Timo Roine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REVBRAIN，一种基于残差矢量量化(RVQ)的深度概率预测方法，一次生成整个预测窗口的EEG信号，实现实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时脑电预测（如脑状态依赖刺激）需低延迟推理。WaveNet虽精度高且自带不确定性度量，但其逐样本自回归推断需评估模型数十至数百次，推理过慢，无法实时应用。

Method: 用残差矢量量化(RVQ)对输出空间进行token化，将连续EEG预测转化为在token化空间中对离散项的一次性生成，从而在整个预测时域上一并输出预测，避免逐点自回归。

Result: 在alpha频段(8–13 Hz)预测200 ms脑电时间序列：在笔记本GPU上实现实时推理，相较WaveNet加速超过50倍，同时预测精度更优。

Conclusion: REVBRAIN实现实时且高精度、概率化的EEG预测，可使精确概率预测融入BCI应用，为研究大脑功能提供新途径。

Abstract: Real-time forecasting of resting-state EEG signals is required by applications like brain-state-dependent stimulation, where computer generated actions are coupled with ongoing neural oscillations. Recently, the deep learning model WaveNet was proposed for the forecasting task. It shows superior accuracy compared to the prevailing method, the linear autoregressive (AR) model, and provides a built-in uncertainty measure due to its probabilistic modeling framework. However, inference using WaveNet is too slow for real-time use due to its sample-by-sample autoregressive operation: a single forecast requires evaluating the model tens or hundreds of times depending on the length of the prediction window.
  In this paper, we present REVBRAIN, a probabilistic deep learning method that produces forecasts in real time by generating the forecast over the whole prediction horizon at once. This is achieved by tokenizing the output space using residual vector quantization (RVQ) and generating forecasts over the items in the tokenized space.
  We tested REVBRAIN for forecasting 200 ms of EEG time series in the alpha-frequency band (8-13 Hz) and compared our results against WaveNet model. We found that REVBRAIN achieved real-time inference on a laptop GPU and an over $50\times$ speedup compared to WaveNet, while reaching superior prediction accuracy.
  By achieving real-time inference speed, REVBRAIN enables incorporating accurate and probabilistic EEG forecasting into BCI applications, opening up new ways for investigating the functioning of the human brain.

</details>


### [258] [Persona Learning from Feedback for Personalized AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38561)
*Yiran Shen,Yu Xia,Liuyi Yao,Prithviraj Ammanabrolu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Persona Learning from Feedback (PLF)，将可复用的、基于偏好的助手人格学习为共享冻结骨干上的独立 LoRA 适配器，并在部署时用监督路由器按请求选择单个或固定组合的适配器，实现无用户微调的自适应个性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人格化方法依赖 persona prompting 或基于人格条件生成结果的微调，但人格描述本身无法指明在具体回答中应改变或保留什么，也无法判断哪些行为适合当前请求。

Method: 在训练阶段，冻结的 self-teacher 同时以人格规范和针对具体回答的反馈为条件，在各适配器自身生成的 prefix 上提供 token 级监督；引入 feedback gate 将纠正性更新集中在被判定为需要改进的回答上；适配器作为独立 LoRA 挂在共享冻结骨干上。部署阶段用监督路由器根据请求或对话选择单个适配器或固定组合。

Result: 在两类设置上评估：带模型生成 critique 的单轮个性化，以及带模拟学生反应的多轮辅导。两种设置下，PLF 在模型判定的偏好契合度上均优于固定人格策略及 persona prompting、persona distillation 等强基线，且不损害通用能力。

Conclusion: 经反馈训练的行为可被复用，用于自适应助手个性化，无需用户特定微调，并保持通用能力不受损。

Abstract: Personalizing AI assistants requires adapting their behavior to users' preferences and task contexts. Existing work elicits assistant personas through persona prompting or finetuning on persona-conditioned generations. However, persona descriptions alone do not identify what to change or preserve in a particular response, or which behaviors suit the current request. We introduce $\textit{Persona Learning from Feedback (PLF)}$, a framework that learns reusable, preference-grounded assistant personas as independent LoRA adapters on a shared frozen backbone. During training, a frozen self-teacher conditions on persona specifications and response-specific feedback to provide token-level supervision on each adapter's own generated prefixes. A feedback gate focuses corrective updates on responses judged to need improvement. At deployment, a supervised router uses the request or dialogue to select a single adapter or a fixed composition, without user-specific finetuning. We evaluate PLF on single-turn personalization with model-generated critiques and multi-turn tutoring with simulated students' reactions. Across both settings, PLF improves model-judged preference fit over fixed-persona policies and strong baselines including persona prompting and persona distillation, demonstrating how feedback-trained behaviors can be reused for adaptive assistant personalization without degrading general capabilities.

</details>


### [259] [Episodic looking - using reinforcement learning to bias perception enhances few-shot and continual learning with protection from forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38577)
*David Rawlinson,Gideon Kowadlo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于互补学习系统（CLS）架构的模型：由短期记忆（STM）负责记忆近期输入，把泛化交给慢速统计的长期记忆（LTM），二者通过LTM产生的鲁棒广义感知空间实现"委派"，从而缓解泛化与持续适应之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型"可泛化"与"可持续适应统计分布变化的近期输入"之间存在根本矛盾；少样本与持续学习设定会偏置或破坏已学表征，损害对更广上下文的泛化，使模型无法克服输入方差。

Method: 采用互补学习系统（Complementary Learning System, CLS）架构：STM将泛化职责委派给慢速、统计性的LTM；委派通过在与LTM产出的鲁棒、广义化感知（generalized perceptions）空间中工作来实现；由此STM可专注于记忆近期输入并避免记忆间干扰。LTM以ResNet为基线。

Result: 在图像分类任务上，CLS模型在域增量持续学习（domain-incremental continual learning）设定下比基线ResNet LTM更抗遗忘；在少样本设定下比其它持续学习模型泛化更好。但在持续学习设定下，总体准确率略低于现有持续学习模型，只是性能特征组合不同。结果分析表明模型受限于LTM的表征及其表征可塑性。

Conclusion: CLS式STM/LTM分离在抗遗忘与少样本泛化上有优势，但整体精度受LTM表征与表征可塑性制约；未来工作将通过对LTM回放STM记忆（replay）来解决该限制。

Abstract: There is a fundamental contradiction between the desire for models that can generalize, and models that can continuously adapt to recent input with changing statistics. These conditions, captured in few-shot and continual learning settings, can bias or disrupt learned representations. This harms generalization to a broader context, leaving the model unable to overcome input variance.
  This paper proposes a solution based on the Complementary Learning System architecture (CLS), in which a Short-Term Memory (STM) delegates generalization to a slow, statistical Long-Term Memory (LTM). Delegation is achieved by working in a space of robust, generalized perceptions produced by the LTM. With this separation, the STM can focus on memorizing recent input and avoiding interference between memories.
  We show that on an image classification task, the resulting CLS model is more resistant to forgetting in a domain-incremental continual learning setting than a baseline ResNet LTM, and generalizes more effectively in a few-shot setting than other continual learners.
  In the continual setting, overall accuracy is slightly worse than existing continual learning models, but with a different mix of performance characteristics. Results analysis suggests that the model is limited by the representations and representational plasticity of the LTM. In future work, we will seek to address this by replaying STM memories to the LTM.

</details>


### [260] [Dual DeltaNet: Value-Side Memory Reorganization for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38578)
*Yixiao Qian,Yaxin Zhang,Song Chen,Jiaxu Liu,Shengze Cai,Chao Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual DeltaNet (DDN)，在Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) 基础上引入双边delta规则：键侧delta修正当前键关联的值，值侧delta按当前值的选定特征重组地址，并推导分块并行训练算法与rank-2递推实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力与状态空间模型以固定大小的循环状态替代softmax注意力，但需谨慎管理状态以保留有用历史关联并抑制干扰。delta规则虽通过修正每个键对应的值来降低干扰，却未显式考虑相关值特征在状态中的分布方式。

Method: 将状态S∈R^{d_k×d_v}视为d_k个地址、每个存储d_v维值的"地址库"：键侧读取S^T k检索k关联的值，值侧读取S e则聚合存储值与选择器e匹配的地址。据此在KDA上增加互补的值侧delta规则，形成双边delta更新，并推导chunkwise并行训练算法及提升推理算术强度的rank-2递推。

Result: 在语言建模、零样本下游任务与检索任务上，DDN相较KDA、GDN-2和Mamba-3取得最强综合性能，且相对KDA仅带来适度的训练与推理开销。

Conclusion: 显式建模"值侧"读取为状态管理提供了新维度，双边delta规则可在有限额外开销下提升线性注意力/SSM类模型的历史关联保留与抗干扰能力。

Abstract: Linear attention and state space models (SSMs) offer efficient alternatives to softmax attention by maintaining a fixed-size recurrent state, but this design requires careful management to preserve useful historical associations and limit interference. The delta rule helps reduce interference through correction of the value associated with each key, but it does not explicitly account for how related value features are distributed across the state. We observe that a state $\boldsymbol{S}\in\mathbb{R}^{d_k\times d_v}$ can be viewed as a bank of $d_k$ addresses storing $d_v$-dimensional values. While a key-side read $\boldsymbol{S}^\top\boldsymbol{k}$ retrieves the value associated with $\boldsymbol{k}$, a value-side read $\boldsymbol{S}\boldsymbol{e}$ aggregates addresses whose stored value features match the selector $\boldsymbol{e}$. Based on this observation, we propose Dual DeltaNet (DDN), which extends Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) with a two-sided delta rule. The key-side delta rule reads with the current key to correct its associated value, while the complementary value-side delta rule reads with selected features of the current value to reorganize the addresses it occupies. We derive a chunkwise parallel training algorithm and a rank-2 recurrence that increases arithmetic intensity during inference. Compared with KDA, GDN-2, and Mamba-3, DDN achieves the strongest overall performance across language modeling, zero-shot downstream tasks, and retrieval, while incurring modest training and inference overhead relative to KDA. Code: https://github.com/ReichtumQian/DualDeltaNet

</details>


### [261] [Rethinking LLM Jailbreak Evaluation: Continuous Risk Landscapes Beyond Binary Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38595)
*Yukai Zhou,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出用连续、上下文条件化的"首token分支风险景观"替代二元ASR指标来刻画LLM越狱，从而揭示解码器暴露度与安全重路由能力两个新性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM越狱评估沿用判别式任务中的二元成功率（ASR）指标，但生成任务的本质是输出一个开放轨迹分布，单次采样只揭示其中一条路径，二元指标因此掩盖了可能生成空间的结构。

Method: 在首token分支上构建连续、上下文条件化的风险景观：固定候选开头（opening），对其后续进行重复采样以估计各分支的风险值，从而得到覆盖"开头分布—续写风险"的连续表征。

Result: 该视角暴露出两个被二元ASR遮蔽的性质：当前解码器暴露度与安全重路由能力；并在多目标模型与多攻击上发现：表面安全输出下存在高风险分支；不同攻击以性质不同的方式重塑模型的开头分布；防御前最强的攻击在自适应防御介入后未必仍最强。该表征还提供了统一比较干净提示、攻击变体与攻击强度的基准。

Conclusion: 越狱应被重新理解为对续写风险景观的形变（deformation），该框架为理解越狱行为与构建更安全的语言模型提供了更具信息量的基础。

Abstract: A binary success indicator is a natural representation for discriminative tasks such as image classification under adversarial perturbations, where each input is mapped to a single prediction. However, generative tasks are fundamentally different: an LLM jailbreak prompt induces a distribution over open-ended trajectories, while a sampled response reveals only one path. Yet LLM jailbreak evaluation typically retains the binary response indicator underlying attack success rate, obscuring the surrounding structure of possible generations. We introduce a continuous, context-conditioned risk landscape over first-token branches, estimated by fixing candidate openings and repeatedly sampling their continuations. This continuous risk-landscape perspective reveals two complementary properties: current decoder exposure and safe rerouting capacity, which have been obscured by binary-indicator-based ASR. The resulting representation also provides a common basis for comparing clean prompts, attacked variants, and attack strength against a known adaptive defense. Across multiple target models and jailbreak attacks, we uncover risky branches beneath apparently safe outputs, identify qualitatively different ways attacks reshape the model's opening distribution, and show that attacks strongest before a defense do not necessarily remain the strongest after the adaptive defense reacts. These findings recast jailbreaks as deformations of the surrounding continuation-risk landscape, establishing a more informative foundation for understanding jailbreak behavior and developing safer language models.

</details>


### [262] [Two-Stage Clipping: Linear-Time Approximate and Exact Mixed-Norm Projections for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38598)
*Michel Barlaud,Jean-Paul Marmorat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两阶段裁剪算子TSC，以O(n^2)复杂度实现ℓ1,∞结构稀疏，替代需O(nm log(nm))的精确近端投影，理论界与实验验证其高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer大模型计算开销大，ℓ1,∞投影可诱导列稀疏以压缩矩阵，但现有近端方法对n×m矩阵需O(nm log(nm))运算，计算与能效低。

Method: 受双层优化启发，将计算分解为两步：先对组级量投影以分配全局稀疏预算并确定活跃组；再以所得值为组内系数的裁剪阈值。并非严格求解双层优化问题。

Result: 对方阵注意力矩阵复杂度为O(n^2)，与系数数量线性；给出TSC与精确近端投影差异的理论界，合成数据显示额外欧氏近似代价很小；基准测试显示相对近端基线显著加速，并验证了范数恒等式。

Conclusion: TSC是高效可扩展的结构稀疏算子，在保持稀疏结构质量的同时大幅降低计算开销，适用于大模型矩阵稀疏化。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) based on Transformer architectures have achieved remarkable performance across artificial intelligence tasks, but their computational cost remains a major bottleneck. Structured sparsification provides a promising approach to reducing this cost. In particular, $\ell_{1,\infty}$ projections can induce column-wise sparsity in model matrices, but existing proximal methods (Prox) typically require $\mathcal{O}(nm\log(nm))$ operations for matrices in $\mathbb{R}^{n\times m}$, limiting their computational and energy efficiency.
  In this paper, we introduce Two-Stage Clipping (TSC), an efficient structured sparsification operator inspired by bilevel optimization. Rather than solving a bilevel optimization problem in the strict sense, TSC decomposes the computation into two successive stages. First, group-level quantities are projected to allocate the global sparsity budget and identify active groups. Second, the resulting values are used as group-wise clipping thresholds for the matrix coefficients. For square attention matrices ($n \times n$), the complexity of our TSC method is $O(n^2)$, which is linear in the number of matrix coefficients.
  We establish a theoretical bound on the discrepancy between TSC and the exact proximal projection and show on synthetic data that the additional Euclidean approximation cost remains small in practice. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate substantial runtime improvements over proximal baselines and validate the theoretical norm identities underlying the proposed construction.

</details>


### [263] [On-Policy Self-Distillation with Teacher Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38637)
*Shufan Shen,Zhongni Hou,Chao Bi,Yufei Zhang,Guojun Yin,Shuhui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)训练中性能无法持续提升的问题，作者将其归因于模型自身"自教学"能力不足、对部分样本给出不可靠指导，并提出Teacher-Evolution Self-Distillation (TESD)，通过教师导向优化与自适应学生导向优化两条路径提升自蒸馏监督质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD通过由特权上下文提示的self-teacher将学生模型的token级分布对齐，缓解序列级后训练范式中的奖励稀疏问题，但训练过程中往往无法获得持续的性能增益。作者发现根因在于模型的self-teaching能力有限，在某些样本上产生不可靠的指导（unreliable guidance）。

Method: 提出TESD：让self-teacher生成rollout并基于任务正确性（task correctness）与上下文独立性（context independence）接收环境反馈。第一，以这些奖励作为训练目标，进行教师导向优化（teacher-oriented optimization），在蒸馏过程中持续增强模型的自教学能力；第二，利用这些奖励估计教师指导可靠性，实施自适应的学生导向优化（student-oriented optimization），对指导不可靠的样本进行降权。

Result: 在多个数据集与多种模型规模上的大量实验表明，TESD稳定优于代表性的token级与序列级方法。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了在OPSD中提升监督质量（supervision quality）的必要性；通过奖励驱动的教师演化与可靠性加权，可有效解决自蒸馏中的不可靠指导问题。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD), which aligns a student model's token-level distribution with that of its self-teacher prompted by privileged context, has emerged as a promising direction to mitigate reward sparsity in sequence-level post-training paradigms. However, OPSD often fails to achieve continuous performance gains during training. In this paper, we find that the model's limited self-teaching capability underlies this phenomenon by generating unreliable guidance on certain samples. To address this problem, we propose Teacher-Evolution Self-Distillation (TESD), which enables the self-teacher to generate rollouts and receive environmental feedback based on task correctness and context independence. First, using these rewards as training objectives, we perform teacher-oriented optimization to continuously enhance the model's self-teaching capability during distillation. Second, by estimating teacher guidance reliability from these rewards, we apply adaptive student-oriented optimization to down-weight samples with unreliable guidance. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets and model scales show that TESD consistently outperforms representative token-level and sequence-level methods, highlighting the necessity of improving supervision quality in OPSD.

</details>


### [264] [CFKS: Continuum-Feature Kernel PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38644)
*Vikas Dwivedi,Balaji Srinivasan,Monica Sigovan,Bruno Sixou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFKS框架：将高斯RBF特征层取连续极限（解析边缘化有界连续特征集），直接以连续特征均值核及其算子作用形式构建确定性PDE求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络PDE求解器需人为设定层数、每层神经元数（特征数）及特征中心选择，带来表示任意性，缺乏确定性与可复现性。

Method: 固定单个高斯RBF特征层，令特征数趋于连续极限，解析地对有界连续高斯特征集做边缘化，得到由给定高斯宽度与有界中心分布参数化的连续特征均值核；线性PDE构造对称算子-核配点系统并以零空间强制物理约束；非线性PDE用线性化初始化+基于解析核导数与雅可比的最小二乘残差最小化；辅以残差引导的配点加密、时空板顺序推进、多尺度核细化。

Result: 有限特征实验证实经验值与算子Gram矩阵收敛于连续极限对应量；数值算例涵盖振荡与多尺度ODE、不规则区域Poisson与双调和问题、奇异摄动、瞬态输运方程及粘性Burgers方程。

Conclusion: CFKS在有限高斯RBF表示与连续特征核PDE求解器之间建立了直接的计算桥梁。

Abstract: Neural-network-based PDE solvers require architectural choices, particularly the number of layers and neurons per layer. Fixing a single Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) feature layer and taking the number of features to the continuum limit removes the need to prescribe a finite feature count or select individual feature centers, thereby reducing representation arbitrariness. Motivated by this principle, we introduce Continuum-Feature Kernel Solver (CFKS), a deterministic framework for PDEs obtained by analytically marginalizing a bounded continuum of Gaussian RBF features. CFKS works directly with the resulting continuum-feature mean kernel and its operator-applied counterparts, parameterized by prescribed Gaussian widths and bounded center distributions. For linear PDEs, CFKS leads to symmetric operator-kernel collocation systems with null-space enforcement of physical constraints. Nonlinear PDEs are addressed through linearized initialization and nonlinear least-squares residual minimization using analytical kernel derivatives and Jacobians. Residual-guided collocation enrichment and sequential space--time-slab marching provide additional resolution strategies, while prescribed multiscale kernel refinement helps resolve localized nonlinear structures. Finite-feature experiments confirm convergence of empirical value and operator Gram matrices toward their continuum counterparts. Numerical studies include oscillatory and multiscale ODEs, irregular-domain Poisson and biharmonic problems, singular perturbations, transient transport equations, and viscous Burgers' equation. The results establish CFKS as a direct computational connection between finite Gaussian-RBF representations and continuum-feature kernel PDE solvers. Codes: https://github.com/vikas-dwivedi-2022/CFKS_Codes

</details>


### [265] [Logical Guidance of Stochastic Interpolants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38648)
*Gerrit Gerhartz,Francesco Alesiani,Jonathan Warrell,Henrik Christiansen,Matheus Ferraz,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 LOGSI 框架，解决 Flow Matching / Stochastic Interpolants 中逻辑引导无法精确复合速度场的问题，通过基于分数的再平衡机制实现逻辑约束采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑引导在扩散模型中可精确复合条件分数，但 FM 与 SI 学习的是速度场而非分数。作者发现否定与异或可在任意插值上精确复合速度，而合取（及其构成的公式）仅在 Gaussian 路径上精确，一般情况下会产生"漂移交互误差"，导致复合速度采样偏离逻辑目标。

Method: 理论分析不同逻辑算子下速度复合的精确性；提出 LOGSI，以 score-based re-equilibration 作为原则性机制，并回退到 Gaussian 路径速度规则作为近似传输预测器；在非 Gaussian bridge 问题中交替执行传输步与分数再平衡步。

Result: 在图像迁移（EMNIST→CMNIST、CelebA）与 QM9 源到目标分子编辑（带逻辑属性约束）任务上，相同网络评估预算下 LOGSI 相比复合速度基线提升了规则符合度，同时在图像任务上提升生成保真度。

Conclusion: LOGSI 可广泛适用于需要引导、且逻辑约束组合自然的随机插值场景，为 FM/SI 的逻辑引导提供了原则化解决方案。

Abstract: Guidance and composition of generative models have applications in visual design, chemistry, and biology. Logical guidance composes conditional scores exactly for diffusion models. However, Flow Matching (FM) and Stochastic Interpolants (SI) learn velocity fields. We show that negation and exclusive disjunction admit exact conditional velocity composition on any interpolant, whereas conjunction, and therefore any formula containing one, is exact on Gaussian paths but in general incurs a drift-interaction error, so composed velocities need not sample the logical target. LOGSI solves this by using score-based re-equilibration as our principled mechanism and falling back to the Gaussian-path velocity rule as an approximate transport predictor. In non-Gaussian bridge problems, generation alternates transport steps with score re-equilibration steps. On image trans fer (EMNIST to CMNIST, CelebA) and QM9 source-to-target molecular editing with logical attribute constraints, LOGSI improves rule conformity at the same network-evaluation budget over composed-velocity baselines, while also improving fidelity on the image tasks. More broadly, LOGSI applies wherever stochastic interpolants need guidance and logical combinations of constraints are natural.

</details>


### [266] [ExplainCC: Explaining Correlated Clusters by Node Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38667)
*Nadeesha Shyami Kumari Herath Mudiyanselage,Florian Adriaens,Nikolaj Tatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究带标签数据下的可解释相关性聚类（Correlation Clustering, CC）问题，提出以标签树（本质为按标签划分对象的决策树）解释聚类结果，要求划分只由可由标签树解释的簇组成，并最小化CC目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类算法日益需要其结果与决策对人类用户清晰可解释；而现有CC方法未利用伴随对象的标签信息来提供解释性。

Method: 形式化该可解释CC问题，并从理论角度分析，证明多项近似困难性结果（包括约束树规模的情形）；提出自顶向下递归的贪心算法，每一步寻找局部最优划分，并通过精心维护计数器来高效计算最佳分裂。

Result: 理论方面给出多项近似困难性结论；实验方面算法可在数分钟内处理超过百万条边的图，在合成数据上能恢复真实划分，并在真实世界网络中产生可解释的簇。

Conclusion: 将标签树作为解释结构可在保证可解释性的同时有效求解相关性聚类，贪心+计数器维护的策略兼顾效率与效果，适用于大规模网络。

Abstract: An increasingly important requirement for clustering algorithms is that decisions and outcomes need to be clearly understandable for human users. We address this requirement for the Correlation Clustering (CC) problem, in case the objects to be clustered are accompanied by labels. In CC one seeks to partition a set of objects into clusters based on pairwise similarity and dissimilarity information. Our proposed model only considers clusters that can be explained by a label tree, which is essentially a decision tree that partitions the objects according to labels. The goal is to find an explainable partition of the data that minimizes the CC objective function. We formalize this problem from a theoretical viewpoint, and prove several hardness of approximation results, also when constraining the size of the tree. Our proposed greedy method finds locally optimal splits, and works recursively in a top-down approach. To efficiently compute the best split, we carefully maintain certain counters. An experimental evaluation demonstrates its effectiveness. The proposed algorithm processes graphs with over million edges in a few minutes, can find the ground truth in synthetic data, and is able to produce interpretable clusters in real world networks.

</details>


### [267] [On the Generalization Gap of Streaming Federated Learning with Markovian Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38686)
*Masoud Kavian,Milad Sefidgaran,Youssef Attia El Hili,Malik Tiomoko,Abdellatif Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用信息论与率失真方法，刻画马尔可夫数据流下流式联邦学习聚合模型的期望泛化差距，揭示通信频率与泛化之间的权衡，并给出LNTP与SANTP的专门界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式联邦学习可在不保留大规模本地数据的前提下从持续到达的数据训练共享模型，但数据流常具时间依赖性，其对泛化性的影响尚缺乏理论理解。

Method: 采用信息论与率失真（rate-distortion）技术推导交叉熵损失下聚合模型的期望泛化差距上界；框架允许各客户端具有异质马尔可夫动力学，并将时间依赖性与本地学习中获得的信息相区分；进一步将界特化到线性下一词预测（LNTP）与自注意力下一词预测（SANTP），并借助历史状态过程分析在适当混合条件下得到依赖上下文长度更优的精细界。

Result: 界显式量化了客户端数量、通信轮数、样本量、数据维度、混合时间与上下文长度的影响：时间依赖性通过混合时间的平方根进入界中；出现通信—泛化权衡，频繁聚合虽利于优化却可能削弱泛化保证；在固定总数据预算下，分布式流式学习相对集中式流式学习可能具有优势。GPT风格语言模型预训练（TinyStories）与真实时间序列预测实验验证了协作与通信频率在时间依赖下对泛化的影响。

Conclusion: 时间依赖性可由混合时间主导刻画；通信频率存在泛化代价，需在优化与泛化间权衡；分布式流式学习在数据预算受限时可优于集中式，历史状态过程分析可改进上下文长度依赖。

Abstract: Streaming federated learning (SFL) allows clients to train a shared model from continuously arriving data without retaining large local datasets. Such streams often exhibit temporal dependence, yet its effect on generalization remains poorly understood. In this paper we study the expected generalization gap of the aggregated model when client data follow Markov processes, using information-theoretic and rate-distortion techniques. Our framework accommodates heterogeneous Markovian dynamics across clients and distinguishes temporal dependence from information acquired during local learning. We establish bounds under cross-entropy loss, specializing them to Linear Next-Token Prediction (LNTP) and Self-Attention Next-Token Prediction (SANTP). These results quantify the effects of client count, communication rounds, sample size, data dimension, mixing time, and context length. In particular, they reveal a communication--generalization tradeoff: although frequent aggregation may help optimization, it can weaken generalization guarantees. They also suggest a potential advantage of distributed over centralized streaming learning under a fixed total data budget. Temporal dependence enters through the square root of the mixing time. By analyzing the history-state process, we obtain refined bounds with improved context-length dependence under suitable mixing conditions. Experiments on GPT-style language-model pretraining with TinyStories and real-world time-series forecasting illustrate how collaboration and communication frequency influence generalization under temporal dependence

</details>


### [268] [SatOps-Decision: Low-Latency Discriminative Decisions for Onboard Satellite Communication Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38697)
*Lifan Mei,Yuxuan Li,Jiaxian Tu,Shenghe Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SatOps-Decision：将 Jev 风格通用判别式决策模型首次用于卫星通信运维，并构建首个专用基准；局部适配的 Laya 编码器加原生决策头单次前向即完成答案选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星通信运维需要低延迟决策；生成式大模型逐 token 解码延迟高，而判别式打分模型可单次前向完成选择，但此前缺乏面向卫星运维的判别式决策模型与专用评测基准。

Method: 方法核心为局部适配（locally adapted）的 Laya 编码器配原生决策头，作为学习式打分器在候选运维答案中择优。基准由人工撰写的运维契约、受控状态变更与独立核验标签构成，具备可审计性；评测指标为完整请求延迟与原始决策准确率，主实验统一使用与训练/开发集分离的 1,024 条留出问题。

Result: 决策中位延迟 90.3 ms，对比 Qwen3.5-0.8B 生成的 493.9 ms 与 OneJev1.6-0.8B 的 388.4 ms，加速 5.47× 与 4.30×（FP32/MPS 实测中位请求时间）。平均原始准确率 85.3%，显著高于冻结 Laya 42.5%、Qwen 生成 40.7%、OneJev 58.6%；作为云端参考，原始 Jev 1.14/1.13 达 87.4/81.9%。

Conclusion: 判别式单次前向打分在卫星运维决策中可实现数量级的延迟优势，且准确率接近云端大模型参考水平，验证了 Jev 式通用判别决策范式在专业运维领域的可行性；局限在于评测限于自建基准与 FP32/MPS 实现。

Abstract: We propose SatOps-Decision, a method for low-latency decisions in satellite communication operations. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first application of Jev-style general-purpose discriminative decision models to satellite operations, and the accompanying benchmark is the first dedicated to this approach. The learned scorer, a locally adapted Laya encoder with its native decision head, selects among supplied operational answers in a single forward pass. The benchmark combines authored operational contracts, controlled state changes and independently checked labels in an auditable construction. Evaluation prioritizes complete-request latency and raw decision accuracy; all main accuracy comparisons use the same 1,024 held-out questions, separate from training and development. SatOps-Decision's local scorer has a median latency of 90.3 ms per decision, versus 493.9 ms for Qwen3.5-0.8B generation and 388.4 ms for OneJev1.6-0.8B: $5.47\times$ and $4.30\times$ speedups based on median request times in the measured FP32/MPS implementations. Its mean raw accuracy is 85.3%, versus 42.5% for frozen Laya, 40.7% for Qwen generation and 58.6% for OneJev. As cloud accuracy references, original Jev 1.14/1.13 reach 87.4/81.9%.

</details>


### [269] [Latent Decision Flow Rewards for Clinical Guideline Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38709)
*Gaurav Bhatt,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GUIDER，通过联合优化决策流控制器与推理策略，利用指南导出的多条决策流计算过程奖励并以答案正确性门控，解决路径—答案鸿沟与路径多重性，在 MedGUIDE 等基准上超越临床强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践指南包含基于证据的条件决策流，是训练推理模型的天然过程监督来源，但存在两个障碍：(1) 路径—答案鸿沟——符合指南的决策流本身并不能唯一确定某患者的正确答答案；(2) 路径多重性——多个有效决策流可支持同一临床决策。现有方法只能部分应对：答案奖励只监督最终决策；基于单一参考流的过程奖励只覆盖每例一个有效路径；对标注流做监督微调则把模型限制在单一轨迹上，甚至降低答案准确率。

Method: GUIDER 同时优化一个决策流控制器和一个推理策略。控制器从指南导出的监督中学习生成合理的决策流；策略为每个病例生成决策流和最终答案。过程奖励由策略在控制器生成的多条决策流上计算（应对路径多重性），并以上述答案正确性作为门控（应对路径—答案鸿沟）。联合训练使过程监督可随策略共同演化。

Result: 在 MedGUIDE 上，Qwen3-14B 骨干的 GUIDER 达到 82.5 加权准确率与 73.5 指南依从度，分别比最强的临床强化学习基线高 3.3 和 3.1 分。在 Qwen3-14B 与 Gemma4-12B 骨干上，GUIDER 在域外基准 AMEGA-LLM、RealMedQA 和 CancerGUIDE 上同样优于 CRPO、MRPO 与 FaithMed。

Conclusion: 通过联合训练控制器与策略、并用多路径过程奖励加答案门控，GUIDER 有效利用指南作为过程监督，兼顾答案准确性与指南依从性，且具备跨骨干与跨基准的泛化能力。

Abstract: Clinical practice guidelines encode evidence-based recommendations as conditional decision flows, which makes them a natural source of process supervision for training reasoning models. Two properties of clinical reasoning make this supervision difficult to use. A decision flow consistent with a guideline does not by itself determine the correct answer for a given patient, which we call the path--answer gap, and several valid decision flows can support the same clinical decision, which we call path multiplicity.
