Current training approaches address parts of this problem. Answer rewards supervise the final decision, and process rewards computed against a reference flow supervise one valid decision flow per case. Supervised fine-tuning on annotated flows teaches guideline-consistent reasoning but constrains the model to one trajectory per case and can reduce answer accuracy. We introduce GUIDER, a method that jointly optimizes a decision flow controller and a reasoning policy. The controller learns to generate plausible decision flows from guideline-derived supervision, and the policy learns to generate a decision flow and a final answer for each patient case. GUIDER computes the process reward for the policy over several controller-generated flows, which accounts for path multiplicity, and gates this reward on answer correctness, which addresses the path--answer gap. Joint training allows the process supervision to evolve with the policy. On MedGUIDE, GUIDER with Qwen3-14B achieves 82.5 weighted accuracy and 73.5 guideline adherence, 3.3 and 3.1 points above the strongest clinical reinforcement learning baseline. With Qwen3-14B and Gemma4-12B backbones, GUIDER also improves over CRPO, MRPO, and FaithMed on the out-of-domain benchmarks AMEGA-LLM, RealMedQA, and CancerGUIDE.

</details>


### [270] [Adversarial Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38732)
*Mohammad Sadegh Akhondzadeh,Soroush H. Zargarbashi,Aleksandar Bojchevski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对抗自蒸馏（ASD），将模型自身的安全拒绝行为从干净提示扩展到对抗扰动空间，在在线采样访问的前缀（含顺从性开头）上进行蒸馏，从而防御包括顺从性预填充注入在内的攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐模型可被对抗扰动绕过；对抗训练是标准防御手段，但由于语言生成的自回归特性，对模板式拒绝做监督微调未必真正教会模型拒绝——证据是当攻击者注入顺从性预填充（compliant prefill）时，对抗训练后的模型仍会顺从。

Method: ASD 让教师模型（同一模型在干净提示下，可默认拒绝，或在上下文中加入额外指令辅助拒绝）产生拒绝知识，再通过在线策略采样，将这一知识蒸馏到学生模型（受攻击的同一模型）实际访问的前缀位置上，这些前缀可能包括顺从性开头。

Result: ASD 的攻击成功率低于当前最先进防御方法，同时在多项选择任务上准确率损失很小，并且即便在出现顺从性开头之后也能恢复拒绝。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏式的在线前缀级监督能有效把安全行为泛化到对抗扰动空间，克服模板微调在自回归生成下的局限，实现更具鲁棒性的拒绝能力。

Abstract: Even though current safety-aligned language models are able to refuse harmful requests, their guard can still be bypassed by adversarial perturbations. As in other domains, adversarial training is considered a standard defense. However, due to the autoregressive nature of language generation, supervised finetuning on template refusals does not necessarily teach the model to refuse. Evidence of this is that the adversarially trained model still complies when the attacker injects a compliant prefill. We propose Adversarial Self-Distillation (ASD), which extends a model's own safe behavior to the space of adversarial perturbations. The teacher (the model itself given the clean prompt) can refuse the harmful request by default, or with the help of an additional instruction in its context. ASD distills this knowledge to the student (which is the same model under attack) at the prefixes that the student visits (due to on-policy sampling). These potentially include compliant openings. ASD achieves lower attack success than state-of-the-art defenses with little loss in multiple-choice accuracy, and it becomes able to recover a refusal even after a compliant opening.

</details>


### [271] [Alpha-TCM: A Nine-Layer Framework for TCM-Guided Multimodal Inference and Structured-Prior Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38754)
*Yucheng Liu,Everett X. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Alpha-TCM：以"可审计系统应分离领域知识、案例证据、概率推断与下游权限"为核心论点，给出九层中医导向系统架构，并用受控合成原型实例化前五层以审计"知识—证据"接口；结果显示上下文知识在小样本合成设定下有益，但不构成贝叶斯特异性优势的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统常将领域知识、案例数据、概率推断与决策权限混为一体，导致知识—证据界面不可审计、难以定位失效来源；作者希望以分层架构明确各层信息契约与失效边界，并在可控条件下检验"上下文知识是否真正带来增益"，而非默认概率（贝叶斯）框架优越。

Method: 提出九层中医导向架构（语义定义、多模态采集、质量控制、带来源标注的知识、概率推断、纵向更新、受限决策、语言交互、人类治理），每层规定信息契约、失效边界、依赖关系与验证状态；并构建受控合成原型实现第1–5层：四个条件高斯编码器分别处理合成舌象、脉波、问诊字段与文本，冻结桥接层预测五维合成结局，版本化上下文先验独立于渲染器编译，融合前剔除重复的参考先验信息；设置等信息对照、先验正则、分级污染与结构腐蚀实验，检验上下文收益、贝叶斯特异性与冲突检测。

Result: 10组配对随机种子×4种训练规模下，上下文知识在600与1200例时相对边际委员会融合降低平均绝对误差，但相对等信息神经对照无优势；完全错误的上下文在各规模均损害性能；先验效应不一致，冲突分数未能追踪个案层面的损害。故结果仅支持低数据合成设定中的上下文结构作用，不支持"贝叶斯特异性优势"。

Conclusion: Alpha-TCM为系统设计提案而非完整临床实现；合成原型仅确立知识—证据接口的可审计性方法与上下文结构的有限收益，不对中医理论及临床、诊断、治疗、因果、纵向或迁移效度作出任何主张。

Abstract: Alpha-TCM makes two contributions under a central thesis: auditable systems should separate domain knowledge, case evidence, probabilistic inference, and downstream authority. First, it proposes a nine-layer TCM-oriented architecture covering semantic definitions, multimodal acquisition, quality control, provenance-bearing knowledge, probabilistic inference, longitudinal updating, constrained decisions, language interaction, and human governance. Each layer specifies its information contract, failure boundary, dependencies, and validation status. This is a system-design proposal, not a claim of complete clinical implementation.
  Second, a controlled synthetic prototype instantiates Layers 1-5 to audit the knowledge-evidence interface. Four conditional-Gaussian encoders process synthetic tongue images, pulse waveforms, inquiry fields, and text, while a frozen bridge predicts a five-dimensional synthetic outcome. A versioned contextual prior is compiled independently of the renderer. Fusion removes duplicated reference-prior information before adding context. Equal-information controls, prior regularization, graded contamination, and structural corruption test contextual benefits, Bayesian specificity, and conflict detection.
  Across ten paired seeds and four training sizes, contextual knowledge improved mean absolute error over marginal committee fusion at 600 and 1,200 cases, but not over equal-information neural controls. Fully incorrect context harmed performance at every size, while prior effects were inconsistent and conflict scores did not track case-level damage. The results support contextual structure in low-data synthetic settings, not a specifically Bayesian advantage. The prototype establishes no claims about TCM theory or clinical, diagnostic, therapeutic, causal, longitudinal, or transfer validity.

</details>


### [272] [Rennala NSGD: Asynchronous Stochastic Optimization Beyond Euclidean Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38773)
*Igor Sokolov,Alexander Tyurin,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在任意范数空间中分析小批量随机梯度下降（SGD）的收敛性，借助κ-正则范数空间框架给出高概率 oracle 复杂度界，并将结论推广到半异步分布式算法 Rennala NSGD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon、Shampoo 等矩阵感知优化器的实证成功表明非欧几何对深度学习（含大模型训练）有重要价值，但一般范数空间中的随机优化理论（尤其分布式场景）仍很匮乏，非欧几何下的噪声假设与复杂度界尚不清晰。

Method: 针对有限维空间任意范数下的光滑非凸优化，分析小批量 SGD；采用κ-正则范数空间框架，把轻尾噪声条件直接用更新范数所诱导的对偶范数表述，使复杂度界通过对偶空间正则常数κ显式反映几何依赖；在此基础上提出半异步分布式算法 Rennala NSGD，在固定且异构的 worker 计算时间下建立墙钟时间（wall-clock）复杂度保证。

Result: 得到高概率 oracle 复杂度界：欧氏情形下恢复到标准小批量 SGD 速率（至多对数因子）；在某些非欧几何与噪声模型下，相比把噪声假设转译为欧氏范数所得的界更紧，可将线性维数依赖降为对数依赖。对 Rennala NSGD，在固定异构计算时间下给出墙钟时间复杂度保证；欧氏情形（κ=1）下其界与经典 Rennala SGD 的参数依赖相匹配（至多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 论文为一般范数空间中的随机非凸优化提供了统一的高概率复杂度分析，并自然延伸到异步分布式训练，说明对偶范数下的轻尾噪声假设与κ正则性可准确刻画几何带来的影响。

Abstract: The empirical success of matrix-aware optimizers, such as Muon (Jordan et al., 2024) and Shampoo (arXiv:1802.09568), highlights the potential of non-Euclidean geometry for deep learning, including large language model (LLM) training. Despite this progress, the theoretical understanding of stochastic optimization in general normed spaces remains limited, particularly in distributed settings. We analyze minibatch stochastic gradient descent (SGD) for smooth nonconvex optimization with respect to an arbitrary norm on a finite-dimensional space. Using the framework of $κ$-regular normed spaces (arXiv:0809.0813), we derive high-probability oracle complexity bounds under a light-tail condition stated directly in the dual norm induced by the update norm. The bounds make the dependence on geometry explicit through the regularity constant $κ$ of the dual space. In the Euclidean case, our bound recovers the standard minibatch SGD rate up to logarithmic factors. For some non-Euclidean geometries and noise models, it is sharper than bounds obtained by converting a Euclidean noise assumption, reducing a linear dimension dependence to a logarithmic one. Building on this framework, we introduce Rennala NSGD, a semi-asynchronous distributed algorithm, and establish wall-clock time complexity guarantees under fixed, heterogeneous worker computation times. In the Euclidean case ($κ=1$), our bound matches the parameter dependence of classical Rennala SGD up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [273] [Auditable Hierarchical Inference with Multimodal Evidence and Bayesian Active Inquiry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38774)
*Yucheng Liu,Everett X. Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出合成评测框架，将客观多模态推理与主动询问分离，用成本敏感期望信息增益(cost-EIG)在33个细粒度证型上做贝叶斯收窄：家族证型准确率87.0%→95.5%，精确细证型42.5%（基线33.0%），但提问数更多，问题效率假设未通过，仅为可复现的合成流程而非临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多模态分类器假设所有有用证据一次性可得，而临床问诊本质上是序贯性的。作者希望把“客观多模态推理”与“主动询问”两类问题解耦评估，避免混淆两者表现，并在受控合成数据上检验主动问询是否带来增益。

Method: 1）构建合成评测框架：目标模型将合成的舌象、双侧多压力脉象和声学观测映射到8个证型家族；2）带溯源的Knowledge Core与仅训练可见的答案模型，支持基于25个类型化问题的贝叶斯推断，从33个细粒度证型中收窄；3）7个安全性问题排除在诊断后验之外；4）共享症状因子较弱，限制细证型对客观表型的直接影响，并设置“仅客观”探针检测泄漏；5）预注册600例合成评测，对比cost-EIG与未优化的固定顺序基线，并做缺失音频路由、全缺失弃答工作流不变式、细证型答案似然置换等消融。

Result: 客观家族准确率87.0%；cost-EIG将家族准确率提升至95.5%、精确细证型准确率至42.5%，固定顺序基线分别为92.7%与33.0%；选择性预测在95.3%覆盖率下家族准确率96.9%；但cost-EIG平均提问数更多（9.795 vs 9.352）；缺失音频路由下家族准确率仍达94.0%；全客观模态缺失时工作流不变式强制弃答；置换细证型答案似然后家族准确率降至78.7%，提示存在信息泄漏；6项冻结检查中5项通过，问题效率假设失败；因仅保留聚合而非配对病例级输出，比较仅为描述性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了可复现的合成工作流，证明主动询问可显著提升家族级与细粒度证型判别，但未能实现问题效率优势；结果不构成临床诊断或治疗有效性证据，且需配对病例级输出才能进行统计比较。

Abstract: Most multimodal classifiers assume that all useful evidence is available upfront, whereas clinical inquiry is sequential. We present a synthetic evaluation framework separating objective multimodal inference from active inquiry. An objective model maps synthetic tongue, bilateral multipressure pulse, and acoustic observations to eight syndrome families. A provenance-bearing Knowledge Core and training-only answer model support Bayesian narrowing over 33 fine terms using 25 typed questions, while seven safety questions remain outside the diagnostic posterior. Weak shared symptom factors limit fine-term influence on objective phenotypes, and an objective-only probe tests leakage.
  In a preregistered 600-case synthetic evaluation, objective family accuracy was 87.0%. Cost-sensitive expected information gain (cost-EIG) raised family accuracy to 95.5% and exact fine-term accuracy to 42.5%, versus 92.7% and 33.0% for an unoptimized fixed-order baseline. Selective family accuracy reached 96.9% at 95.3% coverage. However, cost-EIG asked more questions on average than fixed order (9.795 versus 9.352). Missing-audio routing retained 94.0% family accuracy, and a workflow invariant enforced abstention when all objective modalities were absent. Permuting fine-term answer likelihoods reduced family accuracy to 78.7%. Five of six frozen checks passed, but the question-efficiency hypothesis failed. Comparisons are descriptive because only aggregate, not paired case-level, outputs were retained. These results establish a reproducible synthetic workflow, not clinical diagnostic or treatment validity.

</details>


### [274] [Skillful and realistic precipitation forecasting with reinforced diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38775)
*Ozan Sener,Anand Avati,David Hafner,Jierui Lin,Laura Manduchi,Xiaoying Pang,Langwen Huang,Torsten Hoefler,Russ Schumacher,Vladlen Koltun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FitzRoy 是首个在美国本土范围内同时实现最先进预测技巧与物理真实性的降水预报系统，通过"强化学习精炼的条件离散扩散"框架化解准确性与结构保真度之间的长期权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理模型难以解析中尺度对流，机器学习方法常产生物理上不一致、模糊且系统性低估极端事件的结果；点式准确性与结构真实性之间存在长期权衡，极端降水预报仍未被良好解决。

Method: 提出强化扩散（reinforced diffusion）框架：以条件离散扩散模型为生成骨干，再用强化学习对其进行精炼，从而在保持生成结构真实性的同时提升逐点精度。推理延迟低，支持实时业务部署。

Result: 在最长 24 小时预见期内，FitzRoy 持续达到或超过现有最先进模型，同时保持结构真实性与极端事件捕捉能力；在 2025 年 Texas Hill Country 洪水与 2026 年 Missouri 洪水案例中，预报出破纪录累积降水，而现有模型大多漏报。

Conclusion: 通过弥合真实性与技巧之间的鸿沟，FitzRoy 为高保真降水预报确立了新前沿，并具备实时业务应用潜力。

Abstract: Accurate precipitation forecasting remains elusive as physics-based models struggle to resolve mesoscale convection and machine learning often yields physically inconsistent, blurred results that consistently underestimate extreme events. We present FitzRoy, the first system that simultaneously achieves state-of-the-art predictive skill and physical realism over the continental United States. Through a reinforced diffusion framework, where a conditional discrete diffusion model is refined via reinforcement learning, FitzRoy effectively resolves the long-standing trade-off between pointwise accuracy and structural fidelity. Across lead times up to 24 hours, FitzRoy consistently matches or exceeds state-of-the-art models while preserving structural realism and capturing extreme events. Its low-latency inference further enables deployment in real-time operational settings. In case studies such as the 2025 Texas Hill Country floods and the 2026 Missouri floods, FitzRoy signals record-breaking accumulations that existing models largely missed. By bridging the gap between realism and skill, FitzRoy establishes a new frontier for high-fidelity precipitation forecasting.

</details>


### [275] [Loss is Not Behavior: A Unified Output-Space Analysis of Gradient-Based Machine Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38784)
*Xingjian Zhao,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Malik Magdon-Ismail*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在输出空间刻画梯度式机器遗忘与"仅用保留数据重训练"模型之间的差距，推导线性回归下的闭式解并推广到非线性模型，提出由"遗忘自杠杆"与"保留交叉杠杆"两个杠杆矩阵驱动的统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度式遗忘方法通过优化遗忘损失与保留损失实现"遗忘"，但损失值相近并不代表输出空间行为相近；已有工作表明仅凭高遗忘损失无法证明遗忘结果具有重训练式的输出行为。因此作者追问：当梯度式遗忘器与仅在保留数据上训练得到的模型不同时，该输出空间差距具有怎样的结构。

Method: 在线性回归下推导该差距的闭式表达，并通过广义高斯-牛顿与局部线性化近似推广到非线性模型；据此刻画一个由几何、重加权与投影三者参数化的统一框架，将多种梯度式方法纳入其中，识别出捕获"遗忘-保留纠缠"的两个杠杆矩阵（遗忘自杠杆、保留交叉杠杆），并从理论与实验两方面分析遗忘残差对遗忘/保留输出的影响。

Result: 理论分析与实验均表明，这两个杠杆矩阵驱动遗忘残差对遗忘输出和保留输出的影响，而现有方法仅部分地计入了该几何结构；该框架给出可预计算的杠杆诊断量，用于审计遗忘集。

Conclusion: 输出空间差距的根源在于重训练几何下的遗忘-保留纠缠，可用两个杠杆矩阵统一解释各类梯度式遗忘方法的差异；基于杠杆诊断可在训练前判断哪类梯度式遗忘更可能成功，为遗忘集审计提供实用工具。

Abstract: Gradient-based machine unlearning methods typically ``unlearn'' by optimizing forget and retain losses, but similar losses do not necessarily imply similar output-space behavior. Prior work has shown that high forget loss alone does not certify that unlearning has produced retraining-like output behavior. We therefore ask: when a gradient-based unlearner differs from the model that would have been learned on only the retain data, what structure does the resulting output-space gap have? We derive closed-form expressions for this gap in linear regression, with an extension to nonlinear models via a generalized Gauss-Newton and local linearity approximations. Our analysis puts various gradient-based methods under a unified framework parametrized by geometry, reweighting, and projection. Two leverage matrices emerge that capture the forget-retain entanglement under the retrained model geometry: forget self-leverage and retain cross-leverage. We show both theoretically and empirically that these two leverage matrices drive the impact of the forget residual on the forget and retain outputs, and existing methods account for this geometry only partially. Our framework yields precomputable leverage diagnostics for auditing forget sets to identify what type of gradient-based unlearning is likely to be successful.

</details>


### [276] [Routed Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38796)
*Michael Philip,Mahesh Balakrishnan,David Geraghty,David Mao,Vidhya Venkat,Ilya Mironov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Routed Continual Learning（路由式持续学习）架构，将持续学习建模为"选择学习机制+选择状态簇"的路由问题：把历史轨迹聚类，每簇用记忆库中一种记忆系统维护状态，由 LLM 路由器决定复用已有簇或新建簇，并引入 Context Memory Adapter 衔接记忆系统与智能体循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模部署的智能体反复遇到相关问题，理应互相借鉴经验而非每次从头开始；但不存在跨场景通用的经验保留方式，也无法预知某段经验对未来哪类问题有用。全局历史保留全部细节但代价高且混杂无关经验；压缩为单一固定表示虽降低开销却丢失有价值细节——需要在"细节保留"与"成本/纯度"之间做结构性取舍。

Method: 1) 将过去轨迹分组为簇，每簇从固定记忆系统库中选择一种记忆系统维护状态；2) 每次运行前，LLM 路由器依据问题陈述、记忆系统描述与簇摘要，决定复用现有簇还是新建簇；3) 引入 Context Memory Adapter，作为记忆系统与智能体循环之间的中介层；4) 在由 6 个 CL-Bench 任务共 301 个实例与 τ³-Bench 97 个银行实例交错构成的异构流上评估，覆盖 4 个语言模型、5 次匹配 rollout。

Result: 相对单一全局 ICL 历史，归一化奖励提升可达最大可达归一化奖励的 15–30%；平均归一化奖励高于"仅聚类 + ICL"方案；相对 Global ICL，估计 API 成本降低 65–72%。

Conclusion: 持续学习应被视为一个路由问题——同时选择学习机制与状态簇，而非全局承诺单一表示；该架构在异构任务流上兼顾了效果与成本。

Abstract: Agents deployed at scale repeatedly encounter related problems, and it is natural to let them learn from each other's experience rather than starting over each time. Unfortunately, there is no single way to retain that experience that works well across settings, and no way to know in advance which future problems a given experience will help with. A global history preserves every detail but becomes expensive and mixes together experience that may not belong together; compressing that history into one fixed representation limits the cost but can throw away valuable details.
  We introduce Routed Continual Learning (Routed CL), an architecture that decides how to partition and retain experience. Past trajectories are grouped into clusters, each of which maintains state using one memory system from a fixed library. Before each run, an LLM-based router either reuses an existing cluster or creates a new one, basing its decision on the problem statement, memory system descriptions, and cluster summaries. We also introduce Context Memory Adapter, which mediates between the memory system and the agentic loop.
  We evaluate the architecture on a heterogeneous stream that interleaves 301 instances from the six CL-Bench tasks with 97 banking instances from $τ^3$-Bench. Across four language models and five matched rollouts, Routed Continual Learning raises normalized reward over a single global in-context learning (ICL) history by 15-30% of the maximum attainable normalized reward, achieves higher mean normalized reward than ICL-only clustering, and reduces estimated API cost by 65-72% relative to Global ICL. These results support approaching continual learning as a routing problem that chooses both a learning mechanism and a state cluster rather than committing to one representation globally.

</details>


### [277] [Causal Attention on CPU: A Cache-Aware and Load-Balanced Scheduling Kernel for Prefill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38809)
*Kwun Fung Lau,Frost Mitchell,Kanya Mo,Emani Ashok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CACPU：面向因果全序列预填充的CPU注意力内核，通过分层缓存分块与内核内任务调度，在多级缓存层次上高性能运行，最高相比PyTorch SDPA、FlexAttention与SGLang内核加速2.01倍，集成到SGLang后使Llama-3.1-8B的TTFT降低最多1.74倍且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI带来显著生产力提升，但多智能体并发的长上下文prefill会占满GPU算力，CPU成为可行的替代算力。然而现有CPU注意力内核难以适配多级缓存层次，且缺乏有效的线程调度机制，性能不佳。

Method: 提出CACPU高性能注意力内核，包含两项核心设计：一是分层缓存分块（tiered cache blocking），针对多级缓存层次组织数据分块以提升缓存复用；二是内核内任务调度/分发机制（in-kernel task dispatching），改善线程级并行与负载均衡，整体面向因果（causal）全序列prefill场景优化。

Result: 相较于PyTorch SDPA、FlexAttention和SGLang注意力内核，最高取得2.01倍加速；集成进SGLang后，Llama-3.1-8B的首token时延（TTFT）最高降低1.74倍，同时保持模型精度不变。

Conclusion: 通过面向缓存层次与线程调度的内核级协同设计，CPU可成为长上下文prefill的有效执行平台，缓解GPU在多智能体并发场景下的算力饱和压力，并在真实服务框架中带来端到端时延收益。

Abstract: Agentic AI drives significant productivity gains across diverse domains, but concurrent long-context prefills from multiple agents can saturate GPU compute resources. Although CPUs offer a viable alternative, existing CPU attention kernels fall short in adapting to multi-tier cache hierarchies and lack effective thread scheduling. To address this, we introduce CACPU, a high-performance attention kernel featuring tiered cache blocking and an in-kernel task dispatching mechanism designed for causal full-sequence prefilling. CACPU achieves up to a 2.01x speedup over PyTorch SDPA, FlexAttention, and SGLang attention kernels. Integrated into SGLang, it reduces Time to First Token (TTFT) of Llama-3.1-8B by up to 1.74x while preserving model accuracy.

</details>


### [278] [Discovering Jailbreaks via Curriculum of Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38833)
*Michał Burzyński,Noah T. Rossi,Shomit Basu,Andy Arditi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"模型课程"（Curriculum of Models, CoM）：用强化学习训练调查者模型时，不直接针对经过安全训练的原始目标模型，而是先针对一系列逐步"变难"的目标模型版本训练，从几乎不拒答的版本逐步过渡到原始目标，从而缓解稀疏奖励问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接对经过安全训练的目标模型做越狱发现训练时，几乎全部提示都被拒绝，奖励信号极为稀疏，调查者难以学习。已有工作（PRBO）通过从更"顺从"的目标采样响应并做重要性修正来构造代理奖励，但本文希望探索更直接的替代方案。

Method: CoM 构造目标模型的若干修改版本，按拒答倾向从易到难排列；调查者模型先在几乎不拒答有害请求的版本上训练，当其能稳定诱出目标行为后，再切换到下一个更难的版本，最终到达原始目标模型。评测任务涉及 CBRN 材料、爆炸物与违禁药物，使用 gemma-4-31b-it 与 gpt-oss-120b 两个开放权重目标，对比 PRBO 与无课程的原始目标直接训练两种基线。

Result: 单行为诱导任务中，CoM 比两种基线更可靠地找到成功越狱；多行为同时训练时，CoM 训练的调查者可诱导出留出（held-out）行为，但成功率低于 PRBO。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对目标模型施加简单的课程即可有效缓解稀有行为诱导中的稀疏奖励问题，是一种有前景的方法；不过在跨行为泛化成功率上仍不及 PRBO。

Abstract: Automated jailbreak discovery aims to find prompts that elicit prohibited behaviors from safety-trained language models, helping developers identify vulnerabilities before deployment. One class of approaches uses reinforcement learning to train an investigator model to generate prompts that elicit a specified behavior from a target model. However, training directly against a safety-trained target is difficult because nearly all prompts are refused, leaving the investigator with a sparse reward signal. Prior work addresses this with the propensity bound (PRBO), a proxy reward estimated by sampling responses from a more compliant target, and then adjusting for how likely those responses would have been under the original target.
  We introduce Curriculum of Models (CoM), an alternative method that trains the investigator against a sequence of modified versions of the target, progressing gradually from a version that readily exhibits the specified behavior to the original target. For jailbreak discovery, training begins against a target version that rarely refuses harmful requests, and advances to the next, harder version once the investigator reliably elicits the behavior from the current one.
  We evaluate CoM on elicitation tasks involving CBRN materials, explosives, and illicit drugs, using two open-weight targets, gemma-4-31b-it and gpt-oss-120b. We compare against PRBO and direct training on the original target without a curriculum. When the investigator is trained to elicit a single behavior, CoM finds successful jailbreaks more reliably than either baseline. When trained on multiple behaviors simultaneously, the CoM-trained investigator can elicit held-out behaviors, though PRBO-trained investigators achieve higher success rates. Our results suggest that a simple curriculum over target models offers a promising approach for addressing sparse rewards in rare-behavior elicitation.

</details>


### [279] [What to randomize matters: a controlled study of volume, axis and representation in low-data molecular generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38835)
*Neerav Kaushal,Alisa Teige,Ajay MNV Penmatsa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在低数据条件下训练分子语言模型时，SAFE表示的随机化（原子顺序+片段顺序）远优于仅做SMILES随机化：仅随机化SAFE即可将真实新分子产率提高450倍以上，匹配语料与算力下随机化SAFE的产率是随机化SMILES的2.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小数据集训练化学语言模型时，模型倾向复现训练样本而非学习有效的生成分布。随机化SMILES仅改变原子遍历顺序，SAFE还可改变片段块顺序，但这两类表示变异来源如何影响低数据分子生成尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 用1000个种子分子训练自回归序列模型；在不同语料规模下比较规范SAFE与随机化SAFE；分离SAFE随机化的原子顺序轴与片段顺序轴；在逐分子匹配的训练语料上比较完全随机化SAFE与随机化SMILES；采用Welch检验与Games-Howell事后比较进行统计检验。

Result: (1) 仅随机化SAFE即将真实新分子产率提升450倍以上，使模型从"复现训练集"变为可用的分子生成器；(2) 两轴协同而非简单叠加：单独原子顺序达两者同时随机的约1/10，单独片段顺序约1/7，而两轴同时随机的作用约为其各自贡献之和的4倍；(3) 在语料规模与算力匹配条件下，随机化SAFE的真实产率为随机化SMILES的2.8倍；所有主要结局的差异均经统计检验显著。

Conclusion: 低数据场景下，"随机化什么"（表示维度）而非仅"随机化多少"（增强倍数）决定增强能否产生真正的新分子，而非训练集的新写法；片段顺序这一额外变异轴是SAFE优势的关键来源。

Abstract: Chemical language models can struggle to generate new molecules when trained on small datasets, where a model may reproduce training examples rather than learn a useful generative distribution. Randomized SMILES augmentation varies atom traversal, whereas Sequential Attachment-based Fragment Embedding (SAFE) also allows the order of fragment blocks to vary. How these distinct sources of representational variation affect low-data molecular generation has not been clearly studied. Here, using 1,000 seed molecules to train an autoregressive sequence model, we compare canonical SAFE with randomized SAFE at increasing corpus sizes, isolate atom-order and fragment-order axes of SAFE randomization, and compare fully randomized SAFE with randomized SMILES at molecule-by-molecule matched training corpora. Three findings emerge. First, randomization of SAFE alone raises the genuine yield of novel molecules more than 450-fold, turning a model that merely reproduces its training set into a practical molecular generator. Second, the two axes of randomization act jointly rather than additively: atom order alone reaches a tenth, and fragment order alone a seventh, of the genuine yield obtained when both vary, which is four times their combined contribution. Third, at matched corpus size and compute, randomized SAFE delivers 2.8 times the genuine yield of randomized SMILES. Welch tests with Games-Howell post-hoc comparisons resolve every difference on the primary outcomes. In the low-data regime, thus, what one randomizes rather than only how much determines whether augmentation produces new molecules or merely new spellings of the training set.

</details>


### [280] [Discovery of Diverse and Realistic Financial Tail-Risk Using Generative Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38838)
*Adir Serruya,Gilad Katz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GRID，将连续GFlowNet扩展到高维时序宏观金融数据，用混合策略架构、变量特定分布族和合理性约束生成多样化且真实的尾部风险情景，并利用GFlowNet"按奖励比例采样"的特性在奖励代理不完美时保持探索性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确建模稀有高影响金融事件对稳健风险管理至关重要。基于采样的SMCMC虽能生成多样情景但对高风险区域探索低效；DRL收敛到狭窄情景集合、多样性不足。且金融预测中奖励代理（oracle）的样本外精度普遍较低，需要对该不完美性具有鲁棒性的生成方法。

Method: 采用生成流网络（GFlowNets）——按奖励比例采样的生成模型——构建GRID：基于连续GFlowNet，引入面向高维时序宏观金融数据的灵活混合策略架构、变量特定的分布族，并加入合理性约束以保证生成轨迹的现实性；辅以不同oracle架构与分布族的消融实验。

Result: 在三次真实金融危机上，GRID在情景多样性、现实性和尾部风险覆盖率上均优于RL与MCMC基线；消融实验揭示了噪声奖励下连续GFlowNet行为的实用洞见。

Conclusion: 由于GFlowNet采用按比例采样而非最大化，即使oracle样本外精度低也能保持广泛探索，这构成其核心实用优势；GRID为多样化、真实的金融尾部风险情景生成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate modeling of rare, high-impact financial events is essential for robust risk management. Sampling-based techniques such as Sequential Markov Chain Monte Carlo (SMCMC) generate diverse scenarios but inefficiently explore high-risk regions, while Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) converges on narrow sets of scenarios, limiting diversity. We present GRID, which uses Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) -- samplers that draw objects in proportion to a reward -- to systematically generate diverse and realistic financial tail-risk scenarios. Building on continuous GFlowNets, GRID introduces a flexible mixture-policy architecture with variable-specific distribution families for high-dimensional, sequential macro-financial data, together with plausibility constraints that keep generated trajectories realistic. A central practical advantage is robustness to reward-proxy imperfection: because GFlowNets sample proportionally rather than maximize, they remain broadly exploratory even when the oracle's out-of-sample accuracy is low -- an unavoidable reality in financial forecasting. On three real-world crises, GRID improves scenario diversity, realism, and tail-risk coverage over RL and MCMC baselines, and ablations over oracle architectures and distribution families yield practical insights into continuous GFlowNet behavior under noisy rewards.

</details>


### [281] [Contextual Embeddings Beyond the Gold Chunk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38854)
*Sedigheh Eslami,Markus Krimmel,William Barber,Antoine Chaffin,Maximilian Schall,Jonas Golde,Louis Milliken,Ekaterina Glazkova,Denis Bykov,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 pplx-embed-v2-context 系列上下文嵌入模型，通过从查询感知的上下文压缩模型蒸馏分级块级相关性来训练，无需金标准块标注；并引入 Context-bench 与 Q2D-Chunk 两个标注"答案块+证据块"的基准。9B 模型在两项基准及 ConTEB 上取得新 SOTA，视觉检索能力从视觉语言基座迁移，在 ViDoRe-V3 上达到稠密检索器 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文嵌入模型在训练与评估时默认"一个相关文档只含一个相关块"，而真实场景中检索器既需要找到分散在多个块中的答案，也需要召回用于验证答案的证据。同时，依赖金标准块标签的训练方式受限于分块策略，难以泛化。

Method: 1) 以查询感知的上下文压缩模型产生分级（graded）块级相关性作为蒸馏目标，替代金标准块标签，使训练目标与分块方式解耦；2) 采用视觉语言（vision-language）骨干网络训练，发布 9B 与 0.6B 两个规模、嵌入空间兼容的模型；3) 构建两个同时标注答案块与验证证据块的基准：受控诊断性的 Context-bench 与基于真实搜索查询的 Q2D-Chunk（并开放为公开排行榜）；4) 将分块扩展到图像输入，利用视觉语言基座的视觉检索能力迁移。

Result: 9B 模型在 Context-bench、Q2D-Chunk 及 ConTEB 上均刷新 SOTA；在视觉检索方面，模型在 ViDoRe-V3 上取得稠密检索器中的 SOTA，且性能随图像分块粒度变细而提升，验证了视觉检索能力可从视觉语言基座迁移。

Conclusion: 检索器应同时覆盖跨块答案与验证证据，分级相关性蒸馏可在无块级标注条件下实现这一目标，并天然适配不同分块策略；统一的多模态骨干使文本与图像块检索共享同一范式，细粒度图像分块进一步提升视觉检索效果。

Abstract: Contextual embeddings represent chunks of a document with respect to the document's global context. These models are typically trained and evaluated under the assumption that a relevant document contains only a single relevant chunk. In this work, we argue that in real-world use cases a retriever should not only find answers spread over several chunks but also surface the evidence needed to verify them. To address this, we introduce pplx-embed-v2-context, a family of contextual embedding models trained by distilling graded chunk-level relevance from a query-aware context compression model. This removes the need for gold-chunk labels and makes the training targets independent of the chunking. We utilize a vision-language backbone for training and release pplx-embed-v2-context in 9B and 0.6B sizes with compatible embedding spaces. On the evaluation side, we introduce two benchmarks that label both the chunks that answer a query and the evidence needed to verify the answer. Context-bench is a controlled diagnostic, and Q2D-Chunk is built from real-world search queries. To encourage further research, we also publish Q2D-Chunk as a public leaderboard. Our 9B model sets a new state of the art on both benchmarks and ConTEB.
  We find that the visual retrieval capabilities of our 9B model transfer from its vision-language base model and propose to extend chunking to image inputs. We demonstrate that our models achieve state-of-the-art performance among dense retrievers on visual ViDoRe-V3 and that performance improves with granular image chunking.

</details>


### [282] [Geometric Time Score Estimators: Bridging Self-Predictive Representations and Generative Occupancy Models For Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38901)
*Aravind Venugopal,Grace Liu,Jeff Schneider,Benjamin Eysenbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出几何时间得分估计器（GTSE），通过沿概率路径学习占用密度估计器来统一自预测表征与生成式占用模型两种范式，显著提升目标条件强化学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中的时序推理需要对策略占用测度下未来结果的密度进行估计。自预测表征虽是占用密度估计的主流范式，但其估计质量出奇地差；生成式占用模型虽能准确采样，却难以恢复密度，限制其用于控制。

Method: 提出GTSE框架，沿概率路径学习占用密度估计器，将自预测表征与生成式占用模型两种范式桥接；分析GTSE如何通过改进表征学习隐式地获得更好的占用估计器，并说明如何从该框架中直接提取自预测表征。

Result: 在多个运动与操作任务实验中，GTSE在目标条件强化学习设定下相较最先进方法性能提升2倍至6倍；所学表征可有效迁移至下游RL任务。

Conclusion: GTSE通过概率路径上的密度估计统一了两类占用建模范式，兼具生成模型的表达力与密度估计能力，是一种高效且可迁移的强化学习表征学习框架。

Abstract: In reinforcement learning (RL), temporal reasoning is essential for estimating the density of future outcomes under a policy's occupancy measure. While self-predictive representations have been a dominant paradigm for occupancy density estimation, we find that they yield surprisingly poor estimates. On the other hand, generative occupancy models can produce remarkably accurate samples from the occupancy measure. Yet, they cannot easily recover occupancy densities, limiting their use for control despite their expressivity. In this paper, we propose Geometric Time Score Estimators (GTSE), a framework that bridges the two paradigms by learning occupancy density estimators along probability paths. We analyze how GTSE implicitly induces better occupancy estimators through improved representation learning and show how to directly extract self-predictive representations from this framework. Through experiments across several locomotion and manipulation tasks, we show that GTSE improves performance over state-of-the-art methods by $2\times$ to $6\times$ in goal-conditioned RL settings, and that the representations learned using our framework transfer effectively to downstream RL tasks. Website: https://aravindvenu7.github.io/website/gtse/

</details>


### [283] [Structural-Test Geometry as an Inductive Bias for Partially Observable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38903)
*Todd Y. Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出用"后验边缘化结构测试"导出一个历史间的伪度量，并通过"商保持（QP）世界模型"将共享隐空间欧氏距离对齐到该度量，从而在需要结构泛化的控制任务上显著优于度量解码与结构化信念蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测式世界模型的目标函数未必会按后续控制所需的结构区分来组织隐历史表示；在训练时可访问特权模拟器，因此需要回答：由响应导出的结构信息应当被"预测"、"解码"还是"直接施加于隐几何"，以及这些选择与结构化信念蒸馏相比孰优孰劣。

Method: 定义后验边缘化的结构测试，其响应分布诱导历史空间的伪度量；据此训练商保持（QP）世界模型，即将共享隐表示间的欧氏距离直接对齐到该伪度量（而非仅预测或解码结构量），并在固定六任务诊断套件上与度量解码、结构化信念蒸馏对比，评估留出结构变化下的规划成功率与几何失真（IQD）。

Result: 在留出结构变化下差异最大：QP规划成功率较度量解码提升26.1个百分点[21.5,30.7]，较结构化信念蒸馏提升6.4个百分点[3.8,9.0]。在GridWorld上，度量解码能准确恢复留出结构距离（ρ=.91），但其原生隐几何失真仍大于QP（配对IQD差.22[.20,.24]）。

Conclusion: 结构信息的"关系可恢复性"与"直接几何对齐"可以相互分离：能高精度解码结构并不等于隐几何已被良好组织。直接将隐几何对齐到响应诱导伪度量的QP方法在结构泛化下的控制性能最优，表明对结构信息采取"施加于几何"的策略优于预测/解码及信念蒸馏。

Abstract: Predictive world-model objectives need not organize latent histories according to distinctions required for later control. With privileged simulator access during training, should response-derived structural information be predicted, decoded, or imposed directly on latent geometry--and how do these choices compare with structured belief distillation? We define posterior-marginalized structural tests whose response distributions induce a pseudometric over histories, then train quotient-preserving (QP) world models by aligning shared latent Euclidean distances to that metric. Across a fixed six-task diagnostic suite, the largest differences appear under held-out structural variation: QP improves planning success by 26.1 points $[21.5,30.7]$ over metric decoding and 6.4 points $[3.8,9.0]$ over structured belief distillation. On GridWorld, metric decoding accurately recovers held-out structural distances ($ρ=.91$) while its native latent geometry remains more distorted than QP's (paired IQD difference $.22$ $[.20,.24]$), showing that relational recoverability and direct geometric alignment can diverge.

</details>


### [284] [Lev-3B: A Data Recipe for Compact Probabilistic Log Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38915)
*Leo Betthauser,Yingqi Liu,Uday Shah,Jishang Wei,Sainyam Galhotra,Liang Gou,Vedant Dharnidharka,Hao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出开源的3B参数决策模型Lev-3B（基于Granite-4.1-3B-Base + 共享指针读出），采用"先学有界自然语言决策、后接日志数据"的两阶段课程式训练配方，并在训练内/训练外日志源上系统评估其性能、迁移性与校准性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多日志分析任务本质是在已知选项间做选择（如异常会话检测、攻击技术识别），并需要可校准的概率输出。作者希望以低成本的小模型替代昂贵的前沿大模型服务，并厘清"如何训练"这一配方问题：如何在缺乏目标域日志时仍获得可迁移、可校准的决策能力。

Method: 构建Lev-3B：在Granite-4.1-3B-Base上加入共享指针读出（shared pointer readout）。训练分两阶段：①课程第一阶段，在完全不接触日志数据的情况下学习有界自然语言决策；②日志阶段，将目标任务与覆盖35个辅助任务的通用日志数据混合训练。评估使用单一共享检查点，在训练中出现过的源与完全排除于训练、校准和模型选择的源上分别测试。

Result: 两阶段配方显著优于仅用日志数据训练；加入通用日志数据提升排除源上的表现。Lev-3B以远低于前沿模型的serving成本，在异常检测（含一个被排除的日志系统）上超过提示式前沿模型，但在攻击技术分类上落后。概率在熟悉源上校准良好，但在被排除的日志系统上出现"高置信度漏检异常"。

Conclusion: 小规模、共享检查点的决策模型配合"先决策后日志+通用日志混合"的课程配方，可有效迁移到未见日志源，并在部分任务上以低成本超越大模型；但跨系统分布外校准仍是关键失效点（过度自信漏检）。作者开源检查点、配方、数据清单与预测级产物以支持复现与后续研究。

Abstract: Many log-analysis tasks involve choosing among known options, such as detecting anomalous sessions or identifying attack techniques, and benefit from calibrated probabilities. We present Lev-3B, an open 3B-parameter decision model built on Granite-4.1-3B-Base with a shared pointer readout, and study its training recipe. The recipe has two key parts. First, training follows a curriculum: the model learns bounded natural-language decisions, using no log data, before it sees any logs. Second, the log stage mixes the target tasks with general log data spanning 35 auxiliary tasks unrelated to either evaluation task. We evaluate one shared checkpoint on sources seen in training and on sources excluded from training, calibration and model selection. The two-stage recipe transfers substantially better than training on log data alone. Adding general log data improves performance on excluded sources. At a fraction of their serving cost, Lev-3B outperforms prompted frontier models on anomaly detection, including on an excluded logging system, but trails them on attack-technique classification. Its probabilities are well calibrated on familiar sources, but on the excluded logging system it confidently misses anomalies. We release the checkpoint, recipe, data manifests, and prediction-level artifacts.

</details>


### [285] [Planned Restlessness: Delayed Meta Bandits with Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38955)
*Sudipto Guha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究延迟观测、硬性单回合上限与总预算约束下的episodic有限期贝叶斯bandit（meta-bandit），提出以学习轨迹上的LP松弛为核心的统一规划框架，并用对偶价格分解局部子问题、对执行损失与信息中断进行刻画，给出后验采样的$O(\sqrt{N})$ regret界与LP相对的$1+(2\pi X)^{-1/2}+O(X^{-1})$近似因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典bandit设定通常假设即时观测、无资源硬约束，且不利用跨episode的共享结构。本文关注三个现实耦合因素：每回合从未知但反复出现的先验中抽取臂质量（需同时学习当前质量与先验，即探索—利用—泛化）、观测在已知延迟后到达、以及单回合硬上限与总预算约束。目标是为这类问题提供统一、可证明的规划与认证框架。

Method: 将问题表述为学习轨迹上的统一规划优化，并取其LP松弛：以当前奖励、资源消耗与延续价值（学习状态产生的未来决策价值）共同给实验定价；用对偶价格把臂分解为局部程序，为包括分层Thompson采样在内的任意可行策略提供认证；执行阶段强制满足硬约束，同时保持承诺的学习转移或对其损失进行记账；框架可扩展到风险敏感目标，并为购买观测与buyout选项定价。理论上分析延迟报告带来的"计划性不安分"（臂未被拉时其信息仍随自身早期拉动所产生报告的日历式到达而变化），并在平稳先验、有限奖励字母表、有界报告滞后与非紧预算下证明后验采样的先验学习代价为$O(\sqrt{N})$。

Result: 在平稳先验、有限奖励字母表、有界报告滞后与非紧预算条件下，对反复先验做后验采样的"未知先验代价"为$O(\sqrt{N})$（$N$为回合数，其余参数固定）；对单回合无延迟的$T$步问题、每臂上限$T/X$次拉动，得到LP相对近似因子$1+(2\pi X)^{-1/2}+O(X^{-1})$，优于既有因子，且主导项存在匹配下界。

Conclusion: LP松弛加对偶价格分解为延迟反馈、预算与上限约束下的贝叶斯bandit提供了统一的最优性与策略认证工具，能自然兼容执行时的约束可行性、风险敏感目标与观测购买；延迟造成的"计划性不安分"不破坏学习，先验泛化代价仅为$O(\sqrt{N})$量级，单回合情形下近似因子达到接近1的显式表达并有匹配下界。

Abstract: We study episodic finite-horizon Bayesian bandits, or meta-bandits, with independent arms. Each episode draws fresh arm qualities from unknown recurring priors. Observations arrive after known delays, and plays obey hard per-episode caps and total budgets. A play earns reward and informs both the current quality and the recurring prior: the learner must explore, use, and generalize.
  We formulate a common planning optimization over learning trajectories. Its LP relaxation values experiments through current reward, resource consumption and continuation: the value of future decisions from the learning state produced. Dual prices separate the arms into local programs and certify every admissible policy, including hierarchical Thompson sampling. Execution enforces the hard constraints while preserving the promised learning transitions or accounting for their loss. The framework also represents risk-sensitive objectives and prices purchased observations and buyout options.
  Delayed reports create planned restlessness: an arm's information changes while it is not played, through reports of its own earlier plays arriving on a known calendar. For stationary priors, finite reward alphabets, bounded report lag and nonbinding budgets, posterior sampling over the recurring priors bounds the price of not knowing them by $O(\sqrt{N})$ over $N$ episodes, with other parameters fixed. For one undelayed $T$-step episode with arms capped at $T/X$ plays each, we obtain the LP-relative approximation factor $1+(2πX)^{-1/2}+O(X^{-1})$, improving previous factors, with a matching lower bound for the leading term.

</details>


### [286] [Communication-Efficient Clustered Federated Learning in Non-IID Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38970)
*Martin Isaksson,Rickard Cöster,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Seif Haridi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向6G移动网络的分簇联邦学习（CFL）框架：先用数据域聚类初始化基于模型的聚类，在不上传原始数据的前提下识别并利用客户端相似性，从而应对Non-IID数据并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式学习成为6G高级业务的关键使能技术，但当前与新兴移动网络面临隐私、可扩展性和性能挑战；异构无线环境中天然存在的Non-IID数据进一步恶化标准联邦学习性能，因此需要专门的分簇方案。

Method: 提出CFL框架，在训练过程中将客户端分配给专门化的模型；由数据域聚类为基于模型（model-based）的聚类提供初始化，在共享原始数据的前提下实现客户端相似性识别与利用并降低通信成本。评估采用无线仿真数据集DeepMIMO（受mmWave波束选择启发），并辅以FEMNIST与Rotated CIFAR-10两个Non-IID基准以分离不同的Non-IID特性。

Result: 最优配置相较IFCA在DeepMIMO上准确率提升4.8个百分点，在FEMNIST上提升10.6个百分点，同时FEMNIST下行数据量相比IFCA减少83.1%。

Conclusion: 不同聚类策略适配不同的Non-IID特性；所提出的CFL方法可在未来6G网络中实现资源高效且高性能的分布式学习。

Abstract: Current and emerging mobile networks face increasing privacy, scalability, and performance challenges as distributed learning becomes essential for advanced 6G services. These challenges are amplified by the presence of non-IID data, naturally arising in heterogeneous wireless environments, that degrade the performance of standard Federated Learning (FL) approaches. To address non-IID data across clients, we propose a Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) framework that assigns clients to specialized models during training. Data-domain clustering initializes model-based clustering, enabling identification and exploitation of client similarities without sharing raw data while reducing communication cost.
  Motivated by mmWave beam selection, we evaluate our methods on a wireless simulation dataset (DeepMIMO) and complement it with two non-IID benchmarks (FEMNIST, Rotated CIFAR-10) to isolate different non-IID characteristics. Our best configurations improve accuracy over IFCA by 4.8 pp on DeepMIMO and 10.6 pp on FEMNIST while reducing downlink data volume by 83.1 % on FEMNIST compared to IFCA.
  We demonstrate that different clustering strategies suit different non-IID characteristics. These results show that our CFL methods enable resource-efficient, and high-performance distributed learning in future 6G networks.

</details>


### [287] [The Utility of Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Solar Power Production Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38976)
*Peter Pavlík,Franz M. Rohrhofer,Bernhard C. Geiger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在大规模光伏功率预测任务上，将ADR物理效率模型与TSMixer结合的两种混合架构（特征增强与残差学习）相比纯数据驱动基线，并未带来一致的精度、泛化或数据效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息机器学习（PIML）与混合建模被广泛宣称能结合物理知识与深度学习灵活性，但其在大规模光伏预测中的实际收益缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 构建两种混合预测架构：通过特征增强和残差学习将ADR物理光伏效率模型嵌入TSMixer神经预测架构；在覆盖33个欧洲国家、3713个光伏电站、超过300万样本的数据集上评估收敛速度、预测精度、跨区域可迁移性与数据效率。

Result: 物理信息模型在训练中呈现略快的收敛趋势，但在测试集精度、泛化和有限训练数据下的表现上均未较纯数据驱动基线取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 对于在大规模数据上训练的现代深度预测架构，显式引入ADR物理模型对日前光伏预测的实际收益有限。

Abstract: Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) and hybrid modeling have attracted significant attention as approaches for combining physical knowledge with the flexibility of deep learning. In this work, we investigate whether these approaches deliver their commonly claimed benefits for large-scale photovoltaic (PV) power forecasting. We develop two hybrid forecasting architectures that integrate the ADR physical PV efficiency model with the TSMixer neural forecasting architecture through feature augmentation and residual learning. The proposed models are evaluated on a dataset comprising over 3 million samples from 3,713 PV installations across 33 European countries. We assess convergence speed, forecasting accuracy, transferability across geographical regions, and data efficiency. Although the physics-informed models seem to exhibit slightly faster convergence during training, they do not achieve consistent improvements in test-set accuracy, generalization, or performance under limited training data compared to the purely data-driven baseline. These findings suggest that, for modern deep forecasting architectures trained on large datasets, explicitly incorporating the ADR physical model provides limited practical benefit for day-ahead PV forecasting.

</details>


### [288] [Why Speculative Decoding Works Better Than Predicted on Sparse MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38981)
*Ekagra Ranjan,Komal K. Teru,Bharat Venkitesh,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文实证考察10个稀疏MoE模型（30B–1T参数、8–512专家），推翻"加速比-批大小呈凹形、中等批大小峰值"的传统结论，指出细粒度MoE在低批大小下加速比更高，并提出基于最大熵IPF的路由重叠模型预测唯一专家数（误差<4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作认为MoE模型中投机解码的加速比与批大小呈凹关系，峰值出现在中等批大小（专家饱和所致）。但该结论是否适用于近期出现的细粒度MoE模型尚不清楚；同时验证成本的真实决定因素（是否仅为激活专家字节数）需要重新审视。

Method: 在10个稀疏MoE模型（30B–1T参数、8–512专家）上系统测量投机解码加速比随批大小的变化；在H100上做kernel profiling，比较MoE与attention kernel的带宽利用率并分析L2缓存对共路由专家重复读取的吸收；分析草稿token间的路由重叠对验证阶段唯一激活专家数的影响；用最大熵迭代比例拟合（IPF）框架，从路由重叠统计量预测唯一专家数。

Result: 近期细粒度模型打破凹形趋势，在低批大小下呈现更高加速比；低批大小时细粒度模型的MoE层占运行时比例远小于其权重占比，H100 profiling显示主要原因是MoE kernel相对attention kernel具有更高带宽利用率、且L2吸收共路由专家的重复读取；草稿token间的路由重叠使验证时唯一激活专家数低于独立性假设下的预期；IPF模型预测唯一专家数的误差小于4%，显著缩小了与真实模型曲线的差距，并能从合成路由复现低批大小下的定性趋势。

Conclusion: MoE投机解码的验证成本由激活专家字节、草稿token间时序重叠与GPU执行动态共同决定，而非仅由激活专家字节决定；现有凹形加速比假设对细粒度MoE不再成立，需以路由重叠与硬件层面带宽/缓存效应为核心重新建模MoE投机解码的性能。

Abstract: Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates autoregressive inference by leveraging the memory bound nature of decoding, verifying multiple draft tokens in a single forward pass. For mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, prior work predicts a concave speedup-batch size relationship, with gains peaking at intermediate batch sizes due to expert saturation. We evaluate this across 10 sparse MoE models (30B--1T parameters, 8--512 experts) and find recent fine-grained models break this trend, showing higher speedup at low batch size. We find verification cost is governed not only by activated-expert bytes, but also by temporal overlap across draft tokens and GPU execution dynamics. At low batch size, MoE layers in fine-grained models account for a much smaller share of runtime than of weights; on H100, kernel profiling attributes this mainly to higher bandwidth utilization of MoE kernels relative to attention, with L2 absorbing repeated reads of co-routed experts. Across draft tokens, routing overlap further reduces the unique experts activated during verification relative to independence assumptions. We model this effect using a maximum-entropy Iterative Proportional Fitting (IPF) framework that predicts unique expert counts from routing-overlap statistics with less than 4\% error, and substantially narrows the gap to real-model curves and captures the qualitative low-batch trend from synthetic routing.

</details>


### [289] [Benchmarking Graph Neural Networks for AC Power Flow: The Role of Inductive Biases and Physical Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38985)
*Federico Clerici,Ferran Bohigas-Daranas,Hamid Latif-Martínez,Eduardo Prieto-Araujo,Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt,Pere Barlet-Ros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在统一实验框架下基准比较多种代表性GNN架构用于AC潮流(ACPF)预测，发现各向异性消息传递、显式边特征与全局上下文聚合最关键，2–3层消息传递为最佳深度-宽度折中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电网需要更频繁的系统分析，快速准确的ACPF模型日益重要。GNN天然利用电网拓扑，但各研究在图表示与评测设置上存在差异，导致架构设计选择对预测性能的影响仍缺乏清晰认识。

Method: 构建代表性GNN架构的统一基准：所有模型采用相同预处理与训练流程、可比参数预算，以隔离消息传递机制与归纳偏置的影响；按距松弛母线距离分析误差，并系统消融关键设计组件；在IEEE 14节点与IEEE 118节点系统上评测预测精度与物理一致性。

Result: 各向异性消息传递架构在给定参数与拓扑约束下取得精度与物理一致性的最佳折中；编码线路阻抗的显式边特征与全局上下文聚合对刻画电气交互和全网依赖尤为重要，在拓扑受限的IEEE-118上对电压相角预测尤为关键；固定参数预算下2–3层消息传递最佳，更深无稳定增益；标准残差与根连接在浅层模型中未必有益；聚合算子存在统计精度与物理一致性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究为图神经网络电力系统模型的架构设计提供了实用准则：优先各向异性消息传递、显式阻抗边特征与全局上下文，控制深度在2–3层，并谨慎引入残差/根连接与聚合算子选择。

Abstract: Fast and accurate models for AC power flow (ACPF) are increasingly important as modern electrical grids require more frequent system analyses. Graph neural networks (GNNs) naturally exploit electrical grid topology, yet the effects of architectural design choices on predictive performance remain poorly understood given differences in graph representations and evaluation settings across studies.
  We present a unified benchmark of representative GNN architectures for ACPF prediction under a common experimental framework. All models use identical preprocessing and training procedures and comparable parameter budgets, isolating the impact of message-passing mechanisms and inductive biases. We also analyze errors by distance from the slack bus and systematically ablate key design components. We evaluate predictive accuracy and physical consistency on the IEEE 14-bus and IEEE 118-bus systems.
  Results indicate that anisotropic message-passing architectures offer the most favorable trade-off between predictive accuracy and physical consistency under the studied parameter and topology constraints. Explicit edge features encoding line impedances and global context aggregation are particularly important for representing electrical interactions and system-wide dependencies, especially for voltage-angle predictions on the topologically bottlenecked IEEE-118 grid. Under a budget with approximately fixed parameters, two or three message-passing layers generally provide the best balance between depth and representation width, while additional depth offers no consistent improvement. Ablations also show that standard residual and root connections are not necessarily beneficial in shallow models, and that aggregation operators involve trade-offs between statistical accuracy and physical consistency. These results provide practical guidelines for designing graph-based power system models.

</details>


### [290] [Rethinking GFlowNet Training: Negative Reinforce Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38994)
*Pedro Dall'Antonia,Daniel Csillag,Diego Mesquita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立全梯度与GFlowNet和"解耦采样器"之间min-min动力学的联系，指出采样器通过规避高误差区域来降低观测到的balance误差；据此提出将双人博弈与单策略动力学桥接的方法Negative Reinforce Dynamics (NRD)，通过绑定对抗博弈的参数恢复一个兼顾探索与正确性的单策略动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet通过最小化采样轨迹上的balance error训练，正确性依赖探索：未被访问区域无法施加balance约束，因此自适应探索至关重要。但标准训练采用半梯度更新，把采样分布视为固定，导致探索不足；而全梯度又会以"反探索"方式降低观测误差，造成偏差。作者希望厘清这一张力并设计出既扩展探索又保持正确性的训练动态。

Method: 将全梯度解释为GFlowNet与一个解耦采样器之间的min-min动力学，证明该采样器通过回避高误差区域来降低观测到的balance violation；这一洞见促成双人博弈与单策略动力学的桥接，进而提出NRD：将对抗博弈中各参与方的参数绑定在一起，使双人博弈退化为单一策略动态，无需额外的teacher模型或辅助探索策略。

Result: 在标准基准上，NRD在收敛速度和探索效率两方面均优于现有方法，且不需要训练单独的teacher模型或辅助探索策略。

Conclusion: 全梯度下的反探索效应可由min-min博弈视角统一解释，而将对抗博弈参数绑定可得到同时具备探索扩展性与balance正确性的单策略训练动态，为GFlowNet的自适应探索提供了简洁有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets) are models that learn to sample discrete objects proportional to a target reward. They are trained by minimizing balance errors over sampled trajectories, which inherently ties correctness to exploration: if a region is not visited, balance cannot be enforced there. Adaptive exploration is therefore crucial, yet standard training relies on a semi-gradient update that treats the sampling distribution as fixed at each optimization step. On the other hand, the full gradient reveals a second mechanism that reduces the observed errors in an anti-exploratory manner. In this paper, we establish a connection between the full gradient and min-min dynamics between the GFlowNet and a decoupled sampler, showing that the sampler reduces observed violations by avoiding high-error regions. This insight motivates a bridge between two-player games and single-policy dynamics, which underpins the design of our proposed method, Negative Reinforce Dynamics (NRD). By tying the parameters of an adversarial game together, we recover a single-policy dynamic that extends exploration while maintaining correctness. In standard benchmarks, our method outperforms existing approaches in both convergence speed and exploration efficiency, without training a separate teacher or auxiliary exploration policy.

</details>


### [291] [Fed-IGTD: A federated learning-based framework for tabular-to-image encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39008)
*David Chushig-Muzo,Eva Milara,Francisco J. Lara Abelenda,Luis Carlos Estrada-Petrocelli,Diego H. Peluffo-Ordóñez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fed-IGTD：在联邦学习下仅共享本地统计量以重建全局相关矩阵并生成特征-像素映射，配合CNN与FedAvg训练；并评估两种差分隐私机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格-图像编码方法虽在预测任务中有效，但采用集中式范式，存在隐私泄露、未授权数据采集与访问等风险，需要去中心化方案。

Method: 横向联邦学习方法：客户端共享同一特征空间，仅上传本地统计量，服务器据此重建全局相关矩阵并得到特征到像素的映射，再用FedAvg训练CNN；研究两种DP机制——(i)保护相关矩阵聚合步骤的一次性高斯机制；(ii)结合Renyi-DP记账器的客户端级DP-FedAvg。

Result: 在5个数据集上系统扫描非IID程度与隐私预算：映射构建阶段的隐私保护对预测性能影响很小；DP-FedAvg在中等隐私预算且客户端较少（K=5）时显著降低效用。消融表明该退化主要源于注入噪声，而非均匀平均或更新裁剪。在非IID异质性与类别不平衡下，Macro-F1比准确率对性能退化更敏感。

Conclusion: Fed-IGTD可在不共享原始数据的前提下实现表格-图像编码的联邦训练；聚合阶段的隐私保护代价低，而训练阶段的DP-FedAvg在小规模客户端下效用损失明显，且主要来自噪声注入。

Abstract: Tabular-to-image encoding methods, which transform tabular data into image-like representations, have shown promising results in predictive tasks. Despite their benefits, these methods follow a centralized paradigm, in which the data are collected and models are trained on a central device. This raises privacy and security concerns, particularly the risk of data leakage, unauthorized data collection and access to users' private information. Federated Learning (FL) provides a decentralized framework in which multiple clients jointly optimize a shared model without directly sharing training data. We introduce Fed-IGTD, a horizontal FL-based approach in which clients share a common feature space and upload only local statistics, from which the server reconstructs the global correlation matrix and obtains a feature-to-pixel mapping to train a convolutional neural network via Federated Averaging (FedAvg). We studied two differential privacy (DP) mechanisms: (i) a one-shot Gaussian mechanism protecting the correlation-aggregation step; and (ii) a client-level DP-FedAvg variant protecting the training with privacy loss composed via a Renyi-DP accountant. We evaluated Fed-IGTD on five datasets under a systematic sweep of non-IID severity and privacy budgets. Privacy protection applied to the mapping-construction step has little impact on predictive performance. By contrast, DP-FedAvg substantially degrades utility at moderate privacy budgets with a small client cohort (K=5). Ablation study indicates that this degradation is driven mainly by the injected noise rather than by uniform averaging or update clipping. Macro-F1 exhibits sensitivity to performance degradation than accuracy under non-IID heterogeneity and class imbalance.

</details>


### [292] [Assessing feature scorer results on high-dimensional datasets with t-SNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39009)
*Bruno Iochins Grisci,Mario Inostroza-Ponta,Márcio Dorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"weighted t-SNE"，通过将特征得分融入t-SNE嵌入空间中数据点间的关系，实现特征评分器(feature scorer)及其结果的可视化，并提出轮廓系数作为数值评估手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据可视化文献丰富，但针对特征评分器(feature scorer)及其结果的可视化工作较少；特征评分器种类多且往往缺乏单一指标或真值来保证其质量，因此需要可视化帮助用户选择合适方法并判断结果优劣。

Method: 修改t-SNE嵌入2D空间中数据点之间的关系，使其反映由特征评分器评估的各原始维度的重要性；讨论weighted t-SNE的实现方式，提出以轮廓系数(silhouette coefficient)对结果进行数值评估，并在实验中结合合成数据与真实表格数据以及九种特征评分器（从互信息到神经网络）进行验证。

Result: 每种特征评分器都产生独特的可视化结果，weighted t-SNE可用于比较并选出最适合特定数据集与任务的方法；它也能直观展示机器学习模型所学特征的重要性并揭示其组织数据的方式。

Conclusion: weighted t-SNE是一种有效的特征评分结果可视化工具，可支持特征评分器的比较与选择，并提升机器学习模型的可解释性。

Abstract: While vast literature on high-dimensional data visualization is available, there are not many works regarding the visualization of feature scorers and their results. Feature scorers are algorithms that assign numerical importance to each feature of multi-dimensional datasets. These importance scores can be used in several applications, such as feature selection, knowledge discovery, and machine learning interpretability. There are several feature scorers to choose from, and often no single metric or ground truth is available to guarantee the quality of their results. In this scenario, visualization can become valuable to support the decision of which method to choose and how good its results are. For this goal, this work presents "weighted t-SNE." It modifies the relationship between data points in the embedded 2D space to reflect the importance of each dimension of the original datasets as assessed by a feature scorer. This research discusses how to implement weighted t-SNE, proposes the silhouette coefficient as a numerical evaluation of the results, and shows several examples of its use in practice. Synthetic and real-world tabular datasets are used in the experiments together with nine feature scorers, ranging from Mutual Information to neural networks. Each feature scorer produces unique visualizations, and weighted t-SNE can be used to compare and choose the one that better suits a given dataset and task. Weighted t-SNE can also visually show the importance of features learned by machine learning models and help us see how they are organizing the data, increasing their interpretability.

</details>


### [293] [Representation Complexity Along Learning Trajectories: Trading Width for Precision and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39027)
*Ryan Singh,Miguel De Llanza,Christopher L. Buckley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了过参数化在"发现"目标上的计算作用：把表示复杂度需求与"让目标通过梯度变得可检测"的复杂度需求对比，用Kolmogorov n-宽证明发现k-稀疏parity所需的初始有效宽度可达D^{k-1}量级，远超表示它所需的O(k)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息平面分析显示神经网络在训练中会经过比最终预测器更复杂的表示，但"这种瞬时复杂度是否有计算用途"不清楚。作者要区分"能表示目标"与"能通过梯度反馈发现目标"这两种复杂度需求，解释过参数化为何在发现阶段是必要的。

Method: 提出神经网络学习的oracle模型，用Kolmogorov n-宽量化有效表示复杂度；核心引理把前向表示的复杂度界迁移到梯度查询特征上；结合统计查询下界得到与任务无关的下界：目标相关梯度信息可检测所需的初始有效宽度。针对D维k-稀疏parity，利用其对坐标符号翻转的不变性定义"无符号有效宽度"，在传输兼容的分辨率下求得临界尺度Θ̃_k(D^{k-1}τ²)；并把精度解释为新鲜样本累积，导出宽度-时间前沿。

Result: 对k-稀疏parity：已知目标仅需O(k)宽表示，而一般界已给出表示与发现之间的多项式分离；无符号宽度临界尺度为Θ̃_k(D^{k-1}τ²)（含对数因子），表明发现预测器所需表示资源可大幅超过学会后表示它的资源。实验验证了预测的维度依赖性，并考察优化与泛化如何偏离理想前沿。

Conclusion: 学习中的瞬态高复杂度并非浪费：目标可检测性对表示宽度提出了远高于可表示性的要求，过参数化承担了"信息发现"的计算职能；表示的充分性与可发现性之间存在本质鸿沟。

Abstract: What computational role does overparameterisation play during learning? Information-plane analyses suggest that neural networks can traverse representations substantially more complex than those required by their final predictors. We explore when this transient complexity serves a computational purpose by comparing the representation complexity needed for prediction with that needed to make an unknown target statistically visible through gradient feedback. We introduce an oracle model of neural network learning and use Kolmogorov \(n\)-widths to quantify effective representation complexity. Our main technical result transfers complexity bounds from the forward representation to the corresponding gradient-query features. Combined with statistical-query lower bounds, this gives a task-agnostic lower bound on the initial effective width required for target-dependent gradient information to become detectable. For \(k\)-sparse parity in \(D\) dimensions, a known target admits an \(O(k)\)-width representation, while the general bound already implies a polynomial separation between representation and discovery. We then exploit parity's invariance under coordinatewise sign reflections to identify a sharper unsigned effective width, which removes sign distinctions invisible to the parity information channel. At a transfer-compatible measurement resolution, its critical scale is \(\widetildeΘ_k\left( D^{k-1}τ^2 \right)\), up to logarithmic factors. Thus, the representation resources required to discover a predictor can substantially exceed those required to represent it once learned. Interpreting precision through accumulation over fresh samples yields a corresponding width-time frontier. Experiments support the predicted dimension dependence and examine how optimisation and generalisation depart from this idealised frontier.

</details>


### [294] [FairSplit: Decomposing the Embedding Space for Fair Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39031)
*Andrii Shkabrii,Anna Beer,Ylli Sadikaj,Claudia Plant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairSplit 通过将嵌入空间分解为用于预测的 Fair Space 与隔离敏感信息的 Sensitive Space，借助可学习掩码与 HSIC 约束实现公平分类，并给出人口统计均等差距的理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平分类面临双重挑战：在保持高分类精度的同时限制对敏感信息的依赖；现有方法在多分类任务和非二元敏感属性上存在局限。

Method: 学习嵌入空间的分解：Fair Space 用于预测，Sensitive Space 为敏感属性提供"去处"以隔离敏感信息；训练中同时学习分类任务与分离公平/敏感信息，通过可学习掩码与 Hilbert-Schmidt 独立性准则（HSIC）作为统计依赖度量来引导分解。

Result: 理论上证明人口统计均等差距被两方面上界约束：(i) Fair Space 的学习维度；(ii) Fair Space 与敏感属性之间的 HSIC。在主流公平性基准上的实验显示，FairSplit 在精度-公平性权衡上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: FairSplit 能有效处理多分类任务与非二元敏感属性，克服现有公平分类方法的关键局限，实现有竞争力的精度-公平性权衡。

Abstract: Fair classification faces a twofold challenge: maintaining high classification accuracy while limiting reliance on sensitive information. Our method FairSplit achieves this by learning a decomposition of the embedding space into a Fair Space used for prediction and a Sensitive Space that isolates sensitive information. During training, the model must simultaneously learn the classification task and separate fair from sensitive information. The Sensitive Space gives sensitive attributes a destination, so the Fair Space can be used for prediction without relying on them. This decomposition is guided via a learnable mask and the Hilbert-Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC) as a measure of statistical dependence. We provide theoretical guarantees showing that the demographic parity gap is upper-bounded by both (i) the learned dimensionality of the Fair Space and (ii) the HSIC between the Fair Space and sensitive attributes. FairSplit effectively handles multiclass tasks and non-binary sensitive attributes, overcoming key limitations of existing fair classification methods. Experiments on established fairness benchmarks show that FairSplit achieves competitive accuracy-fairness trade-offs. Code is available at https://github.com/shkabrii/fairsplit.

</details>


### [295] [Exponential-Nemytskii Neural Operators: Learning Field Evolution Across Step Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39041)
*Jingye Li,Jinglai Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种步长依赖由半线性动力学的Duhamel一阶近似所规定的算子，将精确线性传播与非线性局部项结合，使模型可在观测间隔以下的步长上进行无监督外推，并给出步长一致性控制预测误差及两分量可辨识性的理论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变步长算子学习中，模型在观测步长上的一致并不能保证其在其他时间分辨率下的预测正确；且密集多步长的监督数据难以获得。因此需要一种步长依赖具有解析结构、可在观测间隔以下采样的算子。

Method: 基于半线性动力学的Duhamel一阶近似规定步长依赖：精确线性传播（指数/精确解）与场及其导数的局部非线性函数相乘耦合；对学习到的潜变量场采用相同构造得到扩展模型。理论上证明固定空间分辨率下两处步长上的一致性能控制规定范围内预测差，并给出两个分量由两个步长可局部辨识的条件。

Result: 在固定空间分辨率下给出误差控制定理：两处步长的模型一致性可约束整个给定步长区间内的预测差异；给出线性与非线性分量的局部可辨识性条件，支持学习结构的可解释性。实验上基础模型在两个合成问题上、潜变量扩展在两个真实数据集上与多个基线对比评估。

Conclusion: 将Duhamel一阶近似作为结构性归纳偏置，可在无需密集步长监督的情况下实现变步长/任意分辨率算子学习，同时获得理论保证与结构可解释性。

Abstract: In variable-step operator learning, agreement at observed step sizes does not by itself determine predictions at other temporal resolutions. We propose an operator whose step-size dependence is prescribed by a first-order Duhamel approximation of semilinear dynamics. It combines exact linear propagation with a local nonlinear function of the field and its derivatives, allowing evaluation below the observation interval without supervision densely sampled across step sizes. Under suitable conditions and at fixed spatial resolution, we prove that agreement between models at two step sizes controls their prediction differences throughout a prescribed range. We also give conditions for local identifiability of both components from two step sizes, supporting interpretation of the learned structure. A latent extension applies the same construction to learned fields. We evaluate the base model on two synthetic problems and the extension on two real datasets against several baselines.

</details>


### [296] [Reinforcement Learning for the Game of Balatro: A Fast Simulator and Two-Policy Training Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39049)
*Ajay Vikram Periasami,Anjaneya Gupta,Ethan Cheng,Ivan Rosales*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出端到端强化学习流水线（Rust高吞吐模拟器+动态合法动作掩码的精简观测接口+分阶段多智能体PPO），在Balatro上实现首盲注通关率超99%、平均最大底注达3/8的反应式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Balatro具有三大使RL失效的难点：约3000个状态依赖动作空间、数百步决策且奖励稀疏的长时程、卡牌抽取与商店生成的普遍随机性；且该游戏公开的RL结果极少。作者意在构建可复现的高吞吐实验平台并定位策略瓶颈所在。

Method: (i) 高吞吐Rust模拟器，通过BalatroBot与实时游戏客户端自动做状态差分校验；(ii) 精简的观测-动作接口，对每步合法动作做动态掩码；(iii) 多智能体PPO架构，将游戏分解为战术性盲注策略与战略性商店策略，两者共享完整游戏状态但在互斥阶段行动；并通过每智能体奖励路由与显式跨智能体信用分配部分缓解长时程信用分配问题。

Result: 主联合训练运行中，训练指标出现明显相变：首盲注通关率收敛至99%以上，平均回合回报提升约一个数量级，并在平均最大底注3/8处趋于平台。相同奖励下，单智能体基线在贪心与囤积两种失败模式间振荡，无法稳定通过首盲注。

Conclusion: 双策略分解可作为Balatro的强反应式基线；剩余性能差距主要位于长时程战略规划，而非战术性盲注对局。作者据此将未来工作指向战略层规划能力的提升。

Abstract: Balatro, a 2024 roguelike deckbuilder, presents three difficulties that have historically derailed reinforcement learning methods: a 3,000-action state-dependent action space, horizons of several hundred decisions with sparse rewards, and pervasive stochasticity in both card draws and shop generation. Published RL results on the game remain scarce. We introduce an end-to-end pipeline comprising (i) a high-throughput Rust simulator that is automatically state-diffed against the live game client via BalatroBot, (ii) a condensed observation-action interface with dynamic per-step masking of legal moves, and (iii) a multi-agent PPO architecture that decomposes play into a tactical blind policy and a strategic shop policy that share the full game state but act in disjoint phases. Long-horizon credit assignment is partially addressed by per-agent reward routing with explicit cross-agent credit. On the main joint run, training metrics show a sharp phase transition in which the first-blind clear rate converges above 99% and mean episode return increases by roughly an order of magnitude, plateauing at a mean maximum ante of 3 out of 8; single-agent baselines under identical rewards oscillated between greedy and hoarding failure modes and did not stably clear the first blind. These results establish the two-policy decomposition as a strong reactive baseline for Balatro and localise the remaining gap to long-horizon strategic planning rather than tactical blind play.

</details>


### [297] [BASILISC: Bayesian Alignment of Siamese-Integrated Latents for Inferring Single-cell Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39050)
*Sepideh Saran,Mahsa Ghanbari,Uwe Ohler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BASILISC 是一个概率化孪生网络框架，为跨模态单细胞匹配给出带不确定性的对应关系预测，并在细胞类型层面与细胞层面均验证了其不确定性分数的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数单细胞数据为单模态，整合依赖在多模态数据上训练的模型，但现有模型只输出匹配结果、很少给出置信度，无法判断匹配是否可靠。

Method: BASILISC（Bayesian Alignment of Siamese-Integrated Latents）在两种模态上构建概率孪生网络，对跨模态细胞对给出关于对应关系的预测分布；分两个层级实例化：细胞类型层面判断两细胞是否同类型，细胞层面判断哪一细胞是另一模态中的配对者。

Result: 据称是首个用已知真实配对评估的学习式跨模态对应不确定性分数。学到的跨模态相似度反映细胞谱系，未见类型仍可匹配（平衡准确率 0.86，已见类型 0.93），且对分布外细胞的预测不确定性升高；丢弃最不确定的五分之一配对可将准确率由 0.93 提升至 0.98。细胞层面，确定性匹配器的深度集成在基准测试集上达到 0.062 匹配准确率，超过竞赛冠军；在贝叶斯匹配器的分配上，后验对跨越细胞类型边界的错误匹配的识别优于同分数确定性解读（AUROC 0.68 vs 0.64）。

Conclusion: BASILISC 为单细胞跨模态匹配提供了可量化的不确定性，可用于筛选高质量配对与下游分析，并在分布外类型上表现出可解释的不确定性增长。

Abstract: Single-cell assays study the genome one cell at a time. Unimodal assays measure one molecular layer, the newer multimodal assays measure several. Most data are single-modality, so integrating them relies on models trained on multimodal assays, where the cell correspondence across modalities is known. These models return a matching, but rarely a confidence measure. We present BASILISC (Bayesian Alignment of Siamese-Integrated Latents for Inferring Single-cell Correspondence), a framework for matching single-cell representations with uncertainty on the correspondence itself. A BASILISC model is a probabilistic siamese network over the two modalities with a predictive distribution over the correspondence for a cross-modal pair of cells. We instantiate it at two levels: at the cell-type level the question is whether two cells share a cell type, at the cell level which cell is the partner in the other modality. To our knowledge, this is the first uncertainty score on a learned cross-modal correspondence evaluated against a known ground-truth pairing. BASILISC learns a similarity across modalities that reflects cell lineage: an unseen cell type is matched to its lineage relative, matching still works on such held-out types (balanced accuracy 0.86 on unseen against 0.93 on seen types), and the predictive uncertainty rises on these out-of-distribution cells. The uncertainty score also selects high-quality matches for downstream analysis: discarding the least certain fifth of the pairs raises accuracy from 0.93 to 0.98. At the cell level, a deep ensemble of the deterministic matcher reaches 0.062 matching accuracy on the benchmark test set, above the competition winner; on the Bayesian matcher's assignment, the posterior flags the wrong matches that cross a cell-type boundary better than a deterministic read of the same scores (AUROC 0.68 against 0.64).

</details>


### [298] [When Does Closed-Loop Curriculum Adaptation Help GRPO? An Analysis on Countdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39056)
*Yuv Raj Pant,Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy,Kaiqun Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出闭环高斯课程方法 CLOSEDLOOPGAUSSIAN，用周期性探测的分难度准确率反馈动态调节高斯课程采样的节奏与分布宽度，在不改动奖励函数、模型结构和 GRPO 优化器的前提下提升强化微调样本效率，并在符号算术推理任务上优于 vanilla GRPO 与固定开环课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（如 GRPO）在训练分布与模型当前能力不匹配时样本效率低下：过难题目产生全部失败的 rollout，无梯度信号；过易题目优势值（advantage）近似为零，信息量小。固定 easy-to-hard 调度无法随模型能力变化自适应调整。

Method: 保留 Easy-to-Hard 高斯采样器的结构，但引入周期性探测（probe）估计各难度上的准确率，形成闭环控制：模型超前于参考课程时加快训练节奏，落后时减慢，若早期难度出现回退则扩大采样分布宽度（spread）。控制器仅调节难度采样的节奏与分布，不修改奖励函数、模型架构与 GRPO 优化器。

Result: 在符号算术推理任务上，CLOSEDLOOPGAUSSIAN 的精确匹配（exact-match）准确率高于 vanilla GRPO 与固定开环课程，其中在中等难度区间提升最明显——该区间正是组相对学习信号最具信息量的区域。

Conclusion: 基于分难度准确率反馈的闭环课程控制是一种轻量、可插拔的改进手段，可提升组相对类强化微调的样本效率；结论目前主要限于有序难度的符号算术任务，泛化到更广泛推理任务仍需验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning has become an effective post-training paradigm for improving the reasoning ability of large language models, but it remains sample-inefficient when the training distribution is mismatched to the model's current capability. In group-relative objectives such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), problems that are too hard often produce uniformly failed rollouts, while problems that are too easy provide little useful advantage signal. In this work, we introduce CLOSEDLOOPGAUSSIAN, a lightweight adaptive curriculum method for efficient reinforcement fine-tuning on ordered reasoning tasks. Instead of following a fixed easy-to-hard schedule, CLOSEDLOOPGAUSSIAN preserves the structure of an Easy-to-Hard Gaussian sampler while dynamically adjusting its pace and spread using periodic probe estimates of per-difficulty accuracy. The controller accelerates training when the model is ahead of a reference curriculum, slows down when the model falls behind, and broadens sampling when earlier difficulties regress. This feedback mechanism keeps training focused on tasks that are challenging but still learnable, without changing the reward function, model architecture, or underlying GRPO optimizer. Experiments on symbolic arithmetic reasoning task show that CLOSEDLOOPGAUSSIAN improves exact-match accuracy over vanilla GRPO and fixed open-loop curricula, with the strongest gains in intermediate-difficulty regimes where group-relative learning signals are most informative. Codes are available at https://github.com/yuvrajpant56/Closed_Loop_Curriculum.

</details>


### [299] [Closed Gates Leak: Decomposing Adjacent-Layer Cancellation in Gated Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39068)
*Harvey Dam,Ganesh Gopalakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文用"去除前层写入"的消融方法分解相邻层残差流抵消现象，发现抵消主要来自门控MLP对输入方向的整体收缩（damping），而非对"不适当特征"的选择性拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作将相邻层写入残差流的相互抵消解释为"层提出特征、下一层选择性拒绝"的语义机制。作者质疑这一解释，希望厘清抵消的真实来源。

Method: 揭示门控MLP的damping机制：SiLU/GELU在零点以下略为负值，关闭的门仍传递读取量的一个小负倍数，输出权重沿输入权重方向的神经元会减去其所读方向的若干比例；并追踪到承载damping的具体神经元。此外，在继续预训练中退火门激活的负半轴，观察damping与抵消的变化。

Result: damping由门关闭的神经元承载；闭门在方向上平均收缩MLP所读内容，前层写入并不特殊——同层另一个token的写入受到同等或更强的damping，先前据以论证选择性纠正的特征值签名在别的token写入上同样出现。训练中damping增长而"照常写入"保持平坦，抵消在damping超过它时出现；退火门激活负半轴后damping与抵消均消失。

Conclusion: 相邻层抵消是关于模型拒绝哪些特征的弱证据：它源于门控MLP对任意输入方向的平均收缩，而非特征级选择性纠正。

Abstract: In transformer language models with gated multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), adjacent layers write partly against each other into the residual stream. Previous work interpreted this cancellation as layers proposing features and the next layer selectively rejecting the inappropriate ones. We decompose the cancellation by removing the previous layer's write from the input of the next MLP: what the next layer writes along that write splits into what it writes anyway and how much less its MLP writes because the write is present, which we call the damping, and we trace the damping to the neurons that carry it. In gated MLPs the damping is carried by neurons whose gates are closed. SiLU and GELU are slightly negative below zero, so a closed gate still passes a small negative multiple of what the neuron reads, and a neuron whose output weight points along its input weight then subtracts a fraction of the direction it read. Summed over such neurons, closed gates contract the average direction a gated MLP reads, and the previous layer's write is not singled out: the same layer's write at another token is damped as much or more, and the eigenvalue signature from which that work concluded that the correction is selective appears for another token's write as well. Over training the damping grows while what the next layer writes anyway stays flat, and cancellation begins when the damping overtakes it; annealing the negative side of the gate activation away in continued pretraining removes the damping and the cancellation. Adjacent-layer cancellation is therefore weak evidence about which features a model rejects: it is produced by a contraction that gated MLPs apply, on average over directions, to whatever they read.

</details>


### [300] [Generating Critic-Guided Synthetic Experience in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39100)
*Jorge de Freitas,Christos Ziakas,Alessandra Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Critic-Guided Synthetic Experience (CGSE)，将世界模型作为局部转移生成器，借助critic引导在模型内选择动作以扩充离线数据，在三个OGBench操作任务上平均成功率相对提升7.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线数据学习受限于可扩展性，真实环境采集新经验成本高或不安全；世界模型可生成新经验，但在潜空间中学习策略会因想象轨迹的复合误差而受限。

Method: CGSE将世界模型仅用作局部转移生成器：先用离线策略采样候选动作，在信任域内对每个动作做一阶Q函数近似最大化进行动作精修；再用冻结的潜空间世界模型对更新后的动作想象未来状态，由critic挑选最佳者，将合成转移加入回放缓冲区。无需额外真实交互或测试时规划器。

Result: 在三个OGBench操作任务上，CGSE使离线策略的平均成功率相对提升7.1%。

Conclusion: 以critic引导的动作精修配合冻结世界模型生成合成经验，可有效缓解复合误差并提升离线策略性能，且完全离线、推理期无额外开销。

Abstract: Learning from offline datasets is limited in scalability, as collecting new experience is often costly or unsafe in the real world. World models offer an alternative way to generate new experience through a learned dynamics model, yet learning policies in latent space is limited due to compounding errors in imagined rollouts. To this end, we propose using the world model as a local transition generator, leveraging critic guidance to choose actions inside that model to generate synthetic experiences. We study action refinement methodologies, introducing Critic-Guided Synthetic Experience (CGSE) generation in world models. Specifically, CGSE augments an existing offline dataset by sampling candidate actions using an offline policy and refining each to maximize a first-order approximation of the $Q$-function within a trust region. We then use a frozen latent world model to imagine the future state given each updated action, and use the critic to choose the best one, appending this synthetic transition to the replay buffer. Our method requires no additional real-environment interaction or test-time planner, as it generates synthetic experience offline using the world model. On three OGBench manipulation tasks, CGSE improves an offline policy by a relative improvement of $7.1\%$ on the average success rate.

</details>


### [301] [From Local Recovery to Unified Generation: Discovering Triple Set in Knowledge Graphs via Observation-Anchored Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39114)
*Jiaqi Wang,Yisen Gao,Huayang Shi,Junhao Hou,Wengen Li,Jihong Guan,Yichao Zhang,Shuigeng Zhou,Yangqiu Song,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LatentDiff，一个统一的潜在生成框架，将三元组集合预测（TSP）重构为潜在空间图生成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TSP方法依赖基于子图的补全范式，先将原图切分为局部子图再独立补全，导致结构推理碎片化、跨子图依赖建模受限、跨子图预测缺失。

Method: 先用关系感知的残差量化知识图谱自编码器将整图编码为关系感知潜在嵌入，以分块残差码本量化，并通过因子化解码器重建三元组；再在此基础上引入观测锚定的潜在图扩散模型，使目标潜在图表示向观测潜在扰动，反向过程通过渐进式潜在去噪恢复缺失三元组。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上，F_{tsp} 平均较SOTA提升5.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 以全图潜在生成替代局部子图补全，可有效实现统一的TSP建模并取得领先性能。

Abstract: Triple set prediction (TSP) is to recover missing relational triples in a knowledge graph by predicting the triple set instead of isolated links. Existing approaches predominantly rely on the subgraph-based completion paradigm, where the original graph is first partitioned into local subgraphs which are then independently completed. Such a paradigm suffers from fragmented structural reasoning, limited cross subgraph dependency modeling, and missing predictions across subgraphs. This paper proposes LatentDiff, a novel unified latent generative framework for TSP, which reformulates knowledge graph completion as latent space graph generation. LatentDiff first learns a compact latent representation using a relation-aware residual-quantized knowledge graph autoencoder, which encodes the whole graph into relation-aware latent embeddings, quantizes them with chunked residual codebooks, and reconstructs triples through a factorized decoder. On top of this latent space, we introduce an observation-anchored latent graph diffusion model, where the target latent graph representation is perturbed toward the observed latent. The reverse process recovers missing triples through progressive latent denoising. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that LatentDiff outperforms the SOTA in terms of F_{tsp} by 5.4 percentage points on average. Code: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LKGDiff-44A5.

</details>


### [302] [Uncertainty-Guided Cross-Modal Alignment for Diffusion-Based IoT Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39117)
*Mahdi Arab Loodaricheh,Anita Raja,Mohammad Hossein Manshaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可靠性引导的跨模态对齐规则，用归一化VAE后验方差控制模态间定向影响，再进行类条件EDM扩散训练，用于不平衡物联网入侵检测中的少数类攻击样本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的采样行为依赖去噪所在表征空间的几何结构；多模态数据中独立训练的编码器会把同步视图映射到不兼容的潜空间区域，而均匀对齐又会让不确定视图扭曲共享几何。该问题在少数类攻击生成场景尤为关键。

Method: 将表格流属性、DeepInsight图像与数据包序列分别用模态专属VAE编码；以各模态归一化VAE后验方差作为有界的样本级可靠性代理（启发式，非校准概率），构建带非对称stop-gradient的对齐目标进行微调；对齐后取完整潜均值标准化，由类条件EDM联合建模，再用二阶采样器生成成对攻击条件潜块供三个解码器使用。

Result: 在TON-IoT受控实验中，不确定性感知对齐在三种扩散变体中取得最高的平均precision、recall、density与coverage；仅增强训练集后，在未触碰的真实测试集上ROC-AUC 0.9515、PR-AUC 0.8954、平衡准确率0.8395、攻击类F1 0.7940；在两种对齐微调设置匹配的变体中，后验方差加权在表格生成保真度与下游检测上均优于均匀加权。

Conclusion: VAE后验方差可作为有效的样本级可靠性信号来调控跨模态对齐方向，从而改善多模态扩散生成质量与不平衡入侵检测性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models evolve noisy states toward a learned data distribution, so their sampling behavior depends on the geometry of the representation in which denoising occurs. This dependence is especially important for multimodal data: independently trained encoders can map synchronized views to incompatible latent regions, while uniform alignment can let an uncertain view distort the shared geometry. To address this gap, we introduce a novel reliability-guided alignment rule that uses normalized VAE posterior variance to control directional cross-modal influence before diffusion training. We study this issue in minority-attack generation for imbalanced Internet-of-Things intrusion detection. Tabular flow attributes, a DeepInsight image, and a packet sequence are encoded by modality-specific variational autoencoders. Per-modality normalized VAE posterior variances provide bounded, sample-specific weighting signals in an asymmetric stop-gradient alignment objective. We use these quantities as heuristic encoder-derived reliability proxies rather than calibrated probabilistic uncertainties. The full modality-specific latent means obtained after alignment fine-tuning are standardized and modeled jointly by a class-conditional EDM; a second-order sampler then generates paired attack-conditioned latent blocks for the three decoders. In controlled experiments on TON-IoT, uncertainty-aware alignment obtains the highest mean precision, recall, density, and coverage among three diffusion variants. Augmenting only the training split yields ROC-AUC 0.9515, PR-AUC 0.8954, balanced accuracy 0.8395, and attack-class F1 0.7940 on an untouched real test set.
