Among the two variants with matched alignment fine-tuning, posterior-variance-based weighting improves both tabular generative fidelity and downstream detection relative to uniform weighting.

</details>


### [303] [Clean Sheets and Checkpoints: Evidence Complexity for Certifying Sequential Agent Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39124)
*Rohith Yanambaka Venkata,Don McBride,Tiago Sequeira,Md. Al Amin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文把"从预备状态按有序里程碑测试AI智能体危险性能力"形式化为k个里程碑链路（划分为检查点区段），研究零失败审计结果在水平α下能认证"完成概率≤ε"所需的最少零响应数，并给出稀有风险相内部的最优渐近常数与最优评估器结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有危险能力评估常从预备状态在有序里程碑上测试智能体，但"全零记录"（无成功）究竟能认证什么，取决于一次失败尝试抹除/隐藏了多少信息。作者希望量化零响应数的认证效力，并给出可达到最优常数的审计构造，同时说明证书对自适应部署过程的适用边界。

Method: 将任务建模为k个里程碑组成的链，划分为检查点区段；对审计前选定的区段，通过计数可能的完成窗口得到对任意给定预算的证书。该界可推广到沿链转移的自适应部署过程，前提是其每个完整历史后的成功概率被相应审计成功概率所上界（该"传输假设"审计本身不检验）。在稀有风险相内部，区段分为"活跃"（预算内极少完成）与"自由"（几乎总能完成），并给出期望零响应数的渐近表达式与达到该常数的证书，以及渐近最优评估器的响应分配结构。

Result: 最小期望零响应数渐近为 log(1/α)(B^a/(a!ε))^{1/K_A}，其中a个活跃区段共含K_A个里程碑，活跃区段为长度不小于依赖预算阈值的区段；证书可达到该常数。任何渐近最优评估器在极限下须把期望响应数均分给活跃里程碑，其余处份额趋于零。当单个区段由活跃转为自由时，其各里程碑获得的极限份额小于活跃里程碑份额；采用"交叉感知"证书可达到最优常数。对已知或未查询前缀步的选定区段，计数失败循环可进一步收紧证书。

Conclusion: 零失败审计的认证力可被精确刻画：所需零响应数由活跃区段数与其中的里程碑数决定，并存在达到最优渐近常数的证书与相应的最优响应分配原则；证书对自适应过程的适用依赖未被审计检验的传输假设，故实际使用需谨慎。交叉感知证书与失败循环计数是提升紧致性的两个具体手段。

Abstract: Dangerous-capability evaluations often test an AI agent from prepared states at ordered milestones. A clean sheet records no successes. What it certifies about a campaign of $B$ attempts depends on what a failed attempt erases. We model the task as a chain of $k$ milestones divided into checkpoint segments and ask how many zero audit responses certify, at level $α$, a completion probability of at most $ε$. For segments chosen before the audit, counting possible completion windows gives a certificate for any specified budget. The bound also covers adaptive deployment processes that follow the chain's transitions, provided their success probabilities after every complete history are capped by the corresponding audit success probabilities. The audit does not test this transport assumption. In rare-risk phase interiors, each segment is either rarely completed within the budget (active) or nearly always completed (free). The minimum expected number of zero responses is asymptotic to $\log(1/α)\,(B^a/(a!\,ε))^{1/K_A}$, where the $a$ active segments contain $K_A$ milestones and are those at least as long as a budget-dependent threshold. The certificate attains this constant. Every asymptotically optimal evaluator must divide its expected response count equally among active milestones in the limit, with a vanishing share elsewhere. Where a single segment passes from active to free, each of its milestones receives a smaller limiting share than an active milestone; a crossover-aware certificate attains the optimal constant. For selected segments with known or unqueried prefix steps, counting failed cycles can sharpen the certificate.

</details>


### [304] [A Novel Approach to Evaluating Evaluation Metrics for Multi-Output Structured Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39162)
*Akshay Vyas,Angelo Pimienta,Nicholas Ruozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"任务特定近似不变量"作为诊断工具，通过增强损失函数在模型输出中注入领域相关不变量，系统检验视觉与语言多输出结构化预测任务中各类评价指标的可靠性，发现分布型指标显著提升而传统指标变化甚微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出结构化预测任务中训练数据仅提供一个真值标签，但可能存在多个同等有效的输出，导致可靠评估长期困难。作者假设人类评估者会隐式地依据任务特定不变量（如着色图像中的物体边界、翻译中的命名实体）判断输出是否可接受，据此希望建立可系统检验指标可靠性的框架。

Method: 1）引入"近似任务特定不变量"概念，作为诊断工具评估视觉与语言任务的多种现有指标；2）构建评估框架，通过增强损失函数（augmented loss）鼓励模型输出满足领域相关不变量，从而系统测试指标可靠性；3）在实验中对比分布型指标与传统指标对不变量引入的敏感度。

Result: 以增强损失强制不变量后，主流分布型指标（distributional metrics）取得实质性提升，而更传统的指标仅发生边际变化。该不变量驱动的评估揭示了标准指标在何处无法检测有意义的差异，并明确了分布型指标奏效或仍存不足的条件。

Conclusion: 任务特定近似不变量是一种有效的诊断与评估手段，能够暴露现有指标（尤其传统指标）对输出质量差异的不敏感；分布型指标在检测实质差异上更具优势，但仍有其失效边界。

Abstract: In multi-output structured prediction tasks, while only one ground truth label may be provided in the training data, multiple equally valid outputs may be possible, making reliable evaluation a persistent challenge. We postulate that human evaluators implicitly use task-specific invariants, e.g., object boundaries in colorized images or named entities in translations, to judge if an output is acceptable. Under this assumption, we introduce a notion of approximate task-specific invariants and use them as diagnostic tools to evaluate a variety of existing metrics for vision and language tasks. We use these task invariants as part of a framework to systematically test metric reliability by encouraging domain-relevant invariants in model outputs via an augmented loss function. In our experiments, we observe that enforcing invariants with an augmented loss yields substantial improvements in popular distributional metrics while more traditional metrics change only marginally. Through this invariants-driven evaluation, we expose where standard metrics fail to detect meaningful differences, and we highlight the conditions under which distributional metrics succeed or still fall short.

</details>


### [305] [ACAM-J-MARS: Multi-Atlas Riemannian Spectral Representations for Decoding Advanced Meditation States from fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39171)
*Jian Wang,Winson F. Z. Yang,Wugeng Zheng,Matthew D. Sacchet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ACAM-J-MARS表征方法，融合多图谱功能连接、SPD流形上的log-Euclidean切空间投影与低频BOLD谱能量特征，用于从fMRI解码jhana型高级专注吸收冥想状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从fMRI推断高级专注吸收冥想（ACAM-J）状态困难：冥想效应空间分布广、个体间差异可超过个体内状态差异、样本量小。传统方法多采用单模态、欧氏、基于图谱的分类，忽略功能连接矩阵的非欧几何结构，且对时序信息表征不足，导致性能次优。

Method: ACAM-J-MARS（多图谱黎曼谱表征）：用多个图谱计算功能连接；将对称正定（SPD）连接矩阵置于黎曼流形并用log-Euclidean映射投影至切空间；再以低频BOLD谱能量扩充连接特征，以在异质变异下分辨细微状态差异。数据为22名练习者的204个片段，并在多种骨干模型上评估。

Result: 在多种骨干模型上均有提升：早期vs晚期ACAM-J二分类平衡准确率0.757；三分类0.545，较最强基线绝对提升10.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 该表征能改善高级冥想脑状态解码，并有望支撑闭环神经反馈与实时心理生理脑机接口等下游应用。

Abstract: Inferring jhana-type advanced concentration absorption meditation (ACAM-J) states from fMRI data is challenging due to the spatially distributed nature of meditation-related effects, substantial inter-individual variability that can exceed within-subject state differences, and limited sample sizes. Conventional machine-learning approaches often formulate this problem as unimodal, Euclidean, atlas-based classification, thereby neglecting the non-Euclidean geometry of functional connectivity matrices and underrepresenting temporal information in functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI); these limitations can lead to suboptimal classification performance. We propose ACAM-J-MARS (\textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{A}tlas \textbf{R}iemannian \textbf{S}pectral representation), which computes functional connectivity using multiple atlases, represents symmetric positive definite (SPD) connectivity matrices on a Riemannian manifold, projects them into a log-Euclidean tangent space, and augments connectivity-derived features with low-frequency BOLD spectral energy to better resolve subtle state differences under heterogeneous variability. Evaluated on 204 segments from 22 practitioners, ACAM-J-MARS improves performance across multiple backbone models, achieving a balanced accuracy of 0.757 for early versus late ACAM-J classification and 0.545 for three-class classification, corresponding to a 10.8-point absolute improvement over the strongest baseline. These findings suggest that the proposed representation improves the decoding of advanced meditative brain states and may enable downstream applications such as closed-loop neurofeedback and real-time psychophysiological brain-computer interfaces.

</details>


### [306] [Analyzing Condensation on Patterned Surfaces via Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39195)
*Tae E. Cooper,Michael Gee,Sheila Whitman,Jemma Dengel,Anthony Katselas,Duygu Dengiz,Frank Keutsch,Joanna Aizenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通用液滴分割工具 TileSAM3，基于 SAM3 结合分层切块推理与后处理，突破现有方法无法识别非均匀表面液滴的局限，构建 HQDD（36.6 万）与 LQDD（6.4 万）两个数据集，在 LQDD 上平均精度达 0.57（阈值法仅 0.088），并结合 KD-tree 追踪揭示刚度图案表面狭窄活性带内的局部成核与合并行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滴状冷凝中的成核、生长、合并、脱落各阶段速率由非均匀表面特征决定，精确刻画需检测数千液滴；然而现有分割方法无法在非均匀（图案化或随机缺陷）表面上有效检测液滴，尤其难以应对恶劣光照与成像质量差的场景，限制了相关材料研究的速度与成本。

Method: 以 SAM3 为基础构建 TileSAM3：采用分层切块（hierarchical tiling）+ 逐块推理 + 后处理，扩展检测上限并提升小液滴的有效分辨率；同时构建两个数据集——高质量液滴数据集 HQDD（N=365,826，均匀表面）与低质量液滴数据集 LQDD（N=63,577，图案化、缺陷密集且光照恶劣表面）；并将分割结果与 KD-tree 追踪结合用于液滴轨迹分析。

Result: 在 HQDD 上 TileSAM3 高度复现阈值法标注结果；在 LQDD 上平均精度 0.57，较阈值法的 0.088 提升逾 6 倍；在所有评估指标上优于其他深度学习模型。结合 KD-tree 追踪，在刚度图案化表面上识别出狭窄活性带内的局部成核与合并现象。

Conclusion: TileSAM3 使光照与成像条件较差的非均匀基底分析成为可能，突破了此前难以研究的表面类型，有助于降低冷凝材料研究的成本并加快研究进程。

Abstract: Dropwise condensation is key to power and water systems. State-of-the-art condensation surfaces are often inhomogeneous, either patterned by design or random defects. Inhomogeneous surface features dictate the rates of each stage of dropwise condensation-nucleation, growth, coalescence, and shedding. Characterizing these rates requires the precise detection of thousands of droplets. Yet, existing segmentation methods are unable to detect droplets on inhomogeneous surfaces. We developed TileSAM3, a universal droplet segmentation tool built on the Segment Anything Model 3 (SAM3). Hierarchical tiling with per-tile inference followed by post-processing extended SAM3's detection cap and raised the effective resolution of small droplets. We created two datasets: High Quality Droplet Dataset (N=365,826; HQDD) with homogeneous surfaces and Low Quality Droplet Dataset (N=63,577; LQDD) with patterned, defect-laden surfaces under adverse lighting. TileSAM3 closely reproduced thresholding annotations on HQDD, surpassed it over sixfold on LQDD (Average Precision 0.57 versus 0.088), and outperformed other deep learning models on all metrics evaluated. Combined with KD-tree tracking, TileSAM3 identified localized nucleation and coalescence in narrow active bands on a stiffness-patterned surface. TileSAM3 enables analysis of understudied inhomogeneous substrates with poor lighting and videography which facilitates faster, lower cost research of condensation materials.

</details>


### [307] [Sparse Autoencoders as Implicit Diversification Regularisers for Cross-Sectional Cryptocurrency Returns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39205)
*Weijia Han,Lisha Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）可用少量激活特征描述多资产日收益；实证显示其组合波动率更低、所选资产间相关性更低，但风险调整收益不及简单反转策略，且因子身份在不同训练间不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经因子在资产定价与组合构建中广泛应用，但可解释性差，且其对组合风险的作用不明确。作者希望检验稀疏编码能否同时缓解这两个问题：既给出可读的稀疏特征，又能带来更分散的组合。

Method: 对多资产日收益拟合稀疏自编码器，以少量活跃特征表示收益；以加密货币收益为主样本、股票为次级检验；在严格时间顺序的样本外评估框架下，将 SAE 与常见基准（含简单反转策略）比较，并考察组合波动率、所选资产间相关性及跨训练运行的因子稳定性。

Result: SAE 组合的波动率一致更低，该效应可归因于所选资产间相关性更低；但 SAE 在风险调整收益上未超越简单反转策略，且其因子身份在不同训练运行之间不稳定。

Conclusion: 稀疏编码能够支持组合分散化，这一风险效应应明确与 alpha 主张及"稳定因子身份"的主张相区分；SAE 的价值在于风险侧而非收益侧或可解释因子的稳定性。

Abstract: Factor models are central to asset pricing and portfolio construction, but neural factors are often difficult to interpret, and their effect on portfolio risk is unclear. A sparse autoencoder (SAE) may address both concerns by describing daily returns across many assets with only a few active features. We ask whether this sparse representation produces stable financial factors and better-diversified portfolios. We study cryptocurrency returns, use equities as a secondary test, and compare the SAE with common benchmarks in a time-ordered evaluation. We find consistently lower volatility in SAE portfolios and link this result to lower correlations among the selected assets. However, the SAE does not outperform a simple contrarian strategy on risk-adjusted return, and its factor identities are not stable across training runs. Our contribution is to show that sparse coding can support portfolio diversification, while clearly separating this risk effect from claims about alpha or stable factor identities.

</details>


### [308] [Where Position Matters: Profiling-Guided Layer-Wise RoPE Allocation in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39223)
*Zhiwei Liu,Boao Kong,Bing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于剖析（profiling）的分层RoPE分配框架：在固定预算下，依据早期覆盖收益曲线为不同层分配高/低旋转覆盖，并支持MLA中物理旋转维度分配，实现可迁移、高效的架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RoPE在所有Transformer层统一使用相同的覆盖度，忽略了不同层对位置信息需求的异质性；相位干预实验显示旋转相位依赖具有非均匀、模型特异性，覆盖干预则揭示层级的覆盖升级收益，这提示应做分层、差异化的位置编码配置。

Method: 1) 相位偏移（phase-off）干预刻画各层对旋转相位的依赖；2) 覆盖干预建立"覆盖—收益"剖析，据早期覆盖收益曲线在固定预算下做逐层RoPE分配（高/低覆盖）；3) 框架兼容固定宽度Q/K表示与MLA中的物理旋转维度分配；4) 归一化深度映射在同一模型族内迁移剖析结果，使大模型预训练前可用小模型测量值指导分配；5) 简要分析选择与迁移与分数校准、层间交互的关系。

Result: 从头训练的实验中，相较可获得的预算匹配基线取得验证损失改进；解析式资源核算表明物理MLA在质量—存储—计算上具有更优权衡。

Conclusion: 位置需求可被剖析、量化并跨模型规模迁移，从而把位置编码配置纳入可迁移、高效的架构设计，连接位置需求与架构效率。

Abstract: Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) typically uses uniform coverage across Transformer layers despite heterogeneous positional requirements. Phase-off interventions reveal non-uniform, model-specific rotary-phase dependence, while coverage interventions relate it to coverage-upgrade benefits. We propose profiling-guided layer-wise RoPE allocation, assigning high/low coverage under a fixed budget using early coverage-benefit profiles. The framework supports fixed-width query/key representations and physical rotary-dimension allocation in Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA). Normalized-depth mapping transfers profiles within model families, enabling small-model measurements to guide larger-model allocations before pretraining. A brief analysis relates selection and transfer to score calibration and inter-layer interactions. From-scratch experiments demonstrate validation-loss improvements over available budget-matched baselines, while analytical resource accounting identifies favorable quality--storage--compute trade-offs for physical MLA. These results connect positional demand to transferable, efficient architecture design.

</details>


### [309] [IMRouter: Imbalance-aware Temporal Pattern Routing for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39246)
*Xin Yang,Cai Xu,Haishun Chen,Ying Liu,Jie Zhao,Wei Zhao,Ziyu Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出基于LLM的时间序列预测方法普遍忽视"时序模式不平衡（TPI）"问题，提出IMRouter框架，通过模式自适应时序表征学习（PATR）与路由引导语义对齐（RGSA）缓解长尾模式上的预测偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练大语言模型的时间序列预测（TSF）方法主要优化整体预测目标，忽略了不同时序模式出现频率高度不均（TPI）的现象。实证分析显示，TPI在常用TSF基准中普遍存在，且低频模式的预测误差显著更高，形成长尾偏差。

Method: IMRouter包含两个模块：(1) PATR（模式自适应时序表征学习）利用梯度冲突与局部梯度密度指导专家特化，通过密度感知加权惩罚不兼容的专家分配，抑制高频优化信号在路由器训练中的主导地位；(2) RGSA（路由引导语义对齐）通过互信息最大化，将路由器学到的模式特化知识迁移至跨模态对齐过程，从而在不同模式下保留有区分性的时序信息。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明IMRouter有效，尤其在提升低频时序模式的预测性能方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 将时序模式不平衡显式建模并同时作用于表征学习与跨模态对齐，是提升LLM-based TSF在长尾模式上鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Accurate time series forecasting (TSF) is essential for Web applications, such as traffic monitoring and online resource management. Recent advances in leveraging pre-trained large language models (LLMs) have shown promising performance in TSF. However, existing LLM-based TSF methods largely optimize the overall forecasting objective while overlooking temporal pattern imbalance (TPI), where different temporal patterns occur at highly unequal frequencies. Our empirical analysis reveals that TPI is prevalent across widely used TSF benchmarks, with substantially higher forecasting errors on less frequent patterns. To address this issue, we propose IMRouter (Imbalance-aware Temporal Pattern Routing), a novel framework that mitigates TPI-induced biases in both temporal representation learning and cross-modal alignment. Specifically, IMRouter introduces Pattern-Adaptive Temporal Representation Learning (PATR), which leverages gradient conflict and local gradient density to guide expert specialization. By penalizing incompatible expert assignments with density-aware weighting, PATR reduces the dominance of frequent optimization signals in router training. Furthermore, Routing-Guided Semantic Alignment (RGSA) transfers the pattern-specific knowledge learned by the router to cross-modal alignment through mutual information maximization, preserving distinctive temporal information across different patterns. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of IMRouter, particularly in improving forecasting performance on less frequent temporal patterns.

</details>


### [310] [Strongly Adaptive Online Learning Achieves Uniform Discounted Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39292)
*Sifan Yang,Wenhao Yang,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明强自适应遗憾界可直接推出凸、强凸与指数凹损失函数在任意折扣因子下的统一折扣遗憾保证，核心是折扣遗憾对固定比较器的区间遗憾凸组合精确分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作虽借助专门的元算法在连续折扣因子范围内给出统一的折扣遗憾界，但仅适用于凸函数，无法覆盖强凸与指数凹等更强曲率的情形，因此需要新的分析途径来扩展适用范围。

Method: 观察到对固定比较器的折扣遗憾可精确分解为各区间遗憾的凸组合，从而把折扣遗憾问题归约到强自适应遗憾界，直接利用已有的强自适应算法得到各类损失函数的界。

Result: 凸函数情形下对所有折扣因子 λ∈(0,1) 得到 O(√(log T/(1−λ))) 的折扣遗憾界，速率与以往工作一致且覆盖更广的折扣因子范围；强凸与指数凹情形下得到 O(log T (1+log(1/(1−λ)))) 的界。

Conclusion: 强自适应遗憾界蕴含统一折扣遗憾保证，该归约统一处理了凸、强凸与指数凹三类损失，并刻画了不同曲率下折扣遗憾对折扣因子的依赖关系。

Abstract: Discounted regret measures the performance of an online learner by assigning exponentially decaying weights to past losses, reflecting the greater importance of recent losses in non-stationary online learning. While previous work obtains uniform discounted regret bounds over a continuous range of discount factors using a specialized meta-algorithm, its results are limited to convex functions. In this paper, we demonstrate that strongly adaptive regret bounds can directly imply uniform discounted regret guarantees for convex, strongly convex, and exp-concave loss functions. Our key observation is that discounted regret against a fixed comparator admits an exact decomposition into a convex combination of interval regrets. For convex functions, this reduction yields an $O(\sqrt{\log T/(1-λ)})$ discounted regret bound for all discount factors $λ\in(0,1)$, matching the previous rate over a broader range of discount factors. For strongly convex and exp-concave functions, we provide regret bounds of $O\left(\log T\left(1+\log\frac{1}{1-λ}\right)\right)$.

</details>


### [311] [There Is No Free Lunch in RLVR: Every pass@1 Gain Has a pass@k Price](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39295)
*Yinuo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将RLVR建模为基础策略在跨提示共享策略上的指数倾斜，证明任何提升pass@1的更新必然使某些提示的成功率下降（Stiemke对偶下的严格正"价格"），并给出pass@k交叉次数受符号变差约束的紧界，说明交叉是可测量的代价而非可修补的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR训练使模型pass@1上升、但在大采样预算k下被基座模型反超（pass@k交叉）。作者要判定这是RL未能真正增强推理的失败，还是训练不充分导致的暂态现象。已有文献只观察到单次交叉，缺少对该现象产生条件、交叉次数与代价大小的理论刻画，也无法从评测日志中定量诊断。

Method: 把RLVR表述为基础策略在"跨提示共享策略"上的指数倾斜（exponential tilt）。在"排他性专精"下证明：每次提升pass@1的更新都严格降低某个提示的成功率；严格正的提示价格（由Stiemke对偶给出）是充要条件，仅在单个提示上是最优策略被证明不充分。当损失触及最难提示时强制触发pass@k交叉，交叉次数不超过各提示成功率偏移的符号变差——这是全正（totally positive）意义上的紧界，在保序情形退化为已知的单交叉结论。进一步的约束更新刻画出pass@1与pass@k的精确前沿，去除交叉将损失全部pass@1增益。同一倾斜恒等式还推出熵坍缩与可辨识指数的熵—性能定律。最后基于该界、保序检验与价格构造可从评测日志直接计算的诊断量。

Result: (1) pass@1提升必然伴随某些提示成功率下降，且严格正价格是其充要条件；(2) 交叉次数≤逐提示成功率偏移的符号变差，界紧且包含单交叉为保序特例；(3) 约束更新给出pass@1–pass@k精确前沿，消除交叉需付出全部pass@1增益；(4) 统一了熵坍缩与熵—性能定律两种文献，给出可辨识指数；(5) 提出可从任意评测日志读取界、保序检验与价格的诊断方法。

Conclusion: pass@k交叉不是需要修补的缺陷，而是RLVR沿推理前沿移动时必然支付的"价格"；RL无法拓宽推理前沿，只能在前沿上移动。核心结论是"没有免费午餐，只有价格"，且该价格可被测量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) makes a language model solve more problems on the first try and fewer when given many tries: it raises pass@1 while the base model overtakes it at large sampling budget k. Is this a failure of RL to add reasoning, or a transient that more training removes? We settle the question with a theorem. The reversal is a price, and we compute its exact rate. Modeling RLVR as an exponential tilt of a base policy over strategies shared across prompts, we prove that under exclusive specialization every update that raises pass@1 strictly lowers the success of some prompt; a strictly positive price on prompts (Stiemke duality) is the exact condition, and being merely the best strategy on a prompt is provably not enough. When the loss reaches the hardest prompts, it forces a pass@k crossover, and the number of crossings is at most the sign variation of the shift in per-prompt success, a totally positive bound that is tight and that recovers the known single-crossover result as its order-preserving case. The crossover is not a bug to patch: a constrained update traces the exact pass@1 versus pass@k frontier, and removing the crossover entirely costs the entire pass@1 gain. The same tilt identity yields entropy collapse and the entropy-performance law with an identifiable exponent, unifying two literatures under one mechanism. Reinforcement cannot widen the reasoning frontier; it can only move along it, at a price we make measurable by a diagnostic that reads the bound, the order-preserving test, and the price from any evaluation log. There is no free lunch, only a price.

</details>


### [312] [Multi-Solution Inverse Design of Pixelated Patch Antennas Using an Autoregressive Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39300)
*Young-Mok Yun,Seung-Won Shin,Jaeyong Chung,Kyung-Sik Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于自回归Transformer的逆向设计方法，用于10×10像素化贴片天线的多解合成，并经加工实测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素化贴片天线逆向设计方法存在实际局限：BPSO等元启发式方法对初始化与超参数敏感且耗时；串联逆网络难以刻画逆向设计的一对多特性；扩散模型对中等规模二值布局合成而言复杂度和数据需求过高。

Method: 构建自回归Transformer模型，将像素布局作为离散序列逐元素生成，从而对给定目标响应采样出多个可行结构；模型预训练后再以具有陡峭频谱过渡的阶梯状目标响应进行微调，实现双频段设计，无需手工构建详细S11曲线。与BPSO进行对比。

Result: 相较BPSO，所提方法更高效地生成有效且多样的天线候选结构；加工与实测结果表明，不同像素化布局均可实现目标天线响应，验证了框架的多解能力。

Conclusion: 自回归Transformer为像素化贴片天线逆向设计提供了高效、可表达一对多映射且数据需求适中的方案，阶梯状目标响应的微调策略使频段指定更直观，多解特性获实测支持。

Abstract: Deep-learning-based inverse design has emerged as a promising framework for pixelated patch antenna synthesis by enabling broader exploration of discrete geometry spaces and reducing design time compared with conventional template-based methods. Existing methods, including surrogate-assisted metaheuristic optimization, tandem inverse networks, and diffusion models, have advanced the field but still face practical limitations. Metaheuristic methods such as binary particle swarm optimization (BPSO) are sensitive to initialization and search hyperparameters and are often time-consuming, tandem networks have difficulty capturing the one-to-many nature of inverse antenna design, and diffusion models may require excessive complexity and data for moderate-scale binary layout synthesis. In this paper, an autoregressive (AR) transformer is presented for the inverse design of 10 x 10 pixelated patch antennas. Its sequential discrete generation process enables the synthesis of multiple feasible structures for a given target response. The pretrained model is further fine-tuned for dual-band design using step-shaped target responses with sharp spectral transitions, allowing intuitive band specification without manually constructing detailed S11 curves. Compared with BPSO, our proposed method generates valid and diverse antenna candidates more efficiently. Fabricated and measured examples confirm that distinct pixelated layouts can realize the desired antenna responses, thereby demonstrating the multi-solution capability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [313] [Learning to Bound: Monotonic, Calibrated Neural Upper Bounds for MPE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39305)
*Akshay Vyas,Hailiang Dong,Shivvrat Arya,Brij Malhotra,Tahrima Rahman,Vibhav Gogate,Nicholas Ruozzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Learning to Bound (L2B)，用神经网络摊销概率图模型MPE搜索中的界计算成本，通过统计校准保证可采纳性、通过结构约束保证单调收紧，在多个基准上以低数个数量级的成本得到更紧的单调界，并提升高树宽实例上分支定界的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGM中MPE问题在大规模稠密结构模型上不可解；分支定界等anytime系统搜索的效率依赖紧的可采纳上界，而Mini-Bucket Elimination等经典方法计算这些界代价过高，成为瓶颈。

Method: 数据驱动框架L2B：训练神经预测器来摊销界计算成本。针对搜索界的两项关键性质分别施加约束——可采纳性通过统计校准技术实现；随证据累积的渐进收紧通过结构性架构约束实现，使单调性由构造保证。

Result: 在挑战性基准上，L2B在每一类PGM上都以低数个数量级的成本提供更紧且单调的界；与分支定界结合后，在高树宽实例上取得可测量的性能提升。

Conclusion: 学习型界预测可作为经典界计算的廉价替代，在保证可采纳性与单调性的前提下有效加速MPE的精确/anytime搜索。

Abstract: Probabilistic graphical models (PGMs) compactly encode structured joint distributions, but Most Probable Explanation (MPE), which finds the highest-probability assignment to unobserved variables, remains computationally intractable in large, densely structured models. Anytime systematic search, such as branch-and-bound (B&B), is a popular approach, but its efficiency depends on tight admissible upper bounds that are often prohibitively expensive to compute using classical methods like Mini-Bucket Elimination (MBE). We introduce Learning to Bound (L2B), a data-driven framework that amortizes bound computation cost by training a neural predictor. L2B targets two key properties required for effective search bounds: admissibility, which is achieved via statistical calibration techniques, and progressive tightening as evidence accumulates, which is enforced through structural architectural constraints that ensure monotonicity by construction. Across challenging benchmarks, L2B delivers tighter, monotonic bounds at orders-of-magnitude lower cost on every PGM family, and yields measurable improvements when integrated with branch-and-bound on high-treewidth instances.

</details>


### [314] [TaxoMFM: A Taxonomy-Aware Foundation Model for the Human Gut Microbiome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39316)
*Haochen Xue,Anastasia Godneva,Yulong Li,Qi Cao,Zhaodong Wu,Kaiyu Liang,Jionglong Su,Eran Segal,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TaxoMFM，将分类学层级结构融入自监督学习，构建大规模肠道菌群基准TaxoGut-HPP，并在零样本跨队列迁移中取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肠道菌群基础模型多采用扁平的分类单元表示，无法利用已知的分类学层级结构，限制了表征能力与可迁移性；同时缺乏大规模、多任务统一的评测基准。

Method: TaxoMFM通过分类学感知编码与层级组合重构，联合学习各分类层级上的丰度模式及组内相对比例，将分类结构嵌入自监督预训练；并构建TaxoGut-HPP数据集（100,806份肠道菌群样本，来自231个来源队列，覆盖40个分类任务与8个回归任务）。

Result: 在疾病、肠道健康、宿主性状及肝脏超声表型预测任务上取得有竞争力的性能；在目标队列既不参与预训练也不参与下游适配的跨队列零样本迁移中，结直肠癌、2型糖尿病、炎症性肠病的AUROC分别为0.838、0.736、0.722。分析表明模型可跨分类尺度恢复群落变化，并在数据库更新与物种词表变动下保持信息与表征一致性。

Conclusion: 将分类学层级结构纳入自监督学习可有效提升菌群基础模型的表征质量与零样本泛化能力，并在数据与词表演化下保持稳健。

Abstract: Existing gut microbiome foundation models typically use flat taxon representations, limiting their ability to exploit known taxonomic hierarchies. We introduce TaxoMFM, a microbiome foundation model that integrates taxonomic structure into self-supervised learning. TaxoMFM jointly learns abundance patterns across taxonomic ranks and relative proportions within groups through taxonomy-aware encoding and hierarchical compositional reconstruction. We further construct TaxoGut-HPP, comprising 100,806 gut microbiome profiles from 231 source cohorts and covering 40 classification and 8 regression tasks. TaxoMFM achieves competitive performance across disease, gut health, host trait, and liver ultrasound phenotype prediction. In cross-cohort zero-shot transfer, where target cohorts are excluded from both pretraining and downstream adaptation, TaxoMFM achieves AUROCs of 0.838, 0.736, and 0.722 for colorectal cancer, type 2 diabetes, and inflammatory bowel disease, respectively. Further analyses show that TaxoMFM recovers community changes across taxonomic scales, while preserving community information and producing consistent representations under database updates and species-vocabulary shifts. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/TaxoMFM-F16D/.

</details>


### [315] [Class-aware Latent Attention Replay (CLAR): Class Incremental Hazard Diagnosis for Resilient AI in the Manufacturing Industrial Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39323)
*Avranshu Mahanty,Ran Jin,Yingyan Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Class-aware Latent Attention Replay (CLAR)，一种面向多标签、类别不平衡危险诊断流的回放式类增量学习方法：双缓冲（主要存紧凑潜在表示+少量原始样本）、注意力杠杆分数(ALS)上界分类器logit并指导类均衡蓄水池采样、EMA教师减缓潜在空间漂移；理论证明缓冲类均衡且服从ALS加权类分布，并在有限容量下ALS引导回放的梯度误差低于类均衡均匀回放；在MII测试床危险诊断数据上取得同缓冲规模下最高加权F1均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造工业互联网(MII)中AI系统的韧性依赖对数据层、AI流水线层与信息物理层的隐患做及时诊断。实际运行中：新隐患类型持续出现且每类仅少量样本；隐患会共现(多标签)；诊断模型部署在内存受限的计算节点上。现有基于回放的类增量学习(CIL)方法假设单标签、均衡数据流，存储高维原始样本，且样本选择与分类器无关，因此不适用于该场景。

Method: CLAR由三部分构成：(1) 双缓冲，主要存储紧凑的潜在表示，仅保留少量原始样本；(2) Attention Leverage Score (ALS)，对分类器logit给出上界，用于指导类均衡的蓄水池采样；(3) 指数滑动平均(EMA)教师，用于减缓潜在空间漂移。此外对理想化缓冲模型给出理论分析。

Result: 理论结果：对理想化缓冲模型证明缓冲是类均衡的，并服从ALS加权的类分布；当每类容量低于临界值时，ALS引导回放的梯度误差低于类均衡均匀回放的梯度误差。实验：在来自MII测试床的危险诊断数据上，在相同缓冲规模下，CLAR在所评估的回放方法中取得最高的平均加权F1(mean average weighted F1)分数。(摘要未给出具体数值)

Conclusion: CLAR通过潜在表示为主的混合回放、分类器感知的ALS样本选择与EMA教师，缓解了多标签、类别不均衡、小样本新类与内存受限条件下的记忆漂移与选择偏差问题，验证了其在MII隐患诊断增量学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems provide critical computation services in the Manufacturing Industrial Internet (MII), and their resilience depends on timely diagnosis of hazards across the data, AI pipeline, and cyber-physical layers. During operation, new hazard types keep emerging with only a few samples each, hazards co-occur, and the diagnosis model runs on memory-constrained computation nodes. Replay-based class-incremental learning (CIL) can learn new hazard classes while revisiting a small memory of past samples, but existing methods assume single-label, balanced streams, store high-dimensional raw samples, and select them independently of the classifier. We propose Class-aware Latent Attention Replay (CLAR), which combines a dual buffer that stores mostly compact latent representations with a few raw samples, an Attention Leverage Score (ALS) that upper-bounds the classifier logit and guides class-balanced reservoir sampling, and an exponential-moving-average teacher that slows latent-space drift. For an idealized buffer model, we prove that the buffer is class-balanced and follows an ALS-weighted class distribution, and that below a critical per-class capacity, ALS-guided replay has a lower replay-gradient error than class-balanced uniform replay. In the case study, On hazard diagnosis data from an MII testbed, CLAR achieves the highest mean average weighted F1 score among the evaluated replay methods with the same buffer size

</details>


### [316] [RetroChessBench: A Benchmark for Reconstructing and Proving Chess Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39341)
*Tidor-Vlad Pricope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出RetroChessBench（12项逆向国际象棋任务基准），发现GPT 6 Astra High在无工具与有代码执行条件下分别有22/24、18/24次尝试未能给出所需最终答案，平均终端强化学习奖励为14.75%与27.19%，表明前沿模型在规则明确但缺少示范解法的推理任务上存在系统性推理失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型（如GPT 6 Astra）已能参与数学研究，但在规则完全显式的任务中仍出现严重推理失败。作者希望检验数学推理能力的真实泛化程度：模型是否能将学到的原则迁移到缺少详细示范解法的领域。逆向国际象棋被视为"纯推理"试验场——求解者需重建合法棋局历史，并区分"可能发生"与"必然发生"的事件。此外，网络上的逆向棋谱档案通常只给题目与最终着法序列，缺少推导过程，作者推测训练数据中这类"缺失的解释"是推理失败的成因之一。

Method: 构建RetroChessBench基准：共12个任务，覆盖三类题型——精确长度构造、唯一终局、穷举答案集；每题配备参考答案、评分标准（rubrics）与终端奖励环境（terminal-reward environment）。评测条件为每题每条件两次试验，对比"无工具"与"可执行代码"两种设置，以终端强化学习奖励作为量化指标，并对失败案例进行定性错误分析。

Result: GPT 6 Astra High在无工具条件下24次尝试中22次未能产出所需最终答案，在用代码执行条件下为18/24；平均终端RL奖励分别为14.75%和27.19%。错误分析识别出典型推理缺陷：失败的回复会否定合法历史，并将暂时性阻碍误判为永久性阻碍。代码工具的增益有限，说明失败并非单纯计算能力不足。

Conclusion: 前沿模型的数学推理能力在超出熟悉解法模式的领域泛化有限，规则明确并不足以保证正确推理。作者假设训练数据中缺乏详细推导（只含题目与答案）是重要成因。通用智能的主张需要证据表明模型能把学到的原则迁移到少样本、少示范的领域。

Abstract: Frontier language models such as GPT 6 Astra have contributed to research mathematics, demonstrating substantial reasoning capabilities. Yet serious reasoning failures persist even in tasks governed by explicit rules. We demonstrate this in retrograde chess which is a playground for pure reasoning, where solvers reconstruct legal histories and distinguish possible events from forced ones. We introduce RetroChessBench, a 12-task benchmark covering exact-length constructions, unique endings, and exhaustive answer sets, with references, rubrics, and a terminal-reward environment. In two trials per task and condition, GPT 6 Astra High fails to produce the required final answer in 22/24 attempts without tools and 18/24 with code execution; mean terminal RL rewards are 14.75% and 27.19%, respectively. The examples identify faulty deductions: failed responses reject legal histories and treat temporary obstructions as permanent. Online retrograde archives often provide puzzles and final move sequences without detailed derivations. We hypothesize that limited exposure to these missing explanations in web-derived training data contributes to the observed reasoning failures. This questions how far frontier mathematical reasoning really generalizes beyond familiar solution patterns. Claims of general intelligence require evidence that models can transfer learnt principles to areas with few worked examples.

</details>


### [317] [MulClip: A table-free activation function with monotonic and folding regimes for reservoir computing on microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39343)
*Guren Matsumura,Masato Takemoto,Shinzo Koyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MulClip：面向微控制器储层计算的轻量激活函数，仅用比较、绝对值、乘法与减法实现，硬件状态更新时间仅为 tanh 的 0.34 倍，且精度不降反升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环形储层等稀疏 RC 拓扑中乘加运算很少，tanh 的计算开销却占 Cortex-M4 上状态更新时间的 75%，成为主要瓶颈；已有的 tanh 近似仍偏重且表达力有限。

Method: MulClip 用比较、取绝对值、乘法、减法引入曲率；其截断阈值不设在极值点，而设在越过极值后响应回到零的位置，使单一函数同时具备单调区与非单调折叠区，由输入尺度等常规超参数搜索自然决定使用哪一区，无需任务特定调参。文中给出三阶版本，并在硬件上与其他 11 种 tanh 近似对比测量。

Result: 硬件测得状态更新时间为 tanh 的 0.34 倍，快于所评估的全部 11 种 tanh 近似。PC 评估：NARMA-20（预激活处于单调区）上三阶 MulClip 的 NMSE 比 tanh 低 22%；8 位奇偶校验任务上，带折叠区版本将准确率从无折叠单调截断变体的 0.76–0.78 提升至 0.97–0.98。两个任务的片上推理结果与 PC 结果接近。

Conclusion: MulClip 以极低计算代价替代 tanh，兼顾效率与表达力（单调/折叠双区域），在 MCU 上实现近乎无损的片上推理，适合资源受限的储层计算部署。

Abstract: This letter proposes MulClip, a lightweight activation function for reservoir computing (RC) on microcontrollers (MCUs). In a ring reservoir, a representative sparse RC topology, multiply-accumulate operations are few, and evaluating tanh accounted for 75% of the state-update time on a Cortex-M4. MulClip introduces curvature into the response using only comparison, absolute value, multiplication, and subtraction, and places its clipping threshold not at the extremum but at the point where the response returns to zero after passing the extremum. As a result, a single function has both a monotonic regime and a nonmonotonic folding regime that increases the expressiveness of a single unit. Which regime is used is determined by the usual hyperparameter search, e.g., over the input scale, so no task-specific threshold tuning is required. The state-update time measured on hardware was 0.34 times that of tanh and shorter than that of any of the 11 evaluated tanh approximations. In PC-based evaluation, third-order MulClip achieved a 22% lower NMSE than tanh on NARMA-20, where the pre-activations remained in the monotonic regime, and on 8-bit parity it raised the accuracy from 0.76-0.78 for the monotonic-clip variant without folding to 0.97-0.98. For these two tasks, on-device inference yielded nearly the same values as on the PC.

</details>


### [318] [More Is Better? Exploring Latent Correspondence in Federated Hypergraph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39362)
*Bo Yan,Siming Zhang,Siqi Li,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画了潜在跨客户端超边对应关系的可识别性与计算复杂性，提出FedASTRA框架，通过自适应热扩散与掩码潜在预测学习对应关系不变的扩散表示，仅通信模型参数，并证明其渐近最优性与全局风险收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦超图学习中，同一本地数据发布可能对应多种全局超图结构，潜在跨客户端超边对应关系带来根本性的可识别性与计算挑战，需要明确该问题何时可解、何时不可解。

Method: 先刻画恢复潜在对应关系的可处理性（成对指派可处理、多客户端超边匹配一般不可处理）；据此导出交叉熵分解，揭示本地预测何时优于使用额外跨客户端信息的预测器；进而提出FedASTRA：基于自适应热扩散与掩码潜在预测学习对应关系不变的扩散表示，仅传输模型参数。

Result: 证明FedASTRA相对于允许的本地信息具有渐近最优性，并在恢复、表示与更新对齐的显式条件下证明全局总体风险收敛。在8个真实数据集上优于无标识符的联邦图/超图方法；在ogbn-papers100M上达到性能-通信Pareto前沿，Accuracy 50.07、Macro-F1 29.73，每轮仅通信59.60 MiB。

Conclusion: 该工作为联邦超图对应关系学习提供了可处理性刻画与理论依据，并证明无需共享标识符、仅通信参数即可取得通信高效且具理论保证的优异性能。

Abstract: Federated hypergraph learning with latent cross-client hyperedge correspondence poses fundamental identifiability and computational challenges, as the same local release can correspond to multiple global hypergraph structures. We characterize the tractability of recovering these latent correspondences, showing tractable pairwise assignment but generally intractable multi-client hyperedge matching. Building on this characterization, we derive a cross-entropy decomposition that reveals when local prediction can outperform predictors fitted with additional cross-client information. Motivated by this principle, we introduce Federated Adaptive Spectral Training with Representation Alignment (FedASTRA), which learns correspondence-invariant diffusion representations through adaptive heat diffusion and masked latent prediction while communicating model parameters only. We establish its asymptotic optimality with respect to the permitted local information and prove global population-risk convergence under explicit conditions on recovery, representation, and update alignment. Experiments on eight real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of FedASTRA against identifier-free federated graph and hypergraph methods. On ogbn-papers100M, FedASTRA reaches the performance-communication Pareto frontier, achieving 50.07 Accuracy and 29.73 Macro-F1 while communicating only 59.60 MiB per round.

</details>


### [319] [Safe Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning: Zero Constraint Violation and Primal-Averaging Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39409)
*Chenxi Li,Yao Shu,Siyang Gao,Shuang Qiu,Guangxin Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究安全多目标强化学习（safe MORL），提出在未知动力学与约束代价下、学习全程零约束违反的算法：偏好给定情形下结合乐观奖励与悲观代价得到安全帕累托最优策略，并进一步给出免偏好算法与松弛初始安全策略要求的版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多MORL应用需要在满足安全约束的同时平衡冲突目标；核心难点在于动力学与约束代价未知时，既要充分探索又不能违反约束，且已有工作未保证每条执行策略的安全性。

Method: 对给定偏好，将乐观奖励估计与悲观代价界结合，同时处理累积约束与逐步约束；基于平均占据测度导出安全马尔可夫策略，并在平滑Tchebycheff标量化下分析次优间隙。进一步提出免偏好算法：先无偏好安全探索，再对任意偏好离线规划而不额外交互。最后放松对严格可行策略及其代价的已知性假设，允许常数级累积违反。

Result: 偏好给定算法以高概率保证T回合内每条执行策略零约束违反，次优间隙为Õ(1/√T)；免偏好算法在T个探索回合与K次规划迭代下保持相同安全保证，次优间隙为Õ(1/√T+1/√K)；松弛版本至多产生常数累积违反。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首批对每条执行策略均保证安全的样本高效MORL算法，兼顾安全性、样本效率与偏好灵活性。

Abstract: Many applications of multi-objective reinforcement learning (MORL) require policies that balance conflicting objectives while satisfying safety constraints. We study safe MORL with zero constraint violation throughout learning, covering both cumulative and stepwise constraints. A key challenge is to explore sufficiently under unknown dynamics and constraint costs without violating the constraints. For a given preference over objectives, we develop an algorithm to learn a safe Pareto optimal policy by combining optimistic reward estimates with pessimistic cost bounds. With high probability, every policy executed over $T$ episodes incurs zero constraint violation. The algorithm returns a safe Markov policy induced by the averaged occupancy measure and achieves an $\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ suboptimality gap under smooth Tchebycheff scalarization. We further develop a preference-free algorithm that first explores safely without a prespecified preference and then computes a safe policy for any given preference without further interaction. Over $T$ exploration episodes and $K$ planning iterations, it retains the same safety guarantee and achieves an $\widetilde{O}(1/\sqrt{T}+1/\sqrt{K})$ suboptimality gap. Both algorithms require knowledge of a policy that strictly satisfies the constraints and its costs. We then relax these requirements, incurring at most constant cumulative violation. To our knowledge, we provide the first sample-efficient MORL algorithms that guarantee safety for every executed policy.

</details>


### [320] [Dobb: Shard-Local Preconditioning in Block-Diagonal Eigenbases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39412)
*Shi-Yuk Fong,Shuhua Yu,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Dobb（块对角特征基分布式优化器），通过在参数分片内部本地计算矩阵感知更新，避免 TP/FSDP 下的跨设备通信，在 GPT-2 XL 上优于 Dion 且将 FSDP 优化器调用时间从 124.1 ms 降至 9.2 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型参数与优化器状态超出单节点内存时，需在设备间分片张量。在 TP 或 FSDP 下，Muon 等矩阵感知优化器需要跨分片执行全局矩阵运算（如正交化），从而引入大量通信开销。Dion 虽已尝试降低通信，但仍存在优化器调用耗时高的问题。

Method: Dobb 用对角块近似输入激活的二阶矩，并在每个块内跟踪一个完整基；将动量投影到这些特征基上，对系数施加符号归一化（sign normalization），再重构出更新。当每个块恰好位于单个分片内（TP 或 FSDP 下）时，矩阵更新完全本地完成，除激活二阶矩的数据并行同步外无需任何额外通信。

Result: 在 GPT-2 XL（1.56B 参数）上，采用 16 个块的 Dobb 验证损失为 2.476，而 rank fraction 为 1/16 的 Dion 为 2.511；在 32 张 H100 上使用 FSDP 时，Dobb 将摊薄后的优化器调用时间从 Dion 的 124.1 ms 降至 9.2 ms。

Conclusion: 块对角特征基近似能在保持（甚至提升）矩阵感知优化器收敛质量的同时，消除分片场景下的全局矩阵运算通信，显著降低分布式训练中的优化器开销。

Abstract: Training language models whose parameters and optimizer state exceed a single node's memory requires sharding these tensors across devices. Under tensor parallelism (TP) or fully sharded data parallelism (FSDP), matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon introduce communication to perform global matrix operations across shards. We propose Dobb (Distributed Optimizer with Block-diagonal Eigenbases), a matrix-aware optimizer designed to compute updates locally within parameter shards. Dobb approximates the input-activation second moment with diagonal blocks and tracks a complete basis within each block. It projects momentum into these bases, applies sign normalization to the coefficients, and reconstructs the update. When each block lies within one shard under TP or FSDP, Dobb applies its matrix update locally, requiring no communication beyond data-parallel synchronization of the activation second moments. We benchmark Dobb against Dion, a distributed matrix-aware optimizer designed to retain the benefits of orthonormalized updates while reducing communication. On GPT-2 XL (1.56B parameters), Dobb with 16 blocks reaches a validation loss of 2.476, compared with 2.511 for Dion at rank fraction 1/16. Under FSDP on 32 H100 GPUs, Dobb reduces the amortized optimizer-call time from 124.1 ms for Dion to 9.2 ms.

</details>


### [321] [Accuracy is not compatibility: two Evo 2 checkpoints hinge on one FP8 matrix multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39415)
*Xun Ding,Ziyuan Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FP8格式只规定数值存储，不规定乘积如何求和，这一缺口会导致checkpoint失效。作者用H100 FP8 GEMM仿真器mmaemu把Evo 2（DNA语言模型）的精度依赖定位到首个投影层，并仅仿真该层即恢复40B模型在下游变异分类上的AUROC（0.434→0.869）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP8规范仅约束数值存储格式，未约束乘积的累加（求和）方式与舍入行为，因此同一checkpoint在不同硬件上会产生不同结果。Evo 2 的1B与40B checkpoint明确要求Hopper FP8，但在Blackwell（sm_120）与RTX 5070 Ti上均失败，说明"参考算术"未被发布者规定，需要定位并复原其依赖的算术细节。

Method: 构建H100 FP8 GEMM仿真器mmaemu（在捕获的1B投影上比特精确），逐层定位精度敏感点；对比原生结果与fp32累加、以及把精确和按H100实测的1.4%幅度收缩后的结果；在Blackwell平台上仅对单个敏感投影做算术仿真（权重不变）。

Result: 1B的失败依赖集中于21个投影中的第一个：精确求和不通过，按H100的1.4%（而非2.1%）收缩后通过。40B对首个投影敏感：RTX 5070 Ti上原生next-base准确率25.7%（接近随机猜测），仅仿真该投影后BRCA1变异分类AUROC由0.434升至0.869（H100为0.868），RTX PRO 6000上由0.481升至0.867，尽管前向测试仍有小幅未通过。

Conclusion: 模型精度依赖的是特定硬件的参考算术，而非FP8存储格式本身；仅仿真单个敏感投影即可恢复下游性能。建议模型发布时明确指定参考算术，以避免checkpoint跨平台失效。

Abstract: FP8 formats specify how values are stored, not how products are summed; this gap can break checkpoints. Evo 2, a DNA language model, documents a Hopper FP8 requirement for its 1B and 40B checkpoints. On two Blackwell (sm_120) GPUs both fail the developers' forward test (the 7B passes); on an RTX 5070 Ti the 40B's next-base accuracy is 25.7%, near guessing. Greater accuracy breaks them, as BioNeMo saw for the 40B on Hopper: natively, 99.996% of one projection's outputs are correctly rounded (H100: about 30%), and fp32 accumulation of the same operands fails. Our H100 FP8 GEMM emulator, mmaemu (bit-exact on captured 1B projections), localises the dependence to the 1B's first projection (of 21), where exact sums fail but pass when shrunk by the H100's 1.4% (measured in-sample), not 2.1%. The 40B fails with only its first projection native. Emulating only that projection, weights unchanged, raises 40B BRCA1 variant-classification AUROC from 0.434 to 0.869 (H100: 0.868) on the RTX 5070 Ti, despite a narrow forward-test miss, and 0.481 to 0.867 on an RTX PRO 6000. We tested one model family but not training origin; releases should specify reference arithmetic.

</details>


### [322] [LinQuant: Functional Invariances for Low-Precision Linear Recurrences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39418)
*Neehal Tumma,Philipp Nazari,Rohan Cherukuri,Daniela Rus,T. Konstantin Rusch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LinQuant框架：在chunkwise-parallel prefill中利用7种保持全精度计算结果的"不变性"变换（旋转、对角重缩放、衰减与写入门因子的重新放置等），使操作数更利于低精度量化，并在Mamba-2与Nemotron-H上验证了匹配时延下困惑度改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性递推（linear recurrence）正成为softmax注意力的实用替代方案，出现于纯线性与混合架构中，但其低精度推理的成熟度远落后于softmax注意力。Prefill阶段chunkwise-parallel算法将递推大部分转化为稠密矩阵乘，看似是低精度执行的天然路径，然而直接对原生形式量化较为脆弱，精度易退化。

Method: 核心思路是寻找"函数不变性"——在不改变全精度计算语义的前提下改变操作数的表示，从而使量化更友好。作者系统给出七类不变性，包括旋转（rotations）、对角重缩放（diagonal rescalings），以及递推衰减因子（recurrent decay）与写因子（write factors）的替代放置方式；并推导这些变换在跨越linear、gated与delta-rule更新的常见递推族中各自成立的充分条件，说明它们提供互补的自由度以改善低精度精度。工程落地上，将该框架用于增强现有Mamba-2低精度prefill流水线SSDi8，仅接入适用的不变性。

Result: 在Mamba-2 1.3B、2.7B、8B及Nemotron-H 8B上，LinQuant在匹配时延（matched latency）条件下降低了困惑度（perplexity），表明不变性驱动的量化对递推模型具有实际收益。

Conclusion: 低精度递推prefill的关键不在于"直接量化矩阵乘"，而在于先通过保持语义的不变性重塑操作数分布；该不变性框架可迁移至实际流水线（SSDi8），为线性/门控/delta-rule递推族提供了一套通用且可组合的低精度量化设计原则。

Abstract: Linear recurrences are emerging as practical alternatives to softmax attention in large language models, appearing in both standalone and hybrid architectures. However, despite their rapid emergence, low-precision inference remains much less developed for these models than for softmax attention. During prefill, chunkwise-parallel algorithms cast much of the recurrence as dense matrix multiplications, suggesting a natural path to low-precision execution. Simply quantizing these operations in their native form, however, can be fragile. In this work, we identify functional invariances that preserve the full-precision computation while making the operands more amenable to quantization. Building on this observation, we introduce LinQuant, a framework for low-precision recurrent prefill based on seven such invariances, including rotations, diagonal rescalings, and alternative placements of recurrent decay and write factors. We derive when these transformations remain valid across common recurrence families spanning linear, gated, and delta-rule updates, and show that they provide complementary degrees of freedom for improving low-precision accuracy. Finally, we translate this framework to production by augmenting SSDi8, an existing low-precision prefill pipeline for Mamba-2, with the applicable invariances. LinQuant improves perplexity at matched latency on Mamba-2 1.3B, 2.7B, and 8B and Nemotron-H 8B, demonstrating the practical utility of invariance-based quantization for recurrent models.

</details>


### [323] [Softmax Routing Dynamics in Mixture-of-Experts Models: Collapse and Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39442)
*Shengze Xu,Maolin Deng,Tieyong Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究MoE中softmax路由的概率集中现象：刻画其形成机制、持续原因以及被抑制专家的概率恢复动力学，并给出对数律/幂律恢复时间的理论刻画与Transformer-MoE实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax路由容易把概率集中到少数专家上，使其余专家难以获得梯度与样本，形成"抑制"现象；但这一集中如何形成、为何持续、被抑制的概率如何恢复，缺乏统一的理论解释。

Method: 1）固定专家情形下，用stationary-loss barrier识别支撑最优路由输出的面；2）在共同目标平方损失、专家输出可自由训练的模型中，分析慢适应在有限时域上追踪固定专家动力学，并证明任意固定的正常数适应率都给出内点路由极限；3）提出精确的normal-velocity分解，将互补专家协方差与相对梯度优势分离；4）在恢复路径上施加双侧幂次界（对初始质量一致），由系数消失阶数导出对数或幂律恢复时间；5）构造场并数值积分验证阶数；6）给出精确的离散log-odds恒等式，将神经更新与阈值穿越关联；7）在配对Transformer-MoE训练中移除类条件路由器梯度对比项进行消融。

Result: 理论侧：收敛到内点路由极限（而非边界塌缩）；normal-velocity分解清晰区分协方差与梯度优势两类贡献；恢复时间由系数消失阶决定，可为对数或幂律；构造场的积分复现相应阶数；离散log-odds恒等式成立。实验侧：去除类条件路由器梯度对比项后，被抑制类的恢复被延迟，固定token上的累积log-odds降低，且在有/无容量约束下均成立。

Conclusion: 路由集中源于梯度优势与协方差结构的耦合，并在有限适应率下稳定为内点极限；恢复速度可由恢复路径上的幂次界定量预测，类条件路由器梯度对比是加速被抑制专家恢复的关键因素。

Abstract: Softmax routing in mixture-of-experts models can concentrate probability on a subset of experts, suppressing access to the others. We study how this concentration forms, why it persists, and how suppressed probabilities recover. For fixed experts, a stationary-loss barrier identifies the face supporting the optimal routed output. In a common-target square-loss model with freely trainable expert outputs, slow adaptation tracks fixed-expert dynamics on finite horizons, while every fixed positive adaptation rate yields an interior routing limit. An exact normal-velocity decomposition separates complementary-expert covariance from relative gradient advantage. Under positive two-sided power bounds along recovery paths, uniform over initial masses, the resulting coefficient's order of vanishing gives logarithmic or power-law recovery times. Integrations of constructed fields reproduce these orders. An exact discrete log-odds identity relates neural updates to threshold crossing. In paired Transformer-MoE training, removing a class-conditional router-gradient contrast delays confirmed recovery and reduces accumulated log-odds on fixed tokens, both with and without capacity constraints.

</details>


### [324] [End-to-End Guarantees for Masked Diffusion Models under Conditional Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39444)
*Fareed Sheriff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文为掩码扩散模型（MDM）在条件混合（CM）信源下学习到的去噪器给出端到端采样保证，证明经验风险最小化器可达每token超额风险ε'，所需样本量ε'^{-O(τ_c log|V|)}且与序列长度N无关，并证明该指数阶数在常数意义下极小极大最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDM采样界假设去噪器精确或未控制其误差，而训练界仅覆盖训练所用掩码分布；缺乏对学习到的去噪器在解码时端到端误差与样本复杂度的统一刻画。

Method: 引入条件混合（CM）信源类（含地板阶r马尔可夫源与双边Doeblin隐马尔可夫源），其特征为远距离可见token对掩码token后验的平均影响随距离以相关长度τ_c指数衰减；在任意掩码分布下训练经验风险最小化去噪器，并分析其在膨胀式去噪调度下的误差累积。

Result: 在任意掩码分布下，ERM去噪器每token超额风险为ε'，需O(ε'^{-O(τ_c log|V|)})条训练序列且与N无关；指数阶Θ(τ_c log|V|)极小极大最优；端到端KL满足KL(π‖p_alg)≤ε+Nε'，采用T=O(τ_c log(N|V|/ε))步膨胀调度，而固定随机顺序在显式CM源上需Ω(N)步；块自回归采样器跨块链式无附加误差；Λ循环阶r消息传递类在阶r源上具多项式样本复杂度，近似误差逐循环改善直至Λr≈τ_c log(1/ε')饱和；实验支持定性预测。

Conclusion: 论文建立了学习去噪器下MDM的端到端采样与训练联合保证，揭示样本、序列长度与解码步数的可分离关系，并说明更优速率需额外结构；块自回归与循环消息传递可在此基础上获得无额外误差与多项式复杂度，实验验证了理论预测。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models train a denoiser on masked cross-entropy and generate by unmasking many positions in parallel. Existing MDM sampling bounds assume an exact denoiser or leave its error uncontrolled while training bounds cover only the masking distribution on which the model was trained. We present an end-to-end guarantee for a learned denoiser under conditional mixing (CM): stationary sources (e.g. floored order-$r$ Markov and two-sided-Doeblin hidden-Markov sources) for which revealing distant visible tokens moves a masked token's posterior on average exponentially little in the distance with correlation length $τ_c$. We show the empirical risk minimizer trained under any masking distribution attains per-token excess $\varepsilon'$ on that distribution from $\varepsilon'^{-\mathcal{O}(τ_c\log|\mathcal{V}|)}$ training sequences, independent of sequence length $N$ -- one sample budget serves every decoding schedule when each schedule's denoiser is trained under its own weighting. We further show that the exponent's order $Θ(τ_c\log|\mathcal{V}|)$ is minimax-optimal up to constants; more efficient rates require added structure. With this, we bound the end-to-end KL as $\mathrm{KL}(π\Vert p_{\mathrm{alg}})\leq \varepsilon+N\varepsilon'$ under a dilated schedule of $T=\mathcal{O}(τ_c\log(N|\mathcal{V}|/\varepsilon))$ denoising steps versus $Ω(N)$ steps for a fixed random order on an explicit CM source. Finally, we show that block-autoregressive samplers chain across blocks with no added error and that a $Λ$-looped order-$r$ message-passing class on order-$r$ sources achieves polynomial sample complexity, with approximation error improving per loop until saturation at $Λr\approx τ_c\log(1/\varepsilon')$. Experiments on released and retrained models support our results' qualitative predictions.

</details>


### [325] [Smooth Action Chunking Via Movement-Primitive Representations for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39451)
*Shota Nomura,Takayuki Osa,Yasuhiro Kato,Kazuki Ota,Naoki Shitanda,Tatsuya Harada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在action chunking中引入基于运动基元的平滑轨迹表示，并用闭式via-point条件化消除相邻chunk间的不连续，在单次（ACT）与迭代（DiT）两种骨干上把chunk内jerk降低一个数量级/2.4倍、边界不连续减少73–84%，且任务成功率不劣化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: action chunking已成为视觉机器人策略的标准接口（扩散策略到大型VLA模型均采用），但chunk的时序结构只是隐式习得，导致chunk内部与相邻chunk之间轨迹抖动，在真实硬件上表现为可见振动与从动端速度上限的反复饱和。

Method: 将轨迹段表示为平滑基函数（运动基元）的线性组合，使轨迹按构造即平滑，且不改变策略架构、训练目标与推理流程；同时对预测系数施加条件化，使新chunk通过前一个chunk最后一条指令动作处的via-point，从而对第二类抖动给出闭式、推理期修正，无需重训，也不像异步Real-Time Chunking那样局限于异步查询；在单次（ACT）与迭代（DiT）骨干上实例化。

Result: 在仿真与真实双臂ALOHA平台上：单次骨干chunk内jerk降低约一个数量级，迭代骨干降低2.4倍，任务成功率统计上非劣；via-point条件化消除73–84%的边界不连续，每步开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 将运动基元轨迹表示嵌入action chunking，可在不改动现有策略架构与训练流程的前提下，同时显著抑制chunk内抖动与chunk间边界不连续，是一种即插即用的平滑化方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning has become a widely adopted approach for vision-based robot policies, owing to its conceptual simplicity, scalability, and ability to leverage large-scale datasets. As these policies have grown more capable, action chunking has become their standard interface. At each decision step the policy outputs several future actions at once. Backbones from diffusion policies to large vision-language-action models all share it. Whatever backbone produces the chunk, its temporal structure is learned only implicitly. The commanded trajectory is therefore jerky both within a chunk and between successive chunks. On real hardware this appears as visible vibration and as repeated saturation of the follower's velocity limit. We address this issue by incorporating a movement-primitive-based trajectory representation into action chunking. Specifically, a trajectory segment is represented as a linear combination of smooth basis functions, yielding smooth trajectories by construction while leaving the policy architecture, training objective, and inference procedure unchanged. The same representation also yields a closed-form, inference-time correction for the second source of jerk. We condition the predicted coefficients so that the new chunk passes through a via-point at the previous chunk's last commanded action. This correction needs no retraining and, unlike asynchronous Real-Time Chunking, applies to policies queried in one shot. We instantiate it on a one-shot (ACT) and an iterative (DiT) backbone and evaluate in simulation and on a real bimanual ALOHA platform. The representation lowers within-chunk jerk by an order of magnitude on the one-shot backbone and by 2.4 times on the iterative one, with task success statistically non-inferior. The conditioning then removes 73-84% of the boundary discontinuity, at negligible per-step cost.

</details>


### [326] [Sparse Fine-Tuning for Parameter-Efficient Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39455)
*Zhaoxin Wang,Zihang Ding,Handing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从参数空间视角重审参数高效对抗微调：对抗优化高度集中于少数"鲁棒关键参数"(RCPs)，据此提出SAFT，仅更新约5%参数即可超越LoRA类方法并逼近全参数对抗训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗训练(AT)是有效防御，但在大模型上显存开销高，且存在干净-鲁棒精度权衡；将LoRA等参数高效微调引入AT后，相比全参数AT出现明显鲁棒性差距。作者希望从参数空间角度解释并缩小这一差距。

Method: 通过梯度分析发现对抗优化高度集中于一小部分参数（称为Robustness-Critical Parameters, RCPs），表明鲁棒性编码于稀疏子空间；据此提出稀疏对抗微调SAFT：基于对抗梯度显著性识别RCPs，仅更新这些参数并冻结其余参数来执行对抗训练。

Result: 在多种架构与数据集上，SAFT在更少可训练参数下，干净精度与鲁棒精度均优于基于LoRA的方法；仅更新约5%参数时，鲁棒性接近或超过全参数AT。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性可由稀疏参数子空间承载，参数选择（而非低秩结构约束）是参数高效对抗微调的关键；SAFT为降低对抗训练开销同时保持鲁棒性提供了有效且高效的方向。

Abstract: Deep neural networks achieve strong performance across many tasks but remain vulnerable to adversarial perturbations. Adversarial training (AT) is one of the most effective defenses, yet it suffers from high memory cost on large models and the persistent clean-robust accuracy trade-off. Recent work introduces parameter-efficient fine-tuning, such as LoRA, into AT, but this constraint induces a notable robustness gap relative to full-parameter AT. In this work, we revisit parameter-efficient adversarial fine-tuning from a parameter space perspective. Through gradient analysis, we find that adversarial optimization is highly concentrated on a small subset of parameters, which we call Robustness-Critical Parameters (RCPs), suggesting that robustness is encoded in a sparse subspace. Building on this observation, we propose Sparse Adversarial Fine-Tuning (SAFT), which identifies RCPs via adversarial gradient saliency and performs AT by updating only these parameters while freezing the rest. Across architectures and datasets, SAFT outperforms LoRA-based methods in both clean and robust accuracy with fewer trainable parameters, and approaches or surpasses full parameter AT in robustness while updating only about 5\% of parameters.

</details>


### [327] [Achieving Minimax-Optimal First-Order and Best-of-Both-Worlds Regret Simultaneously in Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39457)
*Mengxiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多臂老虎机中的一阶遗憾与"两全其美"(best-of-both-worlds)保证，提出一种高效、无参数算法，消除已有方法中损失相关项的对数间隙，达到极小极大最优一阶遗憾 O(√(K L*))，同时保持随机损失下的对数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pogodin 与 Lattimore (2019) 的算法虽结合了最优最坏情况遗憾与一阶保证，但其损失相关项为 O(√(K L*) log T)，与最优一阶尺度 O(√(K L*)) 之间仍存在对数间隙，本文旨在消除该间隙。

Method: 基于带 Tsallis 熵正则项的 FTRL（Zimmert and Seldin, 2021）；学习率根据学习者的累积损失自适应调整，采样分布由一个标量不动点确定；该构造以学习者损失为度量控制估计成本与稳定性成本之和。

Result: 算法为高效、无参数形式，实现极小极大最优一阶遗憾 O(√(K L*))；对唯一最优臂 i* 的 i.i.d. 损失，同一算法达到 best-of-both-worlds 保证，遗憾为 O(Σ_{i≠i*} log T / Δ_i)，其中 Δ_i 为臂 i 的次优间隙。

Conclusion: 通过 Tsallis 熵正则化 FTRL、损失自适应学习率与标量不动点采样分布，可在无需调参的情况下同时获得最优一阶遗憾与自适应对数遗憾，从而弥合一阶最优性与两全其美保证之间的差距。

Abstract: We study first-order and best-of-both-worlds regret guarantees in multi-armed bandits. Pogodin and Lattimore (2019) proposed an algorithm that combines minimax-optimal worst-case regret with a first-order guarantee whose loss-dependent term is $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{K L^\star}\log T)$, where $T$ denotes the horizon, $L^\star$ denotes the smallest expected cumulative loss among fixed arms, and $K$ denotes the number of arms. However, a logarithmic gap remains between this loss-dependent term and the optimal first-order scale. In this paper, we remove this logarithmic factor by proposing an efficient and parameter-free algorithm that achieves the minimax optimal first-order regret guarantee $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{K L^\star})$. Technically, our algorithm is built upon FTRL with a Tsallis entropy regularizer (Zimmert and Seldin,2021), with a learning rate adapted to the learner's cumulative loss and a sampling distribution determined by a scalar fixed point. This construction controls the combined estimation and stability cost in terms of the learner's loss. Moreover, we show that the same algorithm achieves a best-of-both-worlds guarantee, with $\mathcal{O}(\sum_{i\ne i^\star}\frac{\log T}{Δ_i})$ regret for i.i.d. losses with a unique best arm $i^\star$, where $Δ_i$ denotes the suboptimality gap of arm $i$.

</details>


### [328] [CBE: A Complexity-adaptive Boundary-aware Ensemble Method for Imbalanced Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39466)
*Xun Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出复杂度自适应边界感知集成框架（CBE），将采样权重分布与数据复杂度关联，动态调整欠采样的边界偏好，并在难度不同的不平衡数据集上取得更优的 F-measure 与 G-mean。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类别不平衡会削弱泛化性能；相比单纯的不平衡比，数据复杂度（类别重叠、复杂决策边界）更能客观反映学习难度。边界附近样本难以分类，已成为研究焦点，但多数欠采样方法无法针对不同复杂度的数据集差异化地处理边界样本。

Method: 提出复杂度自适应边界感知集成（CBE）框架：1）构建融合多层次指标的整体复杂度度量；2）依据该度量将不平衡数据集划分为三类（Easy、Median、Hard）；3）使采样权重分布与数据复杂度相挂钩，动态调节欠采样的边界偏好，并结合集成学习。

Result: 在 UCI 与 KEEL 的 24 个基准数据集上开展大量实验，CBE 在 Easy 与 Median 数据集上的 F-measure 与 G-mean 优于多种现有方法，且在困难（Hard）情形下性能不下降。

Conclusion: 以数据复杂度驱动边界采样权重是一种有效且稳健的不平衡分类策略；后续可进一步探讨复杂度度量的通用性与更多层次指标的融合方式。

Abstract: In many real-world classification tasks, datasets exhibit class imbalance, which can degrade the generalization performance of conventional learning algorithms. Data complexity has recently been recognized as a more objective indicator of learning difficulty than the imbalance ratio alone, because class overlap and complex decision boundaries often impair classifiers more severely than imbalance itself. Samples near the decision boundary are inherently difficult to classify, which has motivated many researchers to focus on such instances to improve classifier performance. However, most under-sampling approaches fail to differentiate their sampling of boundary samples across datasets of varying complexity. To overcome this limitation, we propose a complexity-adaptive boundary-aware ensemble (CBE) framework that links the sampling weight distribution to data complexity and dynamically adjusts the boundary preference of under-sampling accordingly. We also introduce an overall complexity measure that combines metrics at multiple levels and use it to divide imbalanced datasets into three categories. Extensive experiments on 24 benchmark datasets from UCI and KEEL show that the proposed method outperforms many existing methods in terms of F-measure and G-mean on Easy and Median datasets, without degrading performance on hard cases.

</details>


### [329] [Topology Survives Shuffling: Robust Long-Context Memory Access Prediction for Graph Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39477)
*Dongyan Sun,Neelesh Gupta,Rajgopal Kannan,Viktor K. Prasanna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoCoMAP——长上下文表征学习框架，解耦观测历史与预测视野，用于图分析访存预测，长上下文显著提升F1并在布局扰动下保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图分析中不规则、依赖拓扑的间接遍历访存流限制了预取；现有ML预测器将短历史与固定视野耦合，只拟合训练轨迹的节点布局而非可迁移的图模式。

Method: LoCoMAP采用长上下文表征学习，历史长度与预测视野独立控制；在CC、PR、SSSP三类工作负载和六类合成图家族上评估，以ER和Grid作随机/确定性对照；设置两种干预：同家族重生成（改变连通性）与节点打乱（破坏布局、保留连通性）。

Result: 18组图-负载组合中，历史从8增至128使F1提升7.8%，视野从8增至512提升88.3%。CC场景重生成扰动小于打乱，免重训时长上下文保留95.4%原F1，并在六家族优于短上下文；完全打乱下CC在ER、BA、Copying Model、SBM上F1提升33.6%（SBM达2.06倍），但WS与Grid无增益，因近零delta质量转入预测器范围外的重尾。真实CC图亦占优。完全打乱下PR长上下文保留57.6%原F1、平均高出短上下文11.0%；两SSSP模型仅保留约30%，因距离更新以新顺序访问顶点数据。

Conclusion: 在这一界限内，长上下文预测器无需重训即可跨相关图与布局保持鲁棒，为可泛化的图预取器提供了依据。

Abstract: Graph analytics generates irregular, topology-dependent memory-access streams via indirect traversals, limiting data prefetching. Accurate prediction enables timely prefetches that hide memory latency. However, ML predictors couple short histories with fixed horizons, fitting the training trace's node layout rather than learning transferable graph patterns. We propose LoCoMAP, a long-context representation-learning framework for memory-access prediction with independently controlled observation history and prediction horizon. We evaluate Connected Components (CC), PageRank (PR), and Single-Source Shortest Paths (SSSP) on six synthetic graph families, with Erdos-Renyi (ER) and Grid as random and deterministic controls. We test two interventions: same-family regeneration changes connectivity, while node shuffling disrupts layout but preserves connectivity. Across 18 graph-workload pairs, extending history from 8 to 128 accesses improves F1 by 7.8%, while extending prediction horizon from 8 to 512 improves F1 by 88.3%. For CC, regeneration is less disruptive than shuffling: without retraining, long context retains 95.4% of original F1 and outperforms short context in six families. Under full shuffle, long context improves CC F1 by 33.6% across ER, Barabasi-Albert, Copying Model, and Stochastic Block Model (SBM), with a 2.06x gain on SBM, but not on Watts-Strogatz or Grid, where near-zero delta mass shifts into heavy tails beyond the predictor's range. Long context outperforms short context on six real-world CC graphs. At full shuffle, long-context PR retains 57.6% of original F1 and averages 11.0% above short context. Both SSSP models retain about 30% of original F1 under full shuffle as distance updates access vertex data in a new order. Within these bounds, long-context predictors are robust across related graphs and layouts without retraining, enabling a generalizable graph prefetcher.

</details>


### [330] [Interpretable by Design Tabular Prediction via In-Context Local Linear Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39506)
*Ran Eisenberg,Steven Berger,Ofir Lindenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TICL$^3$：用表格基础模型引导"共享线性模型+邻域残差线性模型"，实现设计即可解释的预测器，在386组分类评测中仅落后TabICLv2约1.1个百分点，并优于CatBoost与MAPLE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在上下文学习（ICL）下预测精度高，但单个特征如何影响预测难以解释；生物学等场景需要特征贡献以识别生物标志物或风险基因。事后可解释方法给出的重要性分数并不代表模型真正使用的依据，易产生解释错误。

Method: TICL$^3$（Tabular In-Context Local Linear Learning）采用"设计即解释"思路：先学习一个共享线性模型刻画数据集层面的特征效应；再对每个查询，利用基础模型表示检索相似训练样本，在邻域内对残差拟合第二个线性模型；将两者系数相加得到查询特定的线性评分及每个特征的贡献。

Result: 在386组分类基准—数据集评测中，TICL$^3$精度较TabICLv2低1.1个百分点；在266组评测中优于CatBoost；平均support F1较MAPLE提升4.7个点；在两个真实数据"选定特征效用"指标上均取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: 通过让同一模型既产生预测又给出忠实于模型的线性特征贡献，TICL$^3$在保持接近强基线精度的同时显著提升解释效用，验证了"设计即可解释"在表格基础模型上的可行性。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) lets tabular foundation models make predictions without dataset-specific training or fine-tuning. These models achieve strong predictive accuracy, but interpreting how individual features contribute to the prediction remains difficult. In biology, for example, feature contributions can help identify biomarkers or risk genes. Post-hoc interpretability methods assign feature importance scores, but these scores do not indicate what the model actually uses and can lead to explanation errors. We introduce TICL$^3$ (Tabular In-Context Local Linear Learning), an interpretable-by-design predictor that uses a tabular foundation model to guide a simple linear prediction rule. TICL$^3$ first learns a shared linear model to capture dataset-level feature effects. Then, for each query, it retrieves similar training samples using representations from the foundation model and fits a second linear model to the residuals in that neighborhood. The shared model captures broad effects, while the local model corrects errors among similar samples. Their combined coefficients determine each feature contribution to the final query-specific linear score, so the same model makes and explains the prediction. Across 386 classification benchmark-dataset evaluations, TICL$^3$ trails TabICLv2 by 1.1 percentage points and outperforms CatBoost in 266 evaluations. It improves average support F1 over MAPLE by 4.7 points and achieves the best performance on both real-data selected-feature utility metrics.

</details>


### [331] [Last-Iterate Convergence for Constrained MDPs: From Dual Descent to Primal-Dual](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39524)
*Michael Lu,Max Qiushi Lin,Mo Chen,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无限时域折扣约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的策略优化：先给出无需覆盖假设的原始对偶（DD，即对偶下降）分析，再用熵正则化自然策略梯度与对偶正则化消除内层循环，得到高效 PD 算法，并给出改进的 last-iterate 收敛保证，且推广到线性函数逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PD（primal-dual）方法理论保证只对不实用的混合策略成立；DD（dual descent）方法虽能对单一"last iterate"策略给出保证，但需在内层循环中反复求解熵正则化 MDP，实用性差，且已有分析依赖实践中难以满足的强覆盖（coverage）假设。因此需要一种既高效又具 last-iterate 保证、且假设更弱的方法。

Method: 1) 重新分析 DD 方法，去除覆盖假设；2) 结合熵正则化自然策略梯度与对偶正则化，消去 DD 的内层循环，构造高效 PD 算法；3) 提出新的 tracking 分析，用于刻画该算法的 last-iterate 收敛性；4) 在同一理论框架下分析介于 DD 与 PD 之间的截断双层（truncated bilevel）方法；5) 将分析推广到线性函数逼近。

Result: 相比现有方法，所提算法获得更优的 last-iterate 收敛保证；理论框架可统一刻画截断双层方法作为 DD 与 PD 的插值；方法可推广至线性函数逼近；相较于先前的 last-iterate 方法，在更弱的假设下证明了更强的保证。

Conclusion: 该工作通过去覆盖假设的 DD 分析与对偶正则化的高效 PD 设计，兼顾了实用性与单一策略（last-iterate）的收敛保证，并提供可统一分析 DD、PD 及其插值方法的框架，同时扩展至线性函数逼近。

Abstract: We study policy optimization for infinite-horizon, discounted constrained Markov decision processes. While primal-dual ($\texttt{PD}$) methods are widely used in practice, their theoretical guarantees typically hold for an impractical mixture policy. In contrast, dual descent ($\texttt{DD}$) methods output a single policy ("last iterate") with guarantees on its performance. However, such methods require iteratively solving entropy-regularized MDPs in an inner-loop, limiting their practical effectiveness. Moreover, existing analyses rely on strong coverage assumptions that are difficult to satisfy in practice. Consequently, we first provide a new analysis of $\texttt{DD}$ methods that does not require coverage assumptions. We then employ entropy-regularized natural policy gradient and dual regularization to eliminate the inner loop, resulting in an efficient $\texttt{PD}$ algorithm. We develop a novel tracking analysis which shows that, compared to existing methods, the proposed algorithm achieves improved last-iterate convergence guarantees. Furthermore, our theoretical framework allows us to analyze a truncated bilevel method that interpolates between $\texttt{DD}$ and $\texttt{PD}$ methods. Finally, we generalize our analysis to handle linear function approximation. Compared to prior last-iterate methods, we prove stronger guarantees under less restrictive assumptions.

</details>


### [332] [Explainable and Adaptive Machine Learning for Fraud Risk Detection in Bangladesh's Digital Banking Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39556)
*Md Najmus Swaqeeb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个可解释、自适应的机器学习框架（按时间序评估、防泄漏特征工程、概率校准、SHAP解释、自适应风险排序），在合成PaySim数据集上比较四种模型，XGBoost固定阈值表现最佳，Platt校准显著降低Brier分数，自适应top-1%策略以召回下降换取误报减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字银行与移动金融快速扩张，欺诈检测面临严重类别不平衡与运营约束（告警容量有限）的挑战。

Method: 集成时间序评估、防数据泄漏的特征工程、概率校准（Platt）、SHAP可解释性分析及自适应风险排序；使用合成PaySim移动支付数据集，对比Logistic Regression、Random Forest、XGBoost与LightGBM。

Result: XGBoost固定阈值表现最优（PR-AUC 0.5658、ROC-AUC 0.9068、F1 0.5369）；Platt校准将Brier分数从0.1141降至0.0245；SHAP显示时间、收款账户、交易类型与金额为关键驱动特征；自适应top-1%策略将误报从192降至117，但召回从0.4236降至0.2122。

Conclusion: 研究提供可复现的方法学框架，而非特定地区（孟加拉国）的部署性能证据，因PaySim为合成数据；未来需用受治理的真实金融数据验证。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of digital banking and mobile financial services creates growing challenges for fraud detection, particularly under severe class imbalance and operational constraints. This study presents an explainable and adaptive machine-learning framework integrating chronological evaluation, leakage-conscious feature engineering, probability calibration, SHAP-based explainability, and adaptive risk prioritization. Experiments use the synthetic PaySim mobile-money benchmark and compare Logistic Regression, Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM. XGBoost achieved the strongest fixed-threshold performance, with PR-AUC of 0.5658, ROC-AUC of 0.9068, and F1-score of 0.5369. Platt calibration reduced the test Brier score from 0.1141 to 0.0245, improving probability reliability. SHAP analysis identified temporal, destination-account, transaction-type, and amount features as important model drivers. An adaptive top-1% policy reduced false positives from 192 to 117, but recall declined from 0.4236 to 0.2122, demonstrating a clear alert-capacity trade-off. The results provide a reproducible methodological framework rather than evidence of deployment performance in Bangladesh, since PaySim is synthetic. Validation using governed real-world financial data is therefore an important direction for future research.

</details>


### [333] [LatentWeave: Test-Time Latent Optimization for Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39557)
*Fanci Meng,Kun Wang,Guibin Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Jiaming Zhang,Ming Li,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LatentWeave，一种在测试时把多模态推理视为可控潜轨迹的框架，通过分段精炼隐藏状态并用WEAVE token作为推理锚点，在不修改主干参数前提下抑制自回归生成中的误差累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM测试时优化方法多作用于提示或输出层面，潜空间推理过程缺乏控制，难以阻止自回归生成中的误差累积。

Method: LatentWeave包含两部分：1）Structured Latent Weaving，按段逐步精炼中间隐藏状态以纠正出现的偏差；2）Reasoning Anchors，引入轻量WEAVE token稳定推理结构并引导面向答案的生成。二者实现细粒度、步骤感知的潜过程干预，同时冻结主干参数。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准上相比所评估基线取得提升；消融与效率研究分别分析了潜优化、锚点训练及额外测试时计算各自的贡献。

Conclusion: 将推理控制从提示/输出层下沉到潜空间轨迹层面是有效的，且无需改动主干即可获得性能提升；代码已开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in test-time optimization have shown that the multimodal reasoning performance of large multimodal language models (MLLMs) can be improved during inference without modifying backbone model parameters. However, existing approaches primarily operate at the prompt or output level, leaving the latent reasoning process largely uncontrolled and insufficient to prevent error accumulation during autoregressive generation. To address this limitation, we propose LatentWeave, a test-time framework that treats multimodal reasoning as a controllable latent trajectory. LatentWeave performs structured Latent Weaving, progressively refining intermediate hidden states segment-wise to correct emerging deviations, and further introduces Reasoning Anchors via lightweight WEAVE tokens to stabilize reasoning structure and guide answer-oriented generation. These mechanisms are designed to enable fine-grained, step-aware intervention in the latent reasoning process while keeping the main MLLM backbone parameters fixed. Extensive experiments across diverse multimodal reasoning benchmarks report improvements over the evaluated baselines; the contribution of latent optimization, anchor training, and additional test-time computation is analyzed separately in the ablation and efficiency studies. Our code is available at https://github.com/jins7/LatentWeave.

</details>


### [334] [Improving the Intrinsic Interpretability of Differentiable Decision Trees through Euclidean Feature-Concentration Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39596)
*Qiqi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出欧氏集中可微决策树（EU-DDT），通过在内部节点对相对绝对特征权重施加基于归一化平方欧氏距离的正则，诱导少数特征主导并保留软路由；分类任务上大幅降低有效特征数，并用于PPO策略参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微决策树支持端到端优化并提供显式决策路径，但稠密斜向分裂使单个输入特征的作用难以解释；因此希望在不牺牲可微性与概率软路由的前提下，促使节点层面由少数特征主导。

Method: EU-DDT在每个内部节点对相对绝对特征权重施加正则项：以权重向量与均匀分布之间的归一化平方欧氏距离作为惩罚，推动权重集中；同时保留概率软路由以维持可微性。实验在Wine与Digits上以5个匹配随机种子对比，并将EU-DDT作为PPO actor（配合MLP critic）在CartPole-v1与MiniPacman上测试，每个环境2个种子，以节点集中度与最终评估成功率衡量。

Result: 分类任务中平均有效特征数由8.94降至1.54（Wine）、由45.28降至9.06（Digits）；平均测试准确率由100.0%变为93.9%（Wine）、由92.3%升至94.6%（Digits），体现数据集相关的集中度—预测性能权衡。强化学习中集中度一致提高且任务性能得以保持：CartPole-v1与MiniPacman代表性运行最终评估成功率均达100%，平均节点集中度分别由0.1272升至0.7179、由0.0437升至0.7237。

Conclusion: EU正则化可作为降低节点级特征复杂度的有效机制；但强化学习部分仅基于2个随机种子，证据仍属初步，需更多种子与环境的验证。

Abstract: Differentiable decision trees (DDTs) support end-to-end optimisation and explicit decision paths, but dense oblique splits can obscure the role of individual input features. We propose the Euclidean-Concentrated Differentiable Decision Tree (EU-DDT), which regularises the relative absolute feature weights at each internal node using their normalised squared Euclidean distance from the uniform distribution. This encourages dominance by a small number of features while retaining probabilistic soft routing. Across five matched random seeds, EU-DDT reduces the mean effective number of features from 8.94 to 1.54 on Wine and from 45.28 to 9.06 on Digits. Mean test accuracy changes from 100.0% to 93.9% on Wine and from 92.3% to 94.6% on Digits, illustrating the dataset-dependent trade-off between concentration and predictive performance. We also use EU-DDT to parameterise a PPO actor with an MLP critic. Two random-seed runs per environment show a consistent qualitative increase in concentration while preserving effective task performance. In representative CartPole-v1 and MiniPacman runs, EU-DDT reaches 100% final evaluation success, while mean node concentration increases from 0.1272 to 0.7179 and from 0.0437 to 0.7237, respectively. These results support EU regularisation as a mechanism for reducing node-level feature complexity; the reinforcement-learning evidence remains preliminary because only two seeds are evaluated.

</details>


### [335] [A New AFIB Detection Method with Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39665)
*Sara Gjorgjieva,Ana Angjelevska,Dimitar Trajanov,Marjan Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种稳健的房颤(AFIB)检测方法：以相邻心率差值时间序列(dBPM)作为新特征替代传统心搏间期，并采用新的标注方法仅用五分类中的"干净"间期训练、其余视为离群点；最佳模型DNN在独立基准集上二分类F1达95.18%，优于已有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 房颤心律不齐的早期检测每年潜在可挽救大量生命，现有众多算法各有局限；作者希望提升AFIB检测的精度与稳健性。

Method: 在MITDB与LTAFDB两个PhysioNet数据库上，同时评估多种传统机器学习算法与神经网络；提出新的特征工程——使用相邻心率差值时间序列(dBPM)而非传统心搏间期；输入标注为五类，通过新的标注方法仅选取干净间期参与训练，其余作为离群点处理。

Result: 最佳模型为深度神经网络(DNN)，在与训练集不同的基准数据集上二分类房颤的F1分数为95.18%。

Conclusion: 所提方法训练的模型性能优于此前发表的模型，验证了dBPM特征与干净间期筛选策略对提升AFIB检测精度和稳健性的有效性。

Abstract: Early detection of atrial fibrillation (AFIB), characterized by irregular heart rhythms, can potentially save thousands of lives annually [20]. Numerous studies have attempted to address this challenge using various algorithms and methodologies. This paper introduces a novel and robust method that enhances the precision of AFIB detection. Neural Networks (NN) are widely recognized for their ability to identify complex electrocardiogram (ECG) patterns, facilitating the accurate detection of irregular heart rhythms. This study explores multiple conventional machine learning algorithms alongside neural network approaches to develop a robust AFIB detection system. To evaluate the effective- ness and robustness of the proposed method, the MIT BIH arrhythmia database (MITDB) and the Long-term Atrial Fibrillation Database (LTAFDB), both available on the Physionet site. Our approach presents a novel feature engineering technique, employing specific features such as a time series of the differences in successive heart rates (dBPM) rather than traditional inter-beat intervals. The model analyzes inputs labeled into five distinct classes, using only clean intervals identified through a new labeling method for training while treating others as outliers. This strategy ensures a more accurate and reliable AFIB detection process. The best-performing model, a Deep Neural Network (DNN), achieved an F1 score of 95.18% for binary atrial fibrillation classification on a benchmark dataset distinct from the training set. Models trained using our approach have demonstrated superior performance than previously published models.

</details>


### [336] [Event-SAMA: Language-Grounded Video Object Segmentation on Neuromorphic Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39676)
*Sergei Fedorchenko,Max Zehnder,Nikola Zubić,Rong Zou,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文首次将事件相机与指代视频目标分割(RVOS)结合：构建事件流语料Event-SAMA-239K，并提出事件原生基线Event-SAMA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具备微秒级时间分辨率、高动态范围与无运动模糊等优势，但RVOS领域缺乏事件流数据集与模型，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 将SAMA-239K grounded video-chat语料及全部SAMA训练数据（30,650段视频、392,162张图像）通过光流、FILM或saccade上采样接线性事件生成器转换为合成事件流，保留mask、指代表达与对话标注；模型上改进SAMA-4B，采用以预训练RGB权重初始化的双通道极性patch embedding与零初始化事件适配器，进行端到端训练。

Result: MeViS valid_u上24.37 J&F，转换后ReVOS valid子集22.33 J&F（RGB SAMA-4B在MeViS为55.4）；冻结RGB模型配伪RGB渲染或E2VID重建仅13.23与15.94。不重训练即可在真实事件录制上工作（需积累窗口匹配事件密度）。噪声底0.47 J&F的消融显示：多源训练数据(+4.99)、RGB均值事件stem初始化(+4.33)、可训练SAM2 mask decoder(+2.76)有效，事件适配器、部分ViT解冻与极性在噪声内。

Conclusion: 建立了首个事件原生RVOS基线；较旧的部分转换语料快照分数反而更高(+1.38/+1.12)，但因单次运行且训练调度不同，无法就语料规模得出结论。

Abstract: Event cameras offer microsecond temporal resolution, high dynamic range and no motion blur, but referring video object segmentation (RVOS) lacks event-stream datasets and models. We introduce Event-SAMA-239K, a synthetic-event conversion of the SAMA-239K grounded video-chat corpus and all additional SAMA training data: 30,650 video clips and 392,162 images. Optical-flow, FILM or saccade upsampling followed by a linear event generator preserves all mask, referring-expression and dialogue annotations. We also introduce Event-SAMA, adapting SAMA-4B with a two-channel polarity patch embedding initialised from pretrained RGB weights and a zero-initialised event adapter. End-to-end training yields 24.37 $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$ on MeViS valid_u and 22.33 $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$ on the converted ReVOS valid subset, establishing the first event-native RVOS baseline (RGB SAMA-4B: 55.4 on MeViS). Frozen RGB SAMA-4B with pseudo-RGB renderings or E2VID reconstructions of the same events scores 13.23 and 15.94 $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$. Without retraining, Event-SAMA works on real event-camera recordings when the accumulation window matches their event density. A seed-controlled ablation with a measured noise floor of 0.47 $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$ finds gains from diverse training sources over MeViS alone (+4.99), RGB-mean event-stem initialisation (+4.33), and a trainable SAM2 mask decoder (+2.76); the event adapter, partial ViT unfreezing and event polarity are within noise at this budget. An earlier, partially converted corpus snapshot trained with the identical recipe scores 1.38 $\mathcal{J}\&\mathcal{F}$ higher on MeViS and 1.12 higher on ReVOS. Both are single runs that also differ in schedule length, so we draw no conclusion about corpus scale.

</details>


### [337] [Heterogeneous Co-Evolution Learning for Tool-Efficient Search Agents with Zero Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39682)
