*Qin-Wen Luo,Sheng Ren,Xiang Chen,Rui Liu,Jun Fang,Naiqiang Tan,Jun Wang,Hua Chai,Sheng-Jun Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出零标注、工具高效的搜索智能体训练框架 ZEST-Search，通过异质协同进化在线构建验证搜索任务，并用中间难度奖励与工具感知奖励联合优化任务难度与搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体训练面临两大挑战：一是固定或带标注的任务集无法随智能体能力提升而自适应，能力前沿受训练问题制约；二是仅基于结果的强化学习将所有成功轨迹等同对待，忽视冗余搜索，导致答案准确性与工具效率之间存在张力。

Method: 从无标注参考问题出发，采用"答案优先、证据路径引导"的生成流程在线构建经验证的搜索任务；异质提议器围绕求解器不断演进的能力边界自适应生成任务，求解器通过强化学习学习搜索增强推理；对提议器使用中间难度奖励，对求解器使用任务局部工具感知奖励。

Result: 在七个通用领域与多跳问答基准上，无需标注搜索训练数据或专家搜索轨迹，ZEST-Search 持续提升搜索智能体性能，同时获得更高的工具效率；消融分析表明异质生成、自适应难度控制与工具感知优化均有效。

Conclusion: 零标注、工具高效的异质协同进化范式可有效突破固定任务集与结果型奖励的局限，在准确性与搜索效率之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Deep search agents extend language models by interleaving reasoning with external retrieval. However, effective search-agent training requires addressing two challenges beyond final-answer optimization. First, the capability frontier of agents is largely determined by training questions, whereas fixed or labeled task collections cannot adapt as agents improve. Second, outcome-only reinforcement learning treats all successful trajectories equally, even when some correct answers rely on redundant searches, creating an inherent tension between answer accuracy and tool efficiency. To address these issues, we introduce \textsc{ZEST-Search}, a zero-label and tool-efficient framework for search-agent training via heterogeneous co-evolution. Starting from unlabeled reference questions, \textsc{ZEST-Search} constructs validated search tasks online through an answer-first and evidence-path-guided generation process. A heterogeneous proposer adaptively constructs training tasks around the solver's evolving capability boundary, while the solver learns search-augmented reasoning through reinforcement learning. To jointly optimize task difficulty and search efficiency, \textsc{ZEST-Search} employs an intermediate-difficulty reward for the proposer and a task-local tool-aware reward for the solver, encouraging informative tasks and correct answers with fewer redundant searches. Experiments on seven general-domain and multi-hop question answering benchmarks demonstrate that \textsc{ZEST-Search} consistently improves search-agent performance while achieving higher tool efficiency without labeled search-training data or expert search trajectories. Further analyses show that heterogeneous generation, adaptive difficulty control, and tool-aware optimization enable more effective search-agent training.

</details>


### [338] [Smartphone Indoor Localization Enhanced by Hierarchical Multi-Sensor Context Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39691)
*Wenhui Feng,Muhan Weng,Jianyu Liu,Xiansheng Yang,Hao Zhang,Liang Wang,Yuan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出层次化多传感器上下文识别增强的智能手机室内定位框架，将环境/场景/活动概率转为场景事件段与动作上下文段，分别驱动场景约束PDR校正与动作感知RoNIN位置补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机室内定位需低成本、跨场景切换与持机方式变化下仍鲁棒的定位；PDR虽无需基础设施，却受航向漂移、结构转换与持机模式变化导致的误差累积。已有方法把上下文线索当作单一事件流或局部校正信号，难以针对不同类型的定位误差分别纠偏。

Method: 构建数据与模型联合驱动的层次化识别模型，预测环境、场景、活动概率，并转换为场景事件段与动作上下文段。场景事件段通过走廊航向约束、地标吸引、固定滞后回溯、垂直转换处理引导场景约束的PDR校正；动作上下文段驱动基于RoNIN的动作感知位置补偿，抑制持机模式相关漂移。

Result: 在跨楼层与多动作轨迹上以LiDAR-SLAM为参考进行实验，该框架优于PDR与代表性学习式惯性里程计基线，表明层次化语义可提供可靠且面向定位的校正线索。

Conclusion: 层次化多传感器上下文语义能有效区分并对应不同误差源，为智能手机室内行人定位提供低成本、可部署且鲁棒的校正机制。

Abstract: Smartphone indoor localization requires low-cost, robust positioning across scene transitions and phone-use variations. Pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR) enables infrastructure-free deployment but accumulates errors from heading drift, structural transitions, and carrying-mode changes. Existing methods often treat contextual cues as a single event stream or local correction signal, limiting their ability to address distinct localization errors. This paper presents a framework enhanced by hierarchical multi-sensor context recognition. A jointly data- and model-driven recognition model predicts environment, scene, and activity probabilities, which are converted into scene-event and action-context segments. Scene-event segments guide scene-constrained PDR correction through corridor heading constraints, landmark attraction, fixed-lag backtracking, and vertical-transition handling. Action-context segments drive action-aware RoNIN-based position compensation for carrying-mode-dependent drift. Experiments on cross-floor and multi-action trajectories against LiDAR-SLAM references show that the proposed framework outperforms PDR and representative learned inertial odometry baselines, demonstrating that hierarchical semantics provide reliable, localization-specific correction cues.

</details>


### [339] [Implicit Bias of (Stochastic) Gradient Descent on Deep Linear Diagonal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39717)
*Wiebke Bartolomaeus,Holger Rauhut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将（随机）梯度下降识别为受扰动的（随机）镜像梯度下降，从而在任意层数的深层线性对角网络上，将 GD/SGD 的收敛极限刻画为加权 ℓ1 极小化问题，并给出步长的充分条件及其对隐式偏置的定量影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化模型损失存在多个极小值，训练算法与超参数选择会显著影响所求解，即隐式偏置现象。已有工作仅在小初始化下证明了深层线性对角网络梯度流的（近）稀疏收敛，缺乏对更贴近实际的（随机）梯度下降迭代的分析，且不清楚步长等超参如何影响隐式偏置。

Method: 针对任意层数的深层线性对角网络，将（随机）梯度下降重写为受扰动的（随机）镜像梯度下降（mirror gradient descent）形式，据此建立收敛性分析并刻画极限解，将其与加权 ℓ1 极小化相联系；同时给出步长的充分条件。

Result: 证明了（随机）梯度下降的收敛性，将极限解刻画为加权 ℓ1 极小化问题的解；给出了保证结论成立的步长充分条件，并定量刻画了步长大小对隐式偏置（即极限解稀疏性/权重结构）的影响。

Conclusion: 深层线性对角网络中梯度下降的隐式偏置可从镜像下降+扰动视角统一理解：小初始化下的稀疏性结论可推广至 GD 与 SGD 迭代，且步长是可调节隐式偏置的显式超参数。

Abstract: Modern machine learning models are often overparameterized, i.e., have more parameters than training data. This typically implies that the loss function admits multiple minimizers so that training methods (and their hyperparameter choices) have significant influence on the computed solution. We study this so-called implicit bias phenomenon in the context of training deep linear diagonal networks. Previous work has shown that the gradient flow for learning such networks converges to (almost) sparse solutions when initialized small. For an arbitrary number of layers, we extend this analysis to the practically more realistic (stochastic) gradient descent iterations. Our approach identifies (stochastic) gradient descent as a perturbed (stochastic) mirror gradient descent. This allows us to establish convergence and characterize the limiting solution, relating it to weighted $\ell_1$-minimization. Moreover, we provide sufficient conditions on the step sizes and quantify the influence of the step sizes on the implicit bias.

</details>


### [340] [Grounding Predictive Reliability through Masked Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39728)
*Shang Gao,Bernhard Sick,Franz Götz-Hahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MORSE，在掩码表示学习中通过"跨上下文归一化变异"为预测均值不确定性提供可观测语义，等价于NIG中未被边缘似然识别的1/ν因子，从而把不确定性拆分为可验证的"可观测性方差"与数据尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIG证据回归虽将预测变异分解为数据尺度与预测均值两部分，但边缘似然无法识别二者的分配，导致不确定性语义模糊、无法与可观测预测行为对应。

Method: MORSE（Masked Observability for Reliable Structured Encoding）：在可见上下文受控变化下预测同一隐藏目标，用归一化跨上下文变异来锚定预测均值不确定性（充当NIG中未识别的1/ν因子），并用高斯目标学习条件均值与数据尺度；由此定义"策略相对可观测性方差"，度量对可见证据的依赖，区别于训练集重采样变异；观测性估计被摊销为每个观测状态一次前向。

Result: 在基于解析贝叶斯条件均值构建上下文参考的高斯过程基准上，MORSE在全部9种设置中对数方差误差最低，优于证据、集成与dropout估计器；最接近训练集变异语义的估计器在所有设置中扰动模型而非上下文，两种参考在经验上可区分；异方差脉冲基准上，其跨上下文方差随数据增加趋近大样本参考；PTB-XL低标签迁移与IMPTC分布内轨迹预测均有提升。

Conclusion: 跨上下文归一化变异为NIG的1/ν提供了可观测的经验锚点，使不确定性语义可被行为验证，并给出计算高效（单次前向）的不确定性估计方法。

Abstract: An uncertainty estimate is meaningful only if its semantics can be tied to observable predictive behavior. Normal-Inverse-Gamma (NIG) evidential regression provides a structured decomposition of predictive variability into data-scale and predictive-mean components, but marginal likelihood does not identify their allocation. We address this semantic ambiguity in masked representation learning. MORSE (Masked Observability for Reliable Structured Encoding) predicts the same hidden target under controlled changes in visible context and uses normalized cross-context variation to ground predictive-mean uncertainty, while a Gaussian objective learns the conditional mean and data scale. The normalized variation plays the role of the NIG factor $1/ν$, which the marginal leaves unidentified; this yields a policy-relative observability variance that measures dependence on visible evidence, distinct from variation induced by resampling the training set. On a Gaussian-process benchmark with a contextual reference built from analytic Bayes conditional means, MORSE has the lowest log-variance error among compared evidential, ensemble, and dropout estimators in all nine tested settings, while the estimator closest to training-set variation perturbs the model rather than its context in every setting, so the two references separate the estimators empirically. MORSE amortizes observability estimation into one forward pass per observation state. On a heteroscedastic pulse benchmark, its predictor's cross-context variance approaches a large-sample reference as training data increase. MORSE also improves low-label transfer on PTB-XL and in-distribution trajectory prediction on IMPTC.

</details>


### [341] [Going deep, reaching further: What can Boltzmann machines efficiently represent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39731)
*Tobias Reinhart,Gemma De les Coves*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出通用逼近定理忽视表示效率与深度敏感性，且二者本质相关：更深或更高阶交互的模型具有更大的"有效可达集"，从而可高效表示更多函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用逼近定理虽保证表示能力，但不关注高效表示，且对深度等启发式重要性质不敏感；作者希望说明这两个缺陷是相互关联的，并为深度/高阶交互的经验优势提供理论基础。

Method: 引入"有效可达集"（efficient reach）概念并在自旋模型框架下分析：(i) 借助近期对通用自旋模型的刻画给出最大有效可达性的完整特征；(ii) 利用自旋模型的计算复杂性界导出深/浅模型在三种表示任务上的分离；(iii) 应用通信复杂性界证明浅模型的有效可达性随交互阶数构成无限层级。

Result: 深度玻尔兹曼机（DBM）具有最大有效可达性，受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）次最大，k-体 RBM 推广位于两者之间并构成层级；进一步得到深/浅模型在三个表示任务上的分离，以及浅模型按交互阶数排列的无限层级。

Conclusion: 通用机器学习模型在高效表示能力上可存在显著差异，深度与高阶交互带来实际优势；该工作为相关经验观察提供了理论依据。

Abstract: While universal approximation theorems theoretically underpin the representation capabilities of machine learning models, they have two shortcomings: They do not focus on efficient representations and they tend to be insensitive to depth and other heuristically important properties. In this work we show that these two shortcomings are intertwined. We prove that machine learning models which are deeper or have higher-order interactions can efficiently represent a larger set of functions, i.e. have larger efficient reach. It follows that deep Boltzmann machines (DBMs) have maximal efficient reach, restricted Boltzmann machines (RBMs) sub-maximal, and k-body generalizations of RBMs form a hierarchy between the two, even though all are universal. More generally, (i) we provide a full characterization of machine learning models with maximal efficient reach; (ii) we derive separations between deep and shallow models for three representation tasks; and (iii) we prove that the efficient reach of shallow models constitutes an infinite hierarchy indexed by the interaction order. The proofs draw on different methodologies, namely on (i) a recent characterization of universal spin models; (ii) computational complexity bounds for spin models; and (iii) communication complexity bounds applied to shallow models. This work shows that universal machine learning models can strongly differ in their efficient representation capabilities, and provides a theoretical foundation for heuristic observations.

</details>


### [342] [Activation Probes Are Reliable With a Handful of Positive Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39766)
*Riya Tyagi,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在"少数正例 vs 大量负例"的极度不平衡场景下，研究激活探针（activation probes）检测罕见失调行为的数据效率问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监测先进AI的罕见失调（misalignment）面临数据挑战：对齐样本充足，失调样本极少（仅1-10个正例，而负例上千）。因此需要弄清在这种"few vs. thousands"体系下，如何高效训练探针。

Method: 在垃圾邮件检测与诚实性检测任务上测试激活探针，比较"利用大量负例训练"与"平衡训练"在正例极少时的样本效率；并考察LLM上采样（upsampling）增广正例的效果，以及模型规模对探针正例样本效率的影响。

Result: ① 正例仅1-10个时，用大量负例训练平均比平衡训练更具正例样本效率；② LLM上采样可带来约等于"真实正例翻倍"的性能提升，但过度上采样反而损害性能；③ 呈现正向缩放趋势——模型越大，探针的正例样本效率越高。

Conclusion: 应利用可大量获取的负例样本来放大罕见但关键失调样本的信号，从而在正例稀缺条件下提升探针检测效果。

Abstract: Efforts to monitor advanced AI for rare misalignments face a data challenge: abundant aligned examples but only a handful of misaligned ones. We test activation probes in this "few vs. thousands" regime on spam and honesty detection tasks. For our tasks, training with many negative examples is on average more positive-sample-efficient than balanced training for small numbers (1-10) of positive samples. We also find that LLM upsampling can provide a performance boost equivalent to roughly doubling the number of real positive samples, though excessive upsampling hurts performance. Finally, we show a positive scaling trend, where larger models are more positive-sample-efficient to probe. Our findings suggest we should leverage the large number of negative samples available to amplify the signal from rare but critical misalignment examples.

</details>


### [343] [Multi-output LSTM soft sensors for industrial anaerobic co-digestion using LSTM-beta-VAE generative data augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39768)
*Rohit Murali,Benaissa Dekhici,Tao Chen,Dongda Zhang,Michael Short*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出LSTM-β-VAE生成式数据增强框架结合多输出LSTM软测量模型，解决工业厌氧消化数据稀疏、偏态分布导致的稳定性指标预测难题，实现对pH、VFA/碱度比和沼气产量的同步稳健估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厌氧消化（AD）需实时监测pH、挥发性有机酸与碱缓冲容量比、沼气产量等关键变量以维持生物稳定性并防止过程失效。ADM1等机理模型参数繁多、计算昂贵、难以实时标定；而现有数据驱动方法多为单输出架构，仅关注产量预测，忽视稳定性指标的同步预测。此外，工业AD数据集稀疏且集中于稳态工况，基线评估表明随机森林、XGBoost及未增强的LSTM均无法识别向不稳定工况的转变，导致稳定性指标估计性能差。

Method: 构建基于LSTM-beta变分自编码器（LSTM-β-VAE）的生成式数据增强框架，从一年全尺度工厂数据中合成物理上合理的"向不稳定运行转变"序列，安全填充特征空间的稀疏区域；再将增强数据用于训练多输出LSTM（MO-LSTM）软测量模型，实现多目标同步估计。

Result: 在增强数据集上训练的MO-LSTM对所有目标均实现稳健的同步估计，克服了标准算法和未增强LSTM因数据稀缺而无法识别不稳定工况转变的问题。

Conclusion: 该生成式增强与多输出软测量相结合的方法有效缓解数据稀疏性，为工业生物过程的机理-数据混合建模及早期预警软测量提供了基础。

Abstract: Anaerobic digestion (AD) requires close monitoring of key process variables, including pH, the ratio of volatile organic acids to alkaline buffer capacity and biogas production, to maintain biological stability and prevent process failure. While mechanistic frameworks such as the Anaerobic Digestion Model No. 1 are highly parameterised, computationally expensive, and difficult to calibrate for real-time applications, data-driven Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks offer a potentially more adaptable alternative. However, current data-driven approaches in AD are predominantly limited to single-output architectures that focus solely on production yields, as opposed to simultaneous prediction of vital stability indicators. Furthermore, industrial AD datasets are typically sparse and skewed toward steady-state operations. Baseline evaluations demonstrated this scarcity prevents standard algorithms (Random Forest, XGBoost) and unaugmented LSTMs from recognising the transition to unstable operations, resulting in poor estimation of stability indicators. To overcome these challenges, this study proposes a novel generative data augmentation framework using an LSTM-beta-Variational Autoencoder coupled with a multi-output LSTM (MO-LSTM) soft sensor. By synthesising physically valid sequences of these transitions to unstable operations from one year of full-scale plant data, the method safely populates sparse regions of the feature space. Trained on this augmented dataset, the MO-LSTM achieved robust simultaneous estimation across all targets. This approach mitigates data sparsity, providing a basis for hybrid mechanistic-data-driven modelling and early-warning soft sensors in industrial bioprocesses.

</details>


### [344] [Quantum-Inspired Convolutional Networks for High-Resolution Pseudo-Label Generation from Multimodal Earth Observation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39773)
*Abhishek Singh,Giovanni Cuozzo,Alexander Jacob,Claudia Notarnicola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子卷积编码器-解码器网络，仅用粗分辨率MODIS标签监督，从Sentinel-1 SAR与Sentinel-2多光谱数据生成高分辨率伪标签，在DFC2020上优于经典基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率土地覆盖标注稀缺且成本高，需在弱监督（仅有粗分辨率MODIS标签）条件下从多模态对地观测数据生成精细尺度土地覆盖图。

Method: 构建量子启发式卷积编码器-解码器框架：量子卷积块增强特征提取与跨传感器融合；先通过重建分支进行自监督学习，学习多模态输入的鲁棒表示与精细光谱-空间结构，再引入低分辨率标签监督，生成高分辨率伪标签。

Result: 在DFC2020数据集（Sentinel-1 SAR、Sentinel-2多光谱、低分辨率MODIS标签与高分辨率参考图）上，生成伪标签的空间一致性与类别一致性优于经典基线，分类指标表现更好。

Conclusion: 量子启发式深度学习可有效提升低标注遥感场景下的标签生成质量，为对地观测高质量土地覆盖产品提供可扩展路径。

Abstract: Accurate land-cover mapping from multimodal Earth observation (EO) data remains a challenge in regions where high-resolution annotations are scarce or costly to obtain. This work introduces a quantum convolutional encoder-decoder network for pseudo-label generation using Sentinel-1 SAR and Sentinel-2 multispectral imagery, guided only by coarse MODIS-derived land-cover maps. The proposed framework uses quantum-inspired convolutional blocks to enhance feature extraction and cross-sensor fusion, enabling the model to infer a fine-scale semantic structure from heterogeneous inputs. Experiments were conducted on the DFC2020 dataset, which provides a quadruple of Sentinel-1 SAR, Sentinel-2 multispectral, low-resolution MODIS labels and high-resolution reference land-cover maps. The proposed training framework first learns a robust representation of multimodal inputs through the reconstruction branch before any supervision from low-resolution labels is applied. This self-supervised stage enables the model to capture a fine-scale spectral-spatial structure, which is then used to generate high-resolution pseudo-labels. These pseudo-labels are subsequently evaluated through classification metrics on the test data, demonstrating that the proposed approach produces more spatially coherent and class-consistent compared to classical baselines. The results highlight the potential of quantum-inspired deep learning to improve label generation in low-annotation remote sensing scenarios, offering a scalable path towards high-quality land-cover products for EO applications.

</details>


### [345] [Surface Conditioning for Zero-Shot Multivariate Correction in a Marine Biogeochemistry Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39777)
*Gabriela Martínez Balbontín,Anastase Charantonis,Dominique Béréziat,Stefano Ciavatta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量级偏差校正替代方案：利用海洋生物地球化学神经代理模型中自编码器的FiLM条件通路作为观测注入点，推理时用卫星叶绿素a替换模拟表层场，可将校正信号沿水柱向下传播并改善各深度结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于过程的模型偏差校正依赖迭代数据同化与显式协方差建模，计算与实现成本高，需要更轻量的替代方案。

Method: 基于海洋生物地球化学神经网络代理模型；解码器通过Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）以表层变量为条件（原用于注入物理边界强迫）；扩展FiLM输入加入表层叶绿素a，并在推理阶段以卫星观测替换模拟表层场，使表层信息经条件通路注入并沿水柱传播。

Result: 表层替换效应沿水体向下传播，在所有深度上均提升与深度分辨观测的一致性；且校正效应向其他输出变量扩散，其空间分布模式与预期的营养级关系相符。

Conclusion: FiLM条件通路可作为有效的观测注入接口，实现无需显式协方差建模的轻量级BGC偏差校正；表层观测经由条件机制可自洽地约束次表层与多变量预测。

Abstract: Bias correction in process-based models usually means iterative data assimilation with explicit covariance modelling. We propose a lightweight alternative based on a neural emulator of marine biogeochemistry (BGC), showing that the conditioning pathway of its autoencoder can serve as an observation injection point. The decoder is conditioned on surface variables via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), originally designed to incorporate physical boundary forcings. We extend these inputs to include surface chlorophyll-a and replace the simulated field with satellite observations at inference time. The surface substitution propagates through the water column, improving agreement with depth-resolved observations at all depths, and spreads to other output variables in a pattern consistent with expected trophic relationships.

</details>


### [346] [AI at a Turning Point: From ChatGPT through Neural Networks to Neuromorphic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39796)
*Volker Kempe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该专著系统阐述脉冲神经网络（SNN）与神经形态计算，以ChatGPT文本生成模块为例对比Transformer式AI与SNN在编码、学习与网络设计上的差异，并以人脑机制为参照，论证二者在混合系统中的互补关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接主义AI能耗巨大（训练与运行需吉瓦至太瓦时量级），而人脑仅约20瓦；神经形态计算的低功耗与自组织特性因此受到关注，亟需一份兼具可读性与数学严谨性的系统性梳理。

Method: 以通俗但数学严谨的方式综述：解析Transformer的原理、优雅性与计算开销；介绍SNN的编码、学习与网络设计方法——局部学习替代全局误差反传、事件驱动计算、以时间承载信息；并贯穿引用人类神经系统机制作为模型与参照。

Result: 覆盖脉冲时序依赖可塑性（STDP）、群体编码与时间编码、神经工程框架（NEF），以及从Loihi 2到商用边缘处理器的神经形态硬件等主题，形成对比性论述。

Conclusion: SNN并非取代现有AI，而是对其进行扩展；在混合系统中，两类方法可各自部署于其优势所在之处，经典AI与神经形态AI具备互补与相互促进的前景。

Abstract: The tension between the established technologies of connectionist AI and their neuromorphic challengers is attracting growing attention, above all because of the enormous energy demand of the former and the low power consumption of the latter. Developing and running large AI models consumes gigawatt- to terawatt-hours of energy, while the human brain operates on a continuous 20 watts. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) counter this energy demand with a frugal approach and bring elements of self-organization into the methodological repertoire of AI: local learning instead of global error backpropagation, event-driven computing, and time as a carrier of information. They do not replace today's AI but extend it; in hybrid systems each approach can be deployed where its strengths lie. This monograph explains the field in a generally accessible yet mathematically rigorous way. Using the text-generation module of ChatGPT as an example, the principle, elegance, and computational cost of transformer-based AI are presented and compared with the coding, learning, and network-design methods of SNNs. Throughout, relevant mechanisms of the human nervous system serve as model and reference point. Topics include spike-timing-dependent plasticity, population and temporal coding, the Neural Engineering Framework, neuromorphic hardware from Loihi 2 to commercial edge processors, and the prospects for classical and neuromorphic AI to complement and extend each other.

</details>


### [347] [Optimal Contextual Pricing in Non-Stationary Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39803)
*Yiding Feng,Alireza Rezaeimoghadam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非平稳环境下的上下文动态定价：买家价值对上下文与随时间变化的未观测特征线性，变化受总变差预算 V 约束，以"提取买家全部价值"的第一最优为基准度量动态后悔。同质上下文下确定性算法达到 O((√(TV)+1)log T)；异质上下文下提出多尺度浅定价算法，达到 O((d²+d^{3/2}√(TV))log² T)，对固定 d 与下界 Ω(√(TV)+log log T) 在 T 的对数因子内匹配，实现近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典上下文动态定价多假设环境平稳或反馈信息充分，而非平稳环境下卖家每轮仅获得二值购买反馈，且买家价值含随时间漂移的未观测特征；异质上下文还引入新的几何困难——特征小幅变化可能使松弛可行集狭窄却远离当前特征向量。因此需要刻画在总变差预算下确定性算法可达到的最优动态后悔。

Method: (1) 同质上下文：结合二分搜索探索、下包络定价以及周期性重启与不一致触发的重启，构造确定性算法；(2) 异质上下文：提出多尺度浅定价算法，在多个变化尺度上维护松弛可行集与包围椭球；较大尺度提供可靠的定位证书，最小活跃尺度决定利用价格，以应对"窄而远"的几何困难。

Result: 同质上下文后悔为 O((√(TV)+1)log T)；异质上下文后悔为 O((d²+d^{3/2}√(TV))log² T)，其中 d 为上下文维度。对固定 d，两者均与下界 Ω(√(TV)+log log T) 在 T 的对数因子内相匹配，覆盖所有 V∈[0,T]，包括平稳情形。

Conclusion: 在总变差预算刻画的变化环境中，仅凭二值反馈的确定性定价算法可实现近最优动态后悔；多尺度松弛可行集与包围椭球的机制有效克服异质上下文下的几何障碍，对所研究的确定性算法类达到近最优性。

Abstract: We study contextual dynamic pricing in a non-stationary environment. In each round, a seller observes a buyer's context, posts a price, and receives only binary purchase feedback. The buyer's value is linear in the context and an unobserved feature vector that may change over time, subject to a known total variation budget $V$. We measure dynamic regret against the first-best benchmark that extracts each buyer's full value.
  For identical contexts, we develop a deterministic algorithm that combines binary-search exploration, lower-envelope pricing, and periodic and inconsistency-triggered restarts. It achieves regret $O((\sqrt{TV}+1)\log T)$. With heterogeneous contexts, a new geometric difficulty arises: small feature changes can leave a relaxed feasible set narrow but far from the current feature vector. We address this difficulty through a multiscale shallow-pricing algorithm that maintains relaxed feasible sets and enclosing ellipsoids at several variation scales. Larger scales provide reliable localization certificates, while the smallest active scale determines the exploitation price. The algorithm achieves dynamic regret $O((d^2+d^{3/2}\sqrt{TV})\log^2 T)$, where $d$ is the context dimension. For fixed $d$, both guarantees match the lower bound $Ω(\sqrt{TV}+\log\log T)$ up to logarithmic factors in $T$, establishing near-optimality among deterministic algorithms for every $V\in[0,T]$, including the stationary case.

</details>


### [348] [Possibility-based Inference: A Deterministic Framework for Robust and Reliable Uncertainty Quantification in Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39821)
*Seyedfakhredin Musavishavazi,Maryam Alipour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可能性（possibility）的对比预测分级（contrastive grade of prediction），作为潜在后验方法的替代，用于贝叶斯推断中的参数估计与模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键系统的部署需要可靠的不确定性量化，而现有贝叶斯推断在数据精度与性能-精度权衡上存在困难，概率框架对冲突信息刻画不够灵活。

Method: 受模糊集理论影响、以贝叶斯统计为基础，构建可能性表示框架，定义基于可能性的对比预测分级，用于参数估计（近似）与模型选择，替代传统基于潜在后验的量化方式。

Result: 该框架提供了更灵活的不确定与潜在冲突信息的表征方式，形成系统化的数据挖掘方法论，并缓解高专业化深度网络架构产生的过度自信且不合理的预测。

Conclusion: 可能性表示可作为概率贝叶斯推断的补充量化途径，提升不确定环境下机器学习系统行为评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Decision-making under uncertainty remains a key issue in Machine Learning (ML), particularly in the deployment of safety-critical systems, where reliable quantification of system behavior is essential for evaluating behavioral impacts and strengthening confidence in ML models. This contribution, influenced by fuzzy set theory and grounded in Bayesian statistics, addresses challenges related to data precision and the performance-accuracy trade-off in Bayesian inference (BI). To this end, we introduce a possibility-based contrastive grade of prediction as an alternative quantification method to existing latent posterior-based approaches for parameter estimation (approximation) and model selection. Unlike conventional Bayesian inference, which represents uncertainty within the probabilistic framework, the proposed model adopts a possibility-based representation that allows uncertain and potentially conflicting information to be characterized more flexibly. Rather than focusing on isolated aspects of a problem, the resulting mathematical framework provides a systematic approach to data mining while mitigating overly confident, yet implausible predictions generated by highly specialized deep learning architectures.

</details>


### [349] [SHPRR: An Algorithm for Pure Exploration under Constrained Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39837)
*Georgina Nouli,Flore Sentenac,Maximilian Schiffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在"样本量"之外以"相对基线策略的后悔成本"度量实验开销的最优臂识别（BAI）问题，理论证明顺序减半（Sequential Halving）各阶段的探索后悔成本递减，并据此提出按比例分配后悔预算的SHPRR算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BAI以采样次数衡量实验成本，但在实际运营场景中，探索相对于已部署的可信基线策略所产生的后悔（regret）代价同样关键。而现有约束型纯探索研究多针对通用资源，未利用后悔成本的特殊结构——采样更优的臂相对基线成本更低。

Method: 作者在固定采样预算与后悔预算两种设定下研究BAI。理论分析表明，顺序减半（Sequential Halving）各阶段中探索的后悔成本逐阶段下降，并由此推导出更优的最优臂识别概率保证，进而指导后悔预算的更有效分配。基于该洞察提出SHPRR（Sequential Halving with Proportional Regret Rationing），将后悔预算前置投放，使得剩余臂较多时能够进行更多探索。

Result: 理论上给出了关于识别最优臂概率的改进保证；在合成数据与真实数据实验中，SHPRR在最优臂识别性能上优于均匀后悔分配策略及现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 后悔作为探索资源具有可利用的结构性特征，顺序减半的阶段性成本递减为预算分配提供了理论依据；将后悔预算前置的比例分配策略（SHPRR）能显著提升固定后悔预算下的最优臂识别效果。

Abstract: Best Arm Identification (BAI) conventionally measures the cost of experimentation by the number of samples collected. In many operational settings, however, the cost of exploration relative to a trusted baseline already in place is equally important. We study BAI under both a fixed sampling horizon and a regret budget. Unlike generic resources considered in constrained pure exploration, regret has a distinctive structure: sampling better arms is less costly relative to the baseline. We theoretically show that the regret cost of exploration decreases across the phases of Sequential Halving, a widely used pure-exploration algorithm. This observation yields improved guarantees on the probability of identifying the best arm and motivates a more effective allocation of the regret budget. We propose Sequential Halving with Proportional Regret Rationing (SHPRR), which front-loads the regret budget to allow more exploration when many arms remain. Experiments on synthetic and real-world instances show that SHPRR improves best-arm identification performance over uniform regret allocation and existing baselines.

</details>


### [350] [Exploring the Potential of Symbolic Regression in Precision Livestock Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39839)
*Elisabeth Mayrhuber,Maciej Oczak,Michael Iwersen,David Brunner,Viktoria Dorfer,Stephan Winkler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用母猪分娩前后的加速度传感器数据，采用两阶段方法（先检测筑巢行为、再用符号回归预测距分娩剩余时间），证明可解释AI在精准畜牧中兼具可解释性与竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测母猪分娩时间对农场管理和仔猪存活至关重要：可避免仔猪被压死、避免过早将母猪关入产仔栏以保障其福利。但传统机器学习模型存在"黑箱"问题，缺乏可解释性，因此需要可解释AI方法。

Method: 研究分两阶段：第一阶段基于母猪活动水平变化（不使用机器学习）检测筑巢行为；第二阶段采用符号回归（open-box，生成可解释数学公式）预测距分娩的剩余时间，并与支持向量回归、人工神经网络等闭箱模型在可解释性和性能上进行比较。

Result: 180头母猪中有166头成功检测到筑巢行为，其中149头在分娩前48小时内检测成功。符号回归的距分娩时间预测平均绝对误差为9.28小时，在性能和可解释性上均优于闭箱模型。

Conclusion: 可解释AI（特别是符号回归）能够在精准畜牧领域提供可解释结果并具备竞争力性能，为分娩预测与农场决策提供透明支持。

Abstract: The ability to predict farrowing time in pigs is crucial for optimal farm management and ensuring piglet survival. This study investigates the potential of explainable artificial intelligence for precision livestock farming, focusing on acceleration sensor data from sows around the time of farrowing. Accurately predicting farrowing allows farmers to balance both, piglet safety by avoiding piglet crushing events and the mother sow's welfare, by avoiding early crating. Traditional machine learning models often lack interpretability (``closed-box'' problem). Explainable artificial intelligence offers a solution by revealing the decision-making processes within the models. This work is split into two stages. First the detection of nest building behaviour and second the prediction of the remaining time to farrowing. The nest building detection was possible for 166 out of 180 sows and successful within 48 hours before farrowing for 149 sows. The nest building detection is purely based on the change of a sow's activity level and uses no machine learning techniques. Further, this study utilizes symbolic regression, an open-box modelling technique that generates interpretable mathematical formulas, to predict the remaining time until the onset of farrowing. The results are compared with closed-box models, like support vector regression and artificial neural networks, for both explainability and performance. Notably, symbolic regression achieved a mean absolute error of 9.28 hours for the time-to-farrowing prediction, outperforming the closed-box models in terms of performance and interpretability. This research demonstrates that explainable artificial intelligence, specifically symbolic regression, can offer both interpretable results and competitive performance in precision livestock farming.

</details>


### [351] [Surprise Alone Learns Lies: Evidence-Gated Continual Learning for LLMs from Streaming Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39840)
*Abdelkader Metales,Miriam Grabenheinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 以大模型"惊讶度"驱动的持续学习在含虚假信息的流上不安全：植入的谎言天然高惊讶度，几乎被全盘吸收。作者用LoRA学生模型（Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B、Llama-3.2-3B）在真假声明流上比较四种接受信号，发现验证器、校准回归测试、模型自身先验、可信来源路由各有局限且回答不同问题，无一能独立判定真伪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续学习惯用以"惊讶度/困惑度"决定学什么，只保留模型最难预测的输入。当数据流中混入谎言时，该准则失效：与模型已知事实相矛盾的虚假声明在模型信念下概率极低、惊讶度极高，因而被优先吸收。作者要系统检验这一不安全性的程度，并评估可行的防护信号。

Method: 构建含真实与虚假声明的数据流（关于虚构人物的声明与CounterFact真实事实），对LoRA微调的Qwen2.5-1.5B、Qwen2.5-3B、Llama-3.2-3B进行实验，比较四类"接受信号"：(1)验证器键（每条声明须经judge批准）；(2)无关知识上的校准回归测试；(3)模型自身先验对矛盾声明的标记；(4)将矛盾声明路由至可信来源证据。并考察攻击者可访问模型或可植入匹配来源时的对抗情形。

Result: (1)验证器键按验证器准确率过滤谎言，但真实LLM judge过于保守，会拒收无出处真知甚至带来源的更新；(2)校准回归测试只降低学习速度，不能过滤谎言；(3)模型自身先验在无judge时能区分"关于已知事实的谎言"与"真实新事实"（三模型AUC 0.78–0.83），但对同类事实的合法更新无法区分（AUC 0.53），可节省43%的judge调用，但能访问模型的攻击者可构造通过该检测的谎言；(4)若存在攻击者无法植入的可信来源，路由可恢复合法更新，组合系统在学习声明中投毒率最低；但攻击者为30%谎言植入匹配来源时，系统反而不如单独的judge。

Conclusion: 各类接受信号回答的是不同问题，任一种都无法单独判定声明真伪。惊讶度驱动的选择在本源上不具安全性，需要将验证器、自身先验与来源证据组合使用，并正视其各自的可攻击面。

Abstract: Continual learning methods for large language models increasingly decide what to learn by surprise, keeping the inputs the model finds least predictable. This rule is unsafe when the stream contains lies. A planted falsehood about something the model knows has low probability under the model's belief, so it is highly surprising by construction, and a surprise-driven learner absorbs nearly every such lie, at a rate similar to random selection with the same budget. We study the problem with LoRA-adapted students (Qwen2.5-1.5B, Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.2-3B) that learn from a stream of true and false claims about invented people and real CounterFact facts. We report four findings. (1) A verifier key, a judge that must approve each claim, removes lies to the extent that its verifier is accurate; real LLM judges are cautious and reject unsourced truths and even source-backed updates. (2) Calibrated regression tests on unrelated knowledge slow learning down without filtering out lies. (3) The original model's own prior flags claims that contradict what it knows, without a judge. It separates lies about known facts from true new facts (AUC 0.78-0.83 across three models) but not from legitimate updates of the same kind of fact (AUC 0.53). It saves 43% of judge calls, but an attacker with access to the model can choose lies that pass it. (4) If trusted sources exist that an attacker cannot plant, routing contradicted claims to evidence recovers legitimate updates and the combined system has the lowest poison rate among learned claims; when an attacker plants matching sources for 30% of the lies, the system ends up worse than the judge alone. The acceptance signals answer different questions, and none of them decides on its own whether a claim is true.

</details>


### [352] [DWISE: Denoising Workload-Informed Scheduling Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39852)
*Lie Li,Wen Li,Junxiao Shen,Jindong Gu,Guosheng Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）提出调度引擎DWISE：以预填充与去噪共享的块级剩余工作量单位统一调度，并用轻量预测器加首轮隐状态单次修正来预测未知去噪负载，在SGLang上实现平均5.4×、最高14.9×的端到端时延降低且保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有dLLM服务框架沿用AR模型调度策略（FCFS准入与预填充优先），二者均不适配dLLM：dLLM请求解码开销高度异质且到达时未知，FCFS导致严重队头阻塞；预填充优先使仅跑提示的轮次大量批次槽位空闲，大幅削弱并行去噪的效率优势。

Method: DWISE以剩余工作量排序请求，采用预填充与去噪共用的块级单位，使两阶段共享同一轮次；针对未知去噪负载，在准入时用基于提示的轻量预测器，并利用首个去噪轮隐状态做一次修正（隐状态含提示中缺失的模型特有信息且无需额外模型执行）。实现于SGLang。

Result: 在三个dLLM家族共五个模型、突发负载场景下评估：相比SGLang，平均端到端时延降低5.4×，最高14.9×，同时保持任务精度。

Conclusion: dLLM需要负载感知、阶段统一的调度；以可预测的剩余工作量为调度依据可显著提升服务效率。代码将公开。

Abstract: Unlike autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising many tokens in parallel, offering a promising path to efficient inference. Existing serving frameworks, however, schedule dLLM requests with policies designed for AR models, namely first-come-first-served (FCFS) admission and prefill priority. We find that both policies are ill-suited to dLLMs, wasting much of their speed. FCFS ignores that a dLLM request's decoding cost is highly heterogeneous and unknown at arrival, causing severe head-of-line blocking. Prefill priority, widely used in AR-LLM serving to keep time-to-first-token low, sharply cuts dLLM efficiency, as rounds that run only prompts leave most of the batch idle. We present DWISE, a Denoising Workload-Informed Scheduling Engine for efficient dLLM serving. DWISE schedules requests by their remaining work, measured in a block-level unit shared by prefill and denoising, so both phases share a round. To predict the unknown denoising workload, DWISE combines a lightweight prompt-based predictor at admission with a single refinement from the first denoising round's hidden states, which capture model-specific information absent from the prompt and require no additional model execution. Implemented in SGLang and evaluated on five models from three dLLM families under bursty workloads, DWISE reduces mean end-to-end latency over SGLang by 5.4$\times$ on average and up to 14.9$\times$ while preserving task accuracy. The code will be released publicly.

</details>


### [353] [Attention, Dynamic Wave and Hybrid Sequence Mixers in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39854)
*Dmytro Babarytskyi,Oleksandr Babarytskyi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化提出 Dynamic Wave 循环层（输入依赖的指数阻尼+余弦相位调制），在 TinyStories 上以 10.94M 参数对比 Pure Transformer、Pure Wave 与 Hybrid Wave-Attention。Hybrid 困惑度最优（5.95 vs. 6.93 / 7.26），但效率结论由实现而非架构主导：对未融合注意力基线 Pure Wave 省 78.7% 峰值显存，对融合 SDPA 内核则毫无优势且训练慢 8.3%；循环外提元素级项可提速 2.53 倍；混合栈继承较贵混合器的成本；闭式显存模型复现测量并预测注意力超越词表投影的上下文长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 依赖多头注意力，长上下文受二次复杂度制约，催生亚二次循环替代方案。但现有工作报告的效率收益常缺少明确的注意力后端与实现前提，导致结论不可比、难以复现；同时缺乏对"架构优势"与"实现优势"的清晰区分，以及对混合堆叠成本归属与显存构成的定量刻画。

Method: 1) 形式化 Dynamic Wave 循环层：以输入依赖的指数阻尼与余弦相位调制构造递归状态更新；2) 控制变量实验：三组 10.94M 参数配置（Pure Transformer / Pure Wave / Hybrid Wave-Attention），在 TinyStories 上分别评测困惑度、延迟、吞吐与峰值显存，上下文长度延伸至 4096 token；3) 效率对照实验：分别以未融合注意力基线与融合 SDPA 内核为参照，并区分推理/训练模式；4) 实现优化消融：将元素级项提升到递归循环外，不使用自定义 kernel；5) 建立闭式显存模型，对测量值进行拟合验证并外推交叉点。

Result: Hybrid 在完整留出集上困惑度 5.95，优于 Transformer 的 6.93 与 Pure Wave 的 7.26。显存：4096 token 下 Pure Wave 相对未融合注意力基线降低 78.7%（1404.3 MB vs. 6601.6 MB），但相对融合 SDPA 无优势（1404.3 MB vs. 1403.6 MB）；训练模式下 Pure Wave 比 Transformer 贵 8.3%。延迟：元素级项外提带来 2.53 倍加速，与注意力的延迟差距由 2.48 倍缩小至 1.12 倍。混合栈峰值显存与 Pure Transformer 相差 0.1% 以内，即继承较贵混合器的成本而非较便宜者的优势。闭式显存模型复现测量结果，并可预测注意力超过词表投影的上下文长度阈值。

Conclusion: 循环注意力替代方案的效率收益必须相对于"具名的注意力后端"和"具名的实现"来陈述，否则结论不具备可比性；本文的效率差异主要由实现（融合内核、循环外提优化）决定而非架构本身。混合化的合理性应主要建立在质量（困惑度）而非效率之上，因为混合栈的成本由更昂贵的混合器决定。闭式显存模型可作为长上下文设计的先验预测工具。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on Multi-Head Attention (MHA) for sequence modeling, but scaling to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of attention, motivating sub-quadratic recurrent alternatives. This paper formalizes a Dynamic Wave recurrent layer, based on input-dependent exponential damping and cosine phase modulation, and evaluates three 10.94M-parameter configurations (Pure Transformer, Pure Wave, and Hybrid Wave-Attention) on TinyStories under perplexity, latency, throughput and peak VRAM profiling up to 4096 tokens. The Hybrid configuration reaches the best perplexity, 5.95 on the full held-out split against 6.93 for the Transformer and 7.26 for Pure Wave. The efficiency picture, however, is governed by implementation rather than by architecture. Against an unfused attention baseline, Pure Wave uses 78.7% less peak VRAM at a 4096-token context (1404.3 MB vs. 6601.6 MB); against a fused scaled-dot-product attention kernel the same comparison yields no advantage at all (1404.3 MB vs. 1403.6 MB), and in training mode Pure Wave is 8.3% more expensive than the Transformer. Likewise, hoisting elementwise terms out of the recurrence loop speeds the layer up 2.53-fold with no custom kernel, reducing its latency gap to attention from 2.48-fold to 1.12-fold. A hybrid stack is found to inherit the cost of its more expensive mixer rather than the advantage of its cheaper one, its peak VRAM matching the Pure Transformer to within 0.1%. A closed-form memory model reproduces these measurements and predicts the context length beyond which attention overtakes the vocabulary projection. The findings indicate that reported efficiency gains for recurrent attention replacements must be stated relative to a named attention backend and a named implementation, and that hybridization is best justified on quality grounds.

</details>


### [354] [Turing LLM Post-Training: Calibrated Distillation and Efficient Dynamic-MoE Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39876)
*Yue He,Jiahui Hu,Shaman Tang,Hua Zhou,Qiman Wu,Menglin Li,Yizhao Wang,Yuheng Zhang,Da Zhu,Zhou Wang,Anhua Liu,Hanlin Chen,Mingyuan Wang,Rui Xin,Yuhua Wei,Shuang Su,Siqi Liu,Kai Yang,Weichao Huang,Hang Zhang,Honggou Yang,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究动态路由MoE模型的训练后（post-training）整合问题，提出将标定式多教师在线策略蒸馏（C-MOPD）与配套执行路径（容量上限后的有界宽度分发、最终路由重放、异步rollout与训练重叠）结合的完整框架，在Turing-20B-A2B上实现高保真专家整合（四域归一化整合得分0.9988）并显著加速整合过程（2.86×）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态路由MoE中各类专家往往独立优化，整合时需要同时保证专家能力的保留（整合质量）与整合过程的效率。已有方法难以兼顾：多教师蒸馏存在路由支持不匹配与教师间损失尺度差异，串行训练–执行流程也导致整合开销大。

Method: 方法由两部分组成：(1) C-MOPD——在局部Top-K支持上与每个被选中的教师对齐，并采用教师级损失归一化与裁剪（teacher-wise loss normalization and clipping）进行标定；(2) 执行路径——post-capacity有界宽度dispatch、最终路由重放（final-route replay）、异步rollout与训练重叠。消融覆盖无标定、无裁剪、不同K等设置。

Result: Turing-20B-A2B上四域归一化整合得分0.9988，各域至少保留90.16%专家增益；K=32时完整标定目标得分为0.9962，无标定为0.9724，无裁剪为0.9834；8K响应上限下四个数学与代码推理基准均超过共享SFT与Qwen3-8B（均值85.92 vs 80.33 vs 74.21），并在12项更广泛指标中10项优于SFT；PPU上整合步时由398.7s降至139.3s，相对串行执行约2.86×加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明显著的专家整合效果与更高效的整合过程可以并存，但该收益体现的是完整训练与执行框架的整体贡献，而非任何单一组件。

Abstract: We study post-training for dynamically routed mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, focusing on integrating independently optimized specialists while keeping consolidation efficient. Our approach combines calibrated multi-teacher on-policy distillation (C-MOPD), which matches each selected teacher on a local Top-$K$ support with teacher-wise loss normalization and clipping, with an execution path that uses post-capacity bounded-width dispatch, final-route replay, and asynchronous rollout--training overlap. On Turing-20B-A2B, the selected model achieves a four-domain normalized integration score of 0.9988 and retains at least 90.16\% of the measured specialist gain in every domain. At matched $K=32$, the full calibration objective scores 0.9962, compared with 0.9724 without calibration and 0.9834 without clipping. At a matched 8K response cap, the model exceeds both shared SFT and Qwen3-8B on all four reasoning-enabled mathematics and code benchmarks (unweighted mean: 85.92, versus 80.33 for Qwen3-8B and 74.21 for SFT), and improves over SFT on ten of twelve broader evaluation metrics. In a PPU-based profile, the combined execution configuration reduces consolidation step time from 398.7 to 139.3 seconds, an observed 2.86$\times$ speedup over serial execution. These results demonstrate strong specialist integration alongside more efficient consolidation, while reflecting the complete training and execution framework rather than any single component.

</details>


### [355] [TACFormer: Transformer-Based Generative Model for Target-Conditioned De Novo PROTAC Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39891)
*Yossra Gharbi,Rocío Mercado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACFormer：一种基于Transformer的靶点感知生成模型，以ESM-2的POI序列表征为条件，实现PROTAC全分子de novo设计，生成分子新颖、多样且化学空间随POI条件系统性偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PROTAC生成方法多将设计简化为linker生成问题，warhead与E3配体预先固定，限制了化学空间探索，且POI仅通过预设warhead隐式体现，未在生成中以蛋白质层面信息作为显式条件信号。

Method: 提出TACFormer：GPT风格分子解码器以预训练ESM-2的POI序列嵌入为条件，直接生成完整PROTAC分子（而非仅linker），使POI信息引导全分子生成。

Result: 生成的PROTAC新颖性高、化学多样性好；在不同靶点条件下呈现一致的POI依赖性化学空间偏移，特定靶点条件下生成的分子优先富集于留出数据集中对应POI相关的化学邻域，同时外推至新化学型。

Conclusion: 将生物学背景原生融入生成过程，使PROTAC设计转向更具生物学信息引导的降解剂化学空间探索。

Abstract: Targeted protein degradation (TPD) represents a fundamental shift in drug discovery, moving beyond traditional target inhibition toward the event-driven removal of disease-causing proteins. A prominent strategy for TPD is the use of proteolysis-targeting chimeras (PROTACs), heterobifunctional molecules that selectively route a protein of interest (POI) to the ubiquitin-proteasome system for degradation. While PROTAC design still relies heavily on trial-and-error synthesis and experimental testing, deep generative models offer a promising means to explore PROTAC chemical space in silico and generate novel molecules. However, many existing generative methods formulate PROTAC design primarily as a linker-generation problem, with POI-binding (warhead) and E3-ligase-binding components fixed in advance. This restricts exploration of the full PROTAC chemical space and often leaves the POI implicit through the predefined warhead, rather than using protein-level information as an explicit conditioning signal during generation. To address these limitations, we propose TACFormer: a transformer-based generative model for target-aware de novo PROTAC design. The model conditions a GPT-style molecular decoder on pretrained ESM-2 representations of POI sequences, allowing POI-level information to steer full-molecule generation. Our results show that TACFormer generates highly novel and chemically diverse PROTACs while showing consistent POI-dependent shifts in generated chemical space. In particular, molecules generated under specific target conditions preferentially populate chemical neighborhoods associated with POIs in the held-out dataset, while extrapolating to novel chemotypes. By natively integrating biological context into the generative process, TACFormer moves PROTAC design toward a more biology-informed exploration of degrader chemical space.

</details>


### [356] [Data-driven Random-feature Approximation of Koopman Generators of Nonlinear Continuous-time Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39897)
*Ana Čukarska,Maximilian Gollwitzer,Viola Muning Li,Vladyslav Fediukov,Zahra Monfared,Felix Dietrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的随机特征方法，为gEDMD等Galerkin近似构建简约而有效的字典（函数表示），用于从数据学习连续时间动力系统的Koopman生成元，并给出收敛性与理论分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koopman框架通过作用在函数空间上的线性算子表示动力系统，灵活且能刻画系统基本性质；但数值近似Koopman算子/生成元的关键难点在于字典（有限维函数空间表示）的选择，数据无关的字典往往不够有效。

Method: 针对连续时间系统，近似Koopman生成元而非Koopman算子；构造数据驱动的随机特征表示，作为Galerkin型方法（如gEDMD）的字典；该表示由数据引导，区别于数据无关方法，且无需迭代优化。

Result: 在预测与系统分析任务上的实验表明方法准确且快于迭代优化；理论上证明了向量场近似及由此产生轨迹的收敛性，给出gEDMD近似所生成半群的通用收敛判据，并构造反例说明gEDMD一般不保持指数有界性。

Conclusion: 该方法推进了从数据高效学习连续时间动力系统的前沿，有助于基于Koopman框架的可解释、数学上稳健的建模。

Abstract: Learning continuous-time dynamical systems from data is an active area of research. The Koopman formalism is often used here, because it is a flexible approach that is informative about the system's fundamental properties. The formalism represents dynamical systems through linear operators acting on a suitable function space. A practical difficulty arising in the numerical approximation of Koopman operators is the choice of a ``dictionary'', meaning an effective finite-dimensional representation of the function space. In this paper, we focus on learning dynamical systems in continuous-time, and so we consider numerical approximations of the Koopman generator instead of the Koopman operator. Our main contribution is a data-driven random-feature method to obtain a parsimonious and effective function representation for Galerkin approximation methods such as gEDMD. The representation we construct is data-informed, which allows us to improve upon data-agnostic methods. We empirically demonstrate the suitability of our method for forecasting tasks and system analysis. The numerical experiments show that our approach is accurate, and faster than iterative optimization. Further, we prove convergence of our approximation of the vector field and the resulting trajectories. In addition to the numerical results, we also make theoretical contributions, including a general convergence criterion for the semigroup generated by the gEDMD approximation and a counterexample showing that gEDMD does not generally preserve exponential boundedness. With our contribution, we advance the frontier of efficiently learning continuous-time dynamics from data, towards interpretable and mathematically robust modelling based on the Koopman framework.

</details>


### [357] [CovFedAvg: Local Low-Loss Curves and Federated Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39902)
*Federico Lucchetti,Marcus Völp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CovFedAvg方法，利用保留在客户端的本地冗余模型与曲线控制拟合兼容的共享模型端点，无需集中校准数据，并在多任务实验中仅取得相对FedAvg的小幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端只观测预测任务的局部数据，可能学到局部等价但观测支撑之外行为不兼容的模型；论文欲探究冗余模型是否能提供通往兼容共享模型的本地低损失路径。

Method: CovFedAvg在客户端保留本地模型及曲线控制，通过聚合的梯度贡献拟合共同端点，并用分布式训练损失选择实际候选模型；无需集中校准样本或客户端间样本交换。理论层面给出精确选择保证（不劣于上一模型与本轮FedAvg候选）、条件性客户端兼容性界与近似选择界，并保留修正的变换恒等式与全梯度保障定理。

Result: 在合成支撑任务、活动识别受试者以及参与与未来气体传感器批次上，设置5种方法、5个种子、2种网络宽度、20轮且无校准数据：宽度16时CovFedAvg将参与Gas宏平均平衡准确率由FedAvg的88.88%提升至89.84%，而匹配的分布式微调达91.65%。

Conclusion: 结果支持可行性并显示相对FedAvg的适度增益，但未能确立曲线方法相对同等开销优化策略的特定优势；精确求和实验仅验证数据流，端到端隐私仍需额外机制与分析。

Abstract: Clients observing different parts of a prediction task may learn locally equivalent models with incompatible behavior outside their observed support. We study whether redundant models permit local low-loss paths toward a compatible shared model. CovFedAvg retains local models and curve controls on clients, fits a common endpoint through aggregated gradient contributions, and selects an actual candidate model using distributed training losses. No centralized calibration examples or client-to-client sample exchange are required. Exact selection guarantees no higher evaluated training loss than the previous model and the current-round FedAvg candidate; this is not a global-optimality, stationarity, generalization, or differential-privacy guarantee. We state conditional client-compatibility and approximate-selection bounds and retain corrected transformation identities and a separate full-gradient safeguard theorem for reference algorithms. A complementary input-support example recovers the shared absolute-value regression, while matched direct fine-tuning also solves it. Across a synthetic support task, recorded activity-recognition subjects, and participating and future gas-sensor batches, we evaluate five methods, five seeds, two network widths, and 20 rounds without calibration data. At width 16, CovFedAvg raises participating-Gas macro balanced accuracy from FedAvg's 88.88% to 89.84%; matched distributed fine-tuning reaches 91.65%. Results support feasibility and modest gains over FedAvg, but do not establish a curve-specific advantage over equally costly optimization. Exact-sum numerical experiments establish data flow; end-to-end privacy requires additional mechanisms and analysis.

</details>


### [358] [Learning Calibrated Confidence for Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39927)
*Chenxiao Fan,Chongming Gao,Gangyi Zhang,Yaxin Gong,Leyang Shen,Jiakai Wang,Dong Wang,Yang Liu,Haoyan Liu,Xinting Hu,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 MASCAL，让多智能体系统中的每个智能体在强化学习中联合优化任务性能与置信度校准，从而产出正确答案概率，并在交接与最终输出中传递该定量信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答等需要跨多文档收集证据并推理的任务中，多智能体系统通过分工协作完成，上游智能体产出中间答案与支撑证据供下游使用；但上游答案正确性参差不齐，且系统通常不提供量化信号，下游只能依据文本证据自行判断是否信任或核验。

Method: MASCAL 让每个智能体在强化学习中联合优化任务表现与置信度校准，学习为自身答案输出正确概率；上游在交接时传递该概率，为下游决策提供量化依据，最终答案同样附带校准后的概率。此外引入置信度感知加权（confidence-aware weighting），提升对上游概率敏感位置的学习权重，使下游在训练中更充分地从主动使用该信号的决策中学习。

Result: 在覆盖网络检索与推理任务的三类协作架构上，MASCAL 同时提升了两类智能体的任务准确率与校准质量，并可泛化到更深的协作链与不同模型规模。干预分析证实上游置信度会影响下游决策行为，且学习到的最终置信度支持选择性作答与定向复查。

Conclusion: 通过将校准后的正确性概率作为智能体间可传递的量化信号，并结合置信度感知加权训练，MASCAL 有效增强了多智能体信息检索与推理系统的可靠性与可解释性。

Abstract: Complex information seeking on the Web often requires collecting evidence from multiple documents and reasoning across them, as in multi-hop question answering. Multi-agent systems address such tasks by dividing work among specialized agents, where upstream agents produce intermediate answers and supporting evidence that downstream agents build upon. However, upstream answers vary in correctness, and most systems provide no quantitative signal about it, leaving the downstream agent to judge whether to trust or verify what it receives based solely on the textual evidence.
  In this paper, we propose MASCAL. Each agent learns to produce a correctness probability for its answer by jointly optimizing task performance and confidence calibration in reinforcement learning; the upstream agent passes this probability at handoff, giving the downstream agent a quantitative basis for its decisions, and the final answer also carries a calibrated probability, quantifying the reliability of the system output. To guide the downstream agent in using this signal during training, we introduce confidence-aware weighting, which increases the learning weight at positions responsive to the upstream probability, so that the agent learns more from decisions where it actively uses the signal. Across three collaboration architectures spanning Web retrieval and reasoning tasks, MASCAL improves task accuracy and calibration quality for both agents, and generalizes to deeper collaboration chains and different model scales. Intervention analysis confirms that upstream confidence affects downstream decision behavior, and the learned final confidence supports selective answering and targeted rechecking.

</details>


### [359] [Low-Bit GDN Recurrent-State Quantization: Global Gradient-aware Allocation and Pure-Write Sliding Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39931)
*Qinghang Huang,Xuchu Huang,Xunzhao Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文针对线性注意力状态量化中的写回误差累积问题，提出全局梯度感知分配（GGA）与纯写滑动窗口（PW），在Qwen3.5-4B/9B的七项评测上大幅优于STEPQuant@4，并显著缩短过长输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力将历史信息压缩进固定大小的循环状态，降低状态精度可减少单请求存储，但量化写回误差会进入后续循环、降低长推理任务预测质量，并伴随重复生成与过长输出。现有方法在"精度如何分配"与"近期信息如何维护"两点上均未妥善处理。

Method: 提出GGA：结合候选量化残差与对未来预测损失的梯度敏感性，在模型级混合精度预算下为各注意力头分配比特宽度；提出PW：独立以FP8向量对维护窗口内的"纯写"（未量化写回）信息，使其经完整GDN状态转移传播，仅在离开窗口后才并入低位历史状态。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B/9B的七项评测中，仅GGA分配即比STEPQuant@4分别提升19.48/29.84个百分点；加入PW-S8后七任务宏平均达69.83%/78.63%，较STEPQuant@4提升38.39/41.21个百分点。@6设置下GGA使平均生成长度相对STEPQuant降低10.45%/14.65%；GGA@4再加PW-S8进一步降低32.32%/36.77%。相近存储预算下，将容量用于维护近期写回比单纯提高累积状态位宽获得更高分数与更短平均输出。

Conclusion: 精度重分配与窗口维护两种机制在提升任务分数的同时缓解过长输出；表明线性注意力状态量化中，按梯度敏感性分配精度与保留近期高保真写回，是比单纯加宽状态更高效的存储使用方式。

Abstract: Linear attention stores historical information in a recurrent state of fixed size, providing a compact memory representation for long-context inference. Reducing state precision further lowers per-request storage requirements, but quantized writeback errors enter subsequent recurrences, degrade prediction quality on long-reasoning tasks, and can be accompanied by repetitive generation and excessively long outputs. We propose Global Gradient-aware Allocation (GGA) and the Pure-Write Sliding Window (PW) to address precision allocation and recent-information maintenance. GGA combines candidate quantization residuals with gradient sensitivity to future prediction loss to allocate head bit widths under a model-wide mixed-precision budget. PW independently maintains recent pure writes as FP8 vector pairs, propagates them through the full GDN state transition, and merges them into the low-bit historical state only after they leave the window. Across seven evaluations on Qwen3.5-4B/9B, GGA allocation alone improves over STEPQuant@4 by 19.48/29.84 percentage points. Adding PW-S8 yields seven-task macro-averages of 69.83%/78.63%, improving over STEPQuant@4 by 38.39/41.21 percentage points, respectively. Both precision reallocation and window maintenance improve task scores while mitigating excessively long outputs: at @6, GGA reduces mean generation length, averaged equally across tasks, by 10.45%/14.65% relative to STEPQuant; adding PW-S8 to GGA@4 further reduces mean generation length by 32.32%/36.77%. Comparisons at similar storage budgets further show that using additional capacity to maintain recent writes yields higher task scores and shorter mean outputs than solely increasing the bit width of the accumulated state.

</details>


### [360] [H$^2$R: Hierarchical Hint-Based Reward for Mathematical Reasoning via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39934)
*He Tu,Siye Li,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Hierarchical Hint-based Reward（H²R）框架，利用数学问题的子问题层级结构作为 RLVR 训练中的中间监督信号，并构建 HiMath 基准验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法对复杂多步数学推理缺乏有效的中间过程监督，难以支撑长程推理。

Method: 以数学题固有的子问题层级结构构造结构化层级提示（hierarchical hints），将其作为逻辑上可依赖的中间里程碑缩短推理步长；并将该信号整合进奖励设计中（H²R）。同时构建层级化数学基准 HiMath。

Result: 跨多种 LLM，层级提示无需任务特定训练即可提升推理表现；接入 RL 训练后，Qwen2.5-Math-7B 在 HiMath 上的准确率由 30.4% 提升至 39.1%，使用 Oracle Hierarchical Hints 时可达 45.6%。

Conclusion: 将可靠的中间检查点纳入奖励设计，是维持复杂数学推理能力的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards has shown great promise in enhancing mathematical reasoning in large language models. However, existing approaches provide limited intermediate supervision for complex, multi-step reasoning tasks. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{Hierarchical Hint-based Reward}, a framework that leverages the intrinsic sub-question structure of mathematical problems as structured intermediate conditioning signals during RLVR training. Unlike chain-of-thought prompting, these hierarchical hints serve as logically grounded intermediate milestones that effectively reduce the reasoning horizon toward the final answer. To validate this framework, we introduce \textbf{HiMath}, a benchmark of hierarchically structured mathematical problems. Across diverse LLMs, hierarchical hints consistently improve reasoning performance even without task-specific training. When integrated into RL training via H$^2$R, they boost Qwen2.5-Math-7B accuracy on HiMath from 30.4\% to 39.1\% using hierarchical hints, and up to 45.6\% with Oracle Hierarchical Hints. This pronounced improvement demonstrates that incorporating reliable intermediate checkpoints into reward design is essential for sustaining complex mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [361] [When to spend privacy: optimal allocation in private learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39954)
*Umberto Biccari,Yongcun Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将DP-SGD中固定隐私预算在迭代间的分配问题建模为在复合零集中差分隐私（zCDP）约束下的开环控制问题，提出PB-AMD算法在精确耗尽预算的单纯形上优化噪声方差调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私会计只能给出累积隐私代价的上界，却未解决如何把固定预算分配到各次迭代的问题；同时调度选择本身也受预算约束，需与会计一并处理。

Method: 在复合zCDP约束下，将以整个高斯噪声方差调度为决策变量，显式计入调度选择带来的隐私代价；对期望二次终端跟踪目标，证明任一局部极小点都耗尽认证预算，从而导出精确预算的单纯形形式化。提出Privacy-Budget Adjoint Mirror Descent（PB-AMD），将路径伴随梯度与熵式镜像更新结合，保持正值性、收费下限与预算约束。

Result: 在正则性与强制性假设下，证明最优调度的存在性与预算稳定性、设计到训练的效用稳定性，以及确定性、随机与样本平均意义下的平稳性保证；线性二次约化给出显式平方根分配律。实验显示跟踪效果优于多种分配规则，在学习率重启设定下预测效果优于均匀分配（此时学习率分配表现相近），并在共同平稳容差下比投影梯度下降耗时更少。

Conclusion: 该框架通过非线性训练动力学把认证隐私会计与优化联系起来，为预算分配提供了可证明的算法与理论保证。

Abstract: Privacy accounting bounds the cumulative privacy cost of Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent but does not determine how to allocate a fixed budget across iterations. We formulate the complete Gaussian noise-variance schedule as an open-loop control under a composed zero-Concentrated Differential Privacy constraint, explicitly accounting for schedule selection. For expected quadratic terminal tracking, we prove that every local minimizer exhausts the certified budget, yielding an exact-budget simplex formulation. We introduce Privacy-Budget Adjoint Mirror Descent (PB-AMD), combining pathwise adjoint gradients with entropic updates that preserve positivity, charge floors, and the prescribed budget. Under regularity and coercivity assumptions, we establish existence and budget stability of optimal schedules, design-to-training utility stability, and deterministic, stochastic, and sample-average stationarity guarantees. A linear-quadratic reduction yields an explicit square-root allocation law. Experiments demonstrate tracking improvements over several allocation rules and predictive improvements over uniform allocation in a learning-rate restart setting, where learning-rate allocation performs similarly. Comparisons at a common stationarity tolerance show lower schedule-optimization times than projected gradient descent on the tested problems. The framework connects certified privacy accounting with optimization through nonlinear training dynamics.

</details>


### [362] [Observability-Aligned Evidential Masked Pretraining for Reliable VRU Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39958)
*Shang Gao,Franz Götz-Hahn,Konrad Doll,Bernhard Sick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: eCoMAE：证据式掩码预训练方法，通过观测对齐的预测证据提升VRU预测在分布偏移下的可靠性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VRU预测需在部分可观测与分布偏移下运行，可靠性（不确定性）与精度同等重要。掩码预训练天然引入缺失内容，但现有时序掩码自编码方法多为确定性，且不约束置信度如何随可观测性变化。

Method: 提出eCoMAE：块级时间步掩码 + Normal-Inverse-Gamma解码器，配合三种观测感知机制——(1) 对掩码视图与参考视图共同可见位置的证据加权均值一致性；(2) 对掩码位置的证据抑制；(3) 跨随机掩码的表示一致性。

Result: IMPTC VRU意图预测（leave-one-environment-out天气偏移）上，OOD准确率由0.7971提升至0.8068，OOD AURC由0.0718降至0.0648；在IMPTC、Waymo、nuScenes、inD四个数据集的行人轨迹预测上均取得最优微调MSE。

Conclusion: 观测对齐的证据式预训练可在分布偏移下提升可靠性，并具备跨基准与跨任务的泛化有效性。

Abstract: Vulnerable road user (VRU) prediction must operate under partial observability and distribution shift, so reliable uncertainty matters alongside accuracy. Masked pretraining is attractive in this setting because it naturally exposes the model to missing content during self-supervision, but existing time-series masked autoencoding methods are typically deterministic and do not constrain how confidence should change with observability. We introduce eCoMAE, an evidential masked pretraining method for multivariate time series that explicitly aligns predictive evidence with what is observed. eCoMAE combines block-wise time-step masking with a Normal-Inverse-Gamma decoder and three observability-aware mechanisms: uncertainty-weighted mean consistency on positions visible in both a masked view and a reference pass, evidence suppression on masked positions, and representation consistency across stochastic masks. On IMPTC benchmark for VRU intention prediction under leave-one-environment-out weather shift, eCoMAE improves the strongest baseline from 0.7971 to 0.8068 in OOD accuracy and from 0.0718 to 0.0648 in OOD AURC. Additional evaluation on pedestrian trajectory forecasting across IMPTC, Waymo, nuScenes, and inD further yields the best fine-tuning MSE on all four datasets. These results show that observability-aligned evidential pretraining improves reliability under shift and remains effective beyond the primary benchmark.

</details>


### [363] [A physics-informed uncertainty-aware surrogate for $p$-$n$ junction diodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39993)
*Suchita Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种不确定性感知的物理信息代理模型，用于一维p-n结二极管，可依器件长度、掺杂与偏压条件预测内部状态，并据此构建终端电流、耗尽宽度、峰值场与存储电荷等关注量测试集及不确定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体器件仿真（如漂移-扩散方程求解）计算代价高，需要快速且可信的代理模型；同时纯数据驱动代理缺乏物理一致性且难以判断其预测在给定容差内是否可信，因此需要将物理约束与不确定性量化结合。

Method: 构建以器件长度、掺杂浓度和偏置电压为条件输入的物理信息（physics-informed）代理模型，输出器件的内部状态（静电势、电子与空穴密度等）；基于预测的内部状态推导一组关注量（终端电流、耗尽宽度、峰值电场、存储电荷），形成测试套件；并通过基准分析将该形式体系推广到非对称结与二维二极管。

Result: 在一维对称结代理模型上，静电势的中位L1相对误差为0.11%，电子与空穴密度误差分别为1.5%与0.61%；所开发的性能分析方法能够判定代理预测是否落在用户指定容差内。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，不确定性感知的物理信息代理可用于p-n结二极管的快速可靠预测，且内部状态到关注量的推导框架可扩展至非对称结与二维器件，所附不确定性分析为用户提供了预测有效性的判据。

Abstract: We develop an uncertainty-aware physics-informed surrogate for a one-dimensional $p$-$n$ junction diode, conditioned on device length, doping and bias. Using the internal state of the device as predicted by the surrogate we develop a test-suite of quantities of interest including the terminal current, depletion width, peak field and stored charge. The formalism and the quantities are can be extended to asymmetric junction and two-dimensional diodes as shown by means of benchmark analysis. For the one-dimensional symmetric junction surrogate, we obtain 0.11% median $L_1$ error on the electrostatic potential, and 1.5% and 0.61% on the electron and hole densities. Finally, we develop an uncertainty analysis which determines the validity of surrogate answer within the user-specified tolerance.

</details>


### [364] [Enhancing Atrial Fibrillation Detection Through Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40001)
*Sara Gjorgjieva,Ilinka Ivanoska,Marjan Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统比较了4种超参数优化方法（GA、Hyperband、Optuna、SMAC3）在房颤检测中的作用，覆盖决策树、随机森林、XGBoost及RNN、DNN、孪生网络等模型；在PhysioNet心电数据上，Hyperband优化的XGBoost取得最高F1=96.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 房颤检测模型性能高度依赖超参数选择，但不同HPO策略在机器学习与深度学习模型上的相对效果缺乏系统对比，尤其在真实场景的类别不平衡与跨患者泛化条件下尚不明确。

Method: 构建统一评测框架：模型侧包含决策树、随机森林、XGBoost三类传统分类器与RNN、DNN、孪生网络三类深度架构；HPO侧采用遗传算法、Hyperband、Optuna、SMAC3四种方法；数据侧使用PhysioNet心电数据集，并按患者内（intra-patient）与患者间（inter-patient）两种划分评估泛化性与类别不平衡下的鲁棒性。

Result: Hyperband优化后的XGBoost表现最佳，峰值F1达96.95%，优于其余模型—优化器组合。

Conclusion: 合适的HPO策略对提升房颤分类的准确性与鲁棒性具有关键作用，可为临床场景下的模型调优提供实践依据。

Abstract: This study explores the role of hyperparameter optimization (HPO) in enhancing the detection of Atrial Fibrillation (AF) using both machine learning and deep learning models. We evaluated a range of standard classifiers - Decision Tree, Random Forest, and XGBoost classifiers, alongside deep learning architectures including Recurrent Neural Networks, Deep Neural Networks, and Siamese Neural Networks. To fine-tune these models, we applied four HPO methods: Genetic Algorithm, Hyperband, Optuna, and SMAC3. The experiments were conducted using inter-patient and intra- patient data splits to assess model generalization and robustness in the presence of class imbalance, which is reflective of real-world AF occurrence. Our results, based on PhysioNet electrocardiogram datasets, showed that the Hyperband-optimized XGBoost model outperformed all others, achieving a peak F1 score of 96.95%. These findings highlight the critical role of suitable HPO strategies in enhancing the accuracy and robustness of AF classification outcomes in clinical settings.

</details>


### [365] [FIDE: Fiber-Informed Differentiable Experimental Design for Reference-Relative Law Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40009)
*Pietro Zanotta,Ján Drgoňa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出FIDE（纤维信息可微实验设计）框架，在保留基线的设定下，用"信息投影补全聚合约束 + 最小动力学修正排序"来消除测量系统选择中的残余设计自由度，并将其解释为参考系相对的最小修正原理（其平方等于梯度归一化Sobolev类上的期望速率差异）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合测量通常使演化种群规律欠定，多个测量系统可能同样"科学充分"却约束规律的不同侧面，留下残余设计自由度。作者希望回答：在已有动力学模型作为工作基线（除任务显式关注的量外）时，应如何在这一自由度中做出有原则的选择，而不是套用通用的"逼近真实规律"目标。

Method: FIDE对每个候选设计：以共同冻结的参考模型为基准，通过信息投影（I-projection）补全候选的聚合约束，得到完成路径；再以实现该路径所需的动力学修正量排序。所得"Full action"即实现完成路径所需的最小均方速度修正，其平方根等价于在梯度归一化Sobolev类上、相对参考分布的尖锐期望速率差异。由此定义基线保持设计下的条件最小修正原则。

Result: 实验表明：该偏好会改变传感器选择结果；其对被留出的下游预测可能有利也可能有害，取决于基线可靠性；可由聚合预测前瞻性地使用；在32维构型规律上仍保持计算可用性。

Conclusion: 设计选择应被视为在科学充分性之外的一个条件性规范原则：最小修正原则为基线保持的实验设计提供了可解释、可计算的排序依据，但其对下游任务的价值依赖基线质量，而非普遍优于真值恢复型目标。

Abstract: Aggregate measurements generally leave an evolving population law underdetermined, so different measurement systems can be equally adequate for a scientific task while constraining different aspects of the law. We study how to resolve this residual design freedom in baseline-preserving settings, where an existing dynamical model remains the working baseline outside quantities explicitly valued by the task. \textbf{F}iber-\textbf{I}nformed \textbf{D}ifferentiable \textbf{E}xperimental Design (\textbf{FIDE}) completes each candidate's aggregate constraints by information projection relative to a common frozen reference, then ranks scientifically adequate designs by the dynamical revision required to realize the resulting completed paths. The resulting \emph{Full action} is the minimum mean-squared velocity correction needed to realize a completed path; equivalently, its square root is the sharp reference-relative expectation-rate discrepancy over a gradient-normalized Sobolev class. This defines a conditional minimum-revision principle for baseline-preserving design rather than a generic truth-recovery objective. Experiments show that this preference changes sensor selection, can help or harm withheld downstream predictions depending on baseline reliability, can be used prospectively from aggregate predictions, and remains computationally usable for a $32$-dimensional configuration law.

</details>


### [366] [One Vector or Many? Clustered Optimal-Transport Steering under Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40055)
*Illia Makarov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了基于聚类最优传输的激活引导方法（CHaRS族）用于毒性缓解，证明在CHaRS协议下该类方法与DiM（类均值差）统计等价，并提出逐token拟合的变体，该变体在Gemma-2-2B与Granite-3.1-2B上显著优于DiM，且在INT8/NF4量化下依然有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导通过在推理时编辑隐状态来控制语言模型输出，最常用的做法是把类均值差向量（DiM）加到每个隐状态上，但这种方式对每个token施加相同位移，忽略了不同token的分布异质性。CHaRS以最优传输的熵正则计划对两类聚类进行配对，使每个隐状态沿簇位移的混合移动并偏向最近簇。作者希望厘清：这一传输计划相对于DiM究竟带来了什么额外收益，以及该收益在INT8与NF4量化下是否仍然存在。

Method: 1）在理论上给出恒等关系：无论最优传输计划如何，以簇大小为权重对各簇位移求平均恰等于DiM，因此聚类映射只有在token的路由权重偏离簇大小时才可能区别于DiM。2）在Gemma-2-2B与Granite-3.1-2B上进行毒性缓解实验，采用八项预先设定的等价性检验，比较CHaRS协议与Mean-AcT（每一层均加DiM）的统计等价性。3）提出在单个token上拟合的变体，使每个token主要路由到其最近簇，并以toxic-bert及另两个毒性分类器评估效果，同时测量困惑度与在匹配毒性下的流畅度。4）引入由相同簇构造的近似常数映射作为对照，检验“对token区别对待”是否真正起作用。5）在INT8与NF4量化模型上评估该变体，并比较全精度到NF4的直接迁移（不重新拟合）与重拟合的结果，采用多重检验校正。

Result: CHaRS协议下路由权重与簇大小接近，八项预先设定的等价性检验中CHaRS与Mean-AcT全部统计等价。逐token拟合变体在引导强度3（toxic-bert评估）下，Gemma上比DiM少残留37–39%毒性，Granite上少51–61%，而DiM本身已去除76–85%毒性。另两个毒性分类器在Granite上确认增益，在Gemma上仅其中一个确认。等强度下该变体困惑度略高，但在毒性匹配时略比DiM流畅。然而，同簇构造的近似常数映射也带来约30%的额外毒性下降，说明“区别对待token”的作用尚未确证。INT8与NF4量化下该变体仍比DiM残留毒性更低；将全精度变体直接迁移到NF4模型而非重新拟合，经多重检验校正后未出现显著毒性上升。

Conclusion: 聚类最优传输映射相对DiM的增益并非来自传输计划本身——其加权平均恒等于DiM，且在CHaRS原协议下与Mean-AcT无可区分差异；真正的收益来自逐token路由的变体，但近似常数映射也能取得相近效果，因此token级异质性处理的核心作用仍待厘清。该变体在INT8与NF4量化下保持优势，且可从全精度直接迁移至NF4模型，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Activation steering changes what a language model generates by editing its hidden states at inference time. Its most common form adds one vector, the difference of class means (DiM), to every hidden state. CHaRS (concept heterogeneity-aware representation steering) uses optimal transport instead: an entropic plan pairs the clusters of the two classes, and each hidden state moves along a mixture of the cluster displacements, weighted toward its nearest clusters. We study this family of clustered optimal-transport maps for toxicity mitigation in Gemma-2-2B and Granite-3.1-2B, and ask what the plan adds to DiM and whether that survives INT8 and NF4 quantization.
  We prove an identity: whatever the plan, the displacements averaged with the cluster sizes as weights equal DiM, so a clustered map can differ from DiM only for tokens whose routing weights depart from the cluster sizes. In the CHaRS protocol they stay close, and CHaRS is statistically equivalent to Mean-AcT, which adds DiM at every layer, in all eight pre-specified equivalence tests. A variant fitted on individual tokens, which routes each token mainly to its nearest clusters, leaves 37-39% less toxicity than DiM on Gemma and 51-61% less on Granite at steering strength 3 (toxic-bert), where DiM already removes 76-85% of it. Two other toxicity classifiers confirm the gain on Granite, but on Gemma only one does. At equal strength the variant has somewhat higher perplexity, but at matched toxicity it is slightly more fluent than DiM. Yet a nearly constant map from the same clusters also leaves about 30% less toxicity, so the role of treating tokens differently remains open.
  Under INT8 and NF4 the variant still leaves less toxicity than DiM, and transferring it from full precision to the NF4 model instead of refitting it adds no significant toxicity after correction for multiple tests.

</details>


### [367] [Coupled Guidance for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40109)
*Mateo Clémente,Léo Maxime Brunswic,Rui Heng Yang,Amir Rasouli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上分析指出，将扩散模型引导方法直接迁移到流匹配会破坏训练时的耦合分布，导致采样精度控制变弱；据此提出基于几何视角的耦合引导（Coupled Guidance），利用流匹配固有的耦合结构，在机器人任务与图像生成任务上提升样本质量与引导稳定性且无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导方法对扩散模型生成过程的调控至关重要，但流匹配与扩散虽高度相似，将引导直接引入流匹配仍较困难。现有流匹配通用引导框架只是扩散引导方法的直接改编，容易出现不稳定。

Method: 对流匹配中的引导进行理论分析，揭示朴素迁移扩散引导会破坏训练所用耦合分布，从而削弱对采样精度的控制；随后从几何视角出发，提出流匹配专用的耦合引导机制（Coupled Guidance），利用流匹配训练中固有的耦合结构。

Result: 在基于 ManiSkill3 的自定义机器人碰撞避免任务、Adroit 基准的四个灵巧操作任务，以及 ImageNet-128 与 CelebA-HQ-256 上的图像修复、超分辨、去模糊三项图像生成任务上进行了广泛实验；结果显示该框架在所有任务上均提升样本质量与引导稳定性，且不引入任何额外开销。

Conclusion: 流匹配引导需尊重其耦合结构，耦合引导为流匹配提供了专属且稳定的引导方案，兼具性能提升与零额外开销的实用性。

Abstract: Guidance methods are essential tools for steering the generation process in diffusion models, improving the generalizability of stochastic robotic controllers and enabling high-quality controlled image synthesis. Despite strong parallels between flow-matching and diffusion, integrating guidance into flow-matching models remains quite challenging. Recent work has proposed general guidance frameworks for flow-matching, however, they are direct adaptations of diffusion guidance methods and can prove unstable. In this work, we conduct a theoretical analysis of guidance in flow-matching and show that a naive translation of diffusion-based guidance to flow-matching breaks the coupling distribution used during training, resulting in weaker control over sampling accuracy. Building on this insight, we propose Coupled Guidance, a flow-matching-specific guidance mechanism derived from a geometric perspective that leverages the coupling structure inherent to flow-matching training. To validate the effectiveness of our approach, we conduct extensive experimentation on a custom robotic collision avoidance task using ManiSkill3, four robotic dexterous manipulation tasks from the Adroit benchmark, and three image generation tasks, namely inpainting, super-resolution, and deblurring, using the ImageNet-128 and CelebA-HQ-256 datasets. Our findings show that our framework improves sample quality and guidance stability across all tasks, without imposing any additional overhead.

</details>


### [368] [D-MOD: Diffusion mode estimation for accelerated MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40110)
*Md Kawsar Ahmed,Mathews Jacob*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D-MOD：一种基于次优最大化（MM）算法在平滑对数后验下界上求解后验众数的快速确定性MRI重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散先验加速MRI重建多为后验采样器，单次采样随机性大，需平均多样本才能获得可复现高质量结果，计算开销高。

Method: 在平滑对数后验的下界上使用次优最大化（minorize-maximize）算法，可证明目标函数单调上升。每次迭代在当前噪声水平下对当前估计采样S个噪声样本，用预训练扩散模型去噪，将其均值与实测k空间通过共轭梯度（CG）求解融合；并对噪声水平退火，因下界与目标的距离随噪声二次衰减。

Result: 在fastMRI脑部（T1、T2、FLAIR）上，单次D-MOD重建的PSNR超过DPS、DAPS、SNORE-P的10样本有限MMSE估计，且与最强基线（DiffPIR、DDS）相差仅0.31 dB以内；相比MMSE估计快11–73倍。

Conclusion: D-MOD提供了快速、确定性的后验众数重建算法，适合临床场景。

Abstract: Diffusion models are powerful priors for accelerated MRI reconstruction, but most current methods are posterior samplers, which return a random draw from the posterior. A reproducible and high-quality reconstruction often requires averaging many samples, resulting in a high computational cost. We introduce D-MOD, a fast algorithm that instead provides a posterior mode. It uses a minorize-maximize algorithm on a lower bound of the smoothed log-posterior, which provably increases the objective. Each iteration draws S noisy samples of the current estimate at the current noise level, denoises them with a pretrained diffusion model, and combines their average with the measured k-space through a conjugate-gradient (CG) solve. We anneal the noise levels, as the distance between the bound and its target decays quadratically with noise. On fastMRI brain (T1, T2, FLAIR), a single D-MOD reconstruction exceeds, in PSNR, the 10-sample finite-MMSE estimates of DPS, DAPS and SNORE-P and stays within 0.31 dB of the strongest baselines (DiffPIR, DDS). This approach thus provides a fast deterministic reconstruction algorithm (11-73 times faster than MMSE estimates), which is useful in a clinical setting.

</details>


### [369] [Does System Size Matter in Uncertainty Quantification for Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40126)
*Chayaphol Lortaraprasert,Nontawat Charoenphakdee,Wenwen Li,Yong-Bin Zhuang,Yuta Tsuboi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文检验了MLIP中"结构级不确定性分数在不同体系尺寸间可比"这一隐含假设，发现均值与最大值两种聚合方式具有不同且显著的尺寸依赖性，固定阈值判据在大体系中并不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）普遍用不确定性估计来识别分布外（OOD）构型并挑选主动学习样本。实践中常以每原子不确定性的均值或最大值聚合为结构级分数，并与固定阈值比较；这隐含假设该分数与原子数无关，但该假设缺乏系统验证。

Method: 在多组ID/OOD配对上系统刻画两种聚合指标随原子数的变化规律，并考察在大型结构中高不确定性原子所对应结构上进行再训练的实际收益，与真正位于训练分布之外构造的OOD结构作对比。

Result: 两种聚合表现出不同的尺寸依赖：均值聚合对均质体系保持尺寸无关，但在大晶胞中会丧失对缺陷的敏感性；最大聚合在ID体系随尺寸增长（符合极值统计规律），而在含缺陷体系中近似持平，从而压缩了ID-OOD差距。此外，大结构中高不确定性原子虽可能指示新颖局域环境，但基于这些结构再训练几乎无提升，与再训练于构造的OOD结构形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了尺寸不敏感型不确定性判据的实际局限，说明需要引入尺寸感知的校准，以在大尺度原子模拟中获得更可靠的OOD检测与更有效的主动学习选样。

Abstract: Machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) commonly use uncertainty estimates to flag out-of-distribution (OOD) configurations and select structures for active learning. A key question is whether the resulting structure-level uncertainty scores remain comparable across different system sizes. A common practice is to aggregate per-atom uncertainties by their mean or maximum and compare the resulting score against a fixed threshold, implicitly assuming such comparability. We test this assumption across several in-distribution (ID)/OOD pairs by characterizing both aggregations as a function of atom count. The two aggregations exhibit distinct size dependencies. Mean aggregation remains size-independent for homogeneous systems but loses sensitivity to defects in large cells. In contrast, maximum aggregation grows with system size for ID systems while remaining approximately flat for defect-containing systems, narrowing the ID-OOD gap in a manner consistent with extreme-value statistics. Although highly uncertain atoms in large structures may indicate novel local environments, retraining on these structures yields little improvement, in contrast to retraining on OOD structures constructed to lie outside the training distribution. Our results reveal a practical limitation of size-unaware uncertainty criteria and motivate size-aware calibration for more reliable OOD detection in large-scale atomistic simulations.

</details>


### [370] [Escaping Path Mirages in Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40130)
*Junhyeon Bae,Jaebak Hwang,Gwanwoo Choi,Minung Kim,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Branch-aware Graph Planning (BGP)，通过将高变化率点识别为图节点并构建带分段学习的目标条件边，捕捉随机分支结构，避免不可靠分支，从而在高度随机的长时域离线目标条件RL中实现可靠的目标达成，在OGBench上大幅超越先前SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习在随机长时域场景下仍具挑战性：价值估计误差会累积，阻碍可靠的目标达成。已有方法尝试多种途径应对，但核心难题"路径海市蜃楼（path mirage）"依然存在——智能体会过度相信离线数据集中由随机性造成的伪成功轨迹，从而在困难任务上失败。

Method: BGP通过识别高变化率（high-variability）点作为图节点，显式刻画随机分支结构；并设计目标条件边，配合分段学习（segmented learning），以规避不可靠分支、减少路径上不必要的随机性。

Result: 在多个OGBench任务上，BGP显著优于先前最先进的离线目标条件RL方法，即使在高度随机的长时域环境中也能可靠达成目标。

Conclusion: 将随机分支结构显式建模为图规划问题（节点=高变化点，边=分段学习的目标条件边）是缓解path mirage、提升随机长时域离线目标条件RL性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning remains challenging in stochastic long-horizon settings, where compounding value estimation errors hinder reliable goal reaching. While prior methods have sought to address this challenge through various approaches, a key challenge remains path mirage, where agents overcommit to spuriously successful trajectories in offline datasets induced by stochasticity, often leading to failure on difficult tasks. To address this issue, we propose Branch-aware Graph Planning (BGP), which captures stochastic branching structures by identifying high-variability points as graph nodes and designing goal-conditioned edges with segmented learning to avoid unreliable branches and reduce unnecessary stochasticity along paths. As a result, BGP enables reliable goal reaching even in highly stochastic long-horizon environments, and experiments on diverse OGBench tasks show that it substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art offline goal-conditioned RL methods. Our implementation is available at https://umlic.github.io/papers/BGP/.

</details>


### [371] [A Deep Learning Framework for Quantile Demand Forecasting in E-commerce with Doubly Censored Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40138)
*Ziwen Ye,Jiawen Wei,Teng Song,Chen Chen,Guangrui Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一个面向双重删失观测的分位数学习框架及配套评估指标，并给出理论保证，实验表明其优于现有基线且在实际零售运营中具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售需求预测中，分位数估计能提供不确定性量化以增强决策稳健性。但真实销量会被库存截断（上删失）并受临时促销抬高，而目标变量是潜在需求，这类双重删失数据缺乏有效的深度学习不确定量化方法。

Method: 提出一个简洁框架，直接从双重删失观测中学习分位数（分位数回归式估计器），并设计一个针对该数据结构的专用评估指标；同时给出相应的理论保证。

Result: 实验表明该评估指标与oracle校准度量高度相关；所提估计器性能超过现有基线；库存模拟与真实零售应用验证了方法在实际运营中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了双重删失数据下深度学习分位数估计的空白，提供了可靠的不确定性量化工具，可直接支撑零售补货与库存决策。

Abstract: In recent years, a growing number of online retail companies have embraced deep learning models for demand forecasting, leveraging their scalability in processing large-scale datasets.
