Accurate demand forecasts enable improved planning and allow operational flexibility to adjust sales accordingly. Moreover, quantile estimation has gained widespread application as it offers valuable uncertainty quantification, thereby enhancing the robustness of decision-making processes. However, limited research has addressed the effective quantification of uncertainty when target labels are ambiguous. In retail, actual sales can be capped by stock and boosted by temporary promotions, whereas the target is underlying demand. Such scenarios are characterized by double censoring and no deep-learning pipeline yet exists for such data. In this paper, we introduce a straightforward framework for learning quantiles from doubly-censored observations and propose a tailored evaluation metric. Our approach is supported by theoretical guarantees, and we empirically demonstrate that our metric correlates well with the oracle calibration measure. Experimental results show that our estimator surpasses existing baselines. Finally, inventory simulations and real-world applications confirm the practical utility of our method in retail operations.

</details>


### [372] [Mode Dynamics: A Framework for Neural Network Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40143)
*Hossein Honarvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Mode Dynamics 框架，将神经网络学习过程中参数的协同运动分解为若干"集体模态"，并把每个模态映射到依赖状态的输出效应，用可控实验和真实模型轨迹验证其能刻画行为演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究多关注参数轨迹本身或行为变化本身，缺少把参数协同运动与模型输出变化显式联系起来的统一框架；作者希望回答少量"模态"能否重构行为变化、效应是否依赖训练路径、模态复杂度是否随任务与数据变化。

Method: 基于记录的参数轨迹，把参数更新分解为集体模态及其随时间变化的幅度（Decomposition）；检验这些模态诱导的效应能否复原局部与累积的输出变化（Reconstruction）；量化行为贡献在模态间的分布（Concentration）。先在受控的标量回归与下一词预测实验中直接检验三种机制，再在 CIFAR-100 自编码器与 Pythia-70M 公开预训练轨迹的留出输出上评估。

Result: 受控实验显示：少量主导模态即可重构行为；模态诱导的效应依赖训练路径；模态复杂度随任务与数据而变化。在 CIFAR-100 自编码与 Pythia-70M 预训练轨迹上，紧凑的模态子空间与演化中的行为建立了对应关系。

Conclusion: 参数的协同模态运动可以以紧凑子空间的形式解释模型行为的变化，Mode Dynamics 为理解训练动力学与输出演化之间的关系提供了一个可分解、可验证的分析框架。

Abstract: We introduce Mode Dynamics, a framework that connects coordinated parameter motion during neural network learning to changes in model outputs. Starting from a recorded parameter trajectory, we decompose its updates into collective modes and map each mode to its state-dependent output effect. The framework organizes this link through three mechanisms. Decomposition identifies collective modes in parameter motion and their time-varying amplitudes. Reconstruction tests whether the effects induced by those modes recover local and accumulated output change. Concentration quantifies how behavioral contribution is distributed across modes. Controlled scalar regression and next-token experiments directly test these mechanisms. They show that small sets of leading modes reconstruct behavior, induced effects depend on the training path, and modal complexity varies with task and data. We then evaluate the framework on held-out image and language-model outputs using CIFAR-100 autoencoding and a public Pythia-70M pretraining trajectory. Across these settings, the results connect compact modal subspaces of recorded parameter motion to evolving behavior.

</details>


### [373] [FARE: Factor-Aware Retrieval with Error-Guided Fusion for Retail Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40160)
*Chenghao Gu,Zhuokang Shen,Shenghao Wu,Tingxuan Zhu,Weiyi Luo,Haifeng Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FARE是一种因子感知检索与误差引导融合的零售销量预测增强方法：对目标周的每个已知因子分别检索历史周并合并为因子参考，再用两级门控将其与形状参考、主干预测融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售销量受促销与日历事件影响，短期需求波动大。各因子单独看有历史先例，但目标周完整因子组合往往历史样本稀少，导致直接检索整周难以命中，需要检索可靠的部分匹配并按可靠性加权（可靠性在序列间差异显著）。

Method: FARE（Factor-Aware Retrieval with Error-Guided Fusion）：1）按目标周的每个已知因子分别对历史周排序，在固定预算下将选中周合并为"因子参考"；2）以已完成的开发周上的收缩误差及当前候选间分歧为引导，第一级门控将因子参考与来自相似销量历史的"形状参考"混合；3）第二级门控控制该混合结果对主干预测的修正强度；4）两级门控在测试阶段保持固定。

Result: 在 Favorita、M5、Rossmann、Rohlik 四个数据集上，FARE 使五个主干模型的 WAPE 平均分别降低 11.46%、4.06%、14.63%、10.16%；在 Chronos-2 冻结设置下亦优于传统方法与现有增强方法（含监督残差修正与检索增强）。

Conclusion: 通过因子级检索与误差引导的固定门控融合，可在不重训主干的前提下显著降低多种零售预测基准的误差，且与冻结的时序基础模型兼容。代码已开源。

Abstract: Retail sales forecasting underpins inventory planning, yet promotions and calendar events reshape short-term demand. Although each factor usually has historical occurrences, the complete factor combination of a target week often has few precedents. The challenge is therefore to retrieve informative partial matches and weight them by their reliability, which varies across series. We propose FARE(Factor-Aware Retrieval with Error-Guided Fusion), which ranks historical weeks separately for each known target-week factor and merges the selected weeks into a factor reference under a fixed budget. Guided by shrunk errors from completed development weeks and the current disagreement among candidates, a first gate mixes this factor reference with a shape reference from similar sales histories, and a second gate controls how strongly the mixture corrects a backbone forecast. Both gates remain fixed during testing. On Favorita, M5, Rossmann, and Rohlik, FARE reduces the weighted absolute percentage error (WAPE) of five backbones by 11.46%, 4.06%, 14.63%, and 10.16% on average. With Chronos-2 frozen, FARE also outperforms conventional and existing enhancement methods, including supervised residual correction and retrieval augmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/HousonGu/FARE .

</details>


### [374] [Closing the Log Gap: Minimax-Optimal Regret for Episodic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40165)
*Guy Zamir,Victor Boone,Matthew Zurek,Yudong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究片段式表格MDP中的遗憾最小化，首次给出期望遗憾界 $\sqrt{H^3 S A K}$（不含量化多对数因子），与极小极大下界匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 UCBVI 类算法虽能接近极小极大下界，但结果中含有额外的 polylog 因子，尚未实现无额外对数项的匹配，因此需要新的算法与分析工具。

Method: 提出 UCBVI 的变体：不维持严格乐观的估计，改用 MOSS 风格探索奖励，其对数项同时适配访问计数与估计方差；并以占据度加权的方式控制"欠乐观"所导致误差的传播。分析中发展了时间一致（time-uniform）与方差感知的集中不等式。

Result: 在 $K$ 足够大时取得首个 $\sqrt{H^3 S A K}$ 的期望遗憾界，首个无需额外 polylog 因子即匹配极小极大下界；作为推论给出 $(\varepsilon,\delta)$-PAC 最优策略识别算法，在某些参数区间内首个在最优常数意义下匹配 $\Omega(H^3 SA\log(1/\delta)\varepsilon^{-2})$ 下界。

Conclusion: 放松严格乐观性、配合 MOSS 型方差自适应奖励与占据度加权误差控制，可在表格MDP中实现无 polylog 损失的近最优遗憾与 PAC 识别，相关集中不等式具有独立价值。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the regret minimization problem in episodic tabular MDPs. Our algorithm achieves the first expected regret bound scaling as $\sqrt{H^3 S A K}$ for sufficiently large $K$, where $H$ is the episode length, $K$ is the number of episodes, and $S$ and $A$ are the numbers of states and actions, respectively. Our method is the first to match the minimax lower bound \emph{without extra polylog factors}. We introduce a variant of UCBVI which does not maintain strictly optimistic estimates, and uses MOSS-style exploration bonuses whose logarithmic terms adapt to both visit counts and estimated variances. A key challenge is controlling the propagation of errors caused by under-optimism, which we accomplish in an occupancy-weighted way, avoiding logarithmic overhead that would be incurred by uniform-over-rows concentration. Our analysis develops time-uniform and variance-aware concentration bounds that may be of independent interest. As a corollary we also develop an algorithm for $(\varepsilon, δ)$-PAC best-policy identification, which is the first to match the $Ω\big(H^3 SA\log(\frac{1}δ)\varepsilon^{-2}\big)$ minimax lower bound up to constants in some regimes.

</details>


### [375] [Learned Terminal Costs for Short-Horizon Model Predictive Control of Autonomous Trucks in Interactive Highway Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40257)
*Hooman Hamze,Benjamin Houshmand,Deepthi Pathare,Erik Börve,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani,Nikolce Murgovski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPC与强化学习混合框架：用预训练RL模型估计有限时域MPC的终端代价，在重卡高速自动驾驶中实现零碰撞且总代价最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重型卡车质量大、尺寸大、动力学复杂，关键机动高度依赖周围交通配合，需在安全约束与长时域高效决策间权衡。纯MPC受限于需短时域以保证计算可行，终端代价项缺省削弱其效果；纯RL具备长时决策能力但无约束保证，且在稀疏碰撞惩罚下易产生过度保守策略。

Method: 构建MPC-RL混合框架：有限时域MPC通过动态规划求解，其终端代价由预训练RL模型估计，从而兼顾约束满足与长时域决策视野。在含反应式周围交通的仿真环境中评估。

Result: 与零终端代价的纯MPC、基于基础策略进行终端代价估计的rollout方法、以及纯RL相比，混合方法实现零碰撞，且在所有配置中总代价最低。

Conclusion: 以RL模型充当MPC终端代价估计器，可有效弥补短时域MPC的短视性与纯RL的约束缺失/保守性，为重卡高速自动驾驶提供安全且高效的决策方案。

Abstract: Autonomous highway driving for heavy-duty trucks is a challenging control problem. The greater mass, dimensions and intricate dynamics increase the requirements for cooperation from surrounding traffic in critical maneuvers, requiring a careful balance between safety constraints and efficient long-horizon decision making. Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides rigorous constraint satisfaction but relies on terminal cost which can limit its effectiveness at short, computationally affordable horizons. Reinforcement Learning (RL) captures long-term driving behavior but offers no constraint guarantees and tends toward overly conservative policies when trained under sparse collision penalties. In this paper, we propose a hybrid MPC and RL framework for highway driving in autonomous trucks, where a finite-horizon MPC, solved via dynamic programming, uses a pre-trained RL model for terminal cost estimation. We evaluate the framework in a simulation environment with reactive surrounding traffic and compare it against pure MPC with zero terminal cost, rollout with terminal cost estimation using a base policy, and pure RL. Results show that the hybrid approach achieves zero collisions with the lowest cost of any configuration.

</details>


### [376] [From MS/MS evidence to adaptive spectroscopic characterization: Reinforcement learning guided by candidate molecular structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40264)
*Bowen Gao,Lei Zhu,Yiying Wang,Wenjie Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构信息驱动的深度Q网络（SI-DQN），利用现有MS/MS证据决定是否需要进一步表征、请求哪种测量以及何时停止，在保持高识别准确率的同时将额外测量次数降低约92%–94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小分子串联质谱（MS/MS）鉴定常需互补测量（IR、NMR）才能确认结构，但每次都测量成本高。需要一种策略在"鉴定性能"与"后续测量代价"之间做定量权衡，并利用MS/MS后候选结构之间的歧义来指导是否继续测量。

Method: 提出SI-DQN策略：状态由谱图表示、候选排序得分，以及候选化学特征与结构差异的得分加权描述符共同构成；在固定硬候选集（含正确结构）和等归一化采集成本设定下训练强化学习策略，输出"停止/请求哪种测量"的决策。在Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset上以模拟MS/MS、IR、NMR光谱的79,462个留出样本评估，并与规则策略和纯DQN对比。

Result: 三次训练平均：正离子起始Top-1从80.10%升至93.78%，负离子从70.44%升至93.31%，平均额外测量次数仅0.227和0.340；完整采集（4次额外测量）仅达97.72%，而SI-DQN将额外测量数减少94.3%和91.5%。相对规则策略，复合损失（终端排序误差+采集成本）下降19.3%和25.5%。加入结构信息在所有三次训练中均提升准确率并减少请求；在结构分歧大的组中，同等排序不确定性下请求率提升更明显且复合损失降幅更大。

Conclusion: 利用MS/MS后候选结构之间的歧义指导后续表征是有效的，SI-DQN为平衡鉴定性能与后续测量投入提供了定量依据。

Abstract: Small-molecule identification from tandem mass spectrometry (MS/MS) may require complementary measurements. Herein, we propose a structure-informed deep Q-network (SI-DQN) that uses existing MS/MS evidence to decide whether further characterization is needed, which measurement to request, and when to stop. The policy combines spectral representations and candidate rankings with score-weighted descriptors of the candidates' chemical features and structural differences. The policy was evaluated on 79,462 held-out samples with simulated MS/MS, infrared (IR), and nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectra from the Multimodal Spectroscopic Dataset. Evaluation used fixed hard candidate sets containing the correct structure and equal normalized acquisition costs. Averaged across three training runs, SI-DQN increased Top-1 accuracy from 80.10% to 93.78% for positive-ion MS/MS starts and from 70.44% to 93.31% for negative-ion starts. The corresponding mean additional measurement counts were only 0.227 and 0.340 per sample, respectively. Complete acquisition achieved 97.72% accuracy with four additions, whereas SI-DQN reduced the additional measurement count by 94.3% and 91.5%. Relative to a rule-based policy, SI-DQN reduced the composite loss combining terminal ranking error and acquisition cost by 19.3% and 25.5%. Adding structural information to DQN further improved accuracy while reducing requests across all three training runs. At comparable ranking uncertainty, SI-DQN increased request rates relative to DQN more strongly in groups with high structural disagreement, where larger reductions in composite loss were also observed. Together, these results support using the ambiguity among candidate structures after MS/MS analysis to guide further characterization, providing a quantitative basis for balancing identification performance against subsequent measurement effort.

</details>


### [377] [Latent Prediction Yields Cross-Subject Alignable Representations in Sensory-Evoked fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40280)
*Serwan Jassim,Victoria Bosch,Daniel Anthes,Elia Bruni,Tim Kietzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）改造为自监督学习框架，仅用fMRI数据训练被试特异编码器，在脑字幕生成与图像重建任务上达到与参数丰富的监督方法相当的性能，并能无监督对齐不同被试的编码器实现跨被试解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码感觉诱发的fMRI数据是认知神经科学的核心分析工具，而自监督学习（SSL）在该领域探索极少。现有高性能解码流程依赖图像和语言数据端到端监督训练，代价高且难以跨被试、跨数据集整合数据。

Method: 将联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）适配到fMRI领域，仅以脑数据训练被试特定的编码器；编码器完全不接触刺激图像或任何由其导出的表征。在Natural Scenes Dataset（NSD）上评估所学脑嵌入在下游任务（脑字幕生成、图像重建）及跨被试泛化中的表现，并进一步探索独立训练的各被试编码器之间的完全无监督对齐，对齐后将一名被试的神经解码头直接迁移至另一被试。

Result: 自监督方法在脑字幕生成与图像重建上与使用图像和语言数据端到端训练的、参数丰富的监督流程性能相当；独立训练的被试编码器可在不使用图像、不需被试间共享刺激的条件下实现完全无监督对齐；对齐后，一名被试训练的解码头无需微调即可应用于另一被试，并在图像重建与脑字幕生成上保留大部分被试内性能。

Conclusion: 去除对图像与共享刺激的依赖，使该方法朝着跨被试乃至跨数据集汇聚fMRI数据迈进一步。

Abstract: Decoding sensory-evoked functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data is a central analytical tool in cognitive neuroscience. Here we ask whether self-supervised learning (SSL), which has been scarcely explored in this domain, can yield representations that support strong downstream performance. To do so, we adapt a joint embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) to train participant-specific encoders on brain data alone. The encoders never see the stimulus images or any representation derived from them. Applied to the Natural Scenes Dataset (NSD), we evaluate our learned brain embeddings on the downstream tasks of brain captioning and image reconstruction, as well as cross-participant generalization. Our self-supervised approach achieves competitive performance with parameter-rich supervised pipelines that are trained end-to-end with access to image and language data. We further find that encoders trained independently for different participants can be aligned fully unsupervised, using neither images nor stimuli shared between them. After alignment, a neural decoding head trained on one participant can be applied to another without fine-tuning and retains most of its within-subject performance on image reconstruction and brain captioning. By removing the need for images and shared stimuli, our approach is a step towards pooling fMRI data across participants and, eventually, across datasets.

</details>


### [378] [DolphinRouting: Cluster-Level Binary Routing for Disaggregated LLM Inference Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40292)
*Yishuo Yuan,Shaoyu Yang,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DolphinRouting：面向 PD 分离式 LLM 服务的请求级路由策略，通过聚类 + 离线策略搜索 + 在线查表，基于端到端 SLO 反馈学习"本地 prefill / 远程 prefill"二元决策，在真实集群上降低 P90 TTFT 与 P90 TPOT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PD（prefill-decode）分离部署虽隔离了两阶段，但请求级路径选择需同时权衡 prefill 工作量、KV cache 复用情况与 decode 负载，简单启发式难以在给定 P/D 资源配置下满足端到端 SLO。

Method: 将请求按原始 prompt 长度、两个 worker 池上经 cache 调整后的输入长度、以及输出长度信息进行聚类；在聚类粒度上以端到端 SLO 反馈为目标做离线策略搜索，为每个簇学得一个二元动作；在线阶段执行特征抽取、簇分配与查表，决定本地或远程 prefill（k-means 为默认聚类方式）。

Result: 在真实 PD 分离集群上，使用三个公开基准、混合负载与真实数据评测；在混合负载与真实数据上，相对 Dynamo，k-means 版 DolphinRouting 将 P90 TTFT 降低 5.7–7.9%，P90 TPOT 降低 2.6–10.1%。

Conclusion: 以 SLO 反馈驱动的簇级查表路由是一种轻量、可落地且有效的 PD 分离请求调度方案；聚类特征需显式纳入 cache 调整长度与输出长度信息。

Abstract: Prefill-decode (PD) disaggregation isolates the two phases of large language model serving, but request-level path selection must account for prefill work, cache reuse, and decode demand. We present DolphinRouting, which learns this choice from end-to-end SLO feedback under a given P/D resource allocation. Requests are clustered by raw prompt length, cache-adjusted input lengths on both worker pools, and output-length information. Offline policy search learns one binary action per cluster. Online feature extraction, cluster assignment, and table lookup select local or remote prefill. We evaluate DolphinRouting on a real PD-disaggregated cluster using three public benchmarks, a mixed workload, and real data. Across the mixed workload and real data, DolphinRouting with $k$-means reduces P90 TTFT by 5.7--7.9\% and P90 TPOT by 2.6--10.1\% relative to Dynamo.

</details>


### [379] [Causal-prior-constrained deep learning for rapid-intensification warning of western North Pacific tropical cyclones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40304)
*Xinrui Li,Yijun Jin,Yifan Dong,Shoujuan Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalVisionRI：融合红外遥感影像、大气海洋场与观测发现的因果图的因果先验约束深度学习系统，用于西北太平洋24小时快速增强（RI）预警，在2020–2024独立留出集上五成员集成BSS达0.259，显著优于四个参考系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RI是热带气旋最危险且最难预测的阶段，其环境与结构驱动因子在观测中难以识别与分离，现有方法缺乏可审计的物理因果依据。

Method: 构建因果先验约束的深度学习系统：整合红外遥感影像、大气与海洋场以及一个观测发现的因果图；因果图基于2000–2017年522个气旋发现，并给出8个经符号验证的24小时强度变化效应作为可审计因果先验；采用五成员集成，与LDA基线、知识块（knowledge-block）方案、对比学习重建方案对比，并进行消融实验。

Result: 在2020–2024年3004条记录的独立前向留出集上，五成员集成BSS=0.259（相对固定发展气候态），显著超过四个参考系统（三个LDA基线最高仅0.105）；在美国30-kt标准与已发表RITCF标准下，匹配误报率时检测率均高于知识块方案与对比学习方案；消融显示因果图通路在留出集上提升BSS 0.032，2020–2021年提升0.063，并在一个集成成员中贡献显著。

Conclusion: 由观测发现的因果结构约束的深度学习模型为物理可解释的RI预警提供了新途径。

Abstract: Rapid intensification (RI) is one of the most dangerous and unpredictable phases of tropical cyclones, and its environmental and structural drivers are difficult to identify and separate in observations. Here we introduce CausalVisionRI, a causal-prior-constrained deep-learning system for 24-h RI warning in the western North Pacific. It integrates infrared remote sensing imagery, atmospheric and oceanic fields, and an observationally discovered causal graph into a unified model. The graph and eight sign-verified effects on 24-h intensity change provide an auditable causal prior for training. Discovered from 522 cyclones in 2000-2017, it identifies causal pathways linking atmospheric and oceanic conditions, the cyclone's dynamical and structural state, and its 24-h intensity change. On a forward holdout covering 2020-2024 and 3,004 records, the five-member ensemble achieves a Brier skill score (BSS) of 0.259 relative to a fixed development climatology, significantly exceeding four reference systems; the three LDA baselines reach at most 0.105. It also attains higher detection rates than a published knowledge-block scheme and a reconstructed contrastive-learning scheme at matched false-detection rates under our USA 30-kt standard and the published RITCF standard. Ablations show that the graph pathway adds 0.032 BSS across the holdout and 0.063 over 2020-2021, with a significant contribution in one ensemble member. A deep-learning model constrained by observationally discovered causal structure thus offers a new route to physically informed RI warning.

</details>


### [380] [How to Loop Your Transformer: Loop Scaling, Specialization, and Parallel Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40325)
*Chanakya Ekbote,Artidoro Pagnoni,Devendra Singh Sachan,Shruti Bhosale,Mike Lewis,Maryam Fazel-Zarandi,Shashank Rajput,Sujan Kumar Gonugondla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统研究稠密解码器Transformer中的循环（weight reuse）设计：循环全模型在固定参数量下质量最佳，循环中间层块在训练FLOPs受限时回报更高；加入循环专属LoRA、改进并行循环的隐状态传递可进一步提升质量并缓解解码降速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环（重复应用同一Transformer或其部分）可在不增加参数量下加深模型，但其价值取决于受限资源是模型规模、训练算力还是自回归推理效率；此前工作未联合考察这些权衡，也不清楚"每层循环共享权重"是否限制了深层循环的收益。

Method: 从头训练稠密only-decoder Transformer，联合变化三要素：循环哪个部分（全模型 vs 中间层块）、循环次数、循环间信息传递方式。规模最大1B参数、155B tokens、1.07e21 FLOPs、最多32次循环。提出：(1) 为每次循环添加小型循环专属低秩适配器；(2) 改进Parallel Loop Transformer，在相邻token位置间传递隐状态以并行执行各循环阶段，并保留当前token嵌入、对其与前一token隐状态分别归一化后再融合。

Result: 固定参数预算下循环全Transformer质量最强；训练FLOPs受限时循环中间层块回报更好。增加循环并非单调有益：增益会饱和、反转或不稳定。循环专属低秩适配器提升质量，且循环越多增益越大。普通循环存在与循环次数成正比的解码降速；原版Parallel Loop Transformer不能稳定保留普通循环的质量增益，而所提出的嵌入保留+分别归一化方案在各模型规模与训练预算下恢复强性能。

Conclusion: 有效的循环设计需与资源约束和推理场景相匹配：参数受限选全模型循环，算力受限选中间层块循环，并辅以循环专属适配器；推理并行化需修正并行循环的信息传递与归一化方式。

Abstract: Looping in transformer models reuses weights by repeatedly applying the same transformer, or parts of it, making the model deeper without increasing its parameter count. Whether looping is useful depends on which constraint dominates: model size, training compute, or autoregressive inference efficiency. We study these trade-offs jointly in dense decoder-only transformers trained from scratch, varying which parts of the model are looped, how many loops are applied, and how information is passed between loops. Our experiments span models with up to 1B parameters, trained on up to 155B tokens with up to $1.07\times10^{21}$ FLOPs and 32 loops. We find that looping the full transformer generally gives the strongest quality at a fixed parameter budget, whereas looping a middle block of layers gives a better return when training FLOPs are constrained. Adding loops is not uniformly beneficial: gains eventually saturate, reverse, or become unstable. This motivates asking whether forcing every loop to use the same weights limits the benefit of deeper looping. We address this by adding small loop-specific low-rank adapters that improve quality, with larger gains as more loops are added. Looped transformers also suffer a decoding slowdown proportional to the number of loops. Parallel Loop Transformers address this by passing hidden states between loops across adjacent token positions, allowing loop stages to run concurrently. However, in our experiments the original formulation does not consistently retain the quality gains of ordinary looping. We find that preserving the current-token embedding and separately normalizing it and the previous-token hidden state before combining them recovers strong performance across model scales and training budgets. Together, these results show that effective looping requires matching the design to the resource constraint and inference setting.

</details>


### [381] [Factorized Representations of Concurrent Temporal Variables in Pretrained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40328)
*Xinhe Zhang,Arnau Marin-Llobet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 语言模型在文档内可独立维持多个上下文变量；本文用受控文档揭示联合状态由可选择性访问、近似可加的重叠子空间几何组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作识别了单个时间或上下文量的结构化表示，但这些变量在联合层面如何组织（是否为因子化、是否共享子空间）尚不清楚。

Method: 构造带独立相位的"语言切换计时器"和"话题变更计时器"的受控文档；在十个预训练检查点上做线性可解码性分析、移除计时器相关质心平面的干预实验、平面间夹角与跨计时器信息测量，并在3–51个计时器取值及Qwen3 0.6B–14B范围内做稳健性检验，同时考察调度位置对续写概率及质心位移对输出的影响。

Result: 两个计时器均可从同一隐状态中线性解码；移除某一计时器的质心平面会显著削弱对应变量的可访问性，而对同期另一计时器影响很小，尽管两平面非正交且互相含有跨计时器信息；各计时器独立贡献可解释联合质心间大部分可复现变异；这些特征在3–51个取值及0.6B–14B的Qwen3检查点上持续存在；调度位置影响续写概率，但所测试的质心位移未产生一致的计时器特定输出效应。

Conclusion: 结果支持基于选择性可访问性的近似因子化，以及重叠子空间中近似可加的联合状态几何，即多上下文变量以叠加而非严格正交的模块化方式组织。

Abstract: Language models often need to maintain multiple contextual variables that evolve independently within a document. Prior work has identified structured representations of individual temporal or contextual quantities, but their joint organization remains less understood. We study this question using controlled documents with independently phased language-switch and topic-change timers. Across ten pretrained checkpoints, both timers are linearly decodable from the same hidden states. Removing a timer-associated centroid plane substantially reduces access to its associated variable while having little effect on the concurrent timer, although the planes are nonorthogonal and contain cross-timer information. Separate timer contributions also explain most reproducible variation among joint-state centroids. These signatures persist from 3 to 51 timer values and across Qwen3 checkpoints from 0.6B to 14B. Schedule position affects continuation probabilities, but the tested centroid displacement produces no consistent timer-specific output effect. Together, these results support approximate factorization based on selective accessibility and approximately additive joint-state geometry in overlapping subspaces.

</details>


### [382] [A Unified Hessian-Guided Framework for Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40345)
*Mads Toftrup,Marco Federici,Boris van Breugel,Paul Whatmough,Markus Nagel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Hessian/Fisher近似的统一后训练量化框架，联合优化量化范围、线性变换与权值舍入，以输出敏感度为目标，在通道级与块级量化设定下均优于现有变换与舍入方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低位量化需同时决定量化范围、线性变换与权值舍入，但现有方法用局部重构目标各自独立优化这些组件，未考虑量化误差对下游预测的不同影响；直接优化任务级指标虽更忠实，却受量化操作不可微与下游评估代价高昂所限。

Method: 推导权值与激活联合量化引起输出变化的Hessian引导近似，并基于可处理的Fisher近似构造统一的后训练量化框架，在共同的输出敏感度目标下联合优化量化范围、线性变换与权值舍入。

Result: 该方法对现代大语言模型保持实用性，在通道级和块级量化两种设定下均一致优于最先进的变换与舍入方法。

Conclusion: 以输出敏感度为统一目标可克服局部重构目标的偏差，Hessian/Fisher引导的联合优化为低位PTQ提供了可行且更有效的方案。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization enables efficient deployment of large language models by reducing memory footprint and computational cost. Achieving high accuracy at low precision requires selecting quantization ranges, linear transformations, and weight rounding decisions. Existing approaches typically optimize these components independently using local reconstruction objectives, which fail to account for the varying impact of quantization errors on downstream predictions. Direct optimization of task-level metrics would provide a more faithful objective, but is challenging due to the discontinuous nature of quantization operations and the high computational cost of evaluating their downstream effects.
  In this work, we derive a Hessian-guided approximation of the output change induced by joint weight and activation quantization. Based on a tractable Fisher approximation, we formulate a unified post-training quantization framework that enables the joint optimization of quantization ranges, linear transformations, and weight rounding under a common output-sensitivity objective. The resulting method remains practical for modern large language models and consistently outperforms state-of-the-art transformation and rounding methods across both channel-wise and block-wise quantization settings.

</details>


### [383] [Your LR Schedule May Not Be Principled: Effective Learning Rate Scheduling in Weight-Normalized Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40362)
*Qifan Yu,Xinyu Ma,Xingyan Bin,Di He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WN训练的预训练增益主要源于其隐含的有效学习率(ELR)轨迹而非归一化本身；匹配ELR可使WN与WD的训练损失动力学基本重合。据此提出以ELR为调度对象的新调度AIR（仿射调整的$1/\sqrt{t}$衰减），在128B token MoE预训练中优于余弦调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现权重归一化(WN)相对权重衰减(WD)能显著降低预训练损失，但增益来源不明。作者假设增益来自WN诱导的有效学习率(ELR)轨迹而非归一化操作本身，因此学习率调度设计应以ELR而非名义学习率为目标。

Method: （1）通过匹配ELR轨迹对比WN与WD的训练损失动力学，检验增益来源；（2）基于理论推导的$1/\sqrt{t}$衰减，结合仿射调整以满足有限训练周期内预设的峰值与终端学习率，提出AIR调度；（3）分析晚期ELR调度作为增益关键来源的作用机制（共享晚期调度会削弱早期LR选择的影响）；（4）将同一ELR轨迹应用于WD以验证结论的可迁移性。

Result: 匹配ELR轨迹后，WN与WD的训练损失动力学大幅重合；AIR在128B token的MoE预训练中，在训练损失、下游性能与超参数鲁棒性上均优于余弦及其他候选调度，在匹配累积学习率条件下终端训练损失较余弦降低约0.01；将相同ELR轨迹用于WD可复现相应的训练动力学。

Conclusion: ELR是有原则的调度对象，WN的收益主要来自ELR轨迹而非固定范数训练；AIR所代表的ELR导向调度将收益推广至WD，为预训练学习率调度设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Weight-normalized (WN) training is emerging as a compelling alternative to conventional weight decay (WD), with recent studies reporting substantial pretraining loss gains. We show that these gains are driven primarily by the induced effective learning rate (ELR) trajectory rather than by normalization alone. Matching ELR trajectories largely collapses the training loss dynamics of WN and WD. Schedule design should therefore target ELR rather than the nominal learning rate (LR) alone. Building on this principle, we propose AIR (Affine Inverse-square-Root decay), which combines a theoretically derived $1/\sqrt{t}$ decay with an affine adjustment to satisfy prescribed peak and terminal LRs over a finite training horizon. We further identify late-stage ELR scheduling as a key source of its gains, because a shared late schedule attenuates the effect of earlier LR choices. Experiments on 128 B-token Mixture-of-Experts pretraining demonstrate that AIR outperforms cosine and other candidate schedules in training loss, downstream performance, and hyperparameter robustness, reducing terminal training loss by approximately 0.01 relative to cosine at matched cumulative LR. Applying the same ELR trajectory to WD reproduces the corresponding training dynamics, showing that the benefits extend beyond fixed-norm training and establishing ELR as the principled scheduling object.

</details>


### [384] [Is It the Curriculum or Just the Easy Start? Benchmarking Unsupervised Environment Design on Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40364)
*Jakub Gilewicz,Dominik Galus,Piotr Wojdas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一基准，在MuJoCo MJX的五个3D四足机器人上以相同协议比较十四种UED、域随机化与机器人课程方法，并给出按机器人选课程的指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UED方法（PLR、ACCEL、SFL、PAIRED）主要在2D迷宫、栅格世界和BipedalWalker上比较，而足式机器人的地形课程多为手工设计、仅与少数基线对照，缺乏跨方法、跨机器人的统一评测。

Method: 构建基准：五个3D四足机器人在MuJoCo MJX中运行（闭链W01-TEK；开链Unitree Go1、Go2、Google Barkour vB、Boston Dynamics Spot）；层级表示为八个可解释地形参数的向量；十四种UED、域随机化和机器人课程方法在同一协议下驱动同一PPO学生，环境步预算对齐。评测指标包含随机及留出结构化地形上的成功率、带分层bootstrap区间的四分位均值、能力包络及逐层级支配比较；在每个机器人和每个层级集内做方法间比较，并按层级集报告不显著低于最优方法的顶级方法组。

Result: 没有方法能在所有机器人和所有层级集上都进入顶级组。带有简单或低难度训练层级的方法（无论是否经人工整理）占据了Go1、Barkour的分布内随机层级的顶级组，W01-TEK除PAIRED+BiBC外亦然。源自足式机器人实践的“成功率驱动”地形课程进入顶级组最频繁；均匀层级上的域随机化从未进入顶级组；PAIRED在Spot以外从未进入顶级组。在三个机器人的分布内随机层级上，排名第一的方法并非留出地形上的第一。

Conclusion: 课程方法排序高度依赖机器人平台、层级集与分布内/分布外评测设定，不存在通用最优方法；应将评测结果汇总为按机器人选择课程的指南，并强调分布外结构化地形评测的必要性。

Abstract: Unsupervised environment design (UED) methods such as PLR, ACCEL, SFL and PAIRED are compared mostly on 2D mazes, grid worlds and BipedalWalker, while terrain curricula for legged robots are hand-designed and evaluated against a few baselines. We present a benchmark that runs fourteen UED, domain-randomisation and robotics curricula under one protocol on five 3D quadrupeds in MuJoCo MJX: the closed-chain W01-TEK and the open-chain Unitree Go1 and Go2, Google Barkour vB and Boston Dynamics Spot. Levels are vectors of eight interpretable terrain parameters, and all curricula drive the same PPO student at matched environment-step budgets. The evaluation reports success on random and held-out structured terrains, interquartile means with stratified bootstrap intervals, capability envelopes and a level-by-level domination comparison. We compare methods within each robot and report, per level set, the top group of methods not significantly below the best. No method is in the top group on every robot and level set. Methods with easy or low-difficulty training levels, curated or not, fill the top groups on in-distribution random levels of Go1 and Barkour, and of W01-TEK apart from PAIRED+BiBC. The success-driven terrain curriculum from legged-robot practice is in a top group most often, domain randomisation on uniform levels in none, and PAIRED in none outside Spot, where the fourteen methods lie within 3.8 points on random levels of difficulty 0.25. On three robots the leader on in-distribution random levels is not the leader on held-out terrain. We collect the results into a per-robot guide to choosing a curriculum.

</details>


### [385] [Composing and Steering Diffusion Models with Generalized Feynman-Kac Correctors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40383)
*Wael Mattar,Daniel Freedman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出广义Feynman–Kac校正器（GFKC），可在无需重训练的前提下，从多个预训练扩散模型的任意组合目标分布（含奖励倾斜）中正确采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用需要从并非单一预训练模型分布、而是由多个模型组合（并可能向下游奖励高值倾斜）定义的目标分布中采样。直接相加各模型的分数会得到错误样本，而现有校正方法仅适用于特定类型的目标分布。

Method: 将目标视为若干预训练扩散模型的任意函数；证明从任意初始函数到该目标函数的几何插值满足Feynman–Kac偏微分方程，并给出其势函数的闭式解；进一步推广对数概率ODE，使其在由不同速度场驱动的轨迹上追踪每个模型的密度，从而在生成过程中实时评估该势函数。算法完全在对数域中运行，无需额外概念成本即可并入基于奖励的引导，并在线校正生成轨迹。

Result: 在解析高斯模型上，采样结果与真实目标高度吻合；在MNIST训练的扩散模型上，能够从模型的非平凡组合中采样，并实现有效的奖励引导。

Conclusion: GFKC提供了一个有理论依据的统一框架，可对多个预训练扩散模型的任意组合目标进行正确且免训练的在线校正采样，并自然兼容奖励引导。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become the leading paradigm in generative modeling, yet many applications require sampling from distributions that are not those of any single pretrained model, but are instead defined through combinations of several models, possibly tilted toward high values of a downstream reward. Naively combining the scores of the models yields incorrect samples, while existing corrections are restricted to specific families of targets. In this work, we introduce the generalized Feynman-Kac correctors (GFKC), a principled framework for sampling from an arbitrary target function of several pretrained diffusion models. We show that the geometric interpolation between an arbitrary initial function and the target function of the models satisfies a Feynman--Kac partial differential equation, and derive its potential in closed form. To evaluate this potential during generation, we further generalize the log-probability ODE to track the density of each model along trajectories driven by a different velocity field. The resulting algorithm operates entirely in the log domain, incorporates reward-based steering at no additional conceptual cost, and corrects the generation on the fly, without retraining or fine-tuning the models. We validate the method on analytic Gaussian models, where it closely matches the ground-truth targets, and on diffusion models trained on MNIST, where it samples from non-trivial combinations of the models and enables effective steering.

</details>


### [386] [SCARL: Sequence Confidence-Aligned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40407)
*Zhenghong Ke,Jingbo Zhou,Hua Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出SCARL方法，通过将组内相对置信度与优势所认证结果之间的对齐差距注入优势塑形，解决RLVR中序列与置信度错位问题，从而提升推理性能并缓解熵坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR用规则验证器给采样回复打分并强化通过的样本，但策略对某回复的置信度与奖励之间没有任何耦合。分类器训练会把预测概率与标签耦合，而RLVR存在“序列-置信度错位”：优势对置信度不敏感，因此侥幸成功的回复与近乎确定成功的回复获得相同更新，高置信错误也不比探索性错误被更强纠正。这种错位还关联到侵蚀探索的熵坍缩问题。

Method: 提出SCARL（序列置信度对齐强化学习）。它不改变优势的符号，仅用“回复的组内相对置信度”与“该优势所认证的结果”之间的对齐差距重塑每个优势，并归一化为各类别优势预算中的份额。于是错位样本承担更新力度，同优势回复被推向相等置信度。该方法在某一类别置信度一致时可证明退化为原估计器，且不增加额外前向传播，仅新增一个超参数。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B-Base上，平均Pass@32较DAPO提升2.4分，在九种等算力方法中覆盖率与单次采样准确率均为最优；原样应用于四种估计器与四个骨干模型，16种设置中14种得到改善，同时保持策略熵并扩大置信度与正确性的分离度。

Conclusion: SCARL通过将置信度对齐引入优势塑形，使更新强度与样本错位程度挂钩，在提升推理性能的同时修复熵坍缩、增强置信度-正确性校准，且具有低开销与良好泛化性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves language-model reasoning by scoring sampled responses with a rule-based verifier and reinforcing those that pass. Where a classifier's training couples predicted probability to labels, nothing ties an RLVR policy's confidence in a response to its reward: aligned training would drive responses with the same advantage toward equal confidence, yet the advantage is blind to confidence. Under this sequence-confidence misalignment, a success the policy barely found earns the same update as one it produces with near certainty, and a confident error is corrected no harder than an exploratory one. We propose SCARL (Sequence Confidence-Aligned Reinforcement Learning), which reshapes, but never re-signs, each advantage with the alignment gap between the response's group-relative confidence and the outcome its advantage certifies, normalized into shares of each class's advantage budget. Misaligned samples absorb the force, responses with the same advantage are driven toward equal confidence, and repairing the misalignment doubles as a remedy for the entropy collapse that erodes exploration. SCARL provably reduces to its base estimator when a class's confidences agree, adds no extra passes and one hyperparameter. On Qwen3-4B-Base it lifts average Pass@32 by 2.4 points over DAPO and is best among nine compute-matched methods on both coverage and per-draw accuracy; applied unchanged across four estimators and four backbones, it improves 14 of 16 settings while preserving policy entropy and widening the confidence-correctness separation. Our code is available at https://github.com/kezhh163/scarl.

</details>


### [387] [IMPACT: Inverter-Informed Multimodal Learning for Photovoltaic Generation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40418)
*Anushka Katiyar,Cong Feng,Jing Wang,Tristan Liu,Simon Julien,Zachary Jacobs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出物理信息多模态深度学习框架，实现光伏功率10–110分钟超前概率预测，采用四分支架构与在线共形校准，FSS达0.2950，优于全部14个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超短期光伏功率概率预测需同时刻画电站时序历史、逆变器级空间云动态与太阳几何等物理信息，并在多变天空条件下保持可靠的分位数预报。

Method: 四分支架构：图注意力编码器（学习邻接）编码逆变器级空间云动态，另一支编码电站级时序历史，一支输入已知未来太阳几何，一支输入当前气象状态；通过门控交叉注意力融合。几何分支引入空间云异质性与逆变器降功率活动两个前向物理信号，并按各自最具预测性的时间尺度经验加权。解码器以智能持续性基线为锚，学习随预测步长变化的修正量；输出分位数经核加权自适应共形校准在线校准。

Result: 优于全部14个基线，FSS为0.2950，比次优模型高0.099；分支消融显示各分支均有正贡献；在阴天与多变天空条件下仍保持较强预测能力（FSS分别为0.198与0.255）。

Conclusion: 物理引导的多分支融合结合持续性锚定残差学习与在线共形校准，可有效提升多步超前光伏概率预测的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This work develops a physics-informed multimodal deep learning framework for 10- to 110-minute-ahead probabilistic photovoltaic power forecasting. The forecasting model is built on a four-branch architecture that separately encodes plant-level temporal history and inverter-level spatial cloud dynamics through a graph attention encoder with a learned adjacency, known-future solar geometry, and the current meteorological state, fused through gated cross-attention. The geometry branch adds two forward-looking physical signals, spatial cloud heterogeneity and inverter ramp-down activity, each empirically weighted toward the horizon where it is most predictive. The decoder anchors its prediction to a smart persistence baseline, learning a horizon-conditioned correction, and the resulting quantile forecasts are calibrated online through a kernel-weighted adaptive conformal calibration step. The proposed model outperforms all 14 baselines, achieving a forecast skill score (FSS) of 0.2950, 0.099 above the next-best model. A branch-composition ablation shows that every branch contributes positively and the model retains substantial skill in challenging overcast and variable sky conditions (FSS 0.198 and 0.255).

</details>


### [388] [NECA: Neighbor Event Curve Augmentation for Multi-Horizon ICU Deterioration Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40420)
*Chenyang Wang,Jiawei Theresa Tang,Xinran Liu,Xinyu Lu,Xulei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出与模型无关的NECA方法，检索早期窗口相似的训练集患者，将其后续器官功能障碍事件发生时间转换为6/12/24/42小时累积事件曲线特征，拼接原表征供下游预测器使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU器官功能障碍事件的早期预测有助于及时监测与干预，但早期记录测量不规律、临床变量异质、且需跨多个未来时间窗预测风险，导致短期器官特异性风险评估困难。现有监督模型通常将每个患者视为孤立样本，未显式利用相似历史患者的事件发生时间信息。

Method: NECA为model-agnostic方法：基于早期窗口profile检索训练集中相似患者，将其后续器官功能障碍事件起始时间转化为6h、12h、24h、42h的累积事件曲线特征；该特征与原患者表征拼接后输入标准下游预测器。在PhysioNet 2012与4,000条住院记录的MIMIC-IV子集上，跨4类器官功能障碍任务与7种监督学习器评估，并做消融分析。

Result: NECA在四类器官功能障碍任务和七种监督学习器上均提升平均PR-AUC与ROC-AUC。最大增益出现在神经系统功能障碍任务，PhysioNet 2012上任务级平均PR-AUC由0.461提升至0.557。消融显示具有临床意义的检索与分horizon的邻居风险贡献了大部分提升，完整累积曲线提供任务相关的时间上下文。

Conclusion: 相似患者的事件时序可作为结构化的时间风险信号，用于ICU器官功能障碍事件预测；该方法通用性强（模型无关），源代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: Early prediction of ICU organ dysfunction events supports timely clinical monitoring and intervention, but estimating short-term organ-specific risk from early ICU records remains challenging due to irregular measurements, heterogeneous clinical variables, and the need to predict risk across multiple future horizons. Existing supervised models usually treat each patient as an isolated sample and do not explicitly use the observed event timing of similar historical patients. We propose Neighbor Event-Curve Augmentation (NECA), a model-agnostic method that retrieves training-set patients with similar early-window profiles and converts their subsequent organ dysfunction event onset times into cumulative event-curve features over 6h, 12h, 24h, and 42h. These features are concatenated with the original patient representation and used by standard downstream predictors. On PhysioNet 2012 and a 4,000-stay MIMIC-IV subset, NECA improves average PR-AUC and ROC-AUC across four organ dysfunction event tasks and seven supervised learners. The largest gain appears for neurological dysfunction events, where task-level average PR-AUC increases from 0.461 to 0.557 on PhysioNet 2012. Ablations show that clinically meaningful retrieval and horizon-specific neighbor risk drive most of the improvement, while the full cumulative curve adds task-dependent temporal context. These results support similar-patient event timing as a structured temporal risk signal for ICU organ dysfunction event prediction. The source code will be publicly released upon acceptance of this paper.

</details>


### [389] [Uncertainty-aware Feedback Loop Diagnosis: integrating reinforcement learning and conformal prediction for reliable wafer defect classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40421)
*Aleksandr Dekhovich,Carlas Smith,Oleg Soloviev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出不确定性感知反馈闭环诊断算法UFLD，结合强化学习自适应获取测量与共形预测确定最优停止时机，实现高吞吐晶圆图缺陷检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造需高精度、低成本，晶圆图缺陷检测至关重要；但现有深度学习方法多需全表面扫描，阻碍高通量检测系统发展，且现有自适应方法依赖集成模型或额外的置信度估计网络，参数开销大。

Method: UFLD采用强化学习进行自适应测量采集，每一步后输出预测不确定性水平，信息充分即停止；不同于依赖集成模型或辅助置信度估计深度学习模型的做法，该方法使用参数更高效的共形预测框架来确定最优停止时间。

Result: 采集时间减少约40%，检测准确率达98.4%，且无需额外的学习型置信度估计网络；在MixedWM38数据集上验证，优于现有自适应采集流程与标准卷积神经网络。

Conclusion: UFLD以共形预测替代额外的置信度估计网络，在保证高检测精度的同时显著降低采集开销，为高通量晶圆缺陷检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Semiconductor manufacturing is a complex, multi-step process that requires both high precision and low operating costs. Therefore, detecting defects in a high-volume setting is an important component of wafer map analysis for reliable production. However, most state-of-the-art deep learning approaches require a full scan of the surface, which hinders the development of high-throughput inspection systems. To address this problem, we propose Uncertainty-aware Feedback Loop Diagnosis (UFLD), a novel algorithm that employs reinforcement learning for adaptive measurement acquisition and provides a prediction uncertainty level after each step, stopping the process once sufficient information is obtained. Unlike existing methods that rely on ensemble models or auxiliary confidence estimation deep learning models, UFLD utilizes a more parameter-efficient conformal prediction framework to determine the optimal stopping time. The proposed UFLD reduces acquisition time by about $40\%$ and achieves $98.4\%$ inspection accuracy, without requiring an additional learned confidence-estimation network. We validated our approach on the MixedWM38 dataset, demonstrating its advantages over existing adaptive acquisition procedures and standard convolutional neural networks.

</details>


### [390] [A Pretrained Model for Bayesian Filtering in Heterogeneous Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40437)
*Ke Fan,Wen Wu,Haitao Su,Yilin Wang,Yuan Zhou,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FilterFM 是首个面向贝叶斯滤波的预训练基础模型，用单一 checkpoint 在异构动力系统中估计隐状态，并首次实现零样本滤波与清晰的缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态估计局限于两类范式：需要已知动力学的在线贝叶斯滤波，或针对单一系统/参数族从头训练的神经滤波器。核心障碍是结构异质性——不同动力系统的控制方程、物理尺度与噪声水平各异，难以共享表示空间，因而无法构建可复用的通用滤波器。

Method: 提出 FilterFM：（1）分层两级观测序列编码器；（2）先验到后验的状态重建模块，二者共同应对异构系统间观测维度与状态维度的变化；（3）通过分位数预测建模状态不确定性。模型在异构滤波任务混合数据上预训练。

Result: 域内性能优于现有基线，在估计精度、运行时间效率和噪声鲁棒性上均有显著提升；是首个成功实现未见动力学零样本滤波的模型，弥合了状态估计的泛化鸿沟；揭示了基于基础模型的贝叶斯滤波存在清晰缩放行为，并结合全面的组件消融验证了设计的结构最优性。

Conclusion: FilterFM 将基础模型范式引入贝叶斯滤波，以单一 checkpoint 统一处理异构动力系统的状态估计，兼具域内性能优势、零样本泛化能力与可预测的缩放特性，拓展了该领域的研究边界。

Abstract: Filtering has not yet had its foundation-model moment: state estimation is still dominated either by online Bayesian filtering algorithms that require known dy- namics as inputs, or by learning-based neural filters trained from scratch for a single system or parametric family. The core challenge is structural heterogene- ity: dynamical systems differ in their governing dynamics, physical scales, and noise levels, precluding a shared representation space for a reusable filter. To bridge this gap, we introduce FilterFM, the first pretrained model for Bayesian filtering that estimates latent states across heterogeneous dynamical systems with a single checkpoint. FilterFM employs a hierarchical two-level observation se- quence encoder and a prior-to-posterior state-reconstruction module that together handle varying observation and state dimensions across heterogeneous systems, while modeling state uncertainty via quantile prediction. Trained on a mixture of heterogeneous filtering tasks, FilterFM exhibits superior in-domain performance, delivering significant gains in estimation accuracy, runtime efficiency, and noise robustness compared to existing baselines. Beyond in-domain performance, it marks the first model to successfully enable zero-shot filtering on unseen dynamics, bridging the generalization gap in state estimation. Furthermore, we uncover clear scaling behaviors in foundation-model-based Bayesian filtering, which combined with comprehensive component ablation studies, validate the structural optimality of our design and broaden the generalization scope for this research field.

</details>


### [391] [Framework-Conditional Open Materials Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40443)
*Maya M. Martirossyan,Hillary Pan,Philipp Höllmer,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OMatG-FC，一种以结构框架为条件、基于流匹配与随机插值的无机晶体材料de novo生成范式，在框架约束下大幅降低生成维度，实现S.U.N.率最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料生成的首要目标是稳定、唯一、新颖（S.U.N.）的结构，但现有数据挖掘式离子替换与完全de novo生成模型各有约束，且满足S.U.N.的预测结构多来自新化学组分映射到已知结构原型/框架，因此需更灵活地利用这一启发式洞见。

Method: OMatG-FC（Open Materials Framework-Conditional Generation）：以结构框架（晶胞形状与各粒子位置）为条件，对原子种类采用离散流匹配（discrete flow matching），对晶胞晶格体积缩放采用随机插值（stochastic interpolants），同时固定分数坐标与单位体积晶格矢量，从而相比无框架约束生成显著降低问题维度；并统计MP-20中唯一框架数量、揭示重复框架问题并提供框架划分版本。

Result: 在LeMat-GenBench基准上使用三种ML原子间势进行评估，取得凸包以下S.U.N.率的最优（state-of-the-art）表现；相比现有原型约束方法更灵活，能够从已知框架出发发现未见且稳定的新原型。

Conclusion: 以框架为条件的生成可在无需学习原子堆积的情况下高效实现材料发现，是连接原型约束与完全de novo生成的有效折中范式。

Abstract: Theoretical predictions of novel materials rely on a variety of techniques, from data-mining-based ion substitution methods to fully de novo generative models. Each approach imposes specific constraints and encounters unique challenges when extrapolating beyond the distribution of known materials to discover new ones. The foremost goal of materials generation is to produce stable, unique, and novel (S.U.N.) structures. Predicted structures that meet these criteria, however, typically result from novel chemical compositions mapped onto known structural prototypes or frameworks. We leverage this heuristic insight to present a new generative paradigm for the de novo prediction of inorganic crystalline materials constrained by their structural framework---i.e., the unit cell shapes and respective particle positions. Our method, termed OMatG-FC (Open Materials Framework-Conditional Generation), learns via a combination of discrete flow matching on the atomic species and stochastic interpolants for the volume scaling of the unit cell lattice, while fixing in-place the fractional coordinates and the unit-volume lattice vectors, dramatically reducing the dimensionality of the problem compared to unconstrained (framework-free) generation. Our method is more flexible than existing prototype-constrained methods, demonstrating capability for discovery of unseen, stable prototypes derived from known structural frameworks. We also investigate the number of unique frameworks in the MP-20 dataset, demonstrating the propensity of duplicate frameworks within the dataset and providing a framework-split version. We benchmark the performance of our model using the LeMat-GenBench suite using three ML interatomic potentials and achieve state-of-the-art S.U.N. rate below the convex hull, eschewing the need to learn atomic packings for materials discovery.

</details>


### [392] [Reinforcement learning for control of partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40449)
*Nicolò Botteghi,Matteo Tomasetto,Andrea Manzoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于强化学习（RL）的偏微分方程（PDE）控制方法，从统一视角梳理RL与PDE约束优化的联系，并讨论样本效率、泛化与可扩展性等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE控制的动力学系统具有高维状态空间、分布式驱动、非线性动力学和严格实时性约束，传统方法难以应对，而RL有望合成可实时部署、适应多变工况的反馈控制器，因此亟需系统性梳理该领域进展。

Method: 综述性研究：建立RL与PDE约束优化的统一视角，按无模型（model-free）、基于模型（model-based）和多智能体（multi-agent）RL三类方法对PDE控制进行归类与评述，重点关注样本效率、参数变化下的泛化性及高维问题的可扩展性；并以混沌系统镇定与流动控制两个示例说明RL智能体的实际表现与缺陷。

Result: 通过两个演示性案例（混沌系统镇定、流动控制）展示了RL智能体在实践中的性能表现及其潜在陷阱；同时讨论了评估与基准测试规范、现有局限性以及开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 面向科学计算的可信、数据高效且可扩展的RL仍处于发展阶段，需要规范的评估与基准体系，并在样本效率、泛化与高维可扩展性方面继续突破。

Abstract: Controlling dynamical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) remains a challenging task in scientific computing, owing to high-dimensional state spaces, distributed actuation, nonlinear dynamics, and stringent real-time constraints. Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for synthesizing feedback controllers that adapt to varying operating conditions and can be deployed efficiently in real-time. This review provides a unified perspective on RL and PDE-constrained optimization, and surveys model-free, model-based, and multi-agent RL approaches to PDE control, with particular emphasis on sample efficiency, generalization across parametric variations, and scalability to high-dimensional problems. Two demonstrative examples, the stabilization of a chaotic system and a flow control problem, illustrate the performance and pitfalls of RL agents in practice. Finally, we discuss evaluation and benchmarking practices, current limitations, and open research directions toward reliable, data-efficient, and scalable RL for scientific computing.

</details>


### [393] [Rural Readiness: Domain Generalization with Adversarially Chosen Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40468)
*Cynthia Dwork,Andisheh Ghasemi,Lunjia Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出「对抗性选择领域的域泛化」问题，刻画其样本复杂度，发现将竞争目标从100%源域放宽到99%后，样本复杂度由Ω(dk)显著降至O(d)，并将该洞察推广到元分布设定与目标域数据访问的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据丰富机构训练的预测模型部署到新人群（如数据稀缺的农村人群）时往往失效。作者希望借助数据丰富医院的通用知识，为数据稀缺人群构建临床诊断与决策算法，因此需要研究：学习器在任意未见目标域上的表现，能否接近与观测源域证据一致的最差假设。

Method: 形式化定义「对抗性选择领域的域泛化」：仅假设假设类中存在一个在所有k个源域上误差都小的「通用」预测器，在此条件下刻画该目标的样本复杂度（精确到对数因子），并分析其随VC维d的变化；随后将该结论应用于源域按「分布的分布」采样的元分布设定，并考察使用目标域标注数据的影响。

Result: 样本复杂度由Ω(dk)显著降低至O(d)——前提是对学习目标稍作放宽：允许学到的模型与在99%（而非100%）源域上表现良好的假设竞争。将这一洞察应用于元分布设定，得到相对已知上界的相应改进。此外表明，少量目标域标注样本并不能改善源域上的最坏情形样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 对学习目标的小幅放宽（99%而非100%源域）可使样本复杂度从随源域数线性增长降为仅依赖VC维，说明在数据稀缺人群的域泛化中「几乎处处」的通用性代价远低于严格通用性；同时，少量目标域标注数据无法替代源域样本。

Abstract: Predictive models trained at well-resourced data-rich institutions often fail when deployed in new populations. Motivated by the goal of building clinical diagnosis and decision-making algorithms for data-scarce rural populations using the general knowledge learned from data-rich hospitals, we formulate the fundamental question of \emph{domain generalization with adversarially chosen domains}, which asks a learner to perform on any unseen target domain nearly as well as the worst hypothesis consistent with the evidence from the observed source domains.
  Assuming only that the hypothesis class contains a "universal" predictor with small error on all $k$ source domains, we characterize the sample complexity of this objective up to logarithmic factors. Letting $d$ denote the VC dimension of the hypothesis class, we find, somewhat surprisingly, that the sample complexity reduces significantly from $Ω(dk)$ to $O(d)$ under a small relaxation of the learning goal in which, roughly speaking, we allow the learned model to compete with hypotheses that perform well on 99% instead of 100% of the source domains. By applying this insight to the meta-distributional setting, in which source domains are selected according to a distribution on distributions, we obtain a corresponding improvement over known sample complexity upper bounds. We also study the effect of data access to the target domain and show that a small labeled sample from the target domain does not improve the worst-case sample complexity on the source domains.

</details>


### [394] [Nonlinear Dynamical Systems as Scalable and Efficient Physical Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40471)
*Tyler King,Magnus Saebo,Jeremie Laydevant,Haoting Zhen,Maxwell G. Anderson,Tatsuhiro Onodera,Peter L. McMahon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过引入模型压缩等现代ML训练实践，基于非线性动力系统的物理神经网络在仿真中可在MNIST上达到99.2%准确率；以受MLP-Mixer启发的参数高效架构构建物理动力系统核心模块，CIFAR-10准确率可达86.4%（结合预训练与知识蒸馏为92.2%），参数量减少5倍以上，并估算出推理能效与速度可比数字电子神经网络提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进ML模型规模激增带来巨大算力需求，促使发展高能效硬件加速器；物理神经网络被视为潜在高能效加速器，但以往研究报告的推理准确率显著低于传统数字神经网络的最先进水平，因此需要缩小这一精度差距。

Method: 在仿真研究中，对基于非线性动力系统的多种候选物理神经网络采用现代ML训练实践（包括模型压缩）；并针对其中一种动力系统，将其作为受MLP-Mixer启发的参数高效架构的核心构建模块。

Result: MNIST手写数字识别准确率最高达99.2%；单一动力系统在CIFAR-10上准确率达86.4%（预训练加知识蒸馏后为92.2%），而未采用mixer架构的同一系统仅为64%；mixer方法在可训练参数量比基线少5倍以上的情况下取得该精度；回推估算表明推理能效与速度相较数字电子神经网络可能提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 现代ML训练实践与参数高效架构设计可大幅提升物理神经网络的推理精度，使其接近甚至媲美数字神经网络水平，并有望在能效与速度上实现数量级优势，为物理神经网络作为实用加速器提供了可行性依据。

Abstract: The rapid growth in the size of state-of-the-art machine-learning (ML) models has led to increasing compute demands, spurring the development of energy-efficient hardware accelerators. Physical systems trained to perform ML inference, known as physical neural networks, have been proposed as accelerators in part because of their potential to be highly energy efficient. However, the ML inference accuracies reported in most investigations of physical neural networks have been substantially below the state of the art for conventional digital neural networks. In this work, we show in simulation studies that by applying modern ML training practices, including model compression, various candidate physical neural networks based on nonlinear dynamical systems can achieve accuracies as high as 99.2% on MNIST handwritten-digit recognition. We also show for one dynamical system that using it as the core building block of a parameter-efficient ML architecture inspired by MLP-Mixers enables accuracies of up to 86.4% on CIFAR-10 image classification (92.2% with pretraining and knowledge distillation), compared with 64% for the same system without the mixer architecture. The mixer approach achieved its accuracy while using $>5\times$ fewer trainable parameters than the baseline. Finally, we present back-of-the-envelope estimates for inference energy efficiency and speed when using an example physical dynamical system, suggesting that orders of magnitude improvements may be possible over digital-electronic neural networks.

</details>


### [395] [Preserving Utility in Continual LLM Unlearning via Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40483)
*Juan Belieni,Ana Carolina Erthal,Eliezer de Souza da Silva,Diego Mesquita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出持续正交投影（COP），通过将每次遗忘更新的梯度限制在先前遗忘集激活子空间的正交补上，实现LLM的顺序/持续遗忘且互不干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法多为一次性移除设计，难以应对模型生命周期内持续发生的遗忘请求；顺序遗忘时后序更新会抵消先前删除或沿已退化方向推进，导致遗忘效果与模型效用急剧衰减。

Method: COP是一种梯度投影方案：为每层缓存先前遗忘集对应的激活子空间低秩基，并将新的遗忘更新梯度投影到该子空间的正交补上，从而为一阶意义上与先前删除的非干扰性提供可证明保证；仅需存储逐层低秩基，无需历史梯度或过往遗忘集，且作为插件可嵌入任何基于可微损失梯度更新的遗忘方法而不修改其目标函数。

Result: 在TOFU与RWKU标准基准上，COP在连续请求下保持效用的同时不削弱遗忘强度，避免了标准遗忘目标的性能崩溃，且比持续学习专用基线遗忘更有效。

Conclusion: 将遗忘更新约束在先验激活子空间的正交补中是缓解持续遗忘中更新干扰的有效途径，COP以低存储开销、方法无关的插件形式实现了可证明的一阶非干扰顺序遗忘。

Abstract: Unlearning concerns the task of removing the influence of a sensitive portion of the training data, such as protected, copyrighted or private text, from a model without retraining it from scratch. While unlearning requests happen continuously during a model's lifetime, the vast majority of LLM unlearning methods are ill-suited for iterated removal, yielding steep decay in forgetting and utility upon sequential unlearning. This decay stems from interference between successive updates, as each new deletion can undo earlier ones or push the model further along directions already degraded by them. To address this, we introduce Continual Orthogonal Projection (COP), a gradient-projection scheme that restricts each update to the orthogonal complement of activation subspaces cached from prior forget sets. This approach provably guarantees first-order non-interference with earlier deletions. COP stores only a low-rank basis per layer, requiring neither past gradients nor previous forget sets, and plugs into unlearning methods that update the model through gradient steps on a differentiable loss without modifying its objective. Experiments on standard LLM unlearning benchmarks (TOFU and RWKU) show that COP preserves utility across sequential requests without weakening forgetting, preventing the collapse of standard objectives and forgetting more effectively than continual-specific baselines.

</details>


### [396] [The Precision Wall: An Optimizer-Dependent Boundary for Optimization under Power-Law Measurement Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40485)
*Lngel Kyo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出幂律协方差下除法归一化优化器（如Adam）的失效源于数值精度墙，并给出临界指数预测公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩矩阵感知中，幂律测量协方差Σ=diag(j^{-s})虽不引入伪局部极小，但除法归一化一阶优化器在临界指数s*之上停滞，其机理不明。

Method: 在真值处分析最小非规范预期Hessian特征值的渐近阶V^{-(s+1)}D^{-s}，将其与优化器有效分母下界ε_O比较，推导归一化噪声压制稀有方向梯度的机制，并提出含检测滞后δ_O的边界公式s*_O(V,D)。以4组配置校准δ_Adam后冻结，在16格留出网格上检验。

Result: 该公式在全部16格（V∈{64,128,256,512}×D∈{4,8,16,32}）内以0.45误差预测Adam的起始点，可区分固定VD对，并能辨别分母精度墙与条件数墙、符号量化墙；D=2时失效，体现有限D谱交叉，且在ε_0=10^{-8}以下受动力学下限饱和。

Conclusion: 除法归一化优化器的失效可由曲率-精度竞争统一刻画，给出可检验的临界指数预测，并界定其适用边界（小D、极低精度）。

Abstract: In low-rank matrix sensing of a rank-$D$ factor $U^\ast \in \mathbb{R}^{V \times D}$, replacing isotropic Gaussian designs with power-law measurement covariance $Σ= \mathrm{diag}(j^{-s})_{j=1}^V$ leaves the Burer--Monteiro landscape free of spurious local minima, yet division-normalised first-order optimizers stall above a critical exponent $s^\ast$. We trace this failure to a numerical precision wall. At the ground truth, the smallest non-gauge expected Hessian eigenvalue scales at leading order as $V^{-(s+1)}D^{-s}$. When this curvature drops below the effective denominator floor $ε_O$ of optimizer $O$, normalisation noise overwhelms rare-direction gradients, yielding the leading-order boundary $s^\ast_O(V, D) = [\log(1/ε_O) - \log V]/\log(VD) + δ_O$, where $δ_O$ is a metric-dependent detection lag. With $δ_{\mathrm{Adam}}$ calibrated on four configurations and frozen, this formula predicts Adam's onset within $0.45$ across all 16 cells of a held-out grid ($V \in \{64,128,256,512\} \times D \in \{4,8,16,32\}$), separates fixed-$VD$ pairs, and distinguishes denominator-precision walls from conditioning and sign-quantisation walls. The approximation fails at $D = 2$, reflects a finite-$D$ spectral crossover, and saturates below $\varepsilon_0 = 10^{-8}$ at a dynamical floor.

</details>


### [397] [Numerical Integration on Bounded Domains via Learned Reflected Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40499)
*Kirill Korolev,Artur Goldman,Timofei Gritsaev,Tigran Ramazyan,Nikita Morozov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于反射扩散过程的学习式重要性采样方法，用于在有界凸域 D 上估计积分 Z=∫_D r(x)dx，由构造保证样本不越界，并给出无额外边界项的训练目标与无偏估计量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准QMC不针对被积函数自适应选点，重要性采样需要能刻画集中性的提议分布；已有学习式扩散采样器在 ℝ^d 上建模，无法满足 x∈D 的域约束。

Method: 构建由反射动力学生成的提议分布：反射扩散将目标分布逐步推向 D 上的均匀分布，神经网络学习分数模型以逆向该过程；利用反射扩散的变测度公式得到不含额外边界项的训练目标及 Z 的无偏重要性采样估计；对非光滑被积函数，在反射前的 Gaussian 提议密度上推导 REINFORCE 训练梯度估计，避免评估反射转移密度、避免对 r 求导。

Result: 在大量合成目标与真实问题上取得强性能，并随问题维度和蒙特卡洛点数呈现良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: 反射扩散提议为有界凸域上的高维积分提供了既满足域约束又可端到端学习的有效方差缩减方案，对非光滑被积函数同样适用。

Abstract: We study high-dimensional integration on bounded convex domains. Given a non-negative integrand $r$, the goal is to estimate $Z=\int_{D}r(x)\,\mathrm dx$ on $D\subset\mathbb R^d$. Standard Quasi-Monte Carlo does not adapt its evaluation points to the integrand, while effective importance sampling requires a proposal that captures its concentration. Learned diffusion samplers can supply such a proposal, but existing methods are formulated on $\mathbb R^d$ and do not respect the domain constraint $x\in D$. We instead build the proposal from reflected dynamics that stay in $D$ by construction. A reflected diffusion progressively transforms the target distribution toward the uniform distribution on $D$, and a neural score model is trained to reverse this process. A change-of-measure formula for reflected diffusions yields a training objective without additional boundary terms and an unbiased importance-sampling estimator of $Z$. For non-smooth integrands, where reparameterization gradients may be undefined or uninformative, we derive a REINFORCE estimator of the training gradient using Gaussian proposal densities before reflection, without evaluating reflected transition densities or differentiating $r$. We evaluate on numerous synthetic targets and real-world problems, demonstrating strong performance of the proposed method, as well as favorable scaling with the dimension of the problem and the number of points used for the Monte Carlo estimate.

</details>


### [398] [Machine Learning-Based Detection of Mobile Money Scan Messages in Ghana: A Comparative Evaluation of Naive Bayes, Support Vector Machine, and Randon Forest Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40544)
*Ibrahim Salifu,Muhaisin Tiyumba Nantogmah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一套有监督机器学习流水线，用于识别加纳移动货币（MoMo）短信中的诈骗信息与合法信息。基于220条人工标注语料（涵盖18个诈骗与合法子类），经领域特定归一化与TF-IDF（unigram+bigram）后，训练并比较朴素贝叶斯、线性SVM与随机森林，在五折分层交叉验证与20%留出集上评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoMo已成为加纳主流零售支付渠道（2025年12月交易额GHC 518.4亿、活跃账户超2670万），随之而来的是利用SMS/USSD冒充运营商、代理商、亲属或监管机构的社会工程诈骗，目的是骗取PIN、身份信息或资金。现有研究缺乏针对加纳MoMo短信的领域特化检测方案，且需要在低资源、端侧环境下可部署的轻量方法。

Method: 构建220条带标签的加纳MoMo短信语料（18个诈骗/合法子类）；先做领域特定归一化（货币金额、电话号码、USSD短码），再进行unigram与bigram的TF-IDF向量化；采用分层五折交叉验证加20%留出测试集，训练多项式朴素贝叶斯、线性SVM和随机森林三种分类器；评价指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率、F1、MCC与AUC-ROC。

Result: 留出集上朴素贝叶斯与线性SVM的准确率、F1和AUC均为1.00；交叉验证平均准确率分别为96.6%（±3.3）、94.9%（±4.6）和92.0%（±4.2）。具有判别力的n-gram中，诈骗类体现强制性催促使动词（"send""claim""please send"）与PIN索取（"PIN""pincode"）；合法类则出现确认性与警示性措辞（"balance""successful""do not share"）。

Conclusion: 可解释的经典分类器在结构化MoMo诈骗文本上取得较高准确率，适合在低资源、端侧场景下用于运营商或手机终端层面的过滤；但留出集1.00的结果反映小规模模板化语料的上限效应，未来需构建更大规模、自然化、多语言的语料以验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Mobile money (MoMo) is Ghana's preferred retail payment channel, recording GHC518.4 billion in transactions in December 2025 and over 26.7 million active accounts (Graphic Online, 2026). This growth has coincided with increased social-engineering fraud over Short Message Service (SMS) and Unstructured Supplementary Service Data (USSD), where fraudsters impersonate network operators, agents, relatives, or regulators to obtain personal identification numbers (PINs), identity data, or funds. We present a supervised machine-learning pipeline for classifying scam and legitimate Ghanaian MoMo SMS messages using a labelled corpus of 220 messages across 18 scam and legitimate subcategories. Domain-specific normalisation of currency amounts, phone numbers, and USSD codes preceded TF-IDF vectorisation of unigrams and bigrams. Using stratified five-fold cross-validation and a 20% held-out test set, we trained and evaluated Multinomial Naive Bayes, linear support vector machine (SVM), and Random Forest classifiers using accuracy, precision, recall, F1-score, Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), and area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC-ROC). Held-out accuracy, F1, and AUC were 1.00 for Naive Bayes and linear SVM; cross-validated accuracy averaged 96.6% (+/-3.3), 94.9% (+/-4.6), and 92.0% (+/-4.2). Discriminative n-grams reflected imperative urgency ("send," "claim," "please send") and PIN solicitation ("PIN," "pincode"); legitimate messages featured confirmatory and cautionary language ("balance," "successful," "do not share"). Findings are contextualised in Sub-Saharan African SMS fraud detection, small template-based corpus ceiling effects, and a pathway toward larger naturalistic multilingual corpora. Interpretable classical classifiers achieve high accuracy on structured MoMo scam text and suit carrier- or handset-level filtering in low-resource, on-device settings.

</details>


### [399] [Training Foundation Models with a Single Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40564)
*Yuda Song,Alvin Zhang,Fahim Tajwar,Daman Arora,Jeff Schneider,Daniel Khashabi,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Aarti Singh,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出预训练、midtraining、SFT 与 RL 的“分阶段＋换算法”并非必要，提出把各阶段都表示为对模型生成目标分布的极大似然拟合，从而归入统一的“unified training”算法，将训练生命周期转化为“在何时、用什么数据、以何种比例训练”的问题，并在单一 trainer 中混合静态数据与 RL 生成，实验显示更优的精度-算力权衡、任务覆盖与持续学习起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型按预训练→midtraining→SFT→RL 分阶段训练，阶段之间同时改变数据与学习算法，存在算法割裂、工程管线复杂、难以系统化权衡的问题。作者希望论证这种算法层面的分离并非本质，进而为实现统一的训练科学（连接数学原理、训练基础设施与能力发展）奠定基础。

Method: 通过对模型生成的目标分布做极大似然拟合，推导出相应的 RL 目标，使各训练阶段成为同一基于似然的训练算法的特例；由此把训练简化为数据选择、配比与时序安排问题，并把常规分阶段训练视为该框架的特殊情形。工程上实现了一个统一训练系统，在同一 trainer 中混合静态数据与 RL 生成。

Result: 在受控实验与真实场景实验中，unified training 改善了精度-算力（accuracy-compute）权衡并提升任务覆盖，同时保持更广泛的通用能力，并为持续学习提供更强的起点。

Conclusion: 分阶段训练中的算法分离并非根本性的；统一似然视角可将基础模型训练全流程纳入单一算法与系统，为“统一的训练科学”提供数学原理、训练基础设施与能力发展相衔接的理论与实证基础。

Abstract: Foundation models are typically trained in stages: pretraining, midtraining, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement learning (RL), with changes in both data and learning algorithms. We show that this algorithmic separation is not fundamental. By deriving suitable RL objectives as maximum-likelihood fitting to model-generated target distributions, we place all of these stages under a single likelihood-based training algorithm, unified training. This perspective turns the foundation-model training lifecycle into a problem of choosing which data to train on, in what proportions, and when, with conventional staged training as a special case. We instantiate this framework in a unified training system that mixes static data and RL generations within a single trainer. Across controlled and real-world experiments, unified training improves the accuracy-compute tradeoff and task coverage while preserving broader capabilities, and provides a stronger starting point for continual learning. This work lays the groundwork for a unified science of foundation-model training, connecting mathematical principles, training infrastructure, and the development of model capabilities.

</details>


### [400] [An ODE-based Sampler for Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40618)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus,Giacomo Zanella*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出指数残差流（ERFs），为离散扩散模型构造了类似连续情形概率流ODE的确定性采样框架，保持原扩散的边际分布，支持一致性蒸馏与加速采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型的边际分布可由确定性概率流ODE复现，这为加速采样与一致性蒸馏奠定基础；但离散扩散模型是否存在类似对应形式尚属未解问题。

Method: 为每个token与位置的离散状态增补一个连续残差变量，残差按分段定义ODE确定性演化，其向量场依赖当前离散状态及给定扩散模型的前向/反向速率矩阵；当某残差归零时离散状态发生跳变。作者证明该构造保持原扩散边际，并给出实际离散化采样器，特化到masked diffusion与uniform diffusion。

Result: 理论上证明了边际保持性质；ERFs的确定性轨迹支持一致性蒸馏，并在代码与文本生成等多项基准上取得相对先前方法的经验性提升。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型确实存在类似概率流ODE的确定性表述，ERFs为其提供了统一框架，兼具理论保证与实证加速收益。

Abstract: The marginals of a continuous diffusion model can be reproduced by a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), known as the probability flow ODE, which provides a foundation for accelerated sampling and consistency distillation. In this paper we address the question whether an analogous formulation exists for discrete diffusion models and answer it affirmatively by introducing exponential residual flows (ERFs). Our framework augments the discrete state with a continuous residual variable for each token and position. These residuals evolve deterministically according to piecewise-defined ODEs whose vector fields depend on the current discrete state and the forward and backward rate matrices of the prescribed diffusion model. The discrete state changes whenever a residual reaches zero. We prove that this construction preserves the marginals of the original diffusion. We derive a practical discretization of our sampler and specialize it to masked and uniform diffusion. The deterministic trajectories of ERFs support consistency distillation and yield empirical improvements over previous approaches across a variety of benchmarks including code and text generation tasks.

</details>


### [401] [world2vec: Mechanistic world models through latent dynamics learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40637)
*Tobias Schmidt,Rodrigo Gonzalez Laiz,Krisztián Szuppinger,Steffen Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 world2vec，无需奖励信号，直接从观测与动作中学习忠实的机制性世界模型，参数量比 DreamerV3、TD-MPC2、LeWM 少一个数量级以上，在像素连续控制任务上取得 SOTA，并在 71 个吸引子基准上实现准确系统辨识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型虽能获得较强控制性能，但并未忠实刻画环境动力学，因而难以用于机制性可解释性与科学发现；同时作者希望摆脱对奖励信息的依赖。

Method: 提出 world2vec：仅从观测和动作（无奖励）学习潜空间动力学，目标是在长预测时域上忠实复现真实系统轨迹，并以极紧凑的参数规模实现（相较 DreamerV3、TD-MPC2、LeWM 至少减少一个数量级）。

Result: 在基于像素的连续控制环境中达到 SOTA 性能；在包含 71 个吸引子的基准上实现准确的系统辨识。

Conclusion: 世界模型可以在保持高效控制的同时忠实还原系统动力学，从而具备支撑科学发现与机制性解释的潜力。

Abstract: World models can achieve strong control performance without faithfully capturing the dynamics of their environment, limiting their potential for mechanistic interpretability and scientific discovery. Here, we introduce world2vec to learn mechanistic world models without reward information directly from observations and actions. World2vec learns latent dynamics that faithfully reproduce the true system over long prediction horizons, with at least one order of magnitude fewer parameters than DreamerV3, TD-MPC2 and LeWM. This enables state of the art performance in controlling continuous environments from pixels and accurate system identification across a benchmark of 71 attractors, demonstrating how world models are amenable to scientific discovery.

</details>


### [402] [Rethinking Neural Thickets Without Weight Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40649)
*Nicolas Dufour,Elouan Gardès,Amil Dravid,Patrick Pérez,Loic Landrieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了“Neural Thickets”（随机高斯权重扰动可发掘任务专家邻域）的增益，但证明该增益不需要这种几何解释：仅扰动单个解码块即可，甚至完全冻结模型并给输出logits加随机偏置，在多数推理任务上就能匹配或超过全权重扰动。作者提出Perturb-Select-Vote（PSV）框架，用输出多样性+验证集选择+投票来解释，并在视觉域迁移（含对地观测）上验证logit PSV同样有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neural Thickets假设认为预训练语言模型位于权重空间中密集的任务专家邻域内，需用随机高斯扰动来发掘。作者质疑该几何解释是否为增益的必要条件，希望找到一个更简单、无需训练的替代解释与方案。

Method: 1) 复现原方法的增益作为基线；2) 消融：仅扰动单个解码块、冻结模型仅在输出logits上添加随机偏置；3) 形式化为Perturb-Select-Vote（PSV）通用流程：生成扰动预测器→在小验证集上挑选高性能成员→聚合预测；4) 一阶分析说明小权重扰动主要通过诱导的logit偏移起作用；5) 扩展到视觉，在域迁移与对地观测任务上测试logit PSV。

Result: 仅扰动单个解码块已恢复大部分增益；冻结模型+随机logit偏置在多数推理任务上匹配或超过全权重扰动；视觉域迁移（含对地观测）中logit PSV显著提升性能，而全权重扰动额外收益很小；一阶分析表明小权重扰动主要通过logit偏移发挥作用。

Conclusion: 归因于“邻近权重空间专家”的增益，实际上可由输出多样性、验证集选择与投票获得；logit PSV在全冻结模型上实现这些收益，为语言与视觉任务提供了一个简单、免训练的全权重扰动集成替代方案。同时把Neural Thickets与采样、自一致性、验证器引导选择等推理时方法联系起来。

Abstract: The Neural Thickets hypothesis posits that pretrained language models lie in dense neighborhoods of task-specific experts in weight space, which random Gaussian perturbations can uncover. We reproduce the reported gains, but show that they do not require this geometric explanation. Perturbing a single decoder block already recovers much of the improvement, while keeping the model entirely frozen and adding a random bias to its output logits matches or exceeds full-weight perturbations on most reasoning tasks. This suggests an alternative explanation based on output diversity, validation-based selection, and voting. We formalize this view as Perturb-Select-Vote (PSV), a general procedure that generates perturbed predictors, selects high-performing members on a small validation set, and aggregates their predictions.
  This interpretation connects Neural Thickets to established inference-time methods, including sampling, self-consistency, and verifier-guided selection. A first-order analysis further shows that small weight perturbations act primarily through their induced logit shifts. We extend this analysis to vision, where logit PSV substantially improves performance under domain shift, including on Earth observation, while full-weight perturbations provide little additional benefit. Together, these results suggest that the gains attributed to nearby weight-space experts can instead be obtained through output diversity, selection, and voting. Logit PSV realizes these benefits with a fully frozen model, providing a simple, training-free alternative to full-weight perturbation ensembles across language and vision.

</details>


### [403] [Propose-Filter-Release: Scaling Noise to Typical Sensitivity via Private Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40654)
*Moshe Shenfeld,Eliad Tsfadia,Katrina Ligett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 propose-filter-release (PFR) 框架，通过私有过滤剔除"不合群"记录，将仅在数据"友好"时成立的友好差分隐私机制升级为标准差分隐私，且噪声尺度只取决于剩余数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私机制按单条记录的最坏影响来放大噪声，导致少数敏感/异常记录主导噪声尺度，即便其余数据本可支持更小噪声。直接删除这些记录虽可降低噪声，但删除过程本身必须保证隐私。

Method: PFR 由三个步骤组成：私有地提出并过滤掉不合群的记录（propose-filter），再在剩余数据上运行发布机制（release）。其所需组件仅为一个凹的 friendliness 分数（衡量记录与数据集的契合程度）和一个 friendly-DP 发布机制（即被改动记录契合剩余数据时才保证隐私）。PFR 对任意此类分数与机制，在关于记录数的公开条件下即可保证标准差分隐私。

Result: PFR 被证明是与 propose-test-release 同级的通用隐私原语，FriendlyCore 可视为其一种实现。作者通过实例化 PFR 展示了其通用性，涵盖标量统计、主子空间估计、用户直方图的 KL 散度、图的有效电阻以及治疗规则选择。

Conclusion: PFR 提供了一种通用、模块化的方式，把友好条件下的隐私保证转化为对任意输入都成立的标准差分隐私，并显著降低由少数异常记录引起的噪声开销。

Abstract: An estimator can be accurate and stable on typical data, yet sensitive to a few possible records. Differentially private mechanisms commonly scale their noise to the worst-case influence of a single record, which lets these records dictate the noise even when the remaining data would allow far less. Removing them first would restore the smaller scale, but the removal must itself be private.
  We introduce \emph{propose-filter-release} (PFR), which, by privately filtering its input, turns a mechanism that protects the addition or removal of a typical record, one that fits the remaining data, into one that is differentially private in the standard sense, on every input. Using our novel filtering step, PFR requires only a concave friendliness score, which measures how well a record fits a dataset, and a release mechanism that is friendly-DP, that is, private when the changed record fits the remaining data. For every such score and release mechanism, it guarantees differential privacy under public conditions on the number of records, so it is a general privacy primitive, comparable to propose-test-release, and FriendlyCore can be viewed as one of its implementations.
  We illustrate the generality of PFR by instantiating it for scalar statistics, principal subspace estimation, the KL divergence of user histograms, the effective resistances of a graph, and treatment-rule selection.

</details>
